<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绝对可分态与APPT态集合之间包含关系已知，且在qubit-qudit系统中二者相同，但此前缺乏对这两类态纯度的系统上界刻画。本文旨在填补这一空白，给出此类态纯度的一般性上界。

Method: 基于APPT态在任意全局酉变换下仍保持正偏转置（PPT）性质这一约束，推导APPT双量子系统纯度的上界，并将其推广至qubit-qudit情形以得到紧致（sharp）上界。

Result: 给出了APPT双量子系统纯度的上界，该上界同时适用于绝对可分态；在qubit-qudit系统中，该上界精确达到，即为APPT态和绝对可分态的最大纯度。

Conclusion: 该纯度上界为区分两类量子态提供了新工具，并在qubit-qudit维度下实现了紧致刻画，深化了对绝对可分态与APPT态几何结构的理解。

Abstract: A quantum state is called absolutely separable (resp. absolutely positive partial transpose (APPT)) if it remains separable (resp. positive partial transpose (PPT)) under any global unitary transformation. It is known that the set of all absolutely separable states is a subset of the set of all APPT states. Moreover, these two sets are identical for qubit-qudit systems. In this note, we give an upper bound for the purity of APPT bipartite states (and therefore for the purity of absolutely separable bipartite states). For qubit-qudit systems, this upper bound becomes the maximum purity of APPT (and absolutely separable) states.

</details>


### [91] [From Koszul-Complex Stabilizer Models to Superselection Profiles: Topological Rigidity and Nonsplit Extensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09913)
*Hao Song*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的同调框架，通过拓扑超选择剖形（topological superselection profile）$S_\sigma$ 及其上同调层 $E^\ell_\sigma$ 来研究平移对称性富集的拓扑（SET）相。该框架统一了toric-code层级和bivariate-bicycle码，可用于分析平移不变的Calderbank-Shor-Steane（CSS）码，并通过单层性和层间胶合（inter-layer gluing）揭示了SET相的刚性性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作分别研究了toric-code层级和bivariate-bicycle码，但缺乏统一的同调框架来描述这些模型中的平移对称性富集拓扑相（translation-symmetry-enriched topological phases）。作者希望建立一套系统方法，通过代数工具刻画一般平移不变CSS码的SET结构。

Method: 引入与稳定子映射 $(\varphi_X,\varphi_Z)$ 关联的拓扑超选择剖形 $S_\sigma$，其定义为 $\tau_{\geq1}\mathrm{RHom}_R(\overline{\mathrm{coker}\varphi_\sigma}, R)$，其上同调层 $E^\ell_\sigma \cong \mathrm{Ext}^\ell_R(\cdots,R)$ 编码扇区、融合与平移作用。作者采用矩阵级Schanuel引理方法证明刚性定理，并利用Tor计算有限尺寸Z-logicals。

Result: 对于正则Koszul模型，作者显式计算了 $E^\ell_\sigma$，证明 $S_\sigma$ 为单层结构，并通过Tor获得有限尺寸Z-logicals。在有限分辨假设下证明了刚性：非零层和度唯一确定拓扑CSS码的平移SET序。对于素数qudit，该假设自动成立。此外，3D qubit toric码的八种split和nonsplit延拓模型共享相同层但具有截然不同的SET相，表明层间胶合携带单层不可见的SET信息。

Conclusion: 拓扑超选择剖形为研究平移不变CSS码的SET相提供了统一且强大的框架。单层性刻画了正则Koszul模型的特殊结构，而存在共享全部层却属于不同SET相的例子，说明完整理解SET需要同时考虑各层信息及其层间胶合结构。

Abstract: Koszul-complex stabilizer models unify the toric-code hierarchy and bivariate-bicycle codes in one homological framework. To study translation-symmetry-enriched topological (SET) phases in these models and, more generally, in translation-invariant Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS) codes with stabilizer maps $(\varphi_X,\varphi_Z)$, we introduce the associated topological superselection profile $\mathscr S_σ:=τ_{\geq1}\mathbf R\!\operatorname{Hom}_R(\overline{\operatorname{coker}\varphi_σ},R),\ σ=X,Z$. Its cohomology layers $E_σ^\ell:=H^\ell(\mathscr S_σ)\cong_R\operatorname{Ext}_R^\ell(\overline{\operatorname{coker}\varphi_σ},R)$ encode sectors, fusion, and translation action. When finite, the $\ell=1,2,\ldots$ layers describe pointlike, looplike, and higher-dimensional excitations; $\mathscr S_σ$ retains inter-layer gluing. For regular Koszul models, we compute $E_σ^\ell$ explicitly, find $\mathscr S_σ$ single-layer, and obtain finite-size $Z$-logicals from $\operatorname{Tor}$. Single-layer means that at most one positive-degree layer $E_σ^\ell$ is nonzero. Our matrix-level Schanuel-lemma method proves rigidity: under a finite-resolution hypothesis, the nonzero layer and degree uniquely determine a topological CSS code's translation SET order at the stable translation-invariant Clifford level. For prime qudits the hypothesis is automatic, and a finite layer's translation kernel gives the exact minimal coarse graining to a toric-code stack. We realize admissible single-layer data. Beyond this regime, split and nonsplit extensions of 3D qubit toric codes yield eight models sharing all identical $E_σ^\ell$ layers with trivial translation action but having distinct size-dependent ground-state degeneracies, hence distinct translation SET phases. Thus inter-layer gluing carries SET data invisible to individual layers.

</details>


### [92] [Decoupling 2D translation-invariant topological CSS codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09915)
*Yifei Wang,Zhongyi Ni,Mingxin He,Jinguo Liu,Yingfei Gu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明二维平移不变的拓扑CSS码经过最大任意子保持超晶格后，可以局域幺正解耦为环面码（toric codes）与乘积态的叠加，并给出高效算法及超胞尺寸和算子扩展的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知二维平移不变的拓扑CSS码在粗粒化后局域等价于环面码堆叠，但现有构造往往破坏比消除任意子置换平移所需更多的平移对称性，不确定是否存在更基本的障碍。作者旨在证明不存在此类障碍。

Method: 通过过渡到最大任意子保持超晶格（maximal anyon-preserving superlattice），证明每个此类码均可局域幺正解耦为环面码和乘积态。进一步设计了显式构造解耦映射的高效算法，并给出所需超胞尺寸和算子扩展的界限。

Result: 证明在最大任意子保持超晶格上不存在进一步对称性障碍，所有二维平移不变拓扑CSS码均可局域解耦。解耦在一般情况下不需要额外辅助比特（ancillas），且可推广到具有合适边界条件的有限系统。

Conclusion: 研究结果为二维拓扑CSS码的局域分类提供了完整图景，表明任何剩余的结构障碍均不存在，并提供了实用的显式解耦算法可用于实际构造。

Abstract: Two-dimensional translation-invariant topological CSS codes on qubits are known to be locally equivalent, after coarse-graining, to stacks of toric codes. However, existing constructions generally break more translation symmetry than is required to remove anyon-permuting translations, leaving open whether any further obstruction exists. We prove that no such obstruction occurs: after passing to the maximal anyon-preserving superlattice, every such code admits a local unitary decoupling into toric codes and product states. We further provide an efficient algorithm for explicitly constructing the decoupling map, together with bounds on the required supercell size and operator spreading. The decoupling requires no additional ancillas in generic cases and extends to finite systems with suitable boundary conditions.

</details>


### [93] [Time-reparameterisation invariant quantum evolution law: the lack of absolute time does not imply a stationary global state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08065)
*Ognyan Oreshkov,Denis Bouvy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文挑战了在无绝对时间的情况下量子系统必然处于哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）本征稳态的普遍假设，提出了一种时间重参数化不变的量子演化定律。该定律对于给定初始条件预测与薛定谔方程（Schrödinger equation）相同的态空间轨迹，但无法预测非平庸轨迹的遍历速度，其解为薛定谔方程的一切时间重参数化解。与Page-Wootters形式或Dirac哈密顿约束量子化不同，该框架中的整体态并非稳态，并讨论了需要重新审视的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战物理学中一个根深蒂固的信念——当不存在绝对时间参数、量子系统缺少外部时钟参照时，该系统可以假定处于自身哈密顿量的稳态。作者认为这一结论的推导过程中所依赖的假设值得重新审视。

Method: 提出一种时间重参数化不变（time-reparameterisation invariant）的量子演化定律。该定律在给定初始条件时预测与薛定谔方程相同的态空间轨迹，但对于非平凡轨迹不预测其遍历速度，其解涵盖了薛定谔方程的全部时间重参数化解。作者进一步展示了在此框架中，如何相对于内部时钟（internal clock）恢复薛定谔方程的预言。

Result: 该演化定律的所有解都是薛定谔方程解的时间重参数化形式；相对于内部时钟可以恢复薛定谔的标准预言。与Page-Wootters形式或Dirac哈密顿约束量子化方法不同，此处全局态不是稳态（stationary）。

Conclusion: 导致"量子态可视为稳态"这一常见结论的潜在假设需要被重新审视。本文提供一个不一致的替代框架，其中全局态非稳态，从而质疑了无外部时钟量子系统的既定图像。

Abstract: We challenge the common belief that if there is no absolute time parameter in physics, a quantum system described without reference to an external clock can be assumed to be in a stationary state of its Hamiltonian. We present a time-reparameterisation invariant quantum evolution law, which for a given initial condition predicts the same trajectory in state space as the Schrödinger equation, except that for nontrivial trajectories it does not predict the speed at which the trajectory is traversed. The solutions of this evolution law are all time-reparameterised solutions of the Schrödinger equation. We show how the predictions of the Schrödinger equation are recovered relative to an internal clock in this framework. In contrast to the Page-Wootters formalism or Dirac's quantisation of the Hamiltonian constraint, here the global sate is not stationary. We discuss the assumptions leading to the common conclusion that the state can be taken stationary and suggest that they need to be revisited.

</details>


### [94] [Opposite post-processing orders of fermionic horizon channels and their quantum-resource monotonicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08215)
*Shuhao Li,Jin-Ming Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究将非惯性系与黑洞背景下有效单模费米子视界变换重新表述为一对互补的外部和内部量子通道，证明外部通道随相对论参数 q 增加而逐步退化而内部通道方向相反，从而将各类量子资源和关联的单调趋势追溯到这一共同的通道排序结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的相对论量子信息研究往往针对特定输入态和态泛函进行显式计算来确定资源行为，无法厘清反复出现的单调趋势源于具体选择还是更深层的共性结构。作者旨在揭示这一共性结构。

Method: 将有效单模费米子视界变换分解为互补的外部（可访问）与内部（不可访问）量子通道，证明二者存在方向相反的精确后处理（post-processing）排序，并将该关系推广至任意多体情形；此外在Garfinkle-Horowitz-Strominger（GHS）膨胀黑洞时空下基于量子Jensen-Shannon散度（QJSD）进行数值评估。

Result: 任何在对应中间映射（intermediate maps）下非增的态泛函，在同质外部扇区非增而在同质内部扇区非减。该框架适用于纠缠单调量、占据基相干性单调量、优化的Bell泛函量以及压缩散度关联度量等广泛量子资源；数值结果验证了QJSD集体相干性的同质单调性。

Conclusion: 反复出现的单调趋势根源在于统一的通道排序结构；物理设定决定了参数 q 的取值方式，而资源理论单调性决定了哪些输出态量继承该排序。

Abstract: Relativistic quantum-information studies in noninertial and black-hole settings often determine resource behavior through explicit calculations for particular input states and state functionals, leaving unclear whether the recurring monotonic trends originate from those choices or from a common underlying structure. In this work, we formulate the effective single-mode fermionic horizon transformation as a pair of complementary exterior and interior quantum channels, corresponding respectively to the physically accessible and inaccessible sectors, and establish exact post-processing orders in opposite directions. As the relativistic channel parameter $q$ increases, the exterior channel becomes progressively degraded, whereas the interior channel is ordered in the reverse direction. These relations extend to arbitrary multipartite settings. Consequently, every state functional that is non-increasing under the corresponding intermediate maps is non-increasing in homogeneous exterior sectors and non-decreasing in homogeneous interior sectors. The framework therefore applies to broad classes of quantum resources and correlations, including entanglement and occupation-basis coherence monotones, optimized Bell-functional quantities, and contractive-divergence correlation measures. We further numerically evaluate collective coherence based on the quantum Jensen-Shannon divergence (QJSD) in the Garfinkle-Horowitz-Strominger (GHS) dilaton-black-hole spacetime, illustrating the predicted homogeneous monotonicity. The recurring trends are therefore traced to a common channel-ordering structure, while the physical setting determines the parameterization of $q$ and the resource-theoretic monotonicity determines which output-state quantities inherit the order.

</details>


### [95] [How to Test Bell Nonlocality for Gravity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08778)
*Debarshi Das,Mir Alimuddin,Simon Storz,Yiwen Chu,Sougato Bose*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一项实验方案，通过在光学隔离空间中以纠缠自旋耦合质量运动产生空间叠加态，进而在没有任何局域性漏洞（locality loophole）的条件下检验引力场的贝尔非局域性（Bell nonlocality），为首次在文献中提供完全设备无关（device-independent）的引力非经典性验证工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于检验引力非经典性的研究方案尚未能够以无漏洞且设备无关的方式直接验证引力场的贝尔非局域性，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将两个嵌入纠缠自旋（如NV色心自旋纠缠的金刚石）的质量放在彼此光锥之外，自旋与各自质量运动耦合以产生空间叠加态，随后仅对两个质量的引力场进行局域测量。

Result: 研究表明，若引力是量子的，两个纠缠质量将使其引力场纠缠，从而产生能够证明引力贝尔非局域性的关联。陷阱冷却的纳米物体（nano-objects）配合微米级空间叠加即可满足此实验条件。

Conclusion: 该方案首次提供了四项最小化工具：制备引力曲率的爱因斯坦-波多尔斯基-罗森（EPR）态、见证纠缠的引力曲率、排除引力的任何局域实在论描述，以及以完全设备无关的方式实现引力非经典性的无漏洞检验。

Abstract: We propose an experiment to test Bell nonlocality, a genuine nonclassicality, for the gravitational field. Two masses with embedded entangled spins (e.g., two diamonds with their NV-centre spins entangled) are placed well outside each other's light cones, ensuring a locality-loophole-free scenario. The spins are then coupled to the motion of their respective masses to generate spatial superpositions. Finally, local measurements are performed only on the gravitational fields of the two masses. If gravity is quantum, then the two entangled masses would entangle their gravitational fields, leading to correlations certifying Bell nonlocality of gravity. Trapped and ground-state cooled nano-objects with micron-sized spatial superposition are sufficient for this test. This goes beyond the recent proposals to test nonclassicality of gravity by providing, for the first time in the literature, a minimal tool to (i) create Einstein-Podolsky-Rosen (EPR) state of gravitational curvatures, (ii) witness entangled gravitational curvatures, (iii) rule out any local-realist description of gravity, and (iv) achieve a loophole-free test of gravity's nonclassicality in a fully device-independent way.

</details>


### [96] [Spread of Entanglement in Generalized Kicked Ising Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09695)
*Tanay Pathak,Hiromi Ebisu,Tomaž Prosen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究广义kick伊辛链（kicked Ising chain）中纠缠动力学，将模型从标准量子比特情形（局域维度q=2）推广到更高局域维度（q>2），识别出"对偶幺正"（dual-unitary）点并利用转移矩阵法与复制技巧（replica trick）推导出q=3模型中纠缠熵的精确表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准kick伊辛链（local dimension q=2）中对偶幺正点的纠缠动力学已有充分解析刻画，但对于更高局域维度（q>2）的推广模型中是否存在对偶幺正点以及相应的精确纠缠动力学，此前尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 将模型推广至局域维度q>2，分析其时空对偶性（space-time duality）以寻找对偶幺正点；利用针对高维情形特别适配的转移矩阵法（transfer matrix method）和复制技巧，从一类可解初态出发推导q=3（kick Potts型）模型中纠缠熵的精确表达式。

Result: q=3和q=4系统中存在解析上独特的对偶幺正点，而q≥5时由于不存在满足矩阵元条件的唯一kick强度，此类点不存在；在对偶幺正点处，Rényi熵和von Neumann纠缠熵均随时间线性增长，直至达到由子系统尺寸决定的最大值。

Conclusion: 对偶幺正性可推广至更高局域维度，但其存在性依赖于局域维度大小；在q=3情形下，转移矩阵法和复制技巧可精确刻画纠缠熵的线性增长及其饱和行为。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics of entanglement in a generalized version of the kicked Ising chain, extending the model from the standard qubit case (local dimension $q=2$) to higher local dimensions ($q > 2$). We identify the existence of ''dual-unitary'' points where the model's space-time duality allows for exact analytical solutions. Our analysis reveals that while a few unique dual-unitary points exist analytically for systems with local dimensions $q=3$ and $q=4$, such points do not exist for $q \ge 5$ due to the lack of a unique kicking strength that satisfies the required matrix element conditions. Utilizing the transfer matrix method and a replica trick specifically adapted for higher dimensions, we derive exact expressions for the growth of entanglement entropy in the $q=3$ (kicked Potts-type) model starting from a class of solvable initial states. Our results demonstrate that at the dual-unitary point, both Rényi and von Neumann entanglement entropies grow linearly with time until reaching a maximum value determined by the subsystem size.

</details>


### [97] [Quantized Stabilizer-Rényi Boundary Response across Fermionic SPT Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09749)
*M. A. Rajabpour*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究在自由费米子BDI链中，通过体相扣除（bulk-subtracted）方法提取了开放边界下马约拉纳（Majorana）模对稳定子Rényi熵的边界响应，并证明该响应在质量反转下趋近于|Δω|ln2，可用于计数改变费米子SPT指标的Majorana通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放边界承载对称性保护的马约拉纳模，但其在稳定子Rényi熵（stabilizer Rényi entropy）上的印记被非普适的体积律（volume law）所遮蔽，需要一种方法将其分离出来。

Method: 在Rényi指标α=1/2下，作者利用精确有限开链关系、大L的Pfaffian求值以及直接正权重检查，在自由费米子BDI链中隔离出体相扣除的边界响应。

Result: 在质量反转（mass inversion）过程中，该边界响应趋近于|Δω|ln2，其中Δω为绕数（winding number）的变化。该响应在原始体相形变、所测试的局部边界扰动以及破缺的体相对偶性下均保持不变。

Conclusion: 在所研究的族系中，边界响应能够计数改变费米子SPT指标（fermionic SPT index）的马约拉纳通道数量。

Abstract: Open boundaries host symmetry-protected Majorana modes, yet their imprint on the stabilizer Rényi entropy is obscured by a nonuniversal volume law. At Rényi index $α=1/2$, we isolate a bulk-subtracted boundary response in free-fermion BDI chains using exact finite-open-chain relations, large-$L$ Pfaffian evaluations, and direct positive-weight checks. Across a mass inversion, this response approaches $|Δω|\ln 2$, where $Δω$ is the change in winding number. The response survives primitive bulk deformations, the tested local boundary perturbations, and broken bulk duality. Thus, for the families studied here, the boundary response counts the Majorana channels that change the fermionic SPT index.

</details>


### [98] [Quantum Information Flow under String-Diagram Rewriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09823)
*Yi-Yu Lin,Chen-Ye Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了Bob Coecke早期工作中提出的"量子信息流"（quantum information flow）概念，并为其提供显式的字符串图（string diagram）形式化定义。作者区分了仅依赖当前图形呈现的表观通路（apparent through-paths）和可通过连续重写（rewriting）兼容继承到终端解耦裸线因子（bare wire factor）的真实通路（genuine through-paths），并据此形式化定义了"Coecke流线"（Coecke flow line）。文章以ZX演算（ZX calculus）为例加以说明，并将其在量子协议背景下解释为协议字符串图中目标裸线态射因子的受约束、准局域、线状呈现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Coecke早期引入的"量子信息流"概念缺乏显式的形式化框架；本文旨在填补这一空白，为信息流提供严格的字符串图形式化，以支持语义保持重写序列中信息传播路径的精确刻画。

Method: 文章在语义保持的字符串图重写序列中，先区分依赖于当前图形表示的表观通路与可跨重写步骤兼容继承直至终端解耦裸线因子的真实通路，再基于这一兼容继承关系（compatible inheritance relation）形式化定义"Coecke流线"。具体实现以ZX字符串图重写系统为实例进行演示。

Result: 文章给出了Coecke流线的严格形式定义，并在ZX演算框架下展示了其运作方式；在量子协议（quantum protocols）的物理背景下，Coecke流被诠释为协议字符串图中目标裸线态射因子的一种受约束、准局域（quasi-local）、线状（line-like）呈现。

Conclusion: 通过将量子信息流显式化为字符串图上的可兼容继承结构，本文为量子协议中的信息传播路径提供了精确的图形化形式化，并以ZX演算作为主要例证支撑了该框架的有效性。

Abstract: We revisit the notion of ``quantum information flow'' introduced in Bob Coecke's early work and seek to give it an explicit string-diagrammatic formalization. Given a semantics preserving string-diagram rewriting sequence, we first distinguish apparent through-paths, which depend on the current graphical presentation, from genuine through paths, which can be compatibly inherited through successive rewrites to a terminal decoupled bare wire factor. We then formally define a ``Coecke flow line'' in terms of this compatible inheritance relation.
  In particular, we use the ZX calculus, i.e., the ZX string diagram rewriting system, to illustrate the resulting formalism. In the physical setting of quantum protocols, a Coecke flow can be interpreted as a constrained, quasi-local, line-like presentation of a target bare wire morphism factor within the protocol string diagram.

</details>


### [99] [Quantum Relaxometry Under Continuous Wave Excitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07697)
*Vladimir Verkhovlyuk,Chayma Bouchair,Oleg A. Anisimov,Anton Pershin,Adam Gali*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于连续波（continuous-wave）协议的量子弛豫测量（quantum relaxometry）方法，通过测量低频频微波幅度调制下光探测磁共振信号的频率响应来提取自旋-晶格弛豫时间 $T_1$，实现了跨越三个数量级（60 μs 至 200 ms）的高效测量，并在纳米金刚石中展示了定量传感应用及测量速度的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的脉冲协议（pulsed protocols）在测量 $T_1$ 时仅能在有限的时间范围内高效工作，这限制了量子弛豫测量技术的应用范围和通量。作者旨在开发一种能够在更宽时间尺度上高效测量 $T_1$ 的新方法。

Method: 引入一种工作在频域的连续波量子弛豫测量协议：在低频频微波幅度调制条件下测量光探测磁共振（optically detected magnetic resonance, ODMR）信号的频率响应，从自旋系统的特征响应时间中提取 $T_1$。

Result: 该方法实现了从 60 μs 到 200 ms 跨越三个数量级以上的高效 $T_1$ 测量，且在宽温度范围和显著系综不均匀性（ensemble inhomogeneity）条件下均有效。在纳米金刚石中实现了定量弛豫传感，相较脉冲方法获得了显著的测量加速，并为通过优化纳米金刚石尺寸以增强灵敏度提供了实用途径。

Conclusion: 连续波频域协议克服了传统脉冲方法的时间范围限制，为金刚石氮空位（NV）中心及其他固态自旋量子比特的量子弛豫测量提供了一种更高效、适用范围更广的替代方案。

Abstract: Quantum relaxometry is one of the most successful applications of nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers in diamond and, more broadly, solid-state spin qubits, enabling ultrasensitive detection of magnetic noise and paramagnetic species via measurements of the spin-lattice relaxation time $T_1$. Conventional pulsed protocols, however, probe $T_1$ efficiently only over a limited temporal range, which restricts the scope and throughput of the technique. Here we introduce a continuous-wave quantum relaxometry protocol that operates in the frequency domain. By measuring the frequency response of the optically detected magnetic resonance signal under low-frequency microwave amplitude modulation, we extract $T_1$ from the characteristic response time of the spin system. The method enables efficient $T_1$ measurements spanning more than three orders of magnitude -- directly demonstrated from 60 $μ$s to 200 ms in our experiments -- across a broad temperature range and under substantial ensemble inhomogeneity. We further show that this protocol enables quantitative relaxometry-based sensing in nanodiamonds, achieving a substantial speed-up over the pulsed methods and offering a practical approach to optimizing nanodiamond size for enhanced sensitivity.

</details>


### [100] [Autoregressive Projective Quantum Monte Carlo: From a Hermitian to a Non-Hermitian Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09496)
*Lavoisier Wah,Remmy Zen,Flore K. Kunst*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自回归投影量子蒙特卡洛（autoregressive projective quantum Monte Carlo, PQMC）框架，利用循环神经网络（recurrent neural network, RNN）引导随机动力学，在保持多项式计算成本的同时显著提升了精确度，且在厄米与非厄米哈密顿量下均优于传统变分RNN方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确确定量子多体系统（quantum many-body systems）的基态性质仍是一项核心挑战，需要开发更有效的计算方法。

Method: 将自回归采样（autoregressive sampling）纳入投影量子蒙特卡洛框架，利用循环神经网络引导随机动力学过程，以增强蒙特卡洛模拟的采样效率与准确度。

Result: 与标准无引导PQMC相比，自回归PQMC在保持多项式计算成本的同时实现了更高的准确度；与常规变分RNN拟设（variational RNN ansätze）相比，无论系统尺寸大小、哈密顿量是否为厄米（Hermitian）或非厄米（non-Hermitian），该方法均能获得更低的能量和更高的保真度（fidelity）。

Conclusion: 研究结果凸显了神经引导PQMC方法的通用性与强大能力，为复杂量子多体系统中低能态的可扩展模拟开辟了前景。

Abstract: Accurately determining the ground-state properties of quantum many-body systems remains a central challenge. In this work, we introduce an autoregressive projective quantum Monte Carlo (PQMC) framework that leverages recurrent neural networks (RNNs) to guide the stochastic dynamics. By incorporating autoregressive sampling into PQMC, we demonstrate substantial improvements in accuracy compared to standard unguided PQMC, while retaining polynomial computational cost. We benchmark our approach against conventional variational RNN ansätze and find that the autoregressive PQMC consistently achieves lower energies and higher fidelity, regardless of system size or whether the Hamiltonian is Hermitian or non-Hermitian. Our results highlight the versatility and power of neural-guided PQMC methods, paving the way for promising scalable simulations of low-energy states in complex quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [101] [State Diagnostics of Complexity in Open Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09705)
*Komal Sah,Fabio Anza,Alexandra M. Jurgens,James P. Crutchfield*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究有限尺寸量子系统中动力学的复杂性如何从封闭相干演化过渡到相互作用且有效开放行为的过程中涌现，提出基于Wasserstein距离的可区分性度量和态空间覆盖指数两个几何诊断量，应用于量子踢顶模型并发现其随相互作用强度增加而增长，且受到宇称对称性的结构化影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解有限尺寸量子系统从封闭相干演化到开放相互作用动力学中复杂性如何涌现，是量子动力学领域的核心问题之一。现有方法缺乏在对量子态进行几何化表示下统一追踪这种过渡的框架，作者提出一种基于态空间几何的诊断工具来量化该过程中的动力学复杂性。

Method: 采用基于态的几何框架（state-based geometric framework），将混合量子态表示为复射影希尔伯特空间（complex projective Hilbert space）上的概率测度。引入两个互补诊断量：一是基于混合态概率测度表示间的Wasserstein距离的可区分性度量，用于量化对初始态的敏感性；二是态空间覆盖指数（state-space coverage index），用于度量子系统态空间的长时间探索程度。

Result: 将上述诊断量应用于量子踢顶模型（quantum kicked top），发现两个诊断量总体上随相互作用强度增加而增大。其对环境尺寸的依赖受到宇称对称性（parity symmetry）的结构化影响：整数自旋（integer-spin）系统通常比半整数自旋（half-integer-spin）系统表现出更高的敏感性和态空间覆盖度。

Conclusion: 结果表明有限尺寸量子效应显著，该几何方法为在深度量子区（quantum regime）量化动力学复杂性提供了新途径，并揭示了宇称对称性在复杂性涌现中的关键作用。

Abstract: We study the emergence of complexity in finite-size quantum systems as their dynamics transition from closed and coherent evolution to interacting and effectively open behavior. Using a state-based geometric framework, we represent mixed quantum states as probability measures on complex projective Hilbert space. This representation allows us to track how interactions reshape the underlying pure-state geometry. We introduce two complementary diagnostics: a distinguishability measure, based on the Wasserstein distance between probability-measure representations of mixed states, that quantifies sensitivity to initial states, and a state-space coverage index that measures long-time exploration of the subsystem state space. These diagnostics provide a geometric perspective on the emergence and evolution of quantum dynamical complexity. When applied to the quantum kicked top, both diagnostics generally increase with interaction strength. Their dependence on environment size is structured by parity symmetry, with integer-spin systems often exhibiting greater sensitivity and state-space coverage than half-integer-spin systems. These results highlight finite-size quantum effects and provide a geometric approach to quantifying dynamical complexity deep in the quantum regime

</details>


### [102] [A Quantum Coherence Microscope in the Hubbard Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09924)
*Lin Su,Michal Szurek,Alec Douglas,Ceren B. Dag,Markus Greiner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用塔尔博特效应（Talbot effect）引入了一种量子相干显微镜（quantum coherence microscope），能够将近距离外的非对角关联（off-diagonal correlations）映射为具有近单格点分辨率的密度信号，并利用该技术在三维光晶格（three-dimensional optical lattice）的一个层面中局域探测了超流-莫特转变（superfluid-Mott transition），还测量了工程化势能地貌中的非最近邻相干性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子相干性是集体量子现象和新兴量子技术的基础，但尽管量子气体显微镜（quantum gas microscope）能以单原子分辨率提供多体态的投影快照，空间分辨的非对角关联测量仍然难以实现，这是本研究的核心动机。

Method: 基于塔尔博特效应（Talbot effect）构建量子相干显微镜，通过受控的塔尔博特演化将非对角关联映射到具有近单格点（near-single-site）分辨率的位点分辨密度信号上，从而实现对相干性关联的空间测量。

Result: 该技术在三维光晶格（three-dimensional optical lattice）的一个层面中局域探测了超流-莫特转变（superfluid-Mott transition），并在工程化势能地貌（engineered potential landscape）中实现了超越最近邻距离的相干性测量。

Conclusion: 通过受控塔尔博特演化将非对角关联映射到密度信号，该方法开辟了通过定制物质波演化与再捕获来获取密度基以外的可观测量（observables）的可能性。

Abstract: Quantum coherence underlies collective quantum phenomena and emerging quantum technologies. Quantum gas microscopes have transformed quantum simulation by providing projective snapshots of many-body states with single-atom resolution, but spatially resolved measurements of off-diagonal correlations have remained elusive. Here, using the Talbot effect, we introduce a quantum coherence microscope that maps off-diagonal correlations onto site-resolved density signals with near-single-site resolution. We use this technique to locally probe the superfluid-Mott transition in a layer of a three-dimensional optical lattice and to measure coherence beyond nearest neighbors in an engineered potential landscape. By mapping off-diagonal correlations onto density signals through controlled Talbot evolution, this work opens new possibilities for accessing observables beyond the density basis through tailored matter-wave evolution and recapture.

</details>
