# hep-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [Celestial Holography, A Hitchhiker's Guide to the Celestial Sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07568)
*Faïçal Barzi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文是一篇介绍性入门论文（primer），旨在向新研究者系统介绍天体全息（Celestial Holography）这一快速发展的理论框架，该框架致力于在渐近平坦时空（asymptotically flat spacetimes）中描述量子引力。文章从AdS/CFT对应（AdS/CFT correspondence）出发，覆盖Bondi-Sachs形式体系、零无穷远几何、BMS渐近对称群以及由Mellin变换（Mellin transform）构建的核心全息映射，即把四维散射振幅重铸为天体球上二维天体共形场论（CCFT）的相关函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天体全息是描述渐近平坦时空中量子引力的新兴理论框架，但缺乏面向新入门者的系统介绍。本论文旨在填补这一空白，为初学者提供完整的概念图景与技术术语体系。

Method: 论文采用教学式叙述（pedagogical exposition）方法，以AdS/CFT对应为起点逐步过渡到Minkowski空间；系统阐述Bondi-Sachs形式体系与零无穷远的几何结构，介绍BMS渐近对称群的发现过程，并解释通过Mellin变换将四维散射振幅映射为2D天体共形场论相关函数的全息对应机制。

Result: 论文提供了一套直观且数学精确的完整介绍，涵盖天体全息的核心概念框架，包括零无穷远几何、BMS对称群和Mellin变换全息映射，使新入门者能够同时掌握该领域的概念图景与技术支持词汇。

Conclusion: 该入门论文旨在为新人提供探索天体全息这一前沿领域所需的概念基础与技术语言，构建从AdS/CFT到Minkowski空间量子引力描述的完整认知路径。

Abstract: This primer offers an first introduction to Celestial Holography, a rapidly evolving framework aiming to describe quantum gravity in asymptotically flat spacetimes. We start from the established foundation of the AdS/CFT correspondence before venturing into the wilds of Minkowski space. The journey covers the essential Bondi-Sachs formalism and the geometry of null infinity ($\mathscr{I}^\pm$), the discovery of the infinite-dimensional BMS asymptotic symmetry group. We explain the core holographic map given by Mellin transform that recasts four-dimensional scattering amplitudes as correlation functions of a conjectured 2D Celestial Conformal Field Theory (CCFT) on the celestial sphere. The presentation is made intuitive but the mathematics is still precise (hopefully!). It is designed to equip newcomers with both the conceptual landscape and the technical vocabulary to explore this exciting frontier.

</details>


### [104] [Three-loop onset of the wormhole length in DSSYK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07634)
*Eleonora Alfinito,Matteo Beccaria*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在盘面（disk level）上计算了正弦-引力（sine-dilaton gravity）中爱因斯坦-罗森桥（Einstein-Rosen bridge）的初始长度 L₀，将其与双重标度SYK模型（double-scaled SYK, DSSYK）的Krylov扩展复杂度（Krylov spread complexity）对应起来，并通过三圈（three loops）及闭式（closed form）计算延伸了此前已知的单圈结果，同时证明在低温区域圈级展开可重整化为以Schwarzian耦合λβ为级数参数的微扰展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在引力/全息对偶（holographic duality）中，爱因斯坦-罗森桥的长度被认为对应边界理论的量子复杂度（complexity），但这种对应中通常存在一个仅能由全息重整化方案选择的加性常数（additive constant）。本文旨在通过微观的DSSYK模型为这一常数提供无歧义的确定，并系统性地将引力侧的圈展开与边界理论的微扰展开对应起来。

Method: 作者在DSSYK侧计算初始热态（thermofield double state, t=0, 任意温度β）的平均弦数（average chord number）以得到L₀的微观表达式。两圈贡献通过对DSSYK两点函数在重合插入点处的鞍点（saddle point）求值获得，其中个别发散项仅在求和后抵消。三圈贡献则绕过鞍点分析，利用精确递推关系（exact recursion relation）生成长的高温展开序列以验证所提出的Ansatz。低温区域中，通过整理每圈新增β幂次的行为，将半经典级数重新组织为Schwarzian耦合λβ的幂级数，并利用独立的单圈Schwarzian计算检验首阶系数。相同方法还应用于长度方差（variance）和三阶累积量（third-order cumulant）的两圈计算。

Result: L₀被精确地确定为λ乘以热态的平均弦数，从而给出了此前仅依赖全息重整化方案固定之加性常数的微观无歧义确定。两圈计算中发散项在求和中精确抵消；三圈计算通过递推方法在低计算成本下获得了大量独立数据点并验证了Ansatz。低温区域中半经典级数被成功重组为λβ的幂级数，其首阶系数与独立的单圈Schwarzian计算结果一致。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了sine-dilaton引力中爱因斯坦-罗森桥长度与DSSYK模型中Krylov复杂度之间的精确对应，并证明了该对应在多个圈级上均成立，同时通过微观计算解决了全息重整化方案依赖性问题，为复杂度/体积对应（complexity/volume correspondence）提供了更坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: The length of the Einstein-Rosen bridge in sine-dilaton gravity at disk level equals the Krylov spread complexity of the dual double-scaled SYK (DSSYK) model, with the double-scaling parameter $λ$ controlling the semiclassical expansion. We compute the onset value, $L_{0}$, in the thermofield double state at $t=0$ and arbitrary inverse temperature $β$, through three loops and in closed form, extending the known one-loop result. The quantity $L_{0}$ represents the preparation complexity of the initial thermal Hartle-Hawking state. In DSSYK, $L_{0}$ is $λ$ times the average chord number of the thermal state, providing a microscopic, unambiguous determination of an additive constant otherwise fixed only by a choice of holographic renormalization scheme. The two-loop contribution follows from a saddle point evaluation of the DSSYK two-point function at coincident insertion points, where individually divergent contributions cancel only in their sum. At three loops, we bypass the saddle point analysis using an exact recursion relation that generates long high-temperature expansions at low cost, fixing and testing a proposed Ansatz against many independent data points. Finally, in the low-temperature regime each loop order contributes one further power of $β$, reorganizing the semiclassical series into an expansion in the Schwarzian coupling $λβ$, whose leading coefficient we check against an independent one-loop Schwarzian computation. The same methods, applied to the length variance and third-order cumulant at $t=0$, give their semiclassical expansion through two loops.

</details>


### [105] [Towards a Holographic dual of Carrollian BCFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07658)
*Pronoy Chakraborty,Ritankar Chatterjee,Priyadarshini Pandit*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过卡罗尔框架（Carrollian framework）为AdS₃/BCFT₂对应的平面化版本奠定基础，将最近发现的边界卡罗尔共形代数（BCCA）识别为平面时空零无穷远处端世界膜（EOW brane）的对称代数，并验证了该膜的全局与渐近对称性与BCCA完全一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在建立AdS₃/BCFT₂对应的平面（flat）版本，通过卡罗尔框架将已有边界共形场论（BCFT）的平面极限推广到零无穷远的边界情形。

Method: 作者首先讨论边界卡罗尔共形代数（BCCA）——即卡罗尔边界共形场论（Carrollian BCFT）的对称代数；然后在平面时空的零无穷远处选择合适的端世界膜（EOW brane）来识别这一代数，并通过从 AdS 对应物取平面极限（flat limit）以及内在分析两种方式研究该膜的性质。

Result: 研究发现，由端世界膜限制的平面时空的全局对称性（global symmetries）与渐近对称性（asymptotic symmetries）恰好与BCCA的对称性完全重合。

Conclusion: 该结果证实了平面时空边界上的端世界膜与BCCA之间的精确对应关系，为AdS₃/BCFT₂对应的平面版本提供了坚实的对称性基础。

Abstract: In this work we aim to lay the foundation of flat version of AdS$_3$/BCFT$_2$ correspondence through Carrollian framework. We begin with discussing the recently discovered Boundary Carrollian Conformal Algebra (BCCA), the symmetry algebra of Carrollian BCFT. We identify this algebra at the null infinity of flat spacetime with appropriate choice of the end-of-the-world (EOW) brane. The properties of this EOW brane have been analysed both intrinsically and by taking flat limit from its AdS counterpart. We also find that the global as well as asymptotic symmetries of flat spacetime restricted by this EOW brane coincide exactly with the symmetries of BCCA.

</details>


### [106] [Superconformal indices and black hole saddles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07660)
*Maciej Kolanowski,Donald Marolf,Zi-Yue Wang,Wenwen Zheng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 基于AdS/CFT对偶，研究了大N极限下超共形指标（superconformal index）的体理论鞍点结构。发现当Im τ = Im σ 很大时，CFT指标迅速趋近于1，排除了此前被认为相关的具有指数级大N贡献的黑洞鞍点；提出将体路径积分定义为具有余维2奇异的实Lorentz号时空积分，该方法仅产生满足上述边界的鞍点，并通过Stokes现象强制执行该边界，ABJM情形也有类似结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AdS/CFT对应关系中，大N极限下超共形指标应通过对偶体理论中适当鞍点的求和来计算。然而此前提出的黑洞鞍点在某些参数区域与CFT指标的行为不一致，需要重新检验体路径积分中可行的鞍点类型，以正确理解对偶关系在该极限下的实现。

Method: 通过分析N=4 SU(N)超对称杨-米尔斯（supersymmetric Yang-Mills）理论中超共形指标I对势参数σ, τ, Δ̄以及Im τ = Im σ趋于无穷时极限行为的约束，排除不合理的鞍点；随后将体路径积分定义为对具有余维2（codimension-2）奇异性的实Lorentz号时空的积分，研究由此产生的允许鞍点及其通过Stokes现象对边界的强制执行，并举一反三应用于ABJM指标的AdS₄体计算。

Result: 在大Im τ = Im σ极限下，CFT指标I在所有N取值下都迅速趋近于1，因此任何具有指数级大N贡献的黑洞鞍点在该区域都被排除，包括此前文献中提出与这些区域相关的鞍点；基于实Lorentz号时空积分的新方法仅保留满足该边界的鞍点，且该边界通过Stokes现象被自然强制；类似结论同样适用于ABJM超共形指标的AdS₄体计算。

Conclusion: 大N极限下体理论路径积分的鞍点选择必须受到CFT指标在特定参数极限下趋于1的强约束，此前提出的黑洞鞍点在相关区域无效；将路径积分视为对实Lorentz号奇异时空的积分是自洽的框架，其通过Stokes现象自然实现边界条件，且在N=4 SYM与ABJM两种对偶体系中均成立。

Abstract: The AdS/CFT correspondence implies that the superconformal index ${\mathcal I}$ in ${\mathcal N=4}$ SU(N) supersymmetric Yang-Mills theory can be computed using the dual bulk theory. In particular, in the limit of large $N$, the index should be given by a sum over appropriate saddles. However, ${\mathcal I}$ depends on potentials $σ, τ, \vec Δ$ and, at large ${\rm Im}\, τ= {\rm Im}\, σ$, the CFT index ${\mathcal I}$ rapidly approaches $1$ at all values of $N$. As a result, black hole saddles associated with exponentially large contributions in $N$ cannot contribute in this limit. This in particular excludes saddles that were previously suggested to be relevant in such regimes. We thus consider an approach to the bulk path integral motivated by taking it to be defined as an integral over real Lorentz-signature spacetimes with codimension-2 singularities. This approach leads only to saddles that satisfy the above bound, and to the enforcement of this bound via Stokes phenomena. We also find similar results for bulk AdS$_4$ calculations of the ABJM superconformal index.

</details>


### [107] [R7-branes and AdS$_{\text{9}}$ solutions in type IIB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07664)
*Giuseppe Dibitetto,Cameron Gibson,Nicolò Petri,Colin Sterckx*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了IIB型超引力（type IIB supergravity）中新发现的AdS₉背景，在新的坐标系中重新审视这些解并计算了包括全息中心荷（holographic central charge）在内的相关可观测量，随后构造了以这些AdS₉为近地平线几何的非超对称7-膜（7-brane）解，并通过横截面上反射单值性τ→−τ̄的讨论将其与R7膜联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来在IIB型超引力中发现了AdS₉背景，但这些解的几何结构和物理意义尚未被充分理解，特别是其近地平线几何对应的膜（brane）构型仍未明确。本研究旨在通过系统梳理这些背景的坐标形式、计算全息可观测量，并寻找产生这些背景的显式膜解，从而加深对该类AdS₉背景的整体认识。

Method: 首先在更方便的坐标系中重新审视AdS₉背景解，利用该坐标形式显式计算若干相关可观测量，包括全息中心荷。其次，研究由IIB轴矢量标量（axio-dilaton）场源化的非超对称7-膜构型，构造显式的解析7-膜解，使其近地平线几何具有上述AdS₉背景。最后，通过在横向平面施加反射单值性τ→−τ̄，讨论这些7-膜解与R7膜之间的关系。

Result: 作者找到了具有上述AdS₉背景作为近地平线几何的显式解析7-膜解，说明了这些规范解与R7膜可通过反射单值性τ→−τ̄建立联系。

Conclusion: 论文完成了对AdS₉背景的系统性坐标梳理和全息可观测量计算，并成功构造了与之对应的非超对称7-膜解，同时揭示了这些解与R7膜之间通过反射单值性建立的关联，为该类背景的物理图像提供了新的膜构型解释。

Abstract: We consider the recently found $\mathrm{AdS}_9$ backgrounds in type IIB supergravity. We start by reviewing the solutions in a more convenient set of coordinates, which allows us to explicitly compute a few relevant observables, including the holographic central charge. Subsequently, we study non-supersymmetric 7-brane configurations sourced by the IIB axio-dilaton. We find explicit analytic 7-brane solutions that possess the above $\mathrm{AdS}_9$ backgrounds as their near-horizon geometry. Finally, we discuss the relation of these 7-brane solutions to the R7 branes by imposing the reflection monodromy $τ\rightarrow -\bar τ$ in the transverse plane.

</details>


### [108] [Updates on Calabi-Yau manifolds from pairs of non-compact Calabi-Yau manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07694)
*Nam-Hoon Lee*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文综述了通过光滑化拟法诺流形（quasi-Fano manifolds）的正规交叉并集来构造卡拉比-丘流形（Calabi-Yau manifolds）的方法进展，涵盖具有无界第二贝蒂数及其小霍奇数的三维卡拉比-丘流形，以及高于三维的首个非凯勒（non-Kähler）例子。文章还阐述了朗道-金兹堡模型（Landau–Ginzburg models）在镜像对称构造中的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自2010年该方法被引入物理学界以来，通过光滑化正规交叉并集构造卡拉比-丘流形已成为一个重要的研究方向。本文旨在综合讨论该方法在构造具有极端拓扑特征的卡拉比-丘流形方面的最新进展，包括无界第二贝蒂数、极小的霍奇数以及高维非凯勒例子。

Method: 该文采用综述性讨论的方式，围绕光滑化（smoothing）正规交叉并集（normal crossing unions）的构造方法展开，系统梳理了该方法在三维及更高维卡拉比-丘流形构造中的应用，并讨论了朗道-金兹堡模型在镜像构造（mirror constructions）中所扮演的角色。

Result: 该方法成功构造了具有无界第二贝蒂数（second Betti numbers）和极小霍奇数（Hodge numbers）的卡拉比-丘三维流形，并且首次在高于三维的维度上实现了非凯勒的例子构造。

Conclusion: 光滑正规交叉并集方法为卡拉比-丘流形的构造提供了多样化且富有成效的途径，能够产出具有极端拓扑性质的例子，同时朗道-金兹堡模型为相应的镜像构造提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We discuss developments in the construction of Calabi--Yau manifolds by smoothing normal crossing unions of quasi-Fano manifolds, following the introduction of this method to the physics community in 2010. We describe constructions of Calabi--Yau threefolds with unbounded second Betti numbers and with very small Hodge numbers, as well as the first non-Kähler examples in dimensions greater than three. The role of Landau--Ginzburg models in mirror constructions is also explained.

</details>


### [109] [Supertranslations are Soft Dressings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07699)
*Asaad Elkhidir,Donal O'Connell,Radu Roiban*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在KMOC形式论框架下研究电动力学与引力中有质量粒子渐近态的非唯一性，证明相干态 dressing 会导致可观测量发生大规范/BMS变换，并将之联系到守恒荷与软因子分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动力学与引力中的有质量粒子渐近态因缺乏质量间隙（mass gap）而无法被唯一定义，这一自由度在散射可观测量中的物理后果尚需澄清。

Method: 在KMOC形式论（KMOC formalism）中研究有质量粒子态的相干态dressing所带来的经典效应，证明其等价于大规范/BMS变换，并利用软因子分解（soft factorization）而非局部对称性来构建整个框架。

Result: 相干态位移算子可完备化为守恒荷（引力情形为BMS荷），观测值不依赖变换参数的单极与偶极部分至全阶；在标量、阿贝尔规范与引力子中介的双粒子态中，大规范变换导致冲击参数的普适移动，并指出辐射角动量的框架依赖性与总角动量的框架无关性。

Conclusion: 大规范/BMS变换参数直接由dressing参数给出，构造由软因子分解而非局部对称性驱动；通过Schwarzschild度规在Kerr-Schild与De Donder形式之间的框架变换具体展示了这些效应。

Abstract: Asymptotic states of massive particles in electrodynamics and gravity are not uniquely defined because of the absence of a mass gap. Working in the KMOC formalism, we study the classical implications of this freedom in the dressing of massive particle states. We show that coherent-state dressings modify observables by a large gauge/BMS transformation. The associated coherent-state displacement operator can be completed into a conserved charge, the BMS charge in the gravitational case, which implements large gauge transformations on scattering data. The parameter of our dressing is directly the large gauge/BMS transformation parameter, and we explain that observables do not depend on monopolar and dipolar parts of the transformation parameter to all orders. Our construction is driven by soft factorization rather than by an underlying local symmetry, and we illustrate this by exhibiting the same structure in a scalar theory with no gauge symmetry. We explore the action of large gauge transformations on two-particle states for scalar, abelian gauge and graviton mediators, obtaining a universal shift of the impact parameter and clarifying the frame dependence of the radiated angular momentum vis à vis the frame-independence of the total angular momentum. The transformation connecting the canonical BMS frame, in which the Schwarzschild metric takes the Kerr-Schild form, to the intrinsic frame, in which it takes the De Donder form, illustrates these effects concretely.

</details>


### [110] [Bumpy horizons from non-linear sigma models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07736)
*Fabrizio Canfora,Nicolás Grandi,Carla Henríquez-Báez,Julio Oliva*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文证明，在任意偶数维度中，当爱因斯坦方程（Einstein equations）的源为某一特定类别的非线性σ模型（non-linear sigma model）的应力-能量张量时，可以构造具有非恒定曲率的黑洞视界（black hole horizons）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机在于探索在任意偶数维度中构造具有非恒定曲率视界的黑洞解的可行性，超越传统上仅考虑恒定曲率视界的局限性。

Method: 方法是将爱因斯坦方程的源项设定为特定类别非线性σ模型的应力-能量张量，从而在偶数维度中构造非恒定曲率的黑洞视界解。

Result: 结果表明，在任意偶数维度中，借助所选的特定类别非线性σ模型作为源项，非恒定曲率的黑洞视界解是存在的。

Conclusion: 该研究拓展了高维黑洞解的构造空间，表明非恒定曲率视界在任意偶数维度中均可通过合适的物质场源实现。

Abstract: We show that black hole horizons with non-constant curvature can be constructed in any even dimension, when the source of the Einstein equations is given by the stress-energy tensor of a non-linear sigma model of a particular class

</details>


### [111] [Incidence Relations for Self-Dual Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07774)
*Joon-Hwi Kim*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在Penrose非线性引力子（nonlinear graviton）框架下，利用Dunajski-Mason递推与Plebański第二heavenly方程，具体构造了自对偶黑洞时空（Eguchi-Hanson、自对偶Taub-NUT和自对偶Plebański-Demiański）在Kerr-Schild坐标下的关联关系（incidence relation），并给出了闭合形式公式。自对偶Taub-NUT的关联关系对引力耦合常数呈线性依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Penrose非线性引力子构造中，自对偶时空作为扭曲twistor空间中全纯twistor线的模空间而出现，其显式参数化由关联关系给出。然而，对于自对偶黑洞时空，关联关系的具体构造尚不清楚，这限制了该框架在黑洞物理中的应用。

Method: 采用Dunajski-Mason递推方法，在Plebański第二heavenly方程（second heavenly equation）框架下进行实现，通过显式求解Plebański标量（Plebański scalar），在Kerr-Schild坐标中构造Eguchi-Hanson、自对偶Taub-NUT和自对偶Plebański-Demiański三种自对偶黑洞时空的关联关系。

Result: 成功获得上述三种自对偶黑洞时空关联关系的闭合形式表达式。特别地，对于自对偶Taub-NUT解，精确关联关系对引力耦合（gravitational coupling）呈现线性依赖。此外，文中简要展示了零静质量场（zero-rest-mass fields）在自对偶黑洞背景上的构造作为应用。

Conclusion: 本文为自对偶黑洞时空在非线性引力子构造中的显式实现提供了具体的局部构造方案，并揭示了自对偶Taub-NUT解中关联关系的线性耦合结构，为后续在更广泛自对偶背景上的twistor方法应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: In Penrose's nonlinear graviton construction, a self-dual spacetime arises as the moduli space of holomorphic twistor lines in curved twistor space, whose explicit parametrization is concretely given by the incidence relation. We demonstrate a concrete local construction of the incidence relations for self-dual black hole spacetimes in Kerr-Schild coordinates: Eguchi-Hanson, self-dual Taub-NUT, and self-dual Plebański-Demiański. This is facilitated by implementing the Dunajski-Mason recursion in the framework of Plebański's second heavenly equation, for which we explicitly find the Plebański scalar. Our results describe closed-form formulae. Notably, for the self-dual Taub-NUT solution, the exact incidence relation exhibits linear dependence on the gravitational coupling. The construction of zero-rest-mass fields on self-dual black hole backgrounds is briefly sketched as an application.

</details>


### [112] [Four-Fermion Condensates in Curved Spacetimes: A Functional Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07907)
*Stephon Alexander,Heliudson Bernardo,Jiatong Yan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从泛函路径积分视角系统区分了四费米子相互作用中涉及的三种物理场景——微扰散射、真空Nambu-Jona-Lasinio（NJL）不稳定性与有限密度Bardeen-Cooper-Schrieffer（BCS）配对——并计算了弯曲时空中标量通道NJL平均场对能动张量的局域单圈贡献，导出了协变量子有效作用量及对应能量密度和压力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四费米子相互作用广泛出现在粒子物理、多体系以及含费米子的引力理论的有效描述中，但同一局域算符在不同物理场景（微扰散射、真空NJL不稳定性和有限密度BCS配对）中对应的相互作用通道、二次型核与量子态均不同，需给出清晰的区分与统一的泛函处理框架。

Method: 采用教学性的泛函（functional）路径积分方法：首先在in-out泛函中展示态数据，构造用于in-in期望值的闭时路径（closed-time-path）泛函；对于NJL鞍点，引入Hubbard-Stratonovich场以移位费米子质量，利用宇称偶狄拉克行列式生成局域体积项、曲率项及曲率平方项算子；在求协变量子有效作用量后，特化到空间平坦的FLRW（Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker）背景并导出能量密度与压力。

Result: 导出了弯曲时空中NJL平均场的协变量子有效作用量，包含局域体积、曲率和曲率平方项；在FLRW背景上得到相应的能量密度与压力；常数凝聚极限与平坦空间平均场直接计算一致。此外，阐明了有限密度BCS配对所需的额外态与通道数据，并讨论了弯曲时空中的重整化条件。

Conclusion: 该工作为区分微扰散射、真空NJL不稳定性与有限密度BCS配对三种四费米子相互作用场景提供了统一的泛函框架，建立了弯曲时空中NJL平均场能量动量张量的单圈计算方案，并指出BCS配对需要额外的态和通道数据，弯曲时空中的重整化条件仍需进一步明确。

Abstract: Four-fermion interactions appear in effective descriptions of particle physics, many-body systems, and gravitational theories with fermions. Although the same local operator may enter perturbative scattering, a vacuum Nambu-Jona-Lasinio (NJL) instability, or finite-density Bardeen-Cooper-Schrieffer (BCS) pairing, these regimes are distinguished by their interaction channels, quadratic kernels, and quantum states. We give a pedagogical functional account of these distinctions and compute the local one-loop contribution of a scalar-channel NJL mean field to the energy-momentum tensor in curved spacetime. We first display the state data in the in-out functional and construct the closed-time-path functional required for an in-in expectation value. For the NJL saddle, a Hubbard-Stratonovich field shifts the fermion mass, and the parity-even Dirac determinant generates local volume, curvature, and curvature-squared operators. We find the covariant quantum effective action before specializing to a spatially flat FLRW background and derive the corresponding energy density and pressure. The constant-condensate limit agrees with the direct flat-space mean-field calculation. We also explain which additional state and channel data are required for finite-density BCS pairing and comment on renormalization conditions in curved spacetimes.

</details>


### [113] [One gravity theory on the two-dimensional p-adic space and conjectures on $p$-adic AdS/CFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07919)
*Feng Qu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过高维嵌入方法，结合距离、测度和曲率的研究，在p-adic数上的二维空间构建了一种引力理论，并通过与实数情形的比较验证其合理性，最后提出了两个关于p-adic AdS/CFT的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望在p-adic数域上构造引力理论（gravitational theory），并验证其与实数情形下的对应关系是否一致，从而为p-adic AdS/CFT对偶的研究奠定基础。

Method: 采用高维嵌入方法（higher-dimensional embedding approach），并结合距离（distance）、测度（measure）与曲率（curvature）的研究，在p-adic数上的二维空间构造引力理论；补充了基于现有文献的计算，并通过与实数情形的对比来论证理论的合理性，例如检验de Sitter空间、anti-de Sitter空间和Euclidean空间的曲率分别为正、负和零。

Result: 成功在p-adic数上的二维空间构建了引力理论，并通过与实数情形的对比验证了其合理性；提出两个猜想：(1) p-adic AdS/CFT可在任意空间中计算而不仅限于p-adic AdS空间（pAdS），且结果本质上相同；(2) p-adic CFT本质上具有唯一性。

Conclusion: 本文建立的p-adic引力理论为p-adic AdS/CFT对偶的研究提供了新的基础，所提出的两个猜想指出了该领域未来可能的研究方向。

Abstract: By means of a higher-dimensional embedding approach, together with the study of distance, measure, and curvature, we construct a gravitational theory on the two-dimensional space over $p$-adic numbers. Before obtaining the final gravitational theory, we supplement some calculations based on existing literature and justify the reasonableness of the gravitational theory by comparison with the real-number case-for instance, the curvatures of de Sitter(dS), anti-de Sitter(AdS), and Euclidean spaces should be positive, negative, and zero, respectively. Finally, we put forward two conjectures: (1) $p$-adic AdS/CFT can be computed in arbitrary spaces, not only in $p$-adic AdS space($p$AdS), and the results are essentially the same; (2) $p$-adic CFT is essentially unique.

</details>


### [114] [Gauge transformations in Z-space in (anti)holomorphic sector of HS theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08062)
*D. A. Batyaev,A. V. Korybut*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种对(反)全纯生成系统的自洽形变，旨在建立到Vasiliev理论(反)全纯截断的映射。在新的表述中，主场$Λ$可被$\mathrm{d}_z$-精确投影一形式平移，该平移按$C$的阶数诱导字段重新定义，并证明了其非平凡性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在将先前严格固定的主场$Λ$推广为可形变的场，从而将(反)全纯生成系统映射到Vasiliev理论的(反)全纯截断，以建立两者之间的联系。

Method: 作者通过允许$Λ$被$\mathrm{d}_z$-精确投影一形式平移来构造自洽形变；全面描述了投影一形式类别并证明相应投影恒等式；分析了$C$的首阶为零形式的规范变换，给出可被规范吸收的二阶字段重新定义部分的充分必要条件。

Result: $\mathrm{d}_z$-精确形式中$C$的$n$阶项诱导$(n+1)$阶及更高阶的字段重新定义，且$(n+1)$阶重新定义的显式表达式已给出；$Λ$平移所诱导的字段重新定义被证明是非平凡的。

Conclusion: 该形变框架提供了从先前生成系统到Vasiliev理论(反)全纯截断的有效映射工具，且$Λ$的平移无法通过字段重新定义消除，表明这一形变具有本质性的新物理内容。

Abstract: We consider a consistent deformation of the (anti)holomorphic generating system of [arXiv:2209.01966], aiming to provide a map to the (anti)holomorphic truncation of the Vasiliev theory. In our new formulation, the previously rigidly defined master-field $Λ$ can be shifted by $\mathrm{d}_z$-exact projective one-forms. The class of projective one-forms is described comprehensively, and corresponding projective identities are proven. $\mathrm{d}_z$-exact forms of the order $n$ in $C$ induce field redefinition of order $(n+1)$ and beyond. Explicit expression for the $(n+1)$-th order field redefinitions are provided. An analysis of the gauge transformation of the second order in $C$ in zero-forms is performed, identifying necessary and sufficient conditions for the part of the field redefinition of the second order that can be gauged away. The field redefinitions induced by the shift of $Λ$ are proven to be nontrivial.

</details>


### [115] [Organizing transitions and their cascades: Generalized symmetry enforcement in massless flows or Higgs transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08428)
*Yoshiki Fukusumi,Yuma Furuta*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了幺正极小模型（unitary minimal models）M(p,p+1)→M̄(p-1,p)无质量重整化群流（massless renormalization group flow）中广义对称性的作用，证明未破缺的融合环对称性（fusion ring symmetry）FR(SU(2)_{p-2})消除了所有相关扰动，使红外理论成为（弱）对称强制的无隙相（gapless phase），并揭示了（半）整数自旋非简单流在此过程中的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索广义对称性（generalized symmetry）在无质量重整化群流或Higgs跃迁中的角色，考察对称性如何决定红外理论（infrared theory）的稳定性与可能出现的非常规物理行为。

Method: 重新分析幺正极小模型中保持融合环对称性FR(SU(2)_{p-2})的流动系列；通过尺度分析（scaling analysis）检验红外模型的稳定性；利用折叠技巧（folding trick）将对称性映射为（半）整数自旋的非简单流（nonsimple current），并分析其与相关和危险无关扰动的共振效应。

Result: 未破缺的融合环对称性FR(SU(2)_{p-2})消除了红外理论M̄(p-1,p)中的所有相关扰动，使其在尺度分析层面保持稳定；该对称性对应于涉及非阿贝尔任意子（nonabelian anyons）的类库珀对（Cooper pair）变体，其（半）整数自旋非简单流结构是引发相关及危险无关扰动共振效应的根本原因。

Conclusion: 红外理论可解释为（弱）对称强制的无隙相；共振效应可能引发级联相变（cascade of phase transitions）或流向未知不动点，而对称性可作为阻抑此类非常规流动的机制。

Abstract: We study the role of generalized symmetry in massless renormalization group flows or Higgs transitions. In particular, we revisit the massless renormalization group flows in unitary minimal models, $\mathbf{M}(p,p+1) \rightarrow \overline{\mathbf{M}(p-1,p)}$ preserving the fusion ring symmetry $\text{FR} (SU(2)_{p-2})\subset \mathbf{M}(p,p+1)$ where $p$ is an integer satisfying $p>3$. In this series of flows, we demonstrate that the unbroken fusion ring symmetry $\text{FR} (SU(2)_{p-2})$ eliminates all relevant perturbations in the $\overline{\mathbf{M}(p-1,p)}$ model. Hence, the infrared theory $\overline{\mathbf{M}(p-1,p)}$ is stable at the level of the scaling analysis and can be interpreted as a (weak-)symmetry-enforced gapless phase in contemporary theoretical physics. Phenomenologically, by the folding trick, the unbroken fusion ring symmetry corresponds to a (half-)integer spin nonsimple current, a variant of the Cooper pair involving nonabelian anyons generated from the coset or level-rank duality structures. Moreover, we demonstrate that the structure of (half-)integer spin nonsimple current plays a fundamental role in causing the resonance effect of relevant and dangerously irrelevant perturbations. This resonance effect may result in the cascade of phase transitions (or the system flows to unexplored fixed points), and the symmetry can be a stopper of such unconventional flows.

</details>


### [116] [Complex CFTs: Holography and Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08292)
*Luis Camargo-Carlos,Michael Gutperle*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文通过全息（holography）和二维CFT方法研究复共形场论（complex conformal field theories）及其界面，证明复共轭CFT的Im-flip性质，构造复Janus解和数值求解复重整化群（RG）流动界面，并在自由玻色子CFT中构造复杂界面及计算透射与反射系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复共形场论及其界面在理论物理中具有重要性，但对其系统研究尚不充分。作者希望利用全息与二维CFT工具，深入理解复共形场论的性质，特别是复共轭CFT之间的对称性关系以及界面物理。

Method: 采用全息方法证明复共轭CFT的Im-flip性质，构造复Janus解并计算全息可观测量；数值求解复RG流动界面；在CFT侧，针对有和无背景电荷（background charge）的自由玻色子CFT构造复杂界面，构建边界态（boundary states）并计算透射系数和反射系数。

Result: 成功证明了复共轭CFT的Im-flip性质；构造了复Janus解并计算了相关全息可观测量；数值获得复RG流动界面；在自由玻色子CFT中（有/无背景电荷两种情形）构造了复杂界面，并计算了透射和反射系数。

Conclusion: 该工作从全息和CFT两个层面系统研究了复共形场论及其界面，确立了Im-flip性质，并提供了多种可解析或数值计算的界面构造方案，为理解复共形场论的界面物理奠定了基础。

Abstract: We investigate complex conformal field theories and interfaces between such theories, using methods of holography and two dimensional CFT. We use holography to prove the Im-flip property of complex conjugate CFTs. We construct a complex Janus solution and calculate some holographic observables. Complex RG-flow interfaces are obtained numerically. On the CFT side, complex interfaces are constructed for free boson CFTs with and without background charge. Boundary states are constructed, and transmission and reflection coefficients are calculated in both cases.

</details>


### [117] [Carrollian Quantum Mechanics: Time-like, Space-like and Hybrid Sectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08384)
*Mehdi Ahmadi-Jahmani*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过系统性地对Klein–Gordon方程施加$c \to 0$的极端相对论极限（ultra-relativistic contraction），建立了Carroll量子力学的完整理论框架，揭示出三个不同扇区（类时、类空和混合），并逐一给出了各自的不变波方程、连续性方程、概率密度和流，以及盒中粒子和隧穿等典型量子系统的Carrollian描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Carroll物理学（Carrollian physics）作为极端非相对论极限（$c \to 0$）下涌现的对称性框架，其量子力学层面的完备理论尚缺乏系统构建，尤其是在Klein–Gordon方程的收缩处理中涉及的多个扇区及其概率诠释存在未解决的复杂性。

Method: 本文采用解析方法，对Klein–Gordon方程施以系统的极端相对论收缩（ultra-relativistic contraction），在$c \to 0$极限下将其分解为三个扇区，并分别推导各扇区的Carroll不变波方程、连续性方程及概率密度与流。

Result: 三个扇区各自产生Carroll不变波方程：类时扇区（time-like sector）表现出新颖的时间隧穿（temporal tunneling）现象，满足关系$|\mathcal{T}|^2 = 1 + |\mathcal{R}|^2$，反映Klein–Gordon范数的不定性；类空扇区（space-like sector）需要tachyonic色散关系，能量本征态密度为零从而形成零范数（null）态，并在Carrollian几何中具有物理意义；混合扇区（hybrid sector）兼具两者特征，存在tachyonic与非tachyonic两种表述，可理解为非齐次Klein–Gordon方程。各扇区均给出了连续性方程、概率密度与流，并考察了盒中粒子及隧穿等量子系统。

Conclusion: Carrollian量子力学呈现丰富且异于常规量子理论的物理结构，零范数态与时间隧穿等现象源于Carroll几何的内在规定性，本文为该领域提供了统一而自洽的理论框架。

Abstract: We develop a comprehensive theoretical framework for Carrollian quantum mechanics by performing systematic ultra-relativistic contractions of the Klein--Gordon equation in the limit $c \to 0$. This limiting process uncovers three distinct sectors---time-like, space-like, and hybrid---each governed by a Carroll-invariant wave equation and accompanied by a consistent probabilistic interpretation. The time-like sector exhibits a novel temporal tunneling phenomenon, characterized by the relation $|\mathcal{T}|^2 = 1 + |\mathcal{R}|^2$, which reflects the indefinite character of the Klein--Gordon norm. In the space-like sector, the presence of spatial propagation necessitates a tachyonic dispersion relation, which forces the density to vanish for energy eigenstates, yielding zero-norm (null) states that gain physical relevance within Carrollian physics due to the underlying null geometric structure. The hybrid sector combines features of both sectors and admits two distinct formulations, which may be either tachyonic or non-tachyonic, and can be interpreted as an inhomogeneous Klein--Gordon equation. For all three sectors, we derive the corresponding continuity equations, probability densities, and currents, and investigate canonical quantum systems---including the particle in a box and tunneling phenomena---within the Carrollian regime.

</details>


### [118] [Conformally mapping HKLL to flat space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08593)
*Nirmalya Kajuri,Rhitaparna Pal*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了AdS$_3$中保形耦合标量场的两种HKLL重构问题的保形映像（conformal images）。其中一种映射将问题转化为爱因斯坦静态宇宙（Einstein static universe, ESU）的一半上的Dirichlet问题，再经保形变换映射到由均匀加速圆形Dirichlet镜面围成的$(2+1)$维闵可夫斯基空间中的无质量标量问题。作者在平直空间中内部地构造并求解了这个受限镜像问题（restricted mirror problem），并发现了一个自身不满足运动方程的合法涂抹函数（smearing function）实例，同时通过保形映射到平直空间坐标系，简洁地展示了AdS-Rindler楔重构中涂抹函数的发散行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HKLL重构公式是AdS/CFT对应的核心工具，但其涂抹函数在AdS深处的行为以及在不同的几何设定（如全局AdS与AdS-Rindler楔）下的性质仍有待深入理解。本文旨在通过保形映射将HKLL问题转化为更直观的平直空间问题，从而揭示受限谱结构对重构的影响及涂抹函数的奇异行为。

Method: 作者首先将AdS$_3$中保形耦合标量的HKLL重构映射为ESU一半上的Dirichlet问题，再通过进一步的保形变换将其转化为由均匀加速圆形Dirichlet镜面界定的闵可夫斯基空间区域中的无质量标量问题。作者在平直空间中内在性地表述并求解了该受限镜像问题，并以AdS-Rindler楔重构为例，通过保形映射到平直空间适当坐标系来分析涂抹函数的发散机制。

Result: 发现全局HKLL问题经保形映射后在平直空间中并非具有任意边界数据的普通局部镜像问题，镜面响应数据必须属于由归一化全局AdS模式继承的受限谱类（restricted spectral class）。研究中发现了一个自身不满足运动方程的合法涂抹函数的实例。同时，通过保形映射到平直空间的适当坐标系，简洁地展示了AdS-Rindler楔重构中涂抹函数的爆炸（blow-up）行为。

Conclusion: 保形映射为理解HKLL重构问题提供了新颖视角：它揭示了全局AdS与AdS-Rindler楔重构在平直空间表述上的谱约束差异，表明涂抹函数不必然满足运动方程，并给出了一种直观理解涂抹函数在AdS-Rindler楔中发散的方法。

Abstract: We study two conformal images of the HKLL reconstruction problem for a conformally coupled scalar on AdS$_3$. The first mapping is to a Dirichlet problem on one half of the Einstein static universe. A further conformal transformation maps this half-ESU problem to a massless scalar in a region of $(2+1)$-dimensional Minkowski space bounded by an uniformly accelerated circular Dirichlet mirror. However, the global HKLL problem, the resulting flat-space problem is not a generic local mirror problem with arbitrary boundary data. Rather, the mirror response data must belong to a restricted spectral class inherited from normalizable global AdS modes. We formulate and solve this restricted mirror problem intrinsically in flat space. In the process, we find an example of a valid smearing function which itself does not satisfy the equations of motion. We further consider AdS-Rindler wedge reconstruction. By conformal mapping to an appropriate coordinate system in flat space, we find a simple way to see the blow-up of the smearing function.

</details>


### [119] [Thermodynamic stability and geometric thermodynamics of charged black holes in Rastall-massive gravity under quintessence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08660)
*Mohamed Chabab,Samir Iraoui,Hicham Sriba*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在渐近平坦时空背景下，结合Rastall引力与有质量引力（massive gravity）框架推导出嵌入精质场（quintessence field）的新型带电黑洞解，系统研究了其热力学性质、稳定性及相变行为，并建立光子球观测与热力学相变之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在深入理解精质场背景下带电黑洞的热力学行为，并探索其与标准热力学、光子球观测以及几何热力学之间的内在联系，从而为黑洞热力学临界相变提供潜在的观测信号。

Method: 作者在Rastall引力与有质量引力相结合的框架中推导新的带电黑洞解，将精质场结构相关的积分常数解释为热力学变量，分析不同极限情况，并通过热容、吉布斯自由能以及Quevedo度规（Quevedo metric）描述的几何热力学（geothermodynamics）方法检验热力学性质与相变。

Result: 研究发现，在精质场参数取ω_q=-2/3时，该黑洞的整体热力学行为与带电RN-AdS黑洞相似，呈现类似范德瓦尔斯流体的热学特征；光子球可观测量可间接提供黑洞热力学临界相变的观测信号；Quevedo度规下的临界行为与标准热力学分析在小/大黑洞相变及发散点上完全一致。

Conclusion: 精质场参数的有效贡献使得该黑洞展现出与带电RN-AdS黑洞类似的范德瓦尔斯型热力学行为，且标准热力学与几何热力学描述完全自洽；光子球观测有望成为黑洞热力学临界转变的间接观测标志。

Abstract: In the present paper, we derive a new charged black hole solution embedded in a quintessence field within a framework combining the Rastall and massive gravity in an asymptotically flat spacetime. We interpret the integration constant associated with the structural properties of the quintessence field as a thermodynamical variable. In this context, several limiting cases have been explored with the aim to shed light on the role of the model parameters. Then, we analyse the thermodynamic properties and stability of the charged black holes in flat spacetime, where the quintessence field is characterized by the state parameter \( ω_q = -2/3 \). Detailed studies are perfomed to see how the various parameters affect phase transitions between small and large black holes. In particular, we establish a connection between the impact parameter and unstable circular photon orbits with the thermodynamic phase transitions. Our analysis suggest that the photon sphere observables could remarkably provide an indirect observational signature of black hole thermodynamic critical transitions. Generally, thanks to the effective contribution of the quintessence parameter, we found that the overall thermodynamic behavior basically resembles that of the charged RN--AdS black hole with thermal features analogous to of a Van der Waals fluid ones. Finally, we have checked these findings using thermodynamic geometry with the Quevedo metric. Our results showed that the critical behaviour derived from the standard thermodynamics is fully consistent with geothermodynamics description, where the phase structure and divergence points exactly coincide with those identified from heat capacity and Gibbs free energy analyses.

</details>


### [120] [An explicit four-corner dictionary for (2,p) minimal Liouville gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08669)
*A. Alexandrov,V. Belavin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文系统建立了(2,p)极小刘维尔引力（minimal Liouville gravity）在四种代数描述间的显式对应关系，基于无色散τ函数（dispersionless tau-function）层面验证了各方法的一致性，并确认了x↔y交换在亏格零（genus zero）水平上的树级实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: (2,p)极小刘维尔引力存在四种由对偶性（duality）关联的代数描述，但此前它们之间的显式对应关系尚未被完整给出，需要系统厘清各框架间的映射以验证理论一致性。

Method: 分别在Frobenius流形（Frobenius manifold）侧（A₁与Aₚ₋₁两种流形）和谱曲线/拓扑递归（spectral curve/topological recursion）侧（Chebyshev曲线及其x↔y交换）建立对应；利用共振变换（resonance transformation）关联KdV时间与刘维尔耦合，在无色散τ函数层面推导显式对应，并通过一般化Kontsevich积分变换（generalized Kontsevich integral transform）实现树级框架变换。

Result: 四种描述中归一化无关的三点函数比值均与共形场论（conformal field theory）一致；全振幅匹配确定了单一逐点插入因子以复现带符号的Verlinde矩阵（signed Verlinde matrices）；x↔y交换在亏格零水平实现了以势能Φᵖ/p为特征的一般化Kontsevich积分变换的树级框架变换；全部推导在(2,5)至(2,11)模型中验证通过。

Conclusion: 四种代数描述在无色散τ函数层面完全等价，揭示了极小刘维尔引力中Frobenius流形描述与谱曲线描述之间更深层的对偶结构。

Abstract: (2,p) minimal Liouville gravity admits four algebraic descriptions interrelated by dualities. On the Frobenius manifold (FM) side the theory is built either on the $A_{1}$ manifold of $Q(y)=y^{2}+u_{1}$ ($y$-side) or on the $A_{p-1}$ manifold in the variable $x$ ($x$-side). On the spectral curve / topological recursion (SC) side the analogous choice is the Chebyshev curve and its $x \leftrightarrow y$ swap. FM-$y$, SC-$y$ require a resonance transformation between the KdV times and the Liouville couplings, while the other two do not. We work out the explicit correspondence between the four approaches at the level of the dispersionless tau-function. The normalisation-independent three-point ratios agree with conformal field theory in all four formulations, and matching the full amplitudes fixes a single per-insertion factor reproducing the signed Verlinde matrices. The $x \leftrightarrow y$ swap relating the two spectral curve descriptions, recently established in general by Dekinga, Shadrin and Verlinde, is realised at genus zero as a tree-level frame change of the generalised Kontsevich integral transform with potential $Φ^{p}/p$. The derivation is validated across (2,5)--(2,11) models.

</details>


### [121] [The explicit Lorentz invariant QED pair production rates from superstrings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08712)
*J. X. Lu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过类型II超弦理论中两个Dp膜（$1 \le p \le 6$）的开弦对产生（open string pair production）机制给出了通常Schwinger效应的新路径，并在场论极限下系统地得到了不同维度下带电/反带电大质量粒子（标量、旋量或矢量）的显式洛伦兹不变（Lorentz invariant）QED对产生率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在提供Schwinger效应（Schwinger effect）的一种新推导途径，通过弦理论中的Dp膜系统来统一得到不同维度下的QED对产生率，而不局限于传统场论方法。

Method: 研究表明两个平行的Dp膜（$1 \le p \le 6$）处于一定分离距离，其中一个膜承载最一般的恒定电磁背景场（允许对产生发生）。通过计算该系统中开弦对产生率，并取场论极限（field theory limit），系统地导出不同维度下带电/反带电大质量粒子的显式洛伦兹不变QED对产生率。

Result: 从开弦对产生率出发，在场论极限下获得了明晰的洛伦兹不变QED对产生率表达式，适用于多种维度中的标量（scalar）、旋量（spinor）或矢量（vector）带电粒子对（带电/反带电）的产生过程。

Conclusion: 该工作为Schwinger效应提供了基于类型II超弦中Dp膜系统的全新推导路径，并以系统化方式统一了不同维度下各类自旋粒子的QED对产生率。

Abstract: We present a new avenue to the usual Schwinger effect via the open string pair production for a system of two Dp branes with $1 \le p \le 6$ in Type II superstrings. The two Dp are placed parallel at a separation with one of them carrying the most general constant electromagnetic background allowed for the pair production. By taking the so-called field theory limit, we obtain systematically, from the open string pair production rate, the respective \textit{explicit Lorentz invariant} QED pair production rate in diverse dimensions for a pair of charged/anti-charged massive particles, which can be scalars, spinors or vectors.

</details>


### [122] [Understanding Non-Split 2-Group Symmetry: (3+1)D SymTFT, Anomaly and Bordism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08766)
*Zhenbang Gu,Ran Luo,Yi-Nan Wang,Yi Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了具有非平凡 Postnikov 类的有限非分裂 2 群（2-group）对称性，以 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 0-形式对称性与 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 1-形式对称性交织的最简情形为例，通过计算定向与自旋配边群（bordism groups）分类了 $d\leq 5$ 维时空下的反常，并针对 $d=3$ 情形利用融合 2 范畴（fusion 2-categories）和 (3+1)D TQFT 作用量研究了对称性 TFT/TO，包含分类 Landau 范式下的最小边界条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解具有非平凡 Postnikov 类的有限非分裂 2 群对称性是研究广义对称性（generalized symmetries）的重要课题，其异常分类及对称性 TFT（Symmetry TFT）构造尚需系统研究。

Method: 计算定向配边群 $Ω_{d+1}^{\rm SO}(B\mathcal{G})$ 与自旋配边群 $Ω_{d+1}^{\rm Spin}(B\mathcal{G})$（$d\leq 5$）以分类异常；对 $d=3$ 情形，利用融合 2 范畴语言及 (3+1)D TQFT 作用量研究对称性 TFT/TO，并分类其最小拓扑与物理边界条件。

Result: 给出了 $d\leq 5$ 时空维度下该 2 群对称性的异常分类；对于 $d=3$，通过 $\operatorname{Hom}\left(\widetildeΩ_4^{\rm SO}(B\mathcal{G}),U(1)\right) \cong H^4(B\mathcal{G};U(1))\cong\mathbb{Z}_2$ 区分有无 2 群异常的情况；分类了对称性 TFT 的最小拓扑与物理边界条件，并实现了此类非分裂有限 2 群的范畴化 Landau 范式（categorical Landau paradigm）。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了非分裂有限 2 群对称性在异常分类、对称性 TFT 构造及边界条件分类方面的完整框架，为理解更一般的非分裂高阶群对称性提供了基础。

Abstract: We present a systematic study of finite, non-split 2-group symmetries with a non-trivial Postnikov class, focusing on the simplest example with a $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 0-form symmetry and a $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 1-form symmetry, intertwined together by the non-trivial Postnikov class in $H^3(B\mathbb{Z}_2;\mathbb{Z}_2)\cong\mathbb{Z}_2$, denoted by $\mathcal{G}$. We classify the anomalies of this 2-group symmetry for physical theories in $d$-dimensional spacetime, by computing the oriented bordism groups $Ω_{d+1}^{\rm SO}(B\mathcal{G})$ and the spin bordism groups $Ω_{d+1}^{\rm Spin}(B\mathcal{G})$ for $d\leq 5$. For the case of $d=3$, we investigate the Symmetry TFT/TO of the 2-group symmetry $\mathcal{G}$ using the language of fusion 2-categories, as well as (3+1)D TQFT actions, for the cases without or with the 2-group anomaly classified by $\operatorname{Hom}\left(\widetildeΩ_4^{\rm SO}(B\mathcal{G}),U(1)\right) \cong H^4(B\mathcal{G};U(1))\cong\mathbb{Z}_2$. We classify the minimal topological and physical boundary conditions of the Symmetry TFTs, and carry out the categorical Landau paradigm for such non-split finite 2-group.

</details>


### [123] [3d $\mathcal N=4$ rank-zero mirror symmetry, TQFT interfaces, and Zagier duality of Nahm sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08780)
*Yutaka Yoshida*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从$(E_8,T_1)$ Nahm和（Nahm sum）出发，构造了一个三维$\mathcal N=2$ $U(1)^8$ Chern–Simons物质理论（Chern–Simons matter theory）$\mathcal T$与其对偶理论$\mathcal T^\vee$，两者流至三维$\mathcal N=4$秩为零（rank-zero）的镜像超共形场论（mirror SCFT）。其$(A/B)$扭转向量（twisted）TQFT数据、镜像映射与Zagier对偶（Zagier duality）精确对应，最终实现了$(E_8,T_1)$与$(T_1,E_8)$ Nahm系统的完备对偶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在为Nahm和体系建立具体的三维量子场论实现，将代数层面的Zagier对偶嵌入为3d镜像对称与界面（interface）结构的物理机制，从而为相关模形式恒等式提供量子场论解释。

Method: 从$(E_8,T_1)$ Nahm和出发构造3d $\mathcal N=2$ $U(1)^8$ Chern–Simons物质理论$\mathcal T$（level矩阵为$C_{E_8}$），利用$A$-扭转向量半指标（half-index）复现真空字符；通过粒子-涡旋对偶（particle–vortex duality）与规范固定（gauging）构造对偶理论$\mathcal T^\vee$（level矩阵为$C_{E_8}^{-1}$），并运用Bethe方程形式（Bethe-equation formalism）分析$A/B$扭转向TQFT数据与对偶界面（duality interface）。

Result: $A$-扭转向量半指标精确复现真空字符；四个区分Wilson圈半指标与其余字符一致。超共形指标匹配支持两理论流至3d $\mathcal N=4$秩零镜像SCFT。在$(\mathcal D,D_c)$边界条件下，镜像映射逐分量对应Zagier变换；$A/B$扭转向TQFT数据在定向反转与$E_8$框架相位$e^{-2\pi\mathrm{i}/3}$下一致；对偶界面给出环面双线性$Z_{\mathcal I}(\tau)=\chi_{(E_8)_1}(\tau)$。

Conclusion: Zagier对偶被实现为3d镜像对称与界面结构的具体物理对应，这为Nahm系统和弦字符之间的关系提供了新的场论视角。

Abstract: Starting from the $(E_8,T_1)$ Nahm sum for the vacuum character of $T_{10}M_{\rm eff}(11,2)$, we construct a three-dimensional (3d) $\mathcal N=2$ $U(1)^8$ Chern--Simons matter theory $\mathcal T$ with level matrix $C_{E_8}$. Its $A$-twisted vacuum half-index reproduces the character exactly, while four distinguished Wilson loop half-indices agree with the remaining characters to the orders checked. Applying particle--vortex duality and gauging yields a second 3d $\mathcal N=2$ Chern--Simons matter theory $\mathcal T^\vee$ with level matrix $C_{E_8}^{-1}$. Exact superconformal-index matching supports the claim that the two theories flow to 3d $\mathcal N=4$ rank-zero mirror SCFTs. For the $(\mathcal D,D_c)$ boundary condition, the mirror map between the $A$-twisted Wilson loop half-indices of $\mathcal T$ and the $B$-twisted Wilson loop half-indices of $\mathcal T^\vee$ coincides componentwise with the Zagier transformation, realizing a Zagier duality between the complete $(E_8,T_1)$ and $(T_1,E_8)$ Nahm systems. Using the Bethe-equation formalism, we show that the corresponding $A/B$-twisted TQFT data agree up to orientation reversal and the $E_8$ framing phase $e^{-2π\mathrm{i}/3}$. The duality interface gives the torus bilinear $Z_{\mathcal I}(τ)=\sum_iχ_i(τ)χ_i^\vee(τ)=χ_{(E_8)_1}(τ)$, where $\{χ_i\}$ and $\{χ_i^\vee\}$ are the corresponding five-component character bases. Thus Zagier duality is realized as part of a concrete 3d mirror and interface structure.

</details>


### [124] [Running soft theorems in effective field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08792)
*Jonah Berean-Dutcher,Maria Derda,Riley Stewart*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了光子和π介子的软定理（soft theorems），在单圈阶量子场论框架下引入局部有效场论（EFT）修正，发现高阶维数算符仅修正软定理中的局部解析项，而对数软项由低能有效作用量完全决定，并受重整化群（RG）方程约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软定理是散射振幅在零能量极限下的通用行为，但有效场论高阶维数算符对其修正的结构尚不清晰，需系统性研究这些修正如何进入软定理的不同部分（对数项与局部项）。

Method: 在单圈（one loop）阶下，分别以带五维Pauli算符的大质量QED和手性有效理论（Chiral EFT）为框架，分析次领头软光子定理与二次软π介子定理中有效场论修正项的贡献，并利用硬积分区和重整化群方程进行结构分析。

Result: 在大质量QED中，次领头软光子定理的局部解析部分被硬积分区域重整化，而对数软项不受高维算符修正；在双软π介子定理中，对数项系数完全由非线性σ模型（NLSM）拉氏量确定，四位导算符的Wilson系数则出现在局部次领头项中，其形式由重整化群方程决定。

Conclusion: 两类软定理中有效场论修正呈现一致的通用结构：对数软项由树级低能理论固定，而局部项承载高维算符的Wilson系数并通过重整化群方程控制其结构。

Abstract: We study soft theorems for photons and pions with local effective field theory (EFT) corrections up to one loop order. In massive QED with a dimension-five Pauli operator, the local analytic part of the subleading soft photon theorem is renormalized by the hard integration region. On the other hand, as was previously known, the logarithmic soft terms do not receive corrections from higher-dimensional operators. We find that analogous structure appears in the double-soft pion theorem: the coefficients of logarithmic contributions are controlled entirely by the Nonlinear Sigma Model (NLSM) Lagrangian, while the Wilson coefficients of four-derivative operators in Chiral EFT appear in local subleading terms, in a form that is dictated by their RG equations.

</details>


### [125] [Holography in flipped AdS/$\mathbb{Z}$: Another approach to dS holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08837)
*Bin Chen,Zezhou Hu,Xin-Cheng Mao,Shan-Ming Ruan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过解析延拓（analytic continuation）这一构造性路径探索德西特（de Sitter, dS）时空的全息原理，建立了dS时空与一种称为"翻转AdS/ℤ"（fAdS）时空之间的量子场论对应关系，并发展了一种以"翻转CFT"（fCFT）为边界理论的全息描述，验证了其全息两点函数符合共形对称性预期，并通过fCFT₂的Cardy公式正确复现了纯dS₃和Kerr-dS₃中宇宙学视界的贝肯斯坦-霍金熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统dS/CFT对应（dS/CFT correspondence）中存在若干微妙问题，需要寻找新的构造性途径来建立德西特时空的全息描述。

Method: 利用解析延拓将dS时空中的量子场论与fAdS时空中的量子场论建立联系；为一般渐近fAdS时空构造外推字典（extrapolate dictionary），并基于fCFT的共形对称性（conformal symmetry）独立推导全息两点函数进行交叉验证；最后通过fCFT₂的Cardy公式检验宇宙学视界熵。

Result: 成功构建了fAdS的全息描述，得到的全息两点函数与基于fCFT共形对称性的独立推导结果一致；fCFT₂的Cardy公式精确复现了纯dS₃与Kerr-dS₃中宇宙学视界的贝肯斯坦-霍金熵。

Conclusion: fCFT可能为德西特物理提供另一种有效的全息描述起点，该方案在熵计算方面通过了非平凡验证。

Abstract: Motivated by the subtleties in the conventional dS/CFT correspondence, we explore analytic continuation as a constructive route to de Sitter holography. We show that quantum field theories in de Sitter space and in a spacetime that we call flipped $\mathrm{AdS}/\mathbb{Z}$ (fAdS) are related by analytic continuation. We then develop a holographic description of fAdS in terms of a boundary theory referred to as flipped CFT (fCFT). In particular, we construct the extrapolate dictionary for general asymptotically fAdS spacetimes and compute the holographic two-point functions, finding agreement with an independent derivation based on the conformal symmetry of fCFT. The analytic continuation relation between fAdS and dS further suggests that fCFT may provide a starting point for an alternative holographic description of de Sitter physics. As a nontrivial check of this picture, we show that the Cardy formula of fCFT$_2$ reproduces the Bekenstein--Hawking entropies of the cosmological horizons in both pure dS$_3$ and Kerr-dS$_3$.

</details>


### [126] [Numerical Study of Scalar Field Theory on the Fuzzy Onion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08855)
*Matej Hrmo,Samuel Kováčik,Juraj Tekel*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过哈密顿蒙特卡洛（Hamiltonian Monte Carlo）数值模拟方法，研究了洋葱模糊球模型（fuzzy onion model）上的标量场理论相位结构，识别了不同场相、动力学位相变现象，并重建了相变线。研究还对比了该模型与模糊球（fuzzy sphere）的已知相位结构，最终确定了均匀相边界以及无序相与非均匀相之间的临界边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机在于理解三维矩阵模型——由半径逐渐增大的同心模糊球构成的洋葱模型上的标量场理论行为，并探究其与已充分研究的模糊球相位结构之间的异同。

Method: 采用哈密顿蒙特卡洛（Hamiltonian Monte Carlo）数值模拟方法对该模型进行仿真，以研究其相位结构；通过观察动力学位相变现象并尝试重建相变线来分析场相。

Result: 研究识别出多个场相，观察到动力学位相变现象，成功重建了部分相变线；并确定了两条边界：均匀相边界（uniform phase boundary）和位于无序相与非均匀相之间的临界边界（critical boundary）。

Conclusion: 洋葱模糊模型上的标量场理论展示出与模糊球截然不同的相位结构特征，其相图由均匀相边界与无序—非均匀相之间的临界边界所限定，为理解非交换几何背景下的场理论相变提供了新的数值证据。

Abstract: We study the behaviour of the scalar field theory on the fuzzy onion model -- a three-dimensional matrix model consisting of concentric fuzzy spheres of gradually increasing radii. We use a numerical method of Hamiltonian Monte Carlo simulations to study the phase structure of this theory. We identify the field phases, investigate and describe a phenomenon of dynamical phase transitions and attempt to reconstruct phase transition lines. We compare the results with the well-studied phase structure of the fuzzy sphere. Finally, we identify two boundaries on the phase transitions of the theory, a uniform phase boundary and a critical boundary between the disordered and non-uniform phase.

</details>


### [127] [Production of Primordial Black Holes in the Double D3-Brane - anti-D3-Brane Inflation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08870)
*S. -H. Henry Tye*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出弦论中两个D3-反D3膜对（D3-$\bar D3$-brane pair）配对湮灭的暴胀模型扩展，指出首对膜湮灭时产生D1-弦和F1-弦，剩余膜对中的D3-荷场强导致介质3-膜（dielectric 3-branes）——即3-膜与D1-弦形成的中性有限尺寸束缚态——的形成。这些束缚态表现为无压或低压物质，在早期宇宙中可作为原初黑洞（primordial black holes）的种子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索弦论中D3-反D3膜暴胀模型的自然扩展，研究多膜对情形下新出现的物理特征，特别是介质3-膜的形成机制及其宇宙学后果。

Method: 基于弦论中非交换几何（non-commutative geometric）性质，利用Myers提出的介质膜效应，分析D3-反D3膜对湮灭过程中产生的弦及其与剩余膜对荷场的相互作用。

Result: 研究表明，第一对D3-反D3膜湮灭产生D1-弦和F1-弦；在剩余膜对的D3-荷场强作用下，这些弦形成介质3-膜——即中性、有限尺寸的3-膜与D1-弦束缚态，其行为类似无压或低压物质。

Conclusion: 介质3-膜具有宽范围的质量分布，能够在早期宇宙中充当原初黑洞的种子，为弦暴胀模型与原初黑洞形成之间建立联系。

Abstract: A natural extension of the successful $D3$-${\bar D}3$-brane inflation model in string theory is one with two $D3$-${\bar D}3$-brane pairs, giving rise to a novel feature in string-theory. When the first pair annihilates, $D1$-strings and $F1$-strings are produced. Following the non-commutative geometric properties in string theory, it is shown by Myers that the presence of the $D3$-charge field strength of the remaining $D3$-${\bar D}3$-brane pair leads to the formation of dielectric $3$-branes -- neutral, finite-size bound states of $3$-branes with $D1$-strings. They behave as matter with little or no pressure. Spanning a wide range of masses, they seed primordial black holes in the early universe.

</details>


### [128] [Matrix Multiverses Meet Multiple Mythologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08972)
*Sidan A,Tom Banks,Willy Fischler*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种模型：各种类型的渐近德西特（de Sitter, dS）宇宙及其半径$R_n$位于一个最大熵平坦$p=\rho$弗里德曼-罗伯逊-沃克（FRW）宇宙中的黑洞内部。这些dS宇宙具有大量量子态，并在时间尺度$\alpha_n R_n \ln(R_n/\delta_n)$内衰变并与最大熵宇宙达到平衡。由于衰变时间远短于dS回复（recurrence）时间，该模型可用来解释自然界某些常数或宇宙学初始条件为何呈现数学上不自然的数值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在构建一个超越标准dS时空图景的理论框架，以回答为何某些自然常数或宇宙学初始条件呈现"数学上不自然"的取值——因为其他取值可能无法支持任何形式的智慧生命（即实现人择原理的物理基础）。

Method: 作者提出了一种嵌合模型：最大熵的平坦$p=\rho$ FRW宇宙中的黑洞内部容纳多种类型的渐近dS宇宙。模型利用Israel连接条件（junction conditions）设定壳层与dS视界的初始距离$\delta_n$，并推导出这些dS宇宙衰变并均衡化的时间尺度约为$\alpha_n R_n \ln(R_n/\delta_n)$，其中$\alpha_n$为依赖模型的无量纲常数。该时间尺度由沿远离宇宙学视界轨迹运行的探测器所观察。

Result: 模型表明这些衰变时间在指数意义上远短于dS回复时间，而后者在该模型中不再具有物理意义。此外，dS宇宙内部的探测器无法从原则上判定自身是否属于此类嵌套结构的一部分。

Conclusion: 该模型可能为如下主张提供理论基础：某些自然常数或宇宙学初始条件之所以呈现数学上不自然的值，是因为其他取值无法产生任何形式的智慧生命，从而将人择论证纳入一个可检验的物理框架。

Abstract: We present a model in which asymptotically de Sitter universes of various types, and de Sitter (dS) radii $R_n$, live in the interiors of black holes in a maximally entropic flat $p = ρ$ Friedmann-Robertson-Walker universe. These dS universes clearly have many quantum states. We argue that they decay and equilibrate with the maximal entropy universe on time scales of order $α_n R_n {\rm ln} (R_n /δ_n)$, where $α_n$ are model-dependent dimensionless constants and $δ_n$ are the initial distances between the shell, satisfying the Israel junction conditions, and the dS horizon. These are time-scales as viewed by a detector following a trajectory far from the would-be cosmological horizon. These times are all exponentially shorter than dS recurrence times, which have no meaning in this model. It is impossible for a detector inside one of the dS universes to determine whether it is actually part of such a structure. This model could be used as the basis for claiming that certain constants of nature or cosmological initial conditions were chosen to have mathematically unnatural values because other values could not lead to any form of intelligent life.

</details>


### [129] [Superselected ghost theory: perturbation theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09017)
*Bob Holdom*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于精确鬼影奇偶性（ghost parity）的超级选择规则（superselection rule），赋予鬼影量子场论（ghost QFT）概率诠释；通过哈密顿量的相似变换构造保持鬼影奇偶性的微扰论（Z₂PT），并证明其在有限阶上与标准旧式微扰论（OFPT）在非零能量分母处一致，但接触项及主值处方选择带来关键差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准微扰展开中鬼影奇偶性（ghost parity）在有限阶不被保持，阻碍鬼影量子场论的概率诠释。需要构造一种保持该对称性的微扰方案，以在微扰论框架内恢复概率诠释并明确其与标准微扰论的差异。

Method: 通过对哈密顿量实施相似变换 h = gHg⁻¹ = h₀ + h₁ + h₂ + …，构造鬼影奇偶性保持的微扰论（Z₂PT）。依据超级选择规则选择跨扇区能量分母的主值（principal-value）处方，并按跃迁类型而非粒子种类确定处方。逐阶分析第三、四阶展开以揭示Z₂PT与旧式微扰论（OFPT）的关系。

Result: 在三阶，h₁与h₂的乘积结合h₃可在远离零分母处复现OFPT；在四阶且h₁=0时，h₂²与h₄满足类似关系。零分母处的接触项使Z₂PT区别于OFPT，但（在各阶）不改变局域原始紫外发散（primitive UV divergences）。

Conclusion: Z₂PT提供了一种在微扰框架内保持鬼影奇偶性从而确立概率诠释的可行方案，其在非零能量分母处与OFPT一致，而接触项差异不影响原始紫外发散的局域结构。

Abstract: A superselection rule based on an exact ghost parity can endow a ghost QFT with a probability interpretation. However, this ghost parity is not respected at finite order in the standard perturbative expansion. A ghost-parity-preserving perturbation theory (Z$_2$PT) is obtained through a similarity transformation of the Hamiltonian, $h=g H g^{-1}=h_0+h_1+h_2+...$. The resulting expansion is reminiscent of old-fashioned perturbation theory (OFPT) with some significant differences. The superselection rule selects the principal-value prescription for cross-sector energy denominators, with the prescription determined by the type of transition rather than the particle species. At third order, products of $h_1$ and $h_2$ combine with $h_3$ to reproduce OFPT away from vanishing denominators. At fourth order, when $h_1=0$, we show that $h_2^2$ and $h_4$ satisfy the analogous relation. Contact terms from vanishing denominators distinguish Z$_2$PT from OFPT but do not alter the local primitive UV divergences through these orders.

</details>


### [130] [Long-range multipartite entanglement in holographic gapless systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09056)
*Xiantong Chen,Xuanting Ji,Xin-Xiang Ju,Wen-Peng Li,Ya-Wen Sun*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 利用全息（holography）方法研究三维无带隙（gapless）量子系统在强耦合下的长程多体纠缠，指出一大类多体纠缠量具有由红外（IR）几何决定的统一大距离标度指数，并发现长程多体纠缠在不同超标度破坏（hyperscaling-violating）红外几何下可呈现衰减、对数增强、亚广泛增长或体积律等多种标度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无带隙量子系统支持任意长距离关联，产生幂律长程纠缠（power-law long-range entanglement），但强耦合下三维无带隙系统的长程多体纠缠行为尚未得到系统刻画，作者希望揭示其在强耦合下的标度增长性质与IR物理的非局域特征。

Method: 采用全息对偶（holographic duality）框架，将三维无带隙量子系统映射为引力背景中的边界理论；通过分析超尺度破坏红外几何与各向异性AdS₃×ℝ²几何下的全息纠缠量，提取多体纠缠量的大距离标度指数，并系统性比较不同几何参数对应的纠缠长程行为。

Result: 研究表明，一大类多体纠缠量的leading大距离标度指数由红外几何唯一确定；在不同超尺度破坏参数下，长程多体纠缠可表现为衰减、对数增长、亚广泛增长或体积律。对各向异性AdS₃×ℝ²几何，长程纠缠沿一个方向保留，而在横向带隙（gapped）方向变为短程。

Conclusion: 全息无带隙相能够支持丰富且增强的长程多体纠缠，这些纠缠量提供了对底层红外物理的非局域刻画，表明强耦合体系中多体纠缠可随尺度增长并在大距离上被显著增强。

Abstract: Gapless quantum systems support correlations over arbitrarily long distances, giving rise to power-law long-range entanglement. We investigate long-range entanglement at strong coupling for three-dimensional gapless systems using holography, asking whether multipartite entanglement can exhibit scale-growing behavior and become enhanced at large distances. We show that a broad class of multipartite entanglement quantities share the same leading large-distance scaling exponent determined by the IR geometry. To realize different scaling regimes, we consider hyperscaling-violating IR geometries. Depending on the parameters, long-distance multipartite entanglement can decay, become logarithmic, grow subextensively, or reach a volume law. We also analyze an anisotropic IR geometry \(\mathrm{AdS}_{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{2}\), where long-range multipartite entanglement survives along one direction but becomes short-ranged in the transverse gapped directions. These results show that holographic gapless phases can support rich and enhanced long-range multipartite entanglement, providing a nonlocal characterization of the underlying IR physics.

</details>


### [131] [Holographic multipartite entanglement dynamics in AdS$_3$-Vaidya](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09304)
*Xin-Xiang Ju,Ya-Wen Sun,Yang Zhao,Hao-Ran Zhou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了AdS₃/CFT₂全息热化（holographic thermalization）过程中多体纠缠（multipartite entanglement）的动态重组机制，通过n方信息、Markov gap和genuine tripartite multi-entropy等探针揭示纠缠在空间尺度间的重新分布与多体结构的重组规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解全局淬火（global quench）后全息热化过程中多体纠缠如何动态重组，特别是多体纠缠在时空上的演化特征，这是已有单体和两体纠缠研究所未覆盖的方面。

Method: 使用n方信息（n-partite information）(−1)ⁿIₙ探测集体多体纠缠，选取n区域纠缠楔（entanglement wedge）整体连通而所有较少方区域不连通的全息构型，以排除较少方纠缠的贡献；并进一步计算Markov gap和genuine tripartite multi-entropy作为三方纠缠的互补探针。

Result: 多体纠缠的空间范围随时间先扩张后收缩，逐步趋近晚期局域热态；不同参与方数的纠缠在可比的早期时间尺度上发展，但参与方数更多的纠缠弛豫更慢，呈现出从较短到较长空间距离的瞬态传播。Markov gap在局域热化后仍可保持增强，而genuine tripartite multi-entropy呈现非单调演化，对所考虑的相邻三分剖分最终回到真空值。

Conclusion: 全局淬火会在空间尺度上重新分配纠缠并重组其多体纠缠结构，揭示出热化过程中多体纠缠从短距离向长距离的传播效应及不同参与方数纠缠的差异弛豫行为。

Abstract: We study how multipartite entanglement is dynamically reorganized during holographic thermalization following a global quench in AdS$_3$/CFT$_2$. We first use the $n$-partite information $(-1)^n I_n$ to probe collective multipartite entanglement in holographic configurations where the full $n$-region entanglement wedge is connected while all fewer-party ones are disconnected, thereby excluding fewer-party contributions. The spatial range of multipartite entanglement first expands and then contracts as the system approaches its late-time locally thermal state. Entanglement involving different numbers of parties develops on comparable early-time scales, while the entanglement that involves more parties relaxes more slowly, revealing a transient propagation from shorter to longer spatial distances. We further compute the Markov gap and the genuine tripartite multi-entropy as complementary probes of tripartite entanglement. The Markov gap can remain enhanced after local thermalization, whereas the genuine tripartite multi-entropy undergoes a nonmonotonic evolution and returns to its vacuum value for the adjacent tripartition considered in this work. These results show that a global quench redistributes the entanglement across spatial scales and reorganizes its multipartite structure.

</details>


### [132] [Nonperturbative Stabilization of D-Instantons in the Bosonic IIB Matrix Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09598)
*Yu-An Chen,Hikaru Kawai,Henry Liao,Cheng-Tsung Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究玻色型IIB（IKKT）矩阵模型中D瞬子（D-instanton）位置$p^{(i)}_\mu$的坍塌问题，指出其一圈有效势导致的坍塌是微扰截断的假象；通过精确求解N=2双体扇区的U(2)模型，发现两体相互作用在最大对角规范（maximal diagonal gauge）下短距离力有限且排斥，从而两体势在有限距离处具有稳定极小值，表明非坍塌是非微扰效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IKKT矩阵模型中D瞬子位置的一圈有效势众所周知地驱动它们坍缩到单一点，但这一坍缩是否真实物理仍有疑问；作者旨在论证该坍缩是一圈截断的产物，而非微扰的真实非坍缩效应在精确的全圈层次已经存在。

Method: 作者以保持对角与非对角扇区区分的方式规范固定U(N)对称性，并用辅助鬼BRST构造处理剩余U(1)^N对称性；该构造产生新的鬼四脚顶点，使得Faddeev-Popov行列式可进行系统性大分离展开，将有效势组织成多体分解；利用N×N模型两体扇区因子化为N=2副本的性质，用U(2)模型精确求解两体相互作用。

Result: 精确的N=2配分函数在有限分离处是有限的；其朴素Lorenz规范形式在分离量级为1时出现负值区域，归结为Lorenz规范的Gribov歧义；在最大对角规范下，短距离力有限且排斥，两体势在有限分离处具有稳定极小值，表明两个D瞬子不会坍塌到一起。

Conclusion: 结果支持$p^{(i)}_\mu$具有稳定、非坍塌的分布；但确立详细分布及全N体非坍塌需要更高体势能项，留待未来工作。

Abstract: We study the bosonic type IIB (IKKT) matrix model and the fate of the D-instanton positions $p^{(i)}_μ$, the diagonal components of the $d$ Hermitian matrices, whose one-loop effective potential infamously drives them to a single point. We argue that this collapse is an artifact of the leading (one-loop) truncation, while the actual non-collapse of the $p^{(i)}_μ$ is a nonperturbative effect: it is invisible at one loop but already present in the exact (all-loop) two-body interaction. Since the two-body sector of the $N\times N$ model factorizes into copies of $N=2$, this interaction is captured exactly by the $\mathrm{U}(2)$ model, and we find that the two D-instantons do not collapse onto each other. To set up the computation, we gauge-fix the $\text{U}(N)$ symmetry in a way that keeps the diagonal and off-diagonal sectors distinct, and we handle the residual $\mathrm{U}(1)^N$ symmetry with an auxiliary-ghost BRST construction. This construction generates a new ghost four-leg vertex; the resulting Faddeev-Popov determinant admits a systematic large-separation expansion that organizes the effective potential into a many-body decomposition, separating the interaction into two-body, three-body, and higher-body contributions. The exact $N=2$ partition function is finite at finite separation; its naive Lorenz-gauge form develops a negative region at separations of order one, which we trace to a Gribov ambiguity of the Lorenz gauge and resolve with the maximal diagonal gauge--the classical frame containing the perturbative vacuum--where the short-distance force is finite and repulsive, so that the two-body potential develops a stable minimum at finite separation. These results are consistent with a stable, non-collapsed distribution of the $p^{(i)}_μ$; establishing the detailed distribution and full $N$-body non-collapse requires the higher-body potentials and is left to future work.

</details>


### [133] [Schwinger-Keldysh effective field theory of type-B Goldstone: near-diagonal geometry and Berry term](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09776)
*Pei Zheng,Mei Huang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了B型戈德斯通模（type-B Goldstone modes）的有限温度Schwinger-Keldysh有效场论，利用近对角几何组织双时间轮廓结构，通过贝里曲率的横移（transgression）自然得到贝里项，并证明了该横移与动力学KMS条件相容，进而分类正则流形上的全局张量以构造守恒、耗散和噪声扇区，最后在两个具体模型中计算了耗散存在下的色散关系和两点关联函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: B型戈德斯通模在有限温度下其有效场论描述尚不完善，特别是如何将贝里项（Berry term）与Schwinger-Keldysh形式以及耗散效应有机结合，是亟待解决的问题。

Method: 作者采用Schwinger-Keldysh有效场论框架，通过近对角几何（near-diagonal geometry）组织r型场与a型场的双时间轮廓结构；利用贝里曲率的横移直接导出贝里项；在余切流形上分类全局张量以构造守恒、耗散和噪声扇区，并检验与动力学KMS条件的相容性。

Result: 该框架成功构建了包含贝里项的有效场论，证明了贝里横移与动力学KMS条件兼容；基于全局张量分类完整构造了守恒、耗散和噪声项；在两个具体模型中得到了耗散作用下戈德斯通模的色散关系及两点关联函数。

Conclusion: 该工作为B型戈德斯通模在有限温度且含耗散条件下提供了一个几何化的有效场论框架，既保持了贝里项结构，又满足KMS条件，可推广用于描述更多具耗散效应的物理体系。

Abstract: In this work, we formulate a finite temperature Schwinger-Keldysh effective field theory for type-B Goldstone modes. In particular, we organize the required two time contour structure by a near-diagonal geometry in which the r-type field is interpreted as the physical Goldstone configuration and the a-type field is identified as corresponding tangent displacement. Within this geometric viewpoint, the Berry term essential for type-B Goldstones is obtained directly through the transgression of the Berry curvature. Moreover, we show that this exact Berry transgression is compatible with dynamical KMS condition. In order to construct the conservative, dissipative and noise sectors, we classify possible tensors globally defined on the coset manifold. Based on this framework, we discuss in detail two concrete model examples. The dispersion relations of the Goldstone modes and the associated two-point correlation functions are calculated in the presence of dissipation.

</details>


### [134] [Thermal Origin of Black Hole Quasinormal Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09797)
*D. Giataganas,G. F. Giudice,A. Kehagias,F. Quevedo,A. Riotto*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明黑洞合并后辐射的准正则模（quasinormal modes, QNMs）具有精确的热力学解释：探针弦在光子环（photon ring）附近的传播会在其世界面上诱导出 Rindler 视界，其温度由光子环的 Lyapunov 指数决定，而黑洞 QNMs 作为该热系统的热激发涌现出来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞合并后会以特定频率（准正则模）辐射引力波，在 eikonal 极限下这些模式由光子环上的不稳定圆光轨道支配。作者希望揭示这一振荡现象背后是否存在更深层的热力学原理，从而建立黑洞振铃（ringing）的精确热解释。

Method: 作者从探针弦在近环（near-ring）几何中的传播入手，分析其世界面上由 Lyapunov 指数所设定的温度所诱导的 Rindler 视界。随后从两个互补视角显式推导 QNM 谱：微观上通过不稳定横向世界面涨落（transverse worldsheet fluctuations），宏观上通过开放热量子系统（open thermal quantum system）的因果响应函数（causal response function）的极点结构。

Result: 研究表明黑洞 QNMs 可视为光子环上热系统的热激发，因此黑洞的特征振铃可以被理解为该热系统的延迟响应（retarded response）。两个互补推导路径——微观涨落分析与宏观响应函数极点结构——均给出了 QNM 谱。

Conclusion: 该工作为黑洞振铃提供了精确的热力学诠释，表明光子环上的热系统动力学是黑洞 QNM 的微观起源。

Abstract: When a black hole rings after a merger, it emits gravitational waves at characteristic frequencies known as quasinormal modes (QNMs). In the eikonal limit, these modes are governed by the unstable circular light orbits that form the photon ring. In this work, we demonstrate that the ringing of a black hole has a precise thermal interpretation. A probe string propagating in the near-ring geometry acquires an induced Rindler horizon on its worldsheet, with a temperature set by the Lyapunov exponent of the photon ring. Out of this structure, the black hole QNMs emerge as thermal excitations, so that the characteristic ringing of a black hole is the retarded response of a thermal system living on the photon ring. We explicitly derive the QNM spectrum from two complementary perspectives: microscopically, via unstable transverse worldsheet fluctuations, and macroscopically, through the pole structure of the causal response function of an open thermal quantum system.

</details>


### [135] [Closed-form thermal threshold functions for the proper-time renormalisation group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09803)
*Daniele Rizzo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导了有限温度固有时间重整化群（proper-time renormalisation group, PTRG）热阈值函数的闭式表达式，取代了以往逐Matsubara模数值求值的方法。通过Poisson重求和得到由修正贝塞尔函数构成的快速收敛卷绕数级数，并利用代数恒等式将高阶阈值函数归结为同一闭式形式。该结果不仅复现了O(N)对称理论中局部势近似（LPA）及其含反常维数修正（LPA'）的既有结论，还首次将零温反常维数构造推广至有限温度，并实现了定点作为正则化器连续函数的追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限温度PTRG的热阈值函数此前只能逐Matsubara模数值计算，缺乏解析闭式表达。这一不足限制了计算效率与对定点结构随正则化器连续变化的深入理解。

Method: 对标准单参数正则化器族，利用Poisson重求和将Matsubara求和转化为修正贝塞尔函数的卷绕数级数；通过一个代数恒等式将任意高阶阈值函数约化为同一闭式形式（核参数经平移）。尖锐固有时间正则化器（sharp proper-time regulator）作为族中m→∞的精确极限被解析恢复，且其O(1/m)趋近行为受控，并可分解为场依赖项与纯热项两部分。

Result: 基于该闭式表达式，O(N)对称理论在LPA和LPA'截断下的结果与使用优化正则化器的精确Wetterich方程交叉验证一致。突破性结果包括：将已知的零温反常维数构造推广至有限温度；由于闭式对任意实数正则化器参数成立，定点首次可作为正则化器的连续函数追踪，且其变化平滑、有界，沿整条参数线上无任何特殊或病态点。

Conclusion: 该工作在有限温度PTRG框架中建立了从数值到解析的关键推进，既验证了已有结构的正确性，又开辟了连续扫描正则化器参数以研究定点性质的新的可能性。

Abstract: We derive closed-form expressions for the thermal threshold functions of the finite-temperature proper-time renormalisation group (PTRG): until now these have been evaluated numerically, Matsubara mode by Matsubara mode. For the standard one-parameter regulator family, Poisson resummation of the Matsubara sum yields a rapidly convergent winding-number series of modified Bessel functions, and a single algebraic identity reduces every higher threshold function to this same closed form at a shifted kernel parameter. The sharp proper-time regulator, recovered as the exact $m\to\infty$ endpoint of the family with a controlled $O(1/m)$ approach, factorises into a field-dependent and a purely thermal piece.
  Built on them, the local potential approximation (LPA) and its refinement to a running anomalous dimension (LPA$'$) for the $O(N)$-symmetric theory reduce to established results, all cross-checked against the exact Wetterich equation with the optimised regulator. Two findings go beyond reduction. First, the known zero-temperature anomalous-dimension construction is extended here to finite temperature. Second, because the closed form holds for any real regulator parameter, the refined truncation's fixed point can for the first time be tracked as a continuous function of the regulator, rather than at a handful of isolated points, and is found to vary smoothly and remain bounded, with no special or pathological point anywhere on the line.

</details>


### [136] [On the Renormalization in Conformal Quantum Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09858)
*Ioseph L. Buchbinder,Petr M. Lavrov,Thomas M. Sangy,Ilya L. Shapiro*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本篇论文研究了弯曲时空背景中经典共形（conformal）理论在存在规范不变性（gauge invariance）时的一圈（one-loop）发散问题，论证了利用BRST对称性可实现共形不变的圈阶重整化，并将这一结果从半经典理论扩展到共形量子引力（conformal quantum gravity）及其与共形物质场耦合的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当具有规范不变性的共形理论在弯曲时空（curved spacetime）中进行量子化时，需要引入规范固定项（gauge-fixing term）和鬼场作用量（ghosts action），而这两者并不具备共形对称性，使得共形不变重整化的证明成为一个非平凡的问题。早期的形式化证明尚未覆盖有限部分及更复杂的Weyl平方引力（Weyl-squared gravity）规范固定情形，因此需要进一步发展。

Method: 论文依据作者之一1984年提出的形式化证明框架，利用BRST对称性来确保规范固定项和鬼场部分对有效作用量共形变化的贡献相互抵消，从而证明共形不变的重整化可行性。随后将这一论证应用于Weyl平方引力及其与共形物质场的耦合，并作为预备步骤给出了半经典理论中共形不变一圈发散的一般性详细证明。

Result: 证明了在Weyl平方引力中尽管规范固定更为复杂，仍可利用BRST对称性实现一圈共形不变的重整化；同时表明该抵消机制在有效作用量的一圈及更高圈有限部分不再成立，从而导致共形反常（conformal anomaly）的产生。

Conclusion: 本文扩展了早期关于外部引力场中共形物质场的结果，将其应用于包括共形量子引力在内的更一般情形，验证了BRST对称性在共形不变重整化证明中的核心作用，并厘清了共形反常存在的根源。

Abstract: One-loop divergences in classically conformal theory in curved spacetime is a nontrivial issue if the theory under consideration possesses gauge invariance. In this case, quantization involves introducing the gauge-fixing term and the action of ghosts, both of which are not conformal. The formal proof of the conformal invariant renormalizability in this situation, including interacting theories, has been given in the paper from 1984 by one of the present authors. Owing to BRST symmetry, the contributions of the gauge-fixing and ghost sectors to the conformal variation of the effective action cancel each other. This cancellation does not hold in the finite part of the one-loop and higher-loops effective action, that results in the anomaly. In this paper, we extend the early results on conformal matter fields in external gravitational field and apply them to conformal quantum gravity, including the case of conformal gravity coupled to other conformal matter fields. The gauge-fixing in the Weyl-squared gravity is more complicated, nevertheless, the proof of the one-loop conformal invariant renormalization using BRST symmetry is possible. As a preliminary to conformal quantum gravity, we also present a detailed general proof of conformal invariant one-loop divergences in the corresponding semiclassical theory.

</details>


### [137] [Ricci-flat metrics from gauged linear $σ$-models without RG flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09918)
*Dmitri Bykov,Ondrej Hulik,Ulf Lindström,Martin Roček,Rikard von Unge*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类通过带不定号符号（indefinite signature）的规范线性sigma模型（GLSMs）构造的广义锥流形上的Ricci平坦Kähler度规。通过引入以负签名动能项进入sigma模型的“影子坐标”（superfields），作者得到复射影空间乘积上的复锥的显式Ricci平坦度规，并给出了Kähler势的一般公式和具体例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索利用规范线性sigma模型（gauged linear sigma models, GLSMs）构造新的显式Ricci平坦Kähler度规，拓展Calabi-Yau度规和环面几何（toric geometry）的研究方向，并深入理解不定号签名模型的几何意义。

Method: 在GLSM中引入带有负签名动能项的“影子坐标”（shadow coordinates/superfields），通过这一技巧在广义锥流形（generalized conifolds）上构造显式Ricci平坦Kähler度规。文章给出了所得Kähler势的一般公式，并辅以具体算例。

Result: 成功地在复射影空间乘积上的复锥（complex cones over products of projective spaces）上得到了显式的Ricci平坦度规，并提供了一套通用的Kähler势公式及详细的示例；同时提出了一种新颖的广义Kähler规范（generalized Kähler gauging）解释。

Conclusion: 这些结果为Calabi-Yau度规及环面几何的研究提供了新的方向，并引发了关于不定号签名模型几何含义的开放性问题。

Abstract: We investigate a class of Ricci-flat Kähler metrics on generalized conifolds constructed via gauged linear sigma models (GLSMs) with indefinite signature. By introducing shadow coordinates (superfields) entering the sigma model with negative signature kinetic term, we show that these GLSMs yield explicit Ricci-flat metrics on complex cones over products of projective spaces. We provide a general formula for the resulting Kähler potentials, along with detailed examples. Our results suggest new directions for the study of Calabi-Yau metrics and toric geometry, and raise interesting questions about the geometric meaning of indefinite signature models. We also give an interpretation in terms of a novel generalized Kähler gauging.

</details>


### [138] [The quadratic growth of Krylov spread complexity in the BTZ black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09922)
*Aranya Bhattacharya,Mario Flory,Michal P. Heller,Emiliano Rizza,Tim Schuhmann*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于配分函数的Krylov扩散复杂度（Krylov spread complexity）构造方案，可用于热场双态（thermofield-double states）的边界重构，并在BTZ黑洞时空中将其匹配到由外在曲率不变量无穷级数构建的广义“复杂度=任意性”（complexity = anything）体对象，给出从黑洞热力学到Krylov动力学的系统路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子全息复杂度（holographic complexity）所刻画的黑洞内部生长边界量在二维量子引洞（dilaton gravity）之外仍然未知，需要寻找维度无关的边界重构方案。该问题对理解黑洞内部结构及全息对偶的深层机制具有基础意义。

Method: 作者对热场双态的Krylov扩散复杂度给出配分函数构造的批判性分析，利用半经典全息配分函数实现与维度无关的边界重构；在双标度SYK模型（double-scaled Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev model）中比较精确解与半经典结果，并在大中心荷二维共形场论（large-central-charge 2D CFT）中高于Hawking-Page温度时应用于BTZ黑洞背景。

Result: 在双标度SYK模型中，经典极限仅在复杂度重构之后取才可靠，在单个Lanczos系数层面取该极限会丢失关键信息。在BTZ背景下，发现早期二次增长（quadratic growth）后出现中间偏离，随后表现为渐近二次增长的回返行为，而非体积算子的线性晚期行为和标准有限泛函复杂度=任意性类；该边界行为与由外在曲率不变量无限级数构成的广义复杂度=任意性体对象相匹配。

Conclusion: 该构造提供了从黑洞热力学通向Krylov动力学的系统性路径，可自然推广到高维全息黑洞，为全息复杂度的维度无关边界重构提供了新的方案。

Abstract: The boundary quantity that captures the growth of black-hole interiors quantified by holographic complexity remains unknown beyond 2d dilaton gravity. We provide a critical analysis of a partition-function construction of Krylov spread complexity for thermofield-double states that provides a dimension-independent boundary reconstruction from semiclassical holographic partition functions, while developing the present dynamical and bulk construction for the BTZ saddle. In the double-scaled Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev model, where exact and semiclassical results can be compared, we show that the classical limit is reliable only when taken after the complexity has been reconstructed; taking this limit at the level of individual Lanczos coefficients discards essential information. Applying the construction to a large-central-charge two-dimensional conformal field theory above the Hawking-Page temperature dual to a Bañados-Teitelboim-Zanelli black hole, we find an intermediate departure from early-time quadratic growth followed by behavior compatible with a return toward asymptotically quadratic growth, rather than the linear late-time behavior of the volume and the standard finite-functional complexity = anything class. We then match this boundary behavior to a generalized complexity = anything bulk object built from an infinite series of extrinsic-curvature invariants. The construction provides a systematic route from black-hole thermodynamics to Krylov dynamics and can naturally be extended to higher-dimensional holographic black holes.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

### [139] [Second order unfitted ghost-FEM for elliptic interface problems with applications to low-dimensional semiconductor devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09466)
*Clarissa Astuto,Giovanni Nastasi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种非拟合鬼单元有限元方法（unfitted ghost finite element method）用于求解具有不连续扩散系数的椭圆界面问题，并将其应用于低维半导体器件的静电模拟，包括石墨烯场效应晶体管中的自洽漂移扩散-泊松耦合模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 椭圆界面问题在扩散系数不连续的情况下需要精确处理界面，而传统方法常依赖网格生成与重网格化，在复杂界面（如器件中的氧化物/石墨烯界面）下成本高昂且鲁棒性不足。研究者旨在开发一种基于固定笛卡尔网格、无需网格生成的方法，以高效模拟低维半导体器件的静电行为。

Method: 方法基于固定笛卡尔网格，利用水平集函数（level-set function）表示几何界面；采用“回弹回网格”（snapping-back-to-grid）策略控制小割单元问题；通过对称Nitsche公式（symmetric Nitsche formulation）弱施加界面条件。应用于石墨烯场效应晶体管时，静电势在二维氧化物/石墨烯/氧化物结构中求解，石墨烯层中的电荷输运由退化情形下一维双极漂移扩散方程结合场依赖迁移率模型描述，耦合非线性系统通过阻尼不动点迭代与三层几何的域分解（domain decomposition）方法求解。

Result: 在基准椭圆界面问题上，方法对不同几何和系数跳跃均达到解的二阶精度和梯度的—阶精度。在石墨烯场效应晶体管模拟中，数值结果再现了从关态到开态的清晰转移特性，展示了栅电压对二维静电势的影响；结果还表明石墨烯层的有效厚度与离散化会影响横向电势分布，支持采用带显式氧化物/石墨烯界面条件的全二维静电描述。

Conclusion: 所提出的非拟合鬼单元有限元方法能够有效求解具有不连续扩散系数的椭圆界面问题，并为低维半导体器件的静电模拟提供可靠工具；其结果验证了采用二维静电模型并显式处理界面条件的必要性。

Abstract: We develop an unfitted ghost finite element method for elliptic interface problems with discontinuous diffusion coefficients and apply it to the electrostatic simulation of low-dimensional semiconductor devices. The proposed approach is based on a fixed Cartesian grid and represents the geometry by level-set functions, avoiding mesh generation and remeshing even in the presence of interfaces. A snapping-back-to-grid strategy is used to control the small-cut-cell issue, while interface conditions are weakly enforced by a symmetric Nitsche formulation. The method is first validated on benchmark elliptic interface problems with different geometries and coefficient jumps, showing second-order accuracy for the solution and first-order accuracy for its gradient.
  As an application, we consider a graphene field-effect transistor described by a self-consistent drift-diffusion-Poisson model. The electrostatic potential is computed in a two-dimensional oxide/graphene/oxide structure, while charge transport in the graphene layer is modeled by one-dimensional bipolar drift-diffusion equations in the degenerate case and by including a field-dependent mobility model. The coupled nonlinear system is solved by a damped fixed-point iteration combined with a domain-decomposition treatment of the three-layer geometry. Numerical simulations reproduce transfer characteristics with a clear transition from an OFF state to an ON state and show the influence of the gate voltage on the two-dimensional electrostatic potential. The results also indicate that the effective thickness and discretization of the graphene layer affect the transverse potential profile, supporting the use of a full two-dimensional electrostatic description with explicit oxide/graphene interface conditions.

</details>

### [140] [Manton's Exotic Vortex Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08973)
*Takashi Ono*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究Manton奇异涡旋方程（Manton's exotic vortex equation），先后建立了其解的存在性结果、构造了解空间的模空间（moduli space），并建立了该方程与带符号(1,1)伪厄米度量（pseudo-Hermitian metric）的伪厄米–杨–米尔斯联络（pseudo-Hermitian–Yang–Mills connection）之间的维度约化（dimensional reduction）对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该方程源于Manton对经典涡旋方程（classical vortex equation）的一种形变研究，目前对其解的存在性、模空间结构及与其他几何对象间的联系尚缺乏系统认识，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 论文依次开展了三方面工作：首先研究Manton奇异涡旋方程解的存在性问题并建立存在性结果；其次构造该方程解空间的模空间；最后对该方程建立维度约化，具体表现为建立方程解与具有签名(1,1)伪厄米度量的伪厄米–杨–米尔斯联络之间的对应关系。

Result: 获得了Manton奇异涡旋方程解的存在性定理，成功构造了其模空间，并建立了方程解与伪厄米–杨–米尔斯联络在签名(1,1)伪厄米度量下的精确对应关系。

Conclusion: 通过存在性、模空间构造与维度约化三个层面的分析，本文系统揭示了Manton奇异涡旋方程的几何结构，并将其与伪厄米–杨–米尔斯理论建立了联系。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the equation which we call Manton's exotic vortex equation. The equation arose in Manton's study of a deformation of the classical vortex equation [Ma]. We first investigate the existence problem for Manton's exotic vortex equation and establish existence results.
  We then construct the moduli space of solutions.
  Finally, we establish a dimensional reduction for Manton's exotic vortex equation.
  More precisely, we establish a correspondence between solutions of Manton's exotic vortex equation and pseudo-Hermitian-Yang--Mills connections with pseudo-Hermitian metrics of signature (1,1).

</details>


### [141] [A global variational calculus on Fréchet manifolds and the Lagrangian structure of the Einstein evolution equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08987)
*José Antonio Vallejo*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在 Fréchet 流形（Fréchet manifold）上发展了变分法（variational calculus），并将其应用于紧致流形上黎曼度量流形上的爱因斯坦演化方程（Einstein evolution equations）的拉格朗日表述，建立了欧拉-拉格朗日方程、能量守恒、无穷小诺特定理（Noether theorem）以及 Maupertuis-Jacobi 定理，并证明了哈密顿约束与动量约束的传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有变分方法在处理无限维流形上的爱因斯坦演化方程时存在局限，尤其是弱伪黎曼度量（weak pseudo-Riemannian metrics）下的对称性与约束传播问题尚未得到系统处理。作者旨在建立适用于 Fréchet 流形的变分框架，以统一处理爱因斯坦方程中约束条件的几何起源与传播性质。

Method: 在 Fréchet 流形上建立变分法，针对弱伪黎曼度量相关的机械拉格朗日量（mechanical Lagrangians）推导欧拉-拉格朗日方程、能量守恒及不需要对称向量场生成流动的无穷小诺特定理；将该框架应用于 DeWitt 拉格朗日量，将动量约束与诺特动量映射（Noether momentum map）的消失相联系，并推导哈密顿约束传播的点态输运恒等式，最后证明弱伪黎曼度量下的 Maupertuis-Jacobi 定理。

Result: 该框架将动量约束识别为诺特动量映射的消失，并给出其传播的直接证明；导出了哈密顿约束传播所依据的点态输运恒等式；证明了在总标量曲率势（total scalar-curvature potential）的零点之外，受约束的爱因斯坦演化可重参化为共形 DeWitt 度量下的单位速度测地线（unit-speed geodesics）。

Conclusion: 该变分方法为爱因斯坦演化方程提供了一套统一的几何框架，将动量约束与哈密顿约束的传播问题纳入诺特理论与测地线动力学的处理之中，并建立了与共形 DeWitt 度量下测地线运动之间的等价性。

Abstract: We develop a variational calculus for curves on Fréchet manifolds and apply it to the Lagrangian formulation of the Einstein evolution equations on the manifold $\Mm$ of Riemannian metrics of a compact manifold. For mechanical Lagrangians associated with weak pseudo-Riemannian metrics, we establish the Euler--Lagrange equations, energy conservation, and an infinitesimal Noether theorem that does not require the symmetry vector field to generate a flow. Applied to the DeWitt Lagrangian, this framework identifies the momentum constraint with the vanishing of a Noether momentum map and gives a direct proof of its propagation. We also derive the pointwise transport identity \[ \partial_t\bigl(\Ham_λ\,\dv_g\bigr) = 2α\,δ_g(δ_g^-k)\,\dv_g\,, \] which implies propagation of the Hamiltonian constraint. Finally, we prove a Maupertuis-- Jacobi theorem for weak pseudo-Riemannian metrics on Fréchet manifolds and show that, away from the zero set of the total scalar-curvature potential, constrained Einstein evolutions are reparametrized unit-speed geodesics of the conformal DeWitt metric $-4αS_λG^-$.

</details>
