# nucl-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [Hadron-quark phase transitions along proto-neutron-star evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09195)
*P. Laskos-Patkos,P. S. Koliogiannis,Ch. C. Moustakidis*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了中微子捕获与扩散阶段的原初混合星（proto-hybrid star）的热量子态方程（hot hybrid equations of state），并结合Skyrme有效相互作用和向量MIT口袋模型探测了不同电荷守恒假设下此类恒星的演化及其对最大引力质量的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多信使天文学新时代需要在高温度条件下准确且自洽地推导核物质状态方程（equation of state）。本文旨在计算含夸克物质核心的原初中子星在热状态下的混合态方程，以理解其不同演化阶段的结构特征。

Method: 对于强子物质使用了两种不同的Skyrme有效相互作用；对于夸克物质采用了向量MIT口袋模型（vector MIT bag model）。在中微子捕获阶段通过全局轻子分数守恒建模，得到具有扩展混合相的态方程；在中微子扩散后的阶段分别使用Maxwell构造与Gibbs构造模拟局域或全局电荷守恒场景。随后通过求解Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkov方程描述对应混合星构型，并使用恒定静止质量序列考察其结构演化。

Result: 研究发现，无论电荷是全局守恒还是局域守恒，混合星早期演化阶段对其后期最大可能引力质量的确定可能起关键作用。

Conclusion: 原初混合星在其生命早期的热力学历史显著影响后期的引力质量上限，这一结果对理解此类天体的演化轨迹具有重要意义。

Abstract: The new era of multi-messenger astronomy requires the accurate and self-consistent derivation of the nuclear equation of state at high temperature. In the present work, we focused on the calculation of hot hybrid equations of state, studying the different evolution stages of a proto-neutron star with a quark matter core (proto-hybrid star). For the hadronic matter we used two distinct Skyrme effective interactions, while for quark matter the well-known vector MIT bag model was employed. To model the era of trapped neutrinos in the system we considered the global conservation of lepton fraction which resulted in an equation of state with an extended mixed phase. For periods following the neutrino diffusion phase of a proto-neutron star, the equations of state were modelled using both the Maxwell and the Gibbs construction depending on the assumption for either local or global electric-charge conservation. With the use of the derived hybrid models, we solved the Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkov equations to describe the corresponding hybrid star configurations. Finally, we investigated how the structure of proto-hybrid stars evolves, using constant rest mass sequences. We found that regardless of whether electric-charge is globally or locally conserved, the earlier stages of a hybrid star's life may play a crucial role on the determination of its maximum possible gravitational mass in later stages.

</details>

### [143] [Effects of high-pressure synthesis on phase formation and superconducting properties of PrFeAsO1-xFx](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08083)
*Priya Singh,Konrad Kwatek,Tatiana Zajarniuk,Tomasz Cetner,Jan Mizeracki,Shiv J. Singh*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本研究系统考察了高压高温合成（HP-HTS）工艺对Pr基铁基超导体（iron-based superconductors, IBS）PrFeAsO1-xFx（Pr1111）三种不同掺杂浓度样品的影响，发现其效果具有显著的组分依赖性：低掺杂和最优掺杂样品中氟元素掺入增加、致密度提升、超导转变温度（Tc）分别提高约1 K和6 K，而过掺杂样品则因杂质相增多而抑制超导电性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有报道显示高压（HP）合成工艺可提升多种铁基超导家族的超导性能，但其对Pr基氧族化物PrFeAsO1-xFx（Pr1111）的影响尚未被系统研究，需要评估该工艺在Pr1111体系中的适用性与组分依赖效应。

Method: 采用先前为其他铁基超导体系优化的0.5 GPa、1小时高压高温合成条件，从Pr1111的环境压力电子相图中选取欠掺杂（x=0.2）、最优掺杂（x=0.3）和过掺杂（x=0.5）三个代表性组分，系统表征其结构、微结构、电输运和磁学性质。

Result: 高压高温合成在欠掺杂和最优掺杂样品中增强了氟元素的掺入、改善了相形成并获得更致密的微观结构和更好的晶粒连接性；磁测量显示x=0.2和x=0.3样品的Tc分别提高约1 K和约6 K，但临界电流密度仅略有改善；欠掺杂样品的电阻率测量显示Tc略有升高但转变变宽，表明存在残余结构不均匀性；过掺杂样品则出现增强的杂质相分离并伴随超导电性受抑制。

Conclusion: 高压高温合成工艺在Pr1111体系中起效与否强烈依赖于掺杂组分，其效果由氟掺入、相稳定性和微观结构演化的相互作用共同决定，表明需要针对该体系进一步优化HP-HTS工艺条件。

Abstract: Motivated by recent reports of enhanced superconducting performance in several families of iron-based superconductors (IBS) processed by high-pressure (HP) synthesis, we investigate the influence of high gas pressure and high-temperature synthesis (HP-HTS) process on the structural, microstructural, electrical transport, and magnetic properties of Pr-based oxypnictide PrFeAsO1-xFx (Pr1111) using the processing conditions of 0.5 GPa for 1 h previously optimized for other IBS families. Representative underdoped (x = 0.2), optimal doped (x = 0.3), and overdoped (x = 0.5) compositions from the ambient-pressure electronic phase diagram of Pr1111 are selected to evaluate its composition-dependent effects of HP-HTS. The results demonstrate that HP-HTS enhances fluorine incorporation, improves phase formation, and produces a denser microstructure with improved grain connectivity in the underdoped and optimal doped compositions. Magnetic measurements reveal increased in the superconducting transition temperature (Tc) of ~1 K for x = 0.2 and ~6 K for x = 0.3, whereas only a marginal improvement in the critical current density is observed. Electrical resistivity measurements of the underdoped composition show a slight increase in Tc accompanied by a broader resistive transition, indicating residual structural inhomogeneity. In contrast, the overdoped composition exhibits increased impurity phase segregation, accompanied by suppression of superconductivity. These results demonstrate that the effectiveness of HP-HTS in Pr1111 is strongly composition dependent and governed by the interplay among fluorine incorporation, phase stability, and microstructural evolution, highlighting the need for further optimization of the HP-HTS processing conditions.

</details>


### [144] [Quantum Metric Induced Critical Current Anomaly in Flat Band Josephson Junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08120)
*Zhong C. F. Li,Yuxuan Deng,Dmitri K. Efetov,K. T. Law*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文指出，在基于扭转双层石墨烯（twisted bilayer graphene, TBG）的约瑟夫森结（Josephson junction）中，由于平带特性抑制了传统约瑟夫森电流，以及量子度量长度（quantum metric length）与结长相当或更长时出现的量子度量约瑟夫森电流（QMJC），可以解释实验中观测到的"临界电流异常"（critical current anomaly）——即临界电流I_c增加伴随正常态电导G下降。作者通过修正的Lieb格模型和TBG的六带模型验证了这一现象与实验的高度一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统约瑟夫森结理论认为超导临界电流I_c随正常态电导G的增加而增加，但近期在TBG基约瑟夫森结实验中意外观测到I_c增加的同时G反而下降，该异常现象无法被既有理论解释，需要新的物理机制（即量子度量约瑟夫森电流）来阐明。

Method: 作者首先指出传统约瑟夫森电流因平带（flatness of the band）的抑制而减弱，而量子度量长度（quantum metric length）与结长可比或更长时会产生量子度量约瑟夫森电流（QMJC），从而引发临界电流异常。他们利用修正的Lieb格模型进行解析与数值演示，并使用真实的扭转双层石墨烯六带模型（six-band model）进行数值验证，分析G和I_c中的常规贡献与量子度量贡献。

Result: 研究表明G和I_c均由常规贡献与量子度量贡献两部分构成，存在参数区间使得I_c增加而G下降。在TBG六带模型中的计算结果与实验观测呈现高度一致性。

Conclusion: 实验观测到的临界电流异常为量子度量约瑟夫森电流（QMJC）的存在提供了有力证据，而这一机制在传统约瑟夫森结理论中未被考虑。

Abstract: In well-established theories of Josephson junctions, the superconducting critical current \( I_\mathrm{c} \) increases as the normal state conductance \( \mathcal{G} \) increases. However, in a recent experiment in twisted bilayer graphene (TBG) based Josephson junctions, unexpectedly, it was observed that the increase of the critical current is accompanied by a decrease of the normal state conductance. We call this phenomenon the critical current anomaly. In this work, we point out that in the TBG-based Josephson junction, due to the suppression of the conventional Josephson current by the flatness of the band and the quantum metric enabled Josephson current (QMJC), the critical current anomaly can occur. The QMJC appears if the quantum metric length is comparable or longer than the junction length. We show that both \( \mathcal{G} \) and \( I_\mathrm{c} \) have the conventional and the quantum metric contributions, and there are parameter regimes in which \( I_\mathrm{c} \) increases even when \( \mathcal{G} \) decreases. We first demonstrate the critical current anomaly by a simple modified Lieb-lattice model both analytically and numerically. The incredible consistency with the experimental results is demonstrated using a realistic six-band model of twisted bilayer graphene. Therefore, we suggest that the critical current anomaly observed in the experiment provide strong evidence of QMJC which were ignored in well-established theories of Josephson junctions.

</details>


### [145] [Excitron-Induced Pair Fluctuations Reveal Superconductivity in the Electron Gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08498)
*Yasutami Takada*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算低密度三维电子气（dilute 3D electron gas）的电子自能，在r_s约8、温度约10^{-4}E_F处发现尖锐发散，标志二阶相变（second-order phase transition），该临界行为源于准一维超导涨落，由激子（excitron）的虚激发介导，揭示了等离激元驱动的超导在稀薄三维电子气中通过准一维激子动力学显现的新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解超导性如何在稀薄电子体系中涌现是凝聚态物理的核心挑战之一，该问题在低密度体系中尚未得到充分阐明。

Method: 对低密度三维电子气的电子自能Σ(k,iw_n)进行第一性原理计算，系统分析其在不同密度参数（r_s）和温度（T）下的行为，识别相变临界点并追踪其涨落来源。

Result: 在r_s约8、T约10^{-4}E_F处观察到自能的尖锐发散，对应二阶相变；临界行为由激子（一种由电子与纵向电子-空穴对构成的准一维复合体）的虚激发介导的一维超导涨落引起；相变附近观测到赝能隙（pseudogap）和随温度线性变化的逆电子寿命（linear-in-T inverse lifetime），与高温超导材料中的现象相似。

Conclusion: 尽管超导机制本身由等离激元（plasmon）介导，但激子通道为理解其涨落动力学提供了独特窗口，揭示了等离激元驱动的超导在稀薄三维电子气中通过准一维激子动力学显现的意外路径。

Abstract: Understanding how superconductivity can emerge in dilute electronic systems remains a central challenge in condensed matter physics. By performing first-principles calculations of the electron self-energy Σ(k,iw_n) in the low-density three-dimensional electron gas, we identify a sharp divergence at r_s ~ 8 and T ~ 10^{-4}E_ F, signaling a second-order phase transition. This critical behavior originates from one-dimensional superconducting fluctuations mediated by virtual excitations of an excitron---a quasi-1D electronic composite formed by an electron and longitudinal electron-hole pairs. Although the superconducting mechanism itself is plasmon-mediated, the excitron channel provides a unique window into its fluctuation dynamics. Near the transition, we observe a pseudogap and a linear-in-T inverse electron lifetime, reminiscent of phenomena in high-T_c materials. These results reveal an unexpected route by which plasmon-driven superconductivity manifests in the dilute 3D electron gas through quasi-1D excitron dynamics.

</details>


### [146] [Prediction of BaBiO$_3$-like superconducting perovskites in K-doped SrAsO$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09020)
*Zhihong Yuan,Rui Liu,Pengyu Zheng,Zhiping Yin*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 利用第一性原理计算，预测了一种新的钙钛矿化合物SrAsO₃。未掺杂的立方相存在软声子不稳定性，K掺杂至约60%以上时立方相趋于动态稳定，并表现出金属性和常规声子介导超导电性。非局域交换相互作用的引入会增强电子-声子耦合（EPC）强度并提高超导转变温度（T_c），其中Sr₀.₄K₀.₆AsO₃在HSE06杂化泛函下预测的EPC常数λ达到1.41，对应T_c约44.3 K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索新型超导钙钛矿材料，寻找与BaBiO₃类似的、由强电子-声子耦合驱动的超导体，以满足对新超导体系的材料发现与机理理解的需求。

Method: 采用第一性原理（first-principles）计算方法，系统研究SrAsO₃立方相的声子稳定性，考察K掺杂对Sr位点的替代作用，并通过HSE06杂化交换关联泛函考虑非局域交换相互作用，计算EPC常数λ和超导转变温度T_c。

Result: 未掺杂SrAsO₃立方相存在显著软声子不稳定性，K掺杂超过约60%后立方相恢复动态稳定。稳定的K掺杂相为金属态并具常规声子介导超导性；引入非局域交换相互作用可加宽电子带宽、增强EPC并提高T_c，其中Sr₀.₄K₀.₆AsO₃的λ达1.41，预测T_c为44.3 K。

Conclusion: SrAsO₃是一类类BaBiO₃的超导钙钛矿，其超导性源自强电子-声子耦合，非局域交换相互作用对提升超导性能至关重要，为后续实验合成与进一步理论研究提供了明确预测方向。

Abstract: Using first-principles calculations, we predict a new perovskite compound SrAsO$_3$ . The undoped cubic phase has pronounced soft-phonon instabilities, which are gradually suppressed upon K doping the Sr site. The cubic phase becomes dynamically stable for K-doping levels above approximately 60%, and the stabilized K-doped phases are metallic with predicted conventional phonon mediated superconductivity. Moreover, the inclusion of nonlocal exchange interactions broadens the electronic bandwidth, enhances the electron-phonon coupling (EPC) strength, and increases the superconducting transition temperature ($T_c$) of these doped compounds. In particular, the HSE06 hybrid exchange-correlation functional corrected EPC constant $λ$ reaches 1.41 for Sr$_{0.4}$K$_{0.6}$AsO$_3$, corresponding to a predicted $T_c$ of 44.3 K. These results suggest that SrAsO$_3$ is a BaBiO$_3$-like superconducting perovskite driven by strong electron-phonon coupling.

</details>


### [147] [Exchange-induced suppression of superconductivity in a nano-skyrmion lattice - superconductor hybrid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09207)
*Dongfei Wang,Wenbin Li,Eric Mascot,Roland Wiesendanger*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过在Nb(110)表面外延生长的Ir(111)薄膜上制备单层Fe，构建了纳米斯格明子晶格（nano-skyrmion lattice）与超导衬底耦合的混合系统，扫描隧道谱显示超导被Fe纳米斯格明子晶格抑制，紧束缚计算表明随交换耦合增强超导能隙被逐步填充直至超导消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁体-超导体混合系统被视为研究拓扑超导（topological superconductivity）的前沿平台，作者旨在探索将非共线自旋结构（如斯格明子晶格）与超导体耦合，以创造和操控非常规超导态的新途径。

Method: 在Nb(110)表面上通过外延生长制备Ir(111)薄膜，进而沉积单层Fe形成Fe/Ir(111)/Nb(110)异质结构；利用扫描隧道谱（scanning tunneling spectroscopy）测量超导特性，并结合紧束缚（tight-binding）计算模拟交换耦合对超导能隙的影响。

Result: 扫描隧道谱显示Fe纳米斯格明子晶格导致超导能隙（superconducting gap）和相干峰（coherence peaks）同时消失，即超导性被抑制；紧束缚计算表明随交换耦合增加，超导能隙逐渐被填充，最终超导性被完全淬灭。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了磁体-超导体异质结构中实现拓扑非平庸态所需的微观约束条件，为设计基于此类混合体系的拓扑非平庸量子态提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Engineered magnet-superconductor hybrids have recently been identified as promising platforms for the investigation of topological superconductivity. Beyond ferro- and antiferromagnetic structures, coupling non-collinear spin textures, such as skyrmion lattices, to superconductors offers an exciting route for creating and manipulating unconventional superconducting states. In this work, by preparing monolayer Fe on Ir(111) thin films grown epitaxially on a Nb(110) surface, we realize a hybrid system of a nano-skyrmion lattice proximitized to a superconducting substrate. Scanning tunneling spectroscopy shows that superconductivity becomes suppressed by the Fe nano-skyrmion lattice, with both the superconducting gap and coherence peaks disappearing. Tight-binding calculations reveal that with increasing exchange coupling, the gap is progressively filled up and eventually superconductivity gets quenched. These results reveal microscopic constraints for designing topologically non-trivial states based on magnet-superconducting heterostructures.

</details>


### [148] [Revealing Hidden Unconventional Pairing through Nonreciprocal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09751)
*Wen-Bo Dai,Ming Gong,Xianxin Wu,Chui-Zhen Chen,X. C. Xie*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文提出一个对称性解析（symmetry-resolved）框架，通过多端器件中源极与探测器终端互换时产生的非互易电导来识别超导配对对称性，并展示了不同配对类型对应的输运指纹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别Cooper对的配对对称性是理解非传统超导体微观机制的基础步骤，但实验识别极具挑战性，尤其是当非传统配对被主导的s波分量掩盖而无法显现光谱特征时。

Method: 开发了一个对称性解析框架，利用多端器件中交换源极与探测器终端时产生的非互易电导来识别超导配对对称性。非互易输运源于超导序参量的对称性破缺分量，其角度依赖性编码了配对间隙的动量空间结构。

Result: 时间反演破缺的单态配对可诱导非互易电荷输运，而自旋三重态配对则产生非互易自旋响应，为底层序提供不同的输运指纹。该机制以铁基和非中心对称超导体的代表模型予以验证，并给出了多端测量的实验方案。

Conclusion: 该工作推进了对超导体中非互易输运的理论理解，并将其确立为一种对称性选择性探针，可用于在大范围超导材料中识别被隐藏的非传统配对。

Abstract: Identifying the pairing symmetry of Cooper pairs is a fundamental step toward understanding the microscopic mechanisms of unconventional superconductors. However, experimental identification remains a formidable challenge, particularly when unconventional pairing is obscured by a dominant $s$-wave component that masks its spectroscopic signatures. Here, we develop a symmetry-resolved framework to identify superconducting pairing symmetry through nonreciprocal conductance upon exchanging source and detector terminals in multiterminal devices. We show that nonreciprocal transport arises from symmetry-breaking components of the superconducting order parameter and exhibits a characteristic angular dependence that encodes the momentum-space structure of the pairing gap. In particular, time-reversal-breaking singlet pairing induces nonreciprocal charge transport, while spin-triplet pairing generates nonreciprocal spin responses, providing distinct transport fingerprints of the underlying order. We demonstrate this mechanism using representative models of iron-based and noncentrosymmetric superconductors and outline experimental protocols for multiterminal measurements. Our results advance the theoretical understanding of nonreciprocal transport in superconductors, and establish it as a symmetry-selective probe for identifying hidden unconventional pairing in a wide range of superconducting materials.

</details>


### [149] [Superconductivity, pseudogap and marginal Fermi liquid in a relative-momentum-local theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09785)
*Jian-Jian Miao*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文构建了相对动量局域（relative-momentum-local, RML）理论，以t-J模型中反铁磁超交换的自旋单重态Cooper表示为共同线索，将铜氧化物超导体中的d波超导、赝能隙和奇异金属统一为同一Cooper通道的三种红外组织方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铜氧化物超导体中的d波超导、具有费米弧的赝能隙以及具有非相干激发和温度线性电阻率的奇异金属均源于同一掺杂Mott体系，但通常被分开描述，缺乏统一的微观理论框架。

Method: 构建RML理论，利用t-J模型中反铁磁超交换的精确自旋单重态Cooper表示作为统一线索。通过静态RML处方得到精确可解的赝能隙哈密顿量；通过动态RML处方得到具有边缘费米液体标度（marginal-Fermi-liquid scaling）的单圈自能。

Result: 静态RML处方产生具有节点电子极点、反节点能隙、分辨率展宽的费米弧和无隙的电荷-2e Cooper面的赝能隙哈密顿量。Cooper面Lifshitz相变产生比热系数和电荷响应的对数增强而无自旋奇异性。有限质心动量的粒子-粒子连续谱产生Ohm阻尼，动态RML处方得到边缘费米液体自能。

Conclusion: RML理论将单粒子、对敏感与热力学探针关联到同一Cooper通道的三种红外组织方式，并提出零能Cooper面脊线在双电子角分辨光电子能谱（2e-ARPES）中的观测作为决定性实验判据。

Abstract: Cuprate superconductors host \(d\)-wave superconductivity (SC), a pseudogap (PG) with Fermi arcs and a strange metal (SM) with incoherent excitations and temperature-linear resistivity. These regimes emerge from the same doped Mott system but are usually described separately. Here a relative-momentum-local (RML) theory is constructed, with the exact spin-singlet Cooper representation of antiferromagnetic superexchange in the \(t\)-\(J\) model serving as a common thread. Pair correlations organize the low-energy behaviour of this Cooper channel into phase-coherent static SC, phase-incoherent quasi-static PG and phase-incoherent dynamic SM. A static RML prescription yields an exactly solvable PG Hamiltonian with nodal electron poles, antinodal gaps, resolution-broadened Fermi arcs and gapless charge-\(2e\) Cooper surfaces. A Cooper-surface Lifshitz transition generates logarithmic enhancements in the specific-heat coefficient and charge response without a spin singularity. The particle-particle continuum at finite centre-of-mass momentum produces Ohmic damping, while a dynamic RML prescription yields a one-loop self-energy with marginal-Fermi-liquid scaling. Spectral-weight transfer in the physical pair spectrum diagnoses the PG--SM crossover, whereas nonzero phase stiffness and global phase coherence identify SC. The RML theory relates single-particle, pair-sensitive and thermodynamic probes to three infrared organizations of one Cooper channel. A decisive experimental signature would be zero-energy Cooper-surface ridges in two-electron angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (2e-ARPES).

</details>

### [150] [The $q<1$ Random-Cluster Model on Wired Trees: Uniqueness and Negative Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08565)
*Heehyun Park*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究簇权重0<q<1的随机簇模型（random-cluster model）在无限Δ正则树上的有线边界条件（wired boundary condition）行为，证明超临界有线DLR规范具有唯一吉布斯测度（Gibbs measure），即全有线极限，从而完善有线DLR相图；同时证明有线分支间的条件负关联性（CNA+）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 簇权重0<q<1的随机簇模型预期表现出负依赖性（negative dependence），但在一般图（general graphs）上，由于缺乏FKG不等式，甚至连两两负相关（pairwise negative correlation）都是开放问题。本文旨在通过在无限的Δ正则树（regular tree）上研究该模型的有线边界条件，揭示其唯一性、负依赖性与相图结构。

Method: 论文研究无限Δ正则树上带有线边界条件的随机簇模型，定义参数$\widehat p:=p/(p+q(1-p))$和临界值$p_{\mathsf c}:=q/(Δ+q-2)$。利用经典结果（p≤p_c时的乘积有线态和p>p_c时的全有线极限构造），证明超临界有线DLR规范（DLR specification）的唯一吉布斯测度即为此全有线极限。此外，在有限有线树上定义分支连通指示子（branch-connectivity indicators）向量，证明其在外加正场（positive external fields）下满足条件负关联性（conditional negative association, CNA+）。

Result: 有线DLR相图得以完备：p≤p_c时唯一测度为参数$\widehat p$的伯努利键逾渗（Bernoulli bond percolation）；p>p_c时唯 一测度为全有线逾渗极限。在有限有线树上，分支连通指示子向量满足CNA+，因此共享公共顶点的互斥分支集合上有界递增可观测量（bounded increasing observables）的协方差非正。该不等式在唯一无穷体积有线测度中对所有p均成立，且当p≤p_c时取等号。

Conclusion: 本文在无限Δ正则树上完整刻画了随机簇模型（0<q<1）有线DLR的相图，确立了超临界情形下唯一吉布斯测度的存在性，并首次在有线设置下证明了跨分支的负关联性质，为缺乏FKG条件下负相关性的研究提供了重要进展。

Abstract: The random-cluster model with cluster weight $0<q<1$ is expected to exhibit negative dependence, but without FKG even pairwise negative correlation remains open on general graphs. We study the model on the infinite $Δ$-regular tree with wired boundary conditions. Write $\widehat p:=p/(p+q(1-p))$ and $p_{\mathsf c}:=q/(Δ+q-2)$. Classical results identify the product wired state for $p\le p_{\mathsf c}$ and construct a percolative all-wired limit for $p>p_{\mathsf c}$. We prove that the supercritical wired DLR specification has a unique Gibbs measure, namely this all-wired limit. Consequently, the wired DLR phase diagram is complete: the unique measure is Bernoulli bond percolation with parameter $\widehat p$ for $p\le p_{\mathsf c}$, while it percolates for $p>p_{\mathsf c}$.
  We also establish negative dependence across wired branches. On a finite wired tree, the vector of branch-connectivity indicators satisfies conditional negative association under positive external fields (CNA+). Hence bounded increasing observables supported on disjoint collections of branches incident to a common vertex have nonpositive covariance. The same inequality holds in the unique infinite-volume wired measure for every $p$, with equality for $p\le p_{\mathsf c}$.

</details>


### [151] [Overlap distribution of the critical Sherrington-Kirkpatrick model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08752)
*Hang Du,Brice Huang*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究伊辛与球面Sherrington-Kirkpatrick模型在临界逆温度β=1时双副本重叠R_{1,2}的分布，证明其尺度为N^{-1/3}，且淬火分布收敛于由反射Airy_1点过程定义的显式随机概率测度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在回答Talagrand关于N^{2/3}E<R_{1,2}^2>极限值的问题，并理解临界温度下自旋玻璃模型中重叠序参量的涨落行为。

Method: 对球面SK模型，将Gibbs测度表示为R^N上范数为√N的各向异性高斯分布，并在GOE谱边取Airy_1标度极限；对伊辛SK模型，通过球面到立方体比较原理（sphere-to-cube comparison principle）证明两组Gibbs测度下N^{1/3}R_{1,2}的淬火分布渐近一致。

Result: 两类模型中R_{1,2}均具有N^{-1/3}标度，其N^{1/3}倍淬火分布收敛于由反射Airy_1点过程定义的随机测度，并由此得到N^{2/3}E<R_{1,2}^2>的极限值。

Conclusion: 该结果刻画了临界温度时SK模型重叠涨落的普适极限，作为作者先前关于SK自由能涨落工作（du2026fluctuations）的配套研究，展示了比较原理的进一步推论。

Abstract: We study the distribution of the two-replica overlap $R_{1,2}$ in the Ising and spherical Sherrington-Kirkpatrick models at the critical inverse temperature $β= 1$. Our main result shows that in both models, $R_{1,2}$ has scale $N^{-1/3}$, and the quenched distribution of $N^{1/3} R_{1,2}$ converges to an explicit random probability measure defined in terms of the reflected $\mathrm{Airy}_1$ point process. As a consequence, we characterize the limiting value of $N^{2/3} \mathbb{E} \langle R_{1,2}^2 \rangle$, answering a question of Talagrand \cite{talagrand2011mean2}.
  For the spherical SK model, we obtain the limit by representing the Gibbs measure as an anisotropic Gaussian on $\mathbb{R}^N$ conditioned to have norm $\sqrt{N}$, and then passing to the $\mathrm{Airy}_1$ scaling limit at the GOE spectral edge. For the Ising SK model, the proof is based on a sphere-to-cube comparison principle showing that the quenched distributions of $N^{1/3} R_{1,2}$ under the spherical and Ising Gibbs measures asymptotically coincide.
  This paper is a companion to \cite{du2026fluctuations}, where we introduced a related comparison principle to identify the limiting fluctuations of the SK free energy. Most of the arguments in this paper were generated using GPT-5.6 Pro, with the aim of exploring further consequences of the ideas developed in that work.

</details>


### [152] [Rooted Gibbs-DLR Measures in Planar Directed Polymers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09881)
*Christopher Janjigian,Firas Rassoul-Agha,Timo Seppäläinen*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究$\mathbb{Z}^2$上有向聚合物模型中的有根Gibbs-DLR测度，在满足温和假设的遍历随机无序分布下，证明了极值有根Gibbs-DLR测度集合是闭的且全序的，并据此建立了Busemann过程的强存在性与强唯一性，以及$L^1$连续性定理，并推导出相关的概率收敛和大偏差原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解随机有向聚合物模型中Gibbs测度的结构与其对应的Busemann过程之间的关系，是随机介质中界面与测地线的核心理论问题。该研究旨在为正温度和零温度情形下极值Gibbs测度、路径汇聚性质与大偏差原理提供统一的数学框架。

Method: 论文首先证明了极值有根Gibbs-DLR测度集合的闭性和全序性，并将极值性特征刻画为路径汇聚（coalescence）。在此基础上，证明了每个全支撑的极值有根Gibbs测度可规范性生成一个由全体格点索引的、全局一致且汇聚的极值Gibbs测度族；随后利用该结构建立Busemann过程的强存在性与强唯一性，并通过换移协变（shift-covariant）的Busemann余循环（cocycle）推导逆温度、倾斜参数与随机环境联合的$L^1$连续性定理。

Result: 证明了方向可微性条件下的生成极值Gibbs测度的依概率连续性，以及在正温度下生成极值Gibbs测度的依概率收敛性；在零温度下建立了对应的正温度有根Gibbs-DLR测度在路径空间上的淬火（quenched）子序列大偏差原理，其速率函数由零温度Busemann余循环决定，并在该余循环生成的无穷测地线上精确为零。

Conclusion: 该工作将极值Gibbs测度的结构性质与Busemann过程的连续性统一起来，为正负温度下随机聚合物模型的极限行为提供了完整的理论刻画，揭示了路径汇聚、极值测度与无穷测地线之间的深层联系。

Abstract: We study rooted Gibbs-DLR measures in the directed polymer model on $\mathbb{Z}^2$ with an ergodic disorder distribution which satisfies an additional mild hypothesis. We prove that the set of extremal rooted Gibbs-DLR measures is closed and totally ordered, characterize extremality in terms of path coalescence, and show that each fully supported extremal rooted Gibbs measure canonically generates a globally consistent and coalescing family of extremal rooted Gibbs measures indexed by all lattice sites. These families are, moreover, totally ordered.
  Building on this structure, we prove strong existence and strong uniqueness of the associated Busemann process, together with an $L^1$ continuity theorem for the shift-covariant Busemann cocycles which is joint in the inverse temperature, the tilt parameter, and the random environment. This yields, as a corollary, in-probability continuity of the generated extremal Gibbs measures corresponding to directions of differentiability under bounded i.i.d. perturbations of the weights. In positive temperature, it shows in-probability convergence of the generated extremal Gibbs measures. At zero temperature, it also yields quenched subsequential large deviation principles for the corresponding positive-temperature rooted Gibbs-DLR measures on path space. The rate functions are determined by a zero-temperature Busemann cocycle and vanish precisely on the infinite geodesics generated by that cocycle.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

### [153] [Modular resurgent structures for vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08902)
*Miranda C. N. Cheng,Ioana Coman,Veronica Fantini,Claudia Rella*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在前期工作的基础上引入向量值模振兴级数（vector-valued modular resurgent series），其分量在Borel平面中具有单一无穷奇异点塔、平凡的次级振兴级数，而其Stokes常数由一组L-函数系数的线性组合给出。作者将模振兴（modular resurgence）范式推广到向量值情形，并证明模群权1/2和3/2向量值模形式的Eichler积分可在该框架下研究，其中一致theta级数（unary theta series）对应的Eichler积分即量子拓扑中的伪theta函数（false theta functions）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的模振兴研究主要限于标量情形，而许多量子拓扑和数论中自然出现的对象本身是向量值的，需要将模振兴范式扩展到向量值框架，以统一描述更多实例并揭示Stokes常数与L-函数之间的深层联系。

Method: 作者构造向量值模振兴级数，其分量共享单一Borel平面奇异点塔，Stokes常数取为向量L-函数系数的线性组合；通过分析伴随q-级数（q-series）向量与Dirichlet级数向量的相互作用，描述规范成对向量值模振兴级数之间的对称性；并将框架应用于q-Pochhammer符号向量及权1/2和3/2的向量值模形式Eichler积分。

Result: 证明q-Pochhammer符号向量情形等价于标量模振兴的重新封装，而权1/2和3/2向量值模形式的Eichler积分涉及模群的非平凡表示，必须使用向量值框架；并一般地建立了后者的模振兴结构，对一致theta级数给出了完整细节，其Eichler积分即量子拓扑中的伪theta函数。此外提出了相关推测：某些具有模振兴渐近的q-级数向量是向量值量子模形式，可通过中值重求和（median resummation）重构。

Conclusion: 向量值模振兴框架能够统一处理标量情形和涉及模群非平凡表示的情形，展示了Stokes常数与向量L-函数系数之间的线性关系，并揭示了向量值q-级数与Dirichlet级数之间的对称性及与量子模形式的潜在联系。

Abstract: Building on prior results [1], we introduce vector-valued modular resurgent series, whose components exhibit a single infinite tower of singularities in the Borel plane, trivial secondary resurgent series, and Stokes constants given by linear combinations of the coefficients of a vector of $L$-functions. We extend the paradigm of modular resurgence to this setting, emphasizing the role of the Stokes constants and the interplay between the associated vectors of $q$-series and Dirichlet series, and describing the resulting symmetry relating canonical pairs of vector-valued modular resurgent series. Moreover, we conjecture that certain vectors of $q$-series with modular resurgent asymptotics are vector-valued quantum modular forms and can be reconstructed via median resummation. Finally, we show that vectors of $q$-Pochhammer symbols, previously considered in [2], and Eichler integrals of vector-valued modular forms of weight $1/2$ and $3/2$ can be studied within the framework of vector-valued modular resurgence. While the first case amounts to a convenient repackaging of scalar modular resurgence, the second involves a non-trivial representation of the modular group and therefore illustrates the necessity of the vector-valued framework; we establish its modular resurgent structure in general, and work it out in full detail for the unary theta series, whose Eichler integrals are the false theta functions of quantum topology.

</details>

### [154] [Infrared Spectroscopy and Photochemistry of Aromatic Nitriles in Para-Hydrogen Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09889)
*Sam McGrath,Vincent J. Esposito,Linshan Zeng,Thomas H. Speak,Brendan Moore,Pavle Djuricanin,Jun Miyazaki,Takamasa Momose,Ilsa R. Cooke*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 本文报告了在固态对氢（para-H2）基质中对单氰基和二氰基取代苯的振动光谱与光化学实验及理论研究，发现其主要光解产物为HCN和HNC，主解离通道涉及苯环与腈基之间C–C键的断裂，产生苯基（或氰苯基）自由基加CN，并观察到了与苯光解类似的过程光产物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来在金牛座分子云-1中探测到了多种芳香族腈类化合物，需要对这类分子的光谱特征及光化学行为进行表征，以帮助解释JWST对光解离区域芳香族物质的红外观测。

Method: 将氰基苯（benzonitrile）和三种二氰基苯异构体及氘代氰基苯（d5-cyanobenzene）嵌入固态对氢基质中，在193 nm激发下开展光解实验，并辅以振动光谱的理论计算以辅助谱峰归属。

Result: 所有腈类化合物光解的主要产物均为HCN和HNC，推测由CN自由基从para-H2中夺取氢原子而产生；主要光解离通道为苯环与腈基间C–C键的断裂，生成苯基（或氰苯基）自由基加CN；此外还观察到与苯光解中类似的过程光产物。

Conclusion: 本文研究结果可为解释JWST对光解离区域中芳香族化合物的中红外观测提供参考依据。

Abstract: Motivated by recent detections of several aromatic nitriles in Taurus Molecular Cloud-1, we report laboratory and theoretical investigations of the vibrational spectroscopy and photochemistry of singly and doubly cyano-substituted benzene in solid para-hydrogen matrices. We compare the photochemistry of cyanobenzene (benzonitrile) and three dicyanobenzene isomers initiated by excitations at 193 nm. In addition, we report the photochemistry of deuterated cyanobenzene (d$_5$-cyanobenzene), enabling us to determine the major products produced during the cyanobenzene photodissociation. The major products observed in the photolysis of all the nitriles are HCN and HNC, which are likely produced by hydrogen abstraction from para-H$_2$ by the CN radical. This indicates that the major photodissociation channel involves cleavage of the bond between the ring and the nitrile group, forming the phenyl (or cyanophenyl) radical + CN. We observe secondary photoproducts similar to those found during benzene photolysis. Our findings may aid the interpretation of recent JWST mid-infrared observations of aromatics in photodissociation regions.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mtrl-sci'></div>

# cond-mat.mtrl-sci [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [Defect-Controlled Multiferroicity via Stacking Control in Nonmagnetic van der Waals Bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07666)
*Bumseop Kim,Sayed Ali Akbar Ghorashi,Andrew M. Rappe*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将空位局域磁性（vacancy-localized magnetism）与滑移铁电性（sliding ferroelectricity）直接耦合的通用范式，以双层六方氮化硼（hBN）为原型体系，通过第一性原理计算揭示了空位作为局域登记传感器如何打破极性滑移伙伴的简并，并展示了衬底格位决定层间交换作用的缺陷选择性，从而实现对多铁功能（multiferroic functionality）的亚晶格依赖调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非磁性范德华（van der Waals）双层体系中，如何将局部磁性缺陷与滑移铁电极化机制耦合仍是一个未解决的关键问题，作者试图建立一种通用的设计范式来实现这一耦合。

Method: 以双层六方氮化硼（bilayer hexagonal boron nitride）作为原型范德华宿主体系，采用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）研究单空位及层间空位对的磁性和极化行为，并通过外加面外电场（out-of-plane electric field）调控极性登记态。

Result: 单空位可作为局域登记传感器（local registry sensor），通过缺陷中心的极化偏移消除极性滑移伙伴之间的简并性；对于层间空位对，宿主亚晶格完全决定层间交换作用，可稳定亚铁磁（ferrimagnetic）或反铁磁（antiferromagnetic）构型；面外电场可选择极性登记并驱动补偿性Néel序参量的幅度调制。

Conclusion: 该研究为通过堆垛控制（stacking control）在广泛非磁性二维异质结构中实现稳健的、亚晶格依赖的多铁功能工程提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We present a general paradigm that directly couples vacancy-localized magnetism to interfacial sliding ferroelectricity in nonmagnetic van der Waals (vdWs) bilayers. Utilizing bilayer hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) as a prototypical vdWs host system, we use first-principles calculations to show that a single vacancy acts as a local registry sensor, lifting the degeneracy between polar sliding partners via a defect-centered polarization offset. For interlayer vacancy pairs, we discover a defect selectivity where the hosting sublattice fully dictates the interlayer exchange, stabilizing either ferrimagnetic or antiferromagnetic configurations. Applying an out-of-plane electric field selects the polar registry and drives an amplitude modulation of the compensated Néel order parameter. These findings establish a robust, sublattice-dependent engineering of multiferroic functionality via stacking control across a wide class of nonmagnetic 2D heterostructures.

</details>


### [156] [Understanding the Oxygen Reduction Reaction and Oxygen Evolution Reaction in Metal Intercalated Biphenylene Bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07782)
*Henri G. Mendonça,Pedro H. Souza,Walter Orellana,Roberto H. Miwa*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文基于第一性原理（ab initio）计算研究了金属包覆联苯撑双层（biphenylene bilayer）B/M/B（M = Ti, V, Cr, Mn, Fe, Co, Ni, Cu, Nb, Ru, W, Os, Pt）的氧还原反应（ORR）与氧析出反应（OER）催化性能，发现Cu、Pt、Ru和Mn具有最低的ORR过电位，Fe为最佳OER催化剂，并揭示了金属d轨道电荷布居作为最有效的催化活性电子描述符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碳基材料作为非贵金属电催化剂替代品受到广泛关注，但联苯撑双层这类新型碳同素异形体的催化性能尚不清楚。本研究旨在通过内嵌金属原子调控碳框架的电子结构，系统探索金属包覆联苯撑双层在氧还原与氧析出反应中的催化潜力，并建立简单电子描述符以指导高效催化剂筛选。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算对13种不同金属内嵌的B/M/B体系进行系统研究，所有体系中金属大多占据联苯撑晶格的方形碳位（C^468）。利用计算氢电极（computational hydrogen electrode）方法评估各活性位点的反应能量学，并通过金属d带中心（d-band center）、C^468的p_z带中心及相应轨道电荷布居来分析电子结构与催化活性的关联。

Result: Cu、Pt、Ru和Mn分别以0.42、0.44、0.50和0.56 V的过电位表现出最低的ORR活性，Fe以0.44 V过电位成为最活跃的OER催化剂。带中心与过电位之间不存在简单的多项式依赖关系，但能指示最优催化剂所处的有利电子区间；金属d轨道电荷布居则与活性关联最为清晰，尤其是对OER，可形成火山型（volcano-type）曲线。

Conclusion: B/Fe/B被确定为最佳OER催化剂，B/Mn/B最接近ORR最优值；活性碳位点的p轨道布居也能捕捉主要趋势但程度较弱。结果表明，直接可用的电子描述符能够预测金属包覆联苯撑双层中的催化行为，为以碳框架本身驱动反应性的高效催化剂搜索提供了指导。

Abstract: We conducted an {\it ab initio} study of the oxygen reduction reaction (ORR) and oxygen evolution reaction (OER) in metal-encapsulated biphenylene bilayers, B/M/B, with M = Ti, V, Cr, Mn, Fe, Co, Ni, Cu, Nb, Ru, W, Os and Pt. In most systems, the intercalated metal sits at the square carbon sites (C$^{468}$) of the biphenylene lattice. Using a computational hydrogen electrode approach, we evaluated the reaction energetics at these active sites. Several B/M/B systems show competitive ORR and OER performance. Among the investigated systems, Cu, Pt, Ru, and Mn exhibit the lowest ORR overpotentials of 0.42, 0.44, 0.50, and 0.56 V, respectively, while Fe is identified as the most active catalyst for OER with an overpotential of 0.44 V. To understand the catalytic trends, we looked at the electronic structure through the metal $d-$band centers, the C$^{468}$ $p_z-$band centers, and the corresponding orbital charge populations. The band centers did not give a simple polynomial dependence on the overpotentials, though they did point to favorable electronic ranges for the best catalysts. The $d-$orbital charge population of the encapsulated metal, however, correlated most clearly with activity-especially for OER-yielding volcano-type plots. From these, B/Fe/B emerges as the best OER catalyst, while B/Mn/B lies closest to the ORR optimum. The $p-$orbital population at the active carbon site also captures the main trends, albeit less strongly. Overall, these results show that straightforward electronic descriptors can predict catalytic behavior in metal-encapsulated biphenylene bilayers and guide the search for efficient catalysts where the carbon framework itself drives the reactivity.

</details>


### [157] [Sn-Doping in LPCVD-Grown (010) $β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07823)
*Saleh Ahmed Khan,Ahmed Ibreljic,Sourav Sarker,Anhar Bhuiyan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过低压化学气相沉积（LPCVD）生长了Sn掺杂(010) β-Ga₂O₃同质外延薄膜，系统研究了Sn掺入对结构、形貌和电学性质的影响。实现了1.17×10¹⁷至3.06×10¹⁸ cm⁻³的可控室温载流子浓度，并获得了LPCVD生长的Sn掺杂β-Ga₂O₃迄今最高的室温及低温霍尔迁移率（113和380 cm² V⁻¹ s⁻¹），证明LPCVD能够同时实现可控Sn掺杂和优异的结构与电学质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: β-Ga₂O₃作为宽禁带半导体在功率电子器件中具有重要应用前景，但需要实现可控n型掺杂（如Sn掺杂）并生长厚外延漂移层（drift layer），以满足高压器件的需求。本研究旨在系统评估LPCVD生长Sn掺杂β-Ga₂O₃同质外延薄膜的可行性及其结构、形貌和电学性质。

Method: 采用低压化学气相沉积（low-pressure chemical vapor deposition, LPCVD）在(010)β-Ga₂O₃衬底上生长Sn掺杂同质外延薄膜，并系统研究Sn掺入对薄膜结构、形貌和电学性质的影响。通过霍尔测量表征载流子浓度与迁移率，利用摇摆曲线半高宽和均方根粗糙度评估晶体质量，并通过输运模型解析施主能级与受主补偿。

Result: 实现了1.17×10¹⁷至3.06×10¹⁸ cm⁻³的室温载流子浓度可控调节，霍尔迁移率从113降至63 cm² V⁻¹ s⁻¹。薄膜呈单斜β-Ga₂O₃相，近化学计量比，阶梯流形貌，最小摇摆曲线FWHM为68.4角秒，RMS粗糙度为2.63 nm。膜厚为1.66至11.3 μm，生长速率为6.4至16.6 μm h⁻¹。最低载流子浓度样品在室温和84 K时分别获得113和380 cm² V⁻¹ s⁻¹的迁移率，均为LPCVD生长Sn掺杂β-Ga₂O₃的最高报道值。输运模型揭示浅施主激活能为32.7 meV，深施主能级为95 meV，受主补偿浓度为2.0×10¹⁶ cm⁻³。

Conclusion: LPCVD能够实现Sn的可控掺杂，同时保持优异的结构和电学质量，为制备厚β-Ga₂O₃外延漂移层提供了可行途径。高效施主激活和低度补偿表明该方法在功率器件应用中具有潜力。

Abstract: In this work, Sn-doped (010) $β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ homoepitaxial films were grown by low-pressure chemical vapor deposition (LPCVD), and the influence of Sn incorporation on their structural, morphological, and electrical properties was systematically investigated. Controlled room-temperature carrier concentrations ranging from $1.17 \times 10^{17}$ to $3.06 \times 10^{18}$ cm$^{-3}$ were achieved, with corresponding Hall mobilities decreasing from 113 to 63 cm$^2$ V$^{-1}$ s$^{-1}$. The films exhibited the monoclinic $β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ phase, near-stoichiometric composition, and well-defined step-flow morphology, with a minimum rocking-curve FWHM of 68.4 arcsec and an RMS roughness of 2.63 nm. Film thicknesses ranging from 1.66 to 11.3 $μ$m were obtained at growth rates of 6.4 to 16.6 $μ$m h$^{-1}$, demonstrating the ability of LPCVD to produce thick epitaxial layers. The sample with a room-temperature carrier concentration of $1.17 \times 10^{17}$ cm$^{-3}$ exhibited room-temperature and low-temperature Hall mobilities of 113 cm$^2$ V$^{-1}$ s$^{-1}$ and 380 cm$^2$ V$^{-1}$ s$^{-1}$ at 84 K, respectively. Both represent the highest reported values for LPCVD-grown Sn-doped $β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$. Transport modeling of the same sample yielded a shallow donor activation energy of 32.7 meV, a deeper donor level at 95 meV, and a low compensating acceptor concentration of $2.0 \times 10^{16}$ cm$^{-3}$, indicating efficient donor activation and a low degree of compensation. These results demonstrate that LPCVD enables controlled Sn doping while maintaining excellent structural and electrical quality, providing a viable route for realizing thick $β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ epitaxial drift layers.

</details>


### [158] [Spin selective non-van der Waal electride nature in manganese under ambient pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07856)
*Shishir Timilsena,Dinesh Thapa,David James Faller,Prabesh Adhikari,Nicholas Dimakis,Svetlana Kilina*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过第一性原理量子力学计算，在常压下系统研究了元素锰（Mn）三种晶相（α-Mn、β-Mn、hex-Mn）的结构、电子、磁性与电化物（electride）特性，首次确认元素Mn为具有自旋选择性电化物特征的非范德华电化物体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电化物是一类非寻常的离子材料，其中电子在晶格的非核间隙区域中充当阴离子。探索元素态材料是否也能表现出电化物行为，对于拓展电化物概念和开发非常规电子与磁性功能具有重要意义。

Method: 采用第一性原理量子力学计算，对常压（0 GPa）下元素Mn的三种晶相（立方α-Mn、立方β-Mn、六方hex-Mn）进行结构、电子、磁性和电化物特性的系统研究；利用自旋选择电子局域化函数（spin-selective ELF）识别间隙电子；通过Bader电荷分析定量评估从Mn主框架向间隙阴离子电子（IAE）区域的电荷转移；并结合电子态密度（DOS）分析IAE相关态在费米能级（E_F）附近的贡献。

Result: 三种Mn晶相均表现出显著的间隙电子特征，伴随自旋选择性ELF。Bader电荷分析显示有效电荷转移量约为α-Mn的-1.645e、β-Mn的-1.477e、hex-Mn的-1.083e每间隙盆地。电子态密度显示IAE相关态在费米能级附近有有限贡献，并与Mn衍生态共存，证明其直接参与低能电子结构。ELF、有效电荷转移和E_F处IAE电子布居三个方面的证据一致支持元素Mn中存在间隙阴离子电子。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在常压下系统识别了元素Mn中的自旋选择性电化物特征，将元素Mn确立为非范德华电化物体系，并凸显了利用其间隙电子态实现非常规电子与磁性功能的可能性。

Abstract: Electrides are an unusual class of ionic materials in which electrons localized in non-nuclear, interstitial regions act as anions within the crystal lattice. Here, we employ first-principles quantum mechanical calculations to investigate the structural, electronic, magnetic, and electride characteristics of elemental manganese (Mn) at an ambient pressure (0 GPa), focusing on its three crystalline phases: cubic ($α$)-Mn ($I\bar{4}3m,no.217$), cubic ($β$)-Mn ($P4_132, no.213$), and hexagonal ($hex$)-Mn ($P6_3/mmc, no.194$). Our calculations reveal pronounced interstitial-electron character in all three phases, accompanied by spin-selective electron localization function (ELF), establishing elemental Mn as a non-van der Waals electride system. Bader charge analysis indicates substantial electron redistribution from the Mn host framework toward the interstitial anionic-electron (IAE) regions, with an effective charge transfer of approximately $-1.645e$, $-1.477e$, and $-1.083e$ per interstitial basin in $α$-Mn, $β$-Mn, and $hex$-Mn, respectively. The electride character is further supported by the electronic density of states, where the IAE-associated states exhibit finite contributions near the Fermi level ($E_F$) and coexist with Mn-derived states, demonstrating their direct participation in the low-energy electronic structure. The combined electron localization function (ELF), effective charge transfer, and electron population due to IAE at $E_F$ therefore provide consistent evidence for interstitial anionic electrons in elemental Mn. To the best of our knowledge, this work provides the first systematic identification of spin-selective electride character in elemental Mn at ambient pressure, highlighting the possibility of exploiting its interstitial-electron states for unconventional electronic and magnetic functionalities.

</details>


### [159] [Metastability limit of pristine 2D noble metals with high-energy facet: dominance of the Bell-Evans-Polanyi principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07884)
*Xiaoliang Zhong*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用密度泛函理论（DFT）预测超薄Rh、Pd、Ag和Ir的(110)片层会通过晶格收缩自发转变为(100)片层，并揭示该转变过程遵循Bell-Evans-Polanyi（BEP）原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超薄金属片层的结构稳定性对纳米材料工程具有重要意义，理解(110)与(100)取向片层之间的转变机制有助于指导金属纳米结构的可控合成与应用设计。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（density functional theory）计算方法，对Rh、Pd、Ag、Ir四种金属的超薄(110)片层进行结构优化与能量计算，分析(110)与(100)片层之间的转变势垒及其与能量差的关系。

Result: 发现(110)与(100)片层间的转变势垒与能量差呈近似线性关系，符合BEP原理；该原理同时成功描述了各金属的临界厚度，当低于临界厚度时(110)片层不再亚稳，发生自发结构转变。

Conclusion: 超薄金属(110)片层向(100)片层的结构转变由BEP原理主导控制，这一普适关系可用于预测金属纳米片层在不同厚度下的结构稳定性。

Abstract: Using density functional theory, we predict that ultrathin (110) sheets of Rh, Pd, Ag, and Ir tend to transform into their (100) counterparts via lattice contraction. An approximately linear relationship between the transformation barrier and the energy difference between (110) and (100) sheets is revealed, demonstrating that the Bell-Evans-Polanyi (BEP) principle dominates. Furthermore, the critical thicknesses for these metals are also described by the BEP principle: below these thicknesses, the (110) sheets are no longer metastable and undergo spontaneous structural transformation.

</details>


### [160] [Gate-tunable electronic properties of epitaxial Bi (111) films using a printable hexagonal boron nitride ionogel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07900)
*Jagannath Jena,Heather E. Kurtz,Siddhesh Ambhire,Justin S. Wood,Fateme Mahdikhany,Junyi Yang,Eugene Ark,Vinod K. Sangwan,J. Samuel Jiang,Steven S. -L. Zhang,Mark C. Hersam,Anand Bhattacharya*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究利用可打印六方氮化硼离子凝胶（ionogel）栅极，在生长于GaAs (111)衬底上的外延Bi (111)薄膜中实现了低电压顶栅静电调控。磁输运测量揭示了非简谐霍尔电导率与电子-空穴补偿点的系统性演化，且该响应无法用刚性带（rigid band）的费米能级移动解释，而与非刚性带效应及栅压可调的Rashba自旋轨道耦合相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在半金属（semimetal）中实现有效的载流子输运静电调控因强屏蔽效应和多带效应而困难重重。

Method: 采用可打印六方氮化硼离子凝胶栅极，在GaAs (111)衬底上生长的外延Bi (111)薄膜上进行低电压顶栅调控，并通过磁输运测量分析霍尔电导率行为。

Result: 施加幅值小于0.4 V的小栅极电压即可系统性地改变低场霍尔电导率斜率和电子-空穴补偿点；该响应随薄膜厚度和温度变化。已观察到的行为无法用刚性带模型下的费米能级移动解释，表明存在与非刚性带响应和多带效应相关的机制，以及栅压可调的Rashba自旋轨道耦合（Rashba spin orbit coupling）。

Conclusion: 可打印离子凝胶栅极技术为调控拓扑半金属（topological semimetals）中的多带输运提供了一种有力手段。

Abstract: Achieving effective electrostatic control of carrier transport in semimetals remains challenging due to strong screening and multiband effects. We report efficient low voltage top gated control of electronic transport in epitaxial Bi (111) thin films grown on GaAs (111) substrates using a printable hexagonal boron nitride ionogel. Magnetotransport measurements reveal pronounced nonlinear Hall conductivities arising from multiband electron and hole contributions. Remarkably, the application of a small gate voltage (less than 0.4 V in magnitude) leads to a systematic evolution of the low field Hall conductivity slope and the electron-hole compensation point. The response to gate voltage depends upon thickness and temperature. The observed behavior cannot be explained by a conventional Fermi level shift with rigid bands and instead indicates a non rigid band response associated with multiband effects and a gat tunable Rashba spin orbit coupling. Our results establish printable ionogel gating as a powerful approach to tune multiband transport in topological semimetals.

</details>


### [161] [Predicting large-supercell defect formation energies from machine-learning charge density models trained on small supercells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07997)
*Junjie Zhou,Menglin Huang,Shiyou Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于机器学习电荷密度（MLCD）的缺陷形成能预测方法，通过混合不同尺寸的超胞进行训练，仅用96个含16–96个原子的超胞数据即可准确预测360原子超胞中四种本征缺陷的形成能，缺陷级平均绝对误差低于0.05 eV，相比等数据量的机器学习原子间势（MLIP）预测性能显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一性原理缺陷计算常受限于大超胞抑制镜像相互作用带来的高成本；机器学习原子间势虽为替代方案，但训练通常需要数千个结构和数周数据生成周期。作者希望寻找数据效率更高的缺陷形成能预测途径。

Method: 提出机器学习电荷密度（MLCD）路线，利用电荷密度是密度泛函理论（DFT）核心变量这一特性进行缺陷形成能预测；通过整合不同尺寸的小超胞并基于空间电荷密度分析分配其比例来优化训练集，以实现更好的跨尺寸外推（extrapolation）。

Result: 仅使用96个含16–96个原子的超胞作为训练数据，MLCD准确预测了360原子超胞中四种本征缺陷的形成能，缺陷级平均绝对误差低于0.05 eV；相同数据集训练的MLIP误差超过1 eV，显示了电荷密度学习在跨尺寸迁移上的显著优势。

Conclusion: 电荷密度学习比直接的能量-力拟合具有更强的跨尺寸迁移鲁棒性；混合尺寸数据设计可显著降低缺陷预测的成本。

Abstract: First-principles defect calculations are often limited by the cost of the large supercells required to suppress image interactions. Machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) provide another alternative, but training defect MLIPs typically requires thousands of structures and weeks of data generation. Since charge density is the key to density-functional-theory (DFT), we propose a machine-learning charge density (MLCD) route for predicting defect formation energies with higher data efficiency. We optimize the training set by integrating small supercells of varying sizes for better extrapolation, allocating their proportions based on spatial charge-density analysis. With only 96 supercells containing 16--96 atoms as the dataset, MLCD accurately predicts the formation energies of four intrinsic defects in 360-atom supercells, with defect-wise mean absolute error below 0.05 eV. In contrast, MLIPs trained on the same dataset can err by more than 1 eV. These results show that charge-density learning enables more robust cross-size transfer than direct energy-force fitting and that mixed-size data design can substantially reduce the cost of defect prediction.

</details>


### [162] [Local coordination and migration-network topology shape Li-ion transport and delithiation in the low-energy $\varepsilon$-LiMnO$_2$ polymorph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08000)
*Fukuan Wang,Busheng Wang,Yong Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文以低温稳定的ε-LiMnO2多晶型为模型，通过键价位能（bond-valence site-energy）和键价路径分析结合第一性原理计算研究发现，尽管ε相与锂化尖晶石Li2Mn2O4具有相同的四面体类型分数，但两者的0-过渡金属（0-TM）基元分别形成准一维链与三维网络，导致ε相表现出更低的锂迁移能垒（0.35–0.36 eV）及沿c轴的准一维扩散，且基元连通性的差异还影响脱锂过程中的电压响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的岩盐衍生物氧化物正极研究中，锂迁移环境通常仅按O4四面体中间态周围共享面的过渡金属（TM）邻居数目分类，如LiMnO2中的0-TM状态，但这一分类可能忽略了更高壳层配位和四面体连通性对迁移与脱锂行为的影响，亟需更系统的考量。

Method: 以最近报道的低能ε-LiMnO2多晶型为模型体系，采用键价位能和键价路径（bond-valence pathway）分析，结合第一性原理计算、爬坡弹性带（climbing-image nudged elastic band, CI-NEB）方法、从头算分子动力学（ab initio molecular dynamics, AIMD）以及脱锂计算，系统考察四种LiMnO2多晶型中0-TM基元的拓扑结构与空间连通性。

Result: 迁移图谱和四面体统计显示四种LiMnO2多晶型具有不同的拓扑结构与维度；ε相与尖晶石相虽然四面体类型分数一致，但0-TM基元分别形成准一维链和三维网络。CI-NEB计算显示ε相能垒为0.35–0.36 eV，低于尖晶石相的0.41–0.53 eV，差异可能与不同的次近邻角共享壳层有关；AIMD给出表观活化能0.32 eV，方向分辨的均方位移显示锂沿c轴优先迁移。脱锂计算表明0-TM连通性与Li–Li间距差异与锂位演化及电压台阶相关。

Conclusion: 0-TM基元的局部环境与空间连通性深刻影响LiMnO2中的锂迁移和脱锂行为，这为设计亚稳正极材料结构提供了新的思路与指导。

Abstract: In rocksalt-derived oxide cathodes, the local Li-migration environment around an O4 tetrahedral intermediate is commonly classified by the number of face-sharing transition-metal (TM) neighbors. In LiMnO2, the TM species is Mn, and 0-TM denotes the absence of face-sharing Mn neighbors. However, migration and delithiation may also depend on higher-shell coordination and tetrahedral connectivity. Using the recently reported low-energy epsilon-LiMnO2 polymorph as a model, we examine these factors through bond-valence site-energy and bond-valence pathway analyses combined with first-principles calculations. The resulting migration maps and tetrahedral statistics reveal distinct topologies and dimensionalities across four LiMnO2 polymorphs. Although the epsilon phase and the lithiated-spinel phase Li2Mn2O4 (hereafter spinel) have identical tetrahedral-type fractions, their 0-TM motifs form quasi-one-dimensional chains and a three-dimensional network, respectively. Climbing-image nudged elastic band calculations further distinguish the two structures: epsilon-phase barriers are 0.35-0.36 eV, compared with 0.41-0.53 eV in spinel, a difference that may be associated with distinct next-nearest corner-sharing shells. Ab initio molecular dynamics yields an apparent activation energy of 0.32 eV, while direction-resolved mean-squared displacements show preferential Li migration along c, supporting low-barrier quasi-one-dimensional diffusion. Delithiation calculations show that differences in 0-TM connectivity and Li-Li separation between the epsilon phase and spinel are associated with Li-site evolution and calculated voltage steps, suggesting that motif connectivity may influence voltage response. These results link the local environments and spatial connectivity of 0-TM motifs to Li migration and delithiation, providing insights into the design of metastable cathode structures.

</details>


### [163] [Multimodal deep learning framework to predict strain localization of Mg/LPSO two-phase alloys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08071)
*Daiki Kuriki,Fabien Briffod,Takayuki Shiraiwa,Manabu Enoki*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种从三维微观结构图像预测铸态Mg/LPSO两相合金压缩变形下三维局部应变分布的方法。该方法综合提取相体积分数、持久同调图和两相空间相关函数三类微观结构描述符（microstructure descriptor），并采用多模态深度学习（multimodal deep learning）进行预测，精度优于单一描述符。特征重要性分析表明，与加载方向呈45°取向的细长LPSO硬相区域更易产生高应变，与已有研究结论一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mg/LPSO两相合金的局部应变分布与其微观结构特征密切相关，但传统方法难以从三维微观结构图像中准确预测局部变形行为。建立微观结构与变形行为之间的定量关联，对于理解材料力学性能具有重要意义。

Method: 首先通过压缩试验前后的X射线CT图像，应用数字体相关法（digital volume correlation method）获取三维局部应变分布。从各应变测量点周围的三维微观结构图像中提取三类描述符：相体积分数、表征相连通性的持久图（persistent diagram）以及表征相空间分布的两相空间相关函数。由于描述符包含数值数据和图像数据两类，采用多模态深度学习模型进行应变预测，并通过相关性分析和遮挡敏感性分析（occlusion sensitivity analysis）评估各描述符的特征重要性。

Result: 多描述符融合的预测精度显著高于单一描述符。特征重要性分析发现，高应变倾向于出现在LPSO硬相呈细长形且取向与加载方向成45°的区域，该结论与已有研究结果一致。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能有效阐明微观结构与材料变形行为之间的关联，为两相合金变形机制的定量研究提供了可行途径。

Abstract: This study proposes a method for predicting three-dimensional (3D) local strain distribution under compressive deformation of as-cast Mg/LPSO two-phase alloys from 3D microstructure images. The 3D local strain distribution was obtained by applying the digital volume correlation method to X-ray CT images before and after compression tests. Three microstructure descriptors were extracted from the 3D microstructure images around each strain measurement point: volume fractions of the phases, persistent diagrams that can express the connectivity of the phases, and two-phase spatial correlation that can express the spatial distribution of the phases. A deep learning model was then constructed to predict local strain from the three microstructure descriptors. Since two types of descriptors were used in this study, numerical data and image data, multimodal deep learning was employed to make predictions. Thus, the use of multiple microstructure descriptors enabled predictions to be made with higher accuracy than when predictions were made from a single descriptor. Feature importance of the descriptors was assessed through correlation analysis and occlusion sensitivity analysis. The results revealed that high strain tended to occur in the region where the hard phase, LPSO phase, had a large elongated phase oriented at a 45° direction to the loading direction. This result is consistent with other previous studies and indicates that the proposed method is effective in elucidating the relationship between the microstructure and the deformation behavior of the material.

</details>


### [164] [Machine Learning Framework for Magnetic Candidate Discovery in Cerium-Based Compounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08088)
*Joshua A. Torres,Yaser M. Banad,Benjamin O. Tayo,Tej Nath Lamichhane*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个物理引导的计算框架，结合结构筛选、随机森林（Random Forest）分类器、伊辛模型（Ising model）蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）模拟和自编码器（autoencoder）深度学习，用于从已知铈（Ce）基晶体结构中筛选潜在的单轴各向异性铁磁体（Ising ferromagnet），为高性能磁性材料和间隙磁体（gap magnet）的设计提供合成候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铈是储量最丰富的镧系元素，有望缓解高性能磁性材料的匮乏，特别是通过发现适用于间隙磁体的化合物。然而，由于铈基铁磁体的磁性行为强烈依赖于晶体结构、交换几何和电子相互作用，预测具有单轴磁各向异性的铁磁体仍然颇具挑战。

Method: 首先利用随机森林分类器，基于七个结构和SOAP描述符（包括晶胞体积、密度、原子位点、空间群、原子密度、Ce SOAP重叠和过渡金属SOAP重叠）对已知铈基晶体结构进行筛选排序；随后对选定结构开展伊辛模型蒙特卡洛模拟以表征相行为和临界性质；最后使用基于模拟自旋构型亲和特征训练的自编码器识别相演化和转变行为的潜在特征签名。

Result: 从模拟相变中提取的临界指数提供了对磁有序区制和与各向异性相关效应的定量认识；自编码器识别出相演化和转变行为的潜在特征。整体框架筛选出了有望合成的铈基伊辛铁磁体候选材料。

Conclusion: 该框架将结构筛选、统计力学模拟和机器学习整合为一体，加速了有前景的铈基磁性材料的识别，并为实验合成与验证提供了候选化合物。

Abstract: Cerium (Ce), the most abundant lanthanide, offers significant potential for addressing shortages in high-performance magnetic materials, particularly through the discovery of compounds suitable for gap magnets. However, predicting Ce-based ferromagnets with uniaxial magnetic anisotropy remains challenging because their magnetic behavior depends strongly on crystal structure, exchange geometry, and electronic interactions. Here, we present a physics-guided computational framework to screen known Ce-based crystal structures and identify promising Ising ferromagnets for future synthesis. A Random Forest classifier uses seven structural and SOAP descriptors, including unit-cell volume, density, atomic sites, space group, atomic density, Ce SOAP overlap, and transition-metal SOAP overlap, to prioritize candidate compounds. Selected crystallographic structures are then analyzed using Ising-model Monte Carlo simulations to characterize phase behavior and critical properties. Critical exponents extracted from simulated phase transitions provide quantitative insight into magnetic regimes and anisotropy-related effects. We further employ autoencoders trained on affinity-based features from simulated spin configurations to identify latent signatures of phase evolution and transition behavior. Together, this framework integrates structural screening, statistical-mechanical simulation, and machine learning to accelerate the identification of promising Ce-based magnetic materials and provide candidates for experimental synthesis and validation.

</details>


### [165] [Denoising Diffusion Monte Carlo Electron Densities with Physically Informed Variance Stabilization: From Fourier Filters to 3D UNETs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08152)
*Kenneth O. Berard,Brenda Rubenstein,Jaron T. Krogel*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了扩散蒙特卡洛（DMC）计算电子密度的去噪方法，利用密度泛函理论（DFT）密度作为物理先验进行异方差到同方差变换，并在碳金刚石、蓝磷和二氧化钌三种材料上比较了傅里叶变换、回归方法和3D UNET等多种去噪技术，发现方差稳定化后的回归方法在所有材料及高/低噪声极限下均表现最佳，可将DMC模拟成本降低10-100倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 获得精确电子密度对分子和凝聚态体系的基础描述以及下一代密度泛函的开发至关重要，而DMC虽能产生基准质量的数据，但其实空间电子密度含有大量统计噪声，需要有效的去噪手段。

Method: 以信息论的Jensen-Shannon散度作为去噪性能的评判标准；利用DFT密度作为物理先验，通过近似异方差到同方差（heteroscedastic to homoscedastic）变换辅助去噪；系统比较了傅里叶变换、回归方法、3D UNET以及扁平化机器学习模型和2D图像模型等多种去噪技术，在碳金刚石、蓝磷和rutile VO2三种密度变化范围广泛的材料上进行测试。

Result: 简单扁平化的机器学习模型和2D图像模型会产生线条伪影（line artifacts）且难以捕捉完整的空间相关性；而采用方差稳定化后，回归方法在所有考虑的材料的低噪声和高噪声极限下均优于其他方法；最佳去噪器可将密度生成型DMC模拟的计算成本降低10-100倍。

Conclusion: 方差稳定化的回归去噪方法为DMC密度去噪提供了最有效的解决方案，为DFT泛函反演等对噪声敏感的应用开辟了有前景的路径。

Abstract: Obtaining accurate electron densities is important for the fundamental description of molecular and condensed matter systems, as well as for the development of next-generation density functionals. Diffusion Monte Carlo (DMC), in particular, is known to produce benchmark-quality data; however, the predicted real-space electron densities contain substantial amounts of statistical noise. In this work, we study denoising approaches for DMC densities, judged on the basis of the information-theoretic Jensen-Shannon divergence. The denoising is facilitated by an approximate heteroscedastic to homoscedastic transformation leveraging the density functional theory density as a physical prior. We systematically compare a range of denoising techniques-including Fourier transform, regression, and 3D UNETs-on materials showing a wide range of density variations: carbon diamond, blue phosphorus, and rutile VO2. Our results indicate that simple flattened machine learning models and 2D image-based models introduce line artifacts and struggle to capture the full spatial correlation. In contrast, when using variance stabilization, regression methods outperform all others in both the high and low- noise limits across all materials considered. The best denoisers reduce the required cost of density-generating DMC simulations by 10-100x, providing a promising route forward for application in noise-sensitive tasks such as DFT functional inversion.

</details>


### [166] [Universality of PVDF-Li$^+$ Ion Interface Chemistry in Lead-free Perovskite Energy Storage Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08295)
*Arun Kumar,Ayush Kumar Pandey,Ankur Yadav,Vishnu Saraswat,Shiladitya Sengupta,Abhishek Tewari,Monojit Bag*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究发现在无铅钙钛矿超级电容器中，电极粘结剂（binder）并非惰性结构组分，而是决定最优电解液组成的关键参数。通过PVDF负载量与LiTFSI浓度的交叉实验矩阵，发现电容最优值随粘结剂含量沿单一线性关系（界面平衡规则 λ+θ=1）系统性移动；结合机器学习势函数模拟，揭示了PVDF在电极表面的平面构型通过均化锂吸附能、引入氟介导配位并限制锂在二维界面区域运动，从而调控界面储能行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观念将电极粘结剂视为惰性结构组分，其界面作用被忽视；然而在无铅钙钛矿超级电容器中，粘结剂可能通过聚合物-电解质界面显著影响储能性能。本研究旨在揭示粘结剂负载量与最优电解液组成之间的定量关联，并理解其微观机制，从而将粘结剂从被动结构材料转变为主动设计参数。

Method: 采用因子交叉实验设计（factorial matrix），系统改变聚偏氟乙烯（PVDF）负载量与LiTFSI浓度，在CsSnCl₃电极中测定电容最优值。使用预训练的MACE机器学习原子间势函数（machine-learned interatomic potential）进行分子模拟，研究PVDF在CsSnCl₃表面的吸附构型、锂吸附能分布及配位环境。通过调控聚合物表面密度和链长，考察界面锂的容纳能力与聚集行为。

Result: 电容最优值随粘结剂含量沿单一线性关系移动，可由界面平衡规则（Interfacial Balance Rule，λ+θ=1）定量描述，其中λ和θ分别为归一化的锂供应和聚合物贡献。该关系在混合MASnCl₃体系中同样成立，表明最优值由聚合物-电解质界面而非钙钛矿晶格化学决定。模拟显示PVDF在CsSnCl₃表面呈平面构型，同时与阳离子和阴离子位点相互作用，均化锂吸附能并引入氟介导配位，将锂限制于二维界面区域同时保持横向迁移能力。研究发现界面锂容纳能力有限，超出后发生离面聚集。

Conclusion: 界面平衡规则为界面有限资源提供了宏观描述符，在聚合物介导的锂稳定化与有限容纳空间之间实现平衡。粘结剂负载量应被视为聚合物调控储能界面的主动设计参数，而非惰性结构成分。

Abstract: Electrode binders are conventionally regarded as inert structural components. Here, we show that in lead-free perovskite supercapacitors, the binder defines the optimal electrolyte composition. Across a factorial matrix of poly(vinylidene fluoride) (PVDF) loadings and LiTFSI concentrations in CsSnCl$_3$ electrodes, the capacitance optimum shifts systematically with binder content along a single linear relationship, described by the Interfacial Balance Rule ($λ+θ=1$), where $λ$ and $θ$ are the normalized lithium-supply and polymer contributions at the optimized interfacial state. The same relationship holds for hybrid MASnCl$_3$, showing that the optimum is governed by the polymer-electrolyte interface rather than the perovskite lattice chemistry. Simulations using a pre-trained MACE machine-learned interatomic potential show that PVDF adopts a planar configuration on CsSnCl$_3$ and simultaneously interacts with cationic and anionic sites. This configuration homogenizes lithium adsorption energetics, introduces fluorine-mediated coordination, and confines lithium to a two-dimensional interfacial region while preserving lateral mobility. Tuning polymer coverage through surface density and chain length reveals a finite interfacial lithium accommodation capacity that marks the onset of out-of-plane aggregation. The Interfacial Balance Rule provides a macroscopic descriptor of this finite interfacial resource, balancing polymer-mediated lithium stabilization against limited accommodation space. Binder loading is therefore an active design parameter for polymer-regulated energy-storage interfaces.

</details>


### [167] [Pressure-Induced Stacking Disorder and Suppression of Long-Range Sm-type Order in Medium-Entropy Rare-Earth Alloys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08351)
*Raimundas Sereika,Matthew P. Clay,Kallol Chakrabarty,Yogesh K. Vohra*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究利用同步辐射X射线衍射（synchrotron X-ray diffraction）和金刚石压砧（diamond anvil cell）技术，研究了TbHoEr和TbHoDy两种稀土中熵合金（medium-entropy alloy）在高压下的结构演化，发现两种合金均从六方密堆积（hcp）结构直接转变为双六方密堆积（dhcp）相，并绕过Sm型中间相，通过堆垛无序的密堆积态实现相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀土中熵合金（rare-earth medium-entropy alloy）为研究化学无序如何改变4f镧系元素已确立的压力诱导结构演化提供了平台。理解化学无序对高压相变路径的影响有助于揭示极端条件下材料的结构稳定性与相变机制。

Method: 采用同步辐射X射线衍射与金刚石压砧技术，对TbHoEr和TbHoDy两种中熵合金施加高压并进行原位结构表征；通过二维衍射图像（two-dimensional diffraction image）分析过渡区域的散射特征，以探测堆垛无序（stacking disorder）的存在。

Result: 两种合金在高压下均从环境六方密堆积（hcp）结构转变为双六方密堆积（dhcp）相，未观测到明显的体相Sm型中间相；TbHoEr在压缩至70 GPa时进一步出现高压菱方hR24相。二维衍射图中过渡区域呈现条纹状漫散射（streak-like diffuse scattering），表明沿密堆积方向存在堆垛无序和有限的堆垛相干长度（stacking coherence）。

Conclusion: 相变通过堆垛无序的密堆积中间态而非有序的体相Sm型相进行。构型无序（configurational disorder）、局域晶格畸变（local lattice distortion）、堆垛层错能（stacking-fault energetics）与相变动力学共同抑制了长程Sm型有序的发展，表明中熵合金化可从根本上改变稀土材料在极端条件下的压力诱导堆垛路径。

Abstract: Rare-earth medium-entropy alloys provide a platform for investigating how chemical disorder modifies the well-established pressure-induced structural evolution of close-packed $4f$ lanthanides. Here, we study TbHoEr and TbHoDy using synchrotron X-ray diffraction in diamond anvil cells. Both alloys transform from the ambient hexagonal close-packed (hcp) structure to a double hexagonal close-packed (dhcp) phase, while no well-resolved bulk Sm-type intermediate phase is observed. For TbHoEr, compression to 70 GPa further reveals a high-pressure rhombohedral hR24 phase. Unlike the constituent heavy lanthanides, however, both alloys bypass the intermediate Sm-type phase. Two-dimensional diffraction images further reveal streak-like diffuse scattering in the transition region, indicating stacking disorder and limited stacking coherence along the close-packed direction. These observations indicate that the transformation proceeds through a stacking-disordered close-packed state rather than through a well-ordered bulk Sm-type phase. We propose that configurational disorder, local lattice distortion, stacking-fault energetics, and transformation kinetics collectively suppress the development of long-range Sm-type order. The results demonstrate that medium-entropy alloying can fundamentally modify pressure-induced stacking pathways in rare-earth materials under extreme conditions.

</details>


### [168] [Thermo-mechanical Characterization of 2D hexagonal Boron Phosphide (h-BP)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08415)
*Zahabul Islam*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过密度泛函理论（DFT）拟合开发了一种用于二维单层六方磷化硼（h-BP）的经典分子动力学（MD）势函数，并在不同温度（300 K至900 K）及含缺陷条件下系统评估了其拉伸力学性能，揭示了显著的力学各向异性、温度软化效应以及缺陷（Stone-Wales缺陷与空位缺陷）对强度与失效应变的削弱作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: h-BP作为一种具有中等带隙、高热稳定性和优异载流子迁移率的二维材料，在电子、传感和能源应用中具有广阔前景，但其在极端环境下的力学稳定性缺乏系统的原子尺度研究，亟需建立可靠的MD势函数并厘清其力学行为。

Method: 首先基于DFT计算拟合出h-BP的经典MD势函数参数；随后将所导出的参数应用于MD模拟，在沿扶手椅（armchair）和锯齿（zigzag）两个晶向、温度范围为300 K至900 K的条件下进行单轴拉伸加载测试；此外还引入Stone-Wales缺陷和不同空位缺陷以考察结构缺陷对力学性能的影响。

Result: 力学性能存在显著各向异性：在所有温度下，锯齿方向的抗拉强度与弹性模量均高于扶手椅方向；温度升高会因热软化（thermal softening）和原子振动加剧而同时降低抗拉强度与刚度；缺陷研究表明Stone-Wales缺陷和空位缺陷均降低抗拉强度与失效应变，其中双原子空位因局部应力集中和过早裂纹萌生造成最严重的力学性能退化。

Conclusion: 研究结果为h-BP在极端环境中力学稳定性的后续研究提供了基础，明确了晶向、温度与缺陷类型对其力学响应的关键影响规律。

Abstract: This study explores the thermo-mechanical properties of a two-dimensional (2D) monolayer hexagonal boron phosphide (h-BP). h-BP is predicted to possess a moderate band gap, high thermal stability, and excellent carrier mobility, making it suitable for advanced electronic, sensing, and energy applications. A classical molecular dynamics (MD) potential for h-BP was developed using density functional theory (DFT) calculations. The derived parameters were implemented in MD simulations to evaluate mechanical behavior under tensile loading along both zigzag and armchair directions at varying temperatures (300 K to 900 K). The results reveal significant anisotropy in mechanical performance, with higher tensile strength and elastic modulus in the zigzag direction across all temperatures. Increasing temperature reduces both tensile strength and stiffness due to thermal softening and increased atomic vibrations. The influence of structural defects was also investigated, revealing that Stone-Wales and vacancy defects reduce the tensile strength and failure strain of h-BP, with the two-atom vacancy producing the most pronounced mechanical degradation due to localized stress concentration and premature crack initiation. These findings provide a foundation for future research on the mechanical stability of h-BP in extreme environments.

</details>


### [169] [Unified open-boundary electrostatics in real-space density functional theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08474)
*Rajat Kumar,David Codony,Phanish Suryanarayana,Abhiraj Sharma*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种实空间密度泛函理论（real-space density functional theory）中的静电公式化方法，用于统一处理孤立和部分周期系统的开放边界静电问题，包括存在沿开放方向施加的均匀电场的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放边界静电学的处理在孤立体系和部分周期体系的电子结构计算中至关重要，但现有方法缺乏统一且系统的处理方式，尤其对应力张量等量的计算尚无现成实现。

Method: 该工作构造了一个局域静电能泛函，其极值条件在周期方向和开放方向分别满足周期边界条件和狄利克雷边界条件（Dirichlet boundary conditions）的泊松方程（Poisson equation）。利用格林函数（Green's function）方法推导了由体系总电荷密度给出的狄利克雷边界值的解析表达式，并进一步导出了能量、原子力和应力张量的表达式。这些表达式被实现在大规模并行实空间SPARC电子结构代码中。

Result: 通过代表性算例验证了框架的准确性与效率：计算量随真空尺寸呈指数收敛，与成熟的平面波（plane-wave）代码吻合良好，且在相近精度下所需真空区域显著较小。由于现有实现均未提供此类体系的应力张量，作者将应力与能量的数值导数进行对比，二者吻合良好。将该框架应用于计算静态极化率和压电系数，结果与文献报道值非常一致。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个系统统一、准确高效的开放边界静电学处理框架，特别在应力张量计算和减少真空需求方面具有显著优势，可广泛用于孤立和部分周期体系的电子结构模拟。

Abstract: We present an electrostatic formulation in real-space density functional theory that provides a systematic and unified treatment of the open-boundary electrostatics of isolated and partially periodic systems, including in the presence of an applied uniform electric field along the open (finite) directions. Specifically, we formulate a local electrostatic energy functional whose stationarity yields the Poisson equation for the electrostatic potential, subject to periodic and Dirichlet boundary conditions along the periodic and open directions, respectively. Using a Green's function approach, we derive analytical expressions for the Dirichlet values arising from the total charge density of the system. We also derive the expressions for the energy, atomic forces, and stress tensor. We implement the resulting expressions within the large-scale parallel real-space SPARC electronic structure code. Using representative examples, we verify the accuracy and efficiency of the framework, demonstrating exponential convergence of the computed quantities with vacuum size and excellent agreement with established plane-wave codes while requiring significantly less vacuum at comparable accuracy. Since no existing implementation provides the stresses for such systems, we instead verify them against numerical derivatives of the energy, finding close agreement. Finally, we apply the framework to compute static polarizabilities and piezoelectric coefficients, obtaining very good agreement with values reported in the literature.

</details>


### [170] [High Thermal Conductivity of Back-End-of-Line Compatible Diamond Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08534)
*Jinwen Liu,Chufei Cheng,Feifei Tan,Jinquan Zhang,Di Lu,Bing Dai,Jiaqi Zhu,Runsheng Wang,Zhe Cheng*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究报道了在低于400°C的衬底温度下直接在Si上生长的多晶金刚石薄膜，通过拉曼光谱、SEM和TDTR表征，测得室温有效热导率为73和86 W m⁻¹K⁻¹，比传统介电材料高约两个数量级，展示了其在后端（BEOL）热管理中介电散热层的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后端（back-end-of-line, BEOL）热管理需要可在400°C以下热预算（thermal budget）内集成的电绝缘散热介电材料，而传统介电材料热导率较低，无法有效满足散热需求。

Method: 在低于400°C的衬底温度下直接在Si上生长多晶金刚石薄膜，制备了平均厚度分别为760和1000 nm的两种薄膜；采用拉曼光谱（Raman spectroscopy）、扫描电子显微镜（SEM）和时间域热反射法（TDTR）进行表征，并在室温至100°C范围内进行变温TDTR测量；基于灵敏度分析采用各向同性热模型进行分析。

Result: 拉曼光谱显示尖锐的金刚石峰及少量非金刚石碳信号，SEM显示横向生长和较大晶粒尺寸；两种薄膜的室温有效热导率分别为73和86 W m⁻¹K⁻¹，比传统介电材料高约两个数量级。

Conclusion: 低温生长的金刚石薄膜具有显著优于传统介电材料的热导率，展示了其作为介电散热层（dielectric heat-spreading layer）在BEOL热管理中的潜在应用前景。

Abstract: Back-end-of-line (BEOL) thermal management requires electrically insulating heat-spreading dielectric that can be integrated within thermal budgets below 400 C. Here, we report polycrystalline diamond films grown directly on Si at a substrate temperature below 400C. Two films with average thickness of 760 and 1000 nm were characterized by Raman spectroscopy, scanning electron microscopy (SEM), and time-domain thermoreflectance (TDTR). Raman spectra show a sharp diamond peak with minor signatures of non-diamond carbon, while SEM reveals lateral growth and large grain size. Temperature dependent TDTR measurements were performed from room temperature to 100C. Sensitivity analysis indicates that the sensitivity of cross-plane thermal conductivity is comparative to the in-plane thermal conductivity. Accordingly, the films were analyzed using an isotropic thermal model by considering the nearly-isotropic grain structure, yielding room temperature effective thermal conductivity of 73 and 86 W m-1 K-1, respectively. These values are about two orders of magnitude higher than those of conventional dielectric materials and demonstrate the potential of diamond films grown at low temperatures as dielectric heat-spreading layers.

</details>


### [171] [An Automated Magnetron Sputtering Chamber for Ferroelectric Thin Film Deposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08647)
*Stanislav A. Udovenko,Ian Mercer,Sarah Olandt,Ric Wilburn,Kevin Dressler,Susan Trolier-McKinstry,Jon-Paul Maria,Darren C. Pagan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套将现有手动薄膜沉积设备改造为自动化、自主化实验平台的模板，并以磁控溅射（magnetron sputtering）腔室为例，实现了仪器控制、系统与沉积控制及数据采集的自动化，并展示了在半自主确定纤锌矿（wurtzite）Al₁₋ₓ₋ᵧScₓBᵧN薄膜工艺-性能关系中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代材料合成与制造过程的优化可通过有效利用数字数据集（digital datasets）来加速，但大多数现有定制研究基础设施（包括薄膜沉积设备）仍以手动操作为主，与这一新的研究范式不兼容。

Method: 作者提供了将现有手动沉积腔室升级为自动化与自主实验平台的通用模板，以磁控溅射腔室为例，重点实现三方面自动化：仪器自动化、系统与沉积控制，以及同步化的自动数据采集策略。

Result: 以纤锌矿铁电材料合成的磁控溅射腔室升级为实例，展示了该系统在半自主确定工艺-性能关系中的应用——具体为最小化纤锌矿Al₁₋ₓ₋ᵧScₓBᵧN薄膜中的矫顽场（coercive field）。

Conclusion: 该模板为将现有手动科研设备升级为自动化实验平台提供了可行路径，可促进材料合成研究向数据驱动和自主实验范式转型。

Abstract: Optimization of next-generation materials synthesis and manufacturing processes can be accelerated by effective use of digital datasets. However, a majority of existing custom research infrastructure, including that for thin film deposition, is primarily manually operated and not compatible with this new research paradigm. Here, a template is provided for upgrading existing manual deposition chambers to enable automated and autonomous experimentation. As an example, the upgrade of an existing magnetron sputtering chamber dedicated to synthesis of wurtzite ferroelectrics is presented. Focus is placed on automation of instrumentation; system and deposition control; and synchronized and automated data collection strategies. An example use case of the system for semi-autonomous determination of process-property relationships is presented, specifically minimization of coercive field in wurtzite Al$_{1-x-y}$Sc$_x$B$_y$N thin films.

</details>


### [172] [Microscopic Origin of Spin Splitting in Altermagnetic CrSb Thin Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08741)
*Dai Mingyang,Song Hongquan,Kang Zhuo,Xu Yuanji,Tian Fuyang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算系统研究了不同表面取向的CrSb薄层电子结构，发现(2̄1̄10)表面保留显著的交变磁（altermagnetic）自旋劈裂，而(0001)和(101̄0)表面几乎自旋简并。研究揭示维度降低从根本上改变了交变磁自旋劈裂的微观起源：薄膜中需要长程晶胞间共面Cr-Sb跳跃，而Sb-Sb跳跃提供额外贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属CrSb因其巨大的自旋劈裂和高奈尔温度（Néel temperature）成为有前景的自旋电子学材料，但块体和薄膜CrSb中不同的自旋劈裂行为的微观起源尚未解决。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）系统研究不同表面取向的CrSb薄板的电子结构，分析各表面取向下的能带自旋劈裂特征及其与跳跃路径的关系。

Result: 所有考虑的薄板均保持与交变磁相容的自旋群对称性，但电子结构差异显著：(2̄1̄10)表面保留明显的交变磁自旋劈裂，(0001)和(101̄0)表面则几乎自旋简并。维度降低改变了自旋劈裂的微观起源，薄膜中需要长程晶胞间共面Cr-Sb跳跃，Sb-Sb跳跃提供额外贡献。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了降维体系中交变磁性的微观机制，为低维交变磁材料中的自旋劈裂工程提供了普适原理。

Abstract: Altermagnets have recently emerged as promising materials for spintronic applications owing to their momentum-dependent spin splitting. Among them, metallic CrSb is particularly attractive owing to its giant spin splitting and high Néel temperature. However, the microscopic origin of the distinct spin-splitting behaviors in bulk and thin-film CrSb remains unresolved. Here, we systematically investigate the electronic structures of CrSb slabs with different surface orientations using first-principles calculations. Although all considered slabs preserve spin-group symmetries compatible with altermagnetism, they exhibit markedly different electronic structures: the (2$\bar{1}\bar{1}$0) slab retains pronounced altermagnetic spin splitting, whereas the (0001) and (10$\bar{1}$0) slabs display nearly spin-degenerate bands. We demonstrate that dimensional reduction fundamentally changes the microscopic origin of altermagnetic spin splitting. Unlike bulk CrSb, altermagnetic spin splitting in thin films requires long-range inter-unit-cell coplanar Cr--Sb hopping, while Sb--Sb hopping provides an additional contribution. The preservation or suppression of these hopping pathways explains the strong surface dependence of the spin splitting. Our findings establish a microscopic mechanism for understanding altermagnetism in reduced dimensions and provide a general principle for engineering spin splitting in low-dimensional altermagnetic materials.

</details>


### [173] [Distribution of Relaxation Times analysis of evolution of Oxygen Reduction Pathways for ionic conductor infiltration on MIEC cathode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08767)
*Rakhi Saha,Preethi Sudarsan,Manju Kumari,Hee Jung Park,Abdelkrim Mekki,Khalil Harrabi,Somaditya Sen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过分布松弛时间（Distribution of Relaxation Times, DRT）分析，区分了浸渍离子导体Sm0.2Ce0.8O2-δ（SDC）的混合离子电子导体SrFe0.9Ti0.1O3-δ（STF）阴极上氧还原反应（ORR）的各个子过程，揭示了SDC浸渍对氧表面交换和活化过程的优先加速机制，并在800°C下将极化电阻（polarization resistance, RP）降至0.04 Ω·cm²。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固体氧化物燃料电池（SOFC）中的阴极氧还原反应（ORR）动力学迟缓是限制中温运行的主要瓶颈，而通过SDC浸渍可以调控ORR子过程，但传统等效电路法难以区分这些过程。因此需要一种能够解卷积各子过程并揭示其温度与表面重构动力学的分析工具。

Method: 采用分布松弛时间（DRT）分析方法，对没有和经过SDC浸渍的STF阴极进行阻抗谱数据的解卷积处理，从而区分氧气表面交换、活化过程以及电荷转移等不同频率范围的ORR子过程，并考察其随温度和表面重构的变化规律。

Result: DRT分析成功区分了各ORR子过程，发现SDC浸渍在高温下优先加速氧表面交换和活化过程，而非较高频率的与阴极表面相关的电荷转移过程。浸渍后的电池中，各过程得到系统重新分布，极化电阻（RP）在800°C时降低至0.04 Ω·cm²。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了DRT作为解卷积工具的有效性，并将SDC浸渍的STF确立为模型阴极体系，为理解ORR动力学机制和设计高性能SOFC空气电极提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Solid oxide fuel cells (SOFCs) are promising electrochemical energy conversion devices; however, the sluggish cathodic oxygen reduction reaction (ORR) remains a major limitation for intermediate-temperature operation. ORR subprocesses can be modified by infiltrating ionic Sm0.2Ce0.8O2-delta (SDC) on mixed ionic electronic SrFe0.9Ti0.1O3-delta (STF). However, the processes are indistinguishable in most cases and poorly understood using conventional equivalent circuits. Distribution of Relaxation Times (DRT) analysis distinguishes these processes to identify the dynamics with temperature and surface reconstruction. Such analysis is being reported, revealing the connection between increase of active sites, temperature, and polarization resistance (RP). SDC infiltration preferentially accelerates oxygen surface exchange and activation processes over the relatively high frequency charge transfer process related to cathode surface at elevated temperatures for the infiltrated cells. RP was reduced substantially with the systematic redistribution of each process to as low as 0.04 ohm.cm2 at 800 degC. This work underlines the deconvolution of the processes using DRT as a tool, and SDC infiltrated STF as a model cathode system to provide a mechanistic insight of understanding ORR kinetics and design a rationale for developing high-performance SOFC air electrodes

</details>


### [174] [Light-Stabilized Metastable Electronic State in NiO with Enhanced Orbital Hybridization2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08842)
*Thomas C. Rossi,Fabio G. Santomauro,Lars Mewes,Janina Löffler,Dominik Kinschel,Giacomo Rossi,Mario Gutiérrez,Oliviero Cannelli,Boris V. Sorokin,Jochen Rittmann,Jakob Kel,James Budarz,Anna Wach,Adam H. Clark,Emiliano Dal Molin,Maged F. Bekheet,Albert Gili,Sebastian Praetz,Daniel Grötzsch,Delphine Cabaret,Renske M. van der Veen,Majed Chergui*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过Ni K-edge X射线吸收谱研究了光激发对NiO电子结构的调控，发现光激发可产生长寿命亚稳态，增强Ni 3d-O 2p轨道杂化，并将其归因于光致激发后局域电子关联的动态屏蔽效应，提出了光电子材料设计的新原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关联金属氧化物（correlated metal oxides）中光驱动的电子结构调控为优化光伏和光电化学技术所用材料提供了新的机遇，因此研究者希望探索通过光激发手段动态调控此类材料电子性质的机制。

Method: 研究者利用Ni K-edge X射线吸收谱（X-ray absorption spectroscopy）在连续和脉冲紫外激发条件下表征NiO中未占据p态密度（unoccupied p density of states）的结构与电子变化，并配合第一性原理DFT+U+V计算对光谱变化进行归因与分析。

Result: 脉冲激发产生约10^20 cm^-3的高载流子密度，形成寿命约600皮秒的增强杂化状态，同时伴随晶格加热；连续紫外照射则在低得多的约10^13 cm^-3载流子密度下稳定了类似的电子状态且晶格加热可忽略，证明该状态的形成并非单纯由热效应驱动。

Conclusion: 光谱变化源于更强的Ni 3d-O 2p轨道杂化，其机制归因于光激发后局域电子关联（on-site electronic correlations）的动态屏蔽（dynamic screening）导致电荷密度重新分布。由于轨道杂化决定载流子输运和电荷转移能，光致屏蔽为动态调控关联氧化物提供了新机制，并提出光电子材料的新设计原则。

Abstract: Light-driven control of electronic structure in correlated metal oxides offers new opportunities for optimizing materials used in photovoltaic and photoelectrochemical technologies. We show that photoexcitation of NiO, a prototypical transparent semiconductor and hole-transport material, across its charge-transfer gap produces a long-lived metastable state with enhanced Ni 3d-O 2p orbital hybridization. We characterize this state using Ni K-edge X-ray absorption spectroscopy, which probes how structural and electronic changes affect the unoccupied p density of states during continuous and pulsed ultraviolet excitation. Under pulsed excitation, high carrier densities of approximately 10^20 per cubic centimeter generate a state with a lifetime of approximately 600 picoseconds, in which enhanced hybridization coexists with lattice heating. By contrast, continuous ultraviolet irradiation at much lower carrier densities of approximately 10^13 per cubic centimeter stabilizes a similar electronic state with negligible lattice heating, demonstrating that its formation is not solely thermally driven. First-principles DFT+U+V calculations attribute the spectral changes to stronger Ni 3d-O 2p hybridization, which alters the unoccupied Ni 4p states probed by dipole-allowed K-edge transitions. We attribute this change to the dynamic screening of on-site electronic correlations following photoexcitation, which redistributes the charge density. Because orbital hybridization governs carrier transport and charge-transfer energetics, our results identify photoinduced screening as a mechanism for dynamically tuning correlated oxides and suggest new design principles for optoelectronic materials.

</details>


### [175] [A Dataset of Equilibrium State Configurations of Adsorption in Zeolites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08848)
*Marko Petković,Rachna Ramesh,Vlado Menkovski,Sofía Calero*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: AdsZeo是一个包含坐标解析（coordinate-resolved）的平衡甲烷吸附构型数据集，涵盖191种沸石拓扑、4,775个框架实现，共62,075次巨正则蒙特卡洛（grand canonical Monte Carlo）生产模拟，存储了分子坐标、载荷与能量统计等数据，可用于吸附分析、空间统计和机器学习模型开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数高通量吸附数据集仅报告系综平均量（ensemble-averaged quantities）如负载量或等温线，而丢失了分子构型信息，限制了更深入的吸附机理分析和机器学习建模。

Method: 采用巨正则蒙特卡洛模拟方法，在298 K、0.1至100 bar的13个甲烷压力下，对含铝取代、含钠阳离子的沸石框架体系进行平衡吸附模拟，生成并存储生产框架坐标数据，整合到处理后的DuckDB数据库中。

Result: 数据集包含4,775个框架实现、62,075次生产模拟、12,415,000条生产框架记录和1,245,376,215条粒子坐标记录，涵盖甲烷、钠阳离子和框架原子的完整坐标信息及逐帧载荷和能量统计。

Conclusion: AdsZeo提供了跨越框架拓扑、铝含量与分布、钠阳离子排列、压力和甲烷负载等多维变化的坐标解析吸附数据，可广泛用于吸附分析、空间统计、密度估计以及带电沸石孔道中分子构型生成的机器学习模型训练。

Abstract: Zeolites are crystalline nanoporous materials widely used in adsorption, separation, and catalytic processes.
