Molecular simulations are commonly used to predict adsorption properties, but most high-throughput adsorption datasets report only ensemble-averaged quantities such as loadings or isotherms, rather than the molecular configurations from which these averages are obtained. Here, we present AdsZeo, a coordinate-resolved dataset of equilibrium methane adsorption configurations in aluminium-substituted, sodium-containing zeolite frameworks. The processed release contains 4,775 framework realisations derived from 191 zeolite topologies. Each framework realisation was simulated at 13 methane pressures between 0.1 and 100 bar at 298 K using grand canonical Monte Carlo simulations, giving 62,075 production simulations in total. In addition to scalar adsorption records, the dataset stores production-frame methane pseudo-atom coordinates, mobile Na$^+$ cation coordinates, framework atomic coordinates, per-frame loading and energy statistics, and simulation metadata in a processed DuckDB database. The release contains 12,415,000 saved production-frame records and 1,245,376,215 saved particle-coordinate records. AdsZeo provides coordinate-resolved adsorption data across variations in framework topology, aluminium content and distribution, sodium cation arrangement, pressure, and methane loading, enabling reuse for adsorption analysis, spatial statistics, density estimation, and machine-learning models for molecular configuration generation in charged zeolite pores.

</details>


### [176] [Energy-efficient spin Hall nano-oscillators using CoGd ferrimagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09021)
*Jiayu Lei,Raghav Sharma,Shishun Zhao,Fanrui Hu,Yuchen Pu,Chenhui Zhang,Rahul Mishra,Hyunsoo Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了基于Co1-xGdx稀土-过渡金属（rare-earth-transition-metal, RE-TM）亚铁磁（ferrimagnetic）材料的自旋霍尔纳米振荡器（spin Hall nano-oscillation, SHNO），通过组分调控在磁化补偿点附近实现低电流密度（1.43×10⁷ A/cm²）、小磁场（5 mT）和窄线宽（0.61 MHz）同时优化，性能较铁磁对应器件提升约一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于铁磁（ferromagnet）材料的传统自旋霍尔纳米振荡器受限于高阈值电流密度和对外部磁场的较大需求，难以满足实际应用。亚铁磁因具有独特的磁动力学特性和节能自旋电子学器件的潜力，被视为有吸引力的替代方案。

Method: 本研究利用Co1-xGdx合金构建RE-TM亚铁磁自旋霍尔纳米振荡器，通过调整合金组分实现磁化补偿点（magnetization compensation）附近的高性能运行；优化器件在电流注入下驱动自旋轨道力矩（spin-orbit torque）产生振荡。

Result: 优化后的振荡器同时实现低电流密度（1.43×10⁷ A/cm²）、小外加磁场（5 mT）和窄线宽（0.61 MHz），整体性能较铁磁对器件提升约一个数量级。性能增强归因于高自旋轨道力矩效率、低磁各向异性、降低的有效磁化强度以及补偿点附近的非线性最小化。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了RE-TM亚铁磁材料作为下一代自旋电子学器件的有前景的材料平台，为构建节能、高性能的自旋电子振荡器提供了新策略。

Abstract: Conventional spin Hall nano-oscillators (SHNOs) based on ferromagnets face practical limitations due to high threshold current densities and large external magnetic field requirements. Ferrimagnets provide an attractive alternative due to their unique magnetic dynamics and potential for energy-efficient spintronic devices. In this study, we report rare-earth-transition-metal (RE-TM) ferrimagnetic SHNOs utilizing Co1-xGdx alloys, in which compositional tuning enables high-performance operation near the magnetization compensation. The optimized SHNO operates at a low current density (1.43*10^7 A/cm^2), a small magnetic field (5 mT), and exhibits a narrow linewidth (0.61 MHz) simultaneously, showing an order-of-magnitude improvement over its ferromagnetic counterparts. This enhanced performance arises from high spin-orbit torque efficiency, low magnetic anisotropy, reduced effective magnetization, and minimized nonlinearity near the compensation point. These results establish RE-TM ferrimagnets as a promising materials platform for next-generation spintronic devices and offer new strategies for realizing energy-efficient, high-performance spintronic oscillators.

</details>


### [177] [Anharmonic dephasing in the electron-phonon interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09039)
*Mingran Kong,Bartomeu Monserrat*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文推导了包含非谐三声子相互作用效应的电声耦合散射率表达式，并提出了一套可与现有计算流程无缝整合的第一性原理实现方案，最终在多种材料中计算了含声子退相干效应的电声散射率，证明了有限声子寿命对电声耦合评估的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的电声耦合计算通常假设声子具有无限寿命，但非谐声子-声子相互作用会使声子表现出有限寿命，从而影响电声耦合的准确评估。作者旨在弥补这一理论空白。

Method: 首先推导出包含非谐三声子相互作用（anharmonic three-phonon interactions）效应的电声耦合散射率解析表达式；随后描述了可无缝集成到现有电声耦合与声子-声子耦合计算工作流中的第一性原理（first-principles）实现方案。

Result: 在多种材料中完成了包含声子退相干（phonon dephasing）效应的电声散射率计算，并识别和讨论了几种非谐声子影响电声耦合的不同微观机制。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了有限声子寿命在电声耦合评估中的重要性，并提供了在广泛材料与物理现象中探索这些效应的计算平台。

Abstract: Electron-phonon coupling has been a central topic in condensed matter physics for decades, and firstprinciples methods have demonstrated remarkable success in quantitatively capturing its role in a wide variety of physical phenomena and materials. Conventional calculations of electron-phonon coupling typically assume that phonons have infinite lifetimes, but phonons can exhibit finite lifetimes due to anharmonic phonon-phonon interactions. In this work, we derive an expression for the electron-phonon coupling scattering rates including the effects of anharmonic three-phonon interactions, which lead to phonon dephasing and finite phonon lifetimes. We also describe a first-principles implementation of this anharmonic electron-phonon coupling which can be seamlessly integrated within existing workflows for the evaluation of electron-phonon and phonon-phonon coupling interactions. Finally, we present calculations of electron-phonon scattering rates including phonon dephasing in a range of materials, and discuss the different microscopic mechanisms by which anharmonic phonons influence electron-phonon coupling. This study establishes the importance of finite phonon lifetimes in the evaluation of electron-phonon coupling, and provides a platform to explore these effects in a wide range of materials and phenomena.

</details>


### [178] [Wrinkling in Selected Polymer Thin Films Induced by Combined Ion Beam and Humidity Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09041)
*Alessia Danagoulian,Benli Jiang,Nicholas Russo,Colette Abadie,Jalal Karimzadeh Khoei,Grace Pettis,Jocelyn Zhang,Neil Baker,Eda Güney,Omid Moradi,Wei-Jing Chen,Jiaqi Tang,Robert Sims,Kevin E. Smith,Gozde Ozaydin Ince,Karl F. Ludwig*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究调查了三种不同亲水性的聚合物（pHEMA、p4VP、pV4D4）在离子束溅射（ion beam sputtering, IBS）诱导下的表面起皱现象，发现仅pHEMA和p4VP在离子轰击及随后的水蒸气暴露下产生褶皱；XPS和FTIR分析表明，IBS在表面形成石墨化层，起皱的聚合物发生吸水肿胀，而肿胀受刚性基底和硬化表层机械约束从而形成褶皱；pV4D4因吸水少、肿胀有限而不起皱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索离子束溅射诱导聚合物表面起皱现象在不同亲水性材料中的发生条件与机制，理解表面化学变化与水分吸收对褶皱形成的贡献。

Method: 选取三种亲水性不同的聚合物薄膜（pHEMA、p4VP、pV4D4），依次进行IBS处理和随后的水蒸气暴露；利用X射线光电子能谱（XPS）和傅里叶变换红外光谱（FTIR）分别在IBS前、IBS后及湿度暴露后表征表面化学变化和水分吸收情况，并结合实验结果提出起皱机制假设。

Result: XPS显示IBS在表面层中驱动化学变化，形成石墨化（graphitized）表层；pHEMA和p4VP在IBS与水蒸气暴露后发生褶皱，XPS和FTIR证实其表面与体相均吸收水分导致肿胀；pV4D4在相同条件下未出现褶皱，归因于其吸水能力低、肿胀响应有限。

Conclusion: 聚合物起皱的形成机制源于水分吸收引起的肿胀被刚性硅基底和坚硬的石墨化表层机械约束所致；材料亲水性与肿胀程度是决定是否发生离子束诱导起皱现象的关键因素。

Abstract: This study investigates ion beam sputtering (IBS)-induced surface wrinkling phenomena in three polymers with varying hydrophilicity: poly-hydroxy-ethyl-methacrylate (pHEMA), poly-4-vinyl pyridine (p4VP), and poly-2,4,6,8-tetramethyl-2,4,6,8-tetravinylcyclotetrasiloxane (pV4D4). It is observed that pHEMA and p4VP films wrinkle only when exposed to ion bombardment and subsequent water vapor exposure. No wrinkling is observed in pV4D4 under these same conditions. X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy (XPS) and Fourier transform infrared spectroscopy (FTIR) are performed before IBS, after IBS, and after exposure to humidity. XPS shows that IBS drives chemical changes within the surface layer, creating a graphitized film at the surface. For the polymer films that exhibit wrinkling (pHEMA and p4VP), XPS and FTIR indicate water absorption in both the surface and the bulk of the films, resulting in swelling. We conjecture that the formation of wrinkles arises from this swelling being mechanically constrained by the rigid underlying silicon substrate and the stiff graphitized surface layer. In contrast, the absence of wrinkle formation in pV4D4 under the same experimental conditions can be attributed to its comparatively low water absorption and the correspondingly limited swelling response.

</details>


### [179] [Electronic conductivity in anharmonic crystals: Phonon dephasing in the electron-phonon interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09050)
*Mingran Kong,Bartomeu Monserrat*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了电子与有限寿命声子（finite-lifetime phonons）相互作用的完整理论，并在第一性原理（first-principles）框架下实现。研究发现非谐退相（anharmonic dephasing）会显著增强金属MgB₂中的电子-声子散射率，抑制电导率，使计算值更接近实验。该工作确立了有限声子寿命在评估电子-声子散射中的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有第一性原理电子-声子相互作用计算均假设声子具有无限寿命，但该近似在非谐声子-声子相互作用较强时可能失效。为更准确地描述强非谐体系中的电子输运性质，需要发展考虑有限声子寿命的理论框架。

Method: 本文构建了电子与经历退相的有限寿命声子相互作用的理论，并在第一性原理中对MgB₂进行了实现。微观上，声子-声子相互作用创造了新的散射通道，扩大了电子-声子散射可用的相空间（phase space），从而增强了散射率。

Result: 在MgB₂中，非谐退相大幅增强了电子-声子散射率，显著抑制了电导率，使玻尔兹曼输运方程（Boltzmann transport equation）框架下的计算值更接近实验结果。该微观机制表明非谐退相可能对许多金属的电导率起重要作用。

Conclusion: 该理论及第一性原理实现为在其他材料中探索非谐退相与电子-声子相互作用的耦合机制提供了坚实基础。研究确立了有限声子寿命在评估电子-声子散射中的重要性，为更广泛材料的输运性质研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: The electron-phonon interaction underpins many material properties, for example, the conductivity of metals and the optoelectronic response of semiconductors. First-principles calculations of the electron-phonon interaction are a powerful tool to quantitatively describe many of these properties in increasingly complex materials. However, one key assumption of all calculations is that phonons have infinite lifetimes, an approximation that may break down when anharmonic phonon-phonon interactions are strong. In this work, we present a theory for the interaction of electrons with finite-lifetime phonons experiencing dephasing. Using a first-principles implementation of the theory, we find that anharmonic dephasing dramatically enhances electron-phonon scattering rates in metallic MgB2. Microscopically, phonon-phonon interactions create new scattering channels that increase the phase space available for electron-phonon scattering. As a result, anharmonic dephasing strongly suppresses conductivity in MgB2, bringing the calculated values substantially closer to experiment within the Boltzmann transport equation framework. This example establishes the importance of finite phonon lifetimes in the evaluation of electron-phonon scattering, and the microscopic mechanism suggests that anharmonic dephasing could play an important role in the conductivity of many metals. More broadly, our theory and first-principles implementation of anharmonic dephasing in the electron-phonon interaction provides a solid foundation to explore this regime in other materials.

</details>


### [180] [Altermagnetism-Induced Spin-resolved electronic structure in Janus FeX0.5Y0.5 Monolayers (X, Y = S, Se, Te)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09054)
*Mengyang Zhang,Jie Li,Yifei Chen,Shifang Li,Zhentao Fu,Jianxin Zhong*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文通过Kondo型模型和第一性原理计算，预测了一系列基于铁基超导体的Janus FeX0.5Y0.5单层材料，这些结构展现出显著的电子自旋劈裂态、大的拓扑带隙（51.4 meV）和高的Néel温度（415 K），并提出了一种基于交错磁（altermagnetism）策略来在超导体系中工程化自旋劈裂态的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在超导材料中实现自旋分辨的电子性质是前沿课题，既能带来新颖的基础物理，也为无耗散自旋基器件提供可能。作者旨在通过设计新型超导衍生结构来探索这一方向。

Method: 该方法利用Kondo型模型与第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations），从铁基超导体（如FeSe、FeTe和FeS）出发，构建Janus FeX0.5Y0.5单层系列结构，并系统考察其电子结构性质。

Result: 预测的Janus结构具有显著的自旋劈裂电子态、大的拓扑带隙（51.4 meV）和高Néel温度（415 K）。研究发现谷极化可被面内应变有效调控，谷极化的反常霍尔电导可通过移动费米能级进行操纵。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了基于交错磁的超导体系自旋劈裂态工程化新策略，为超导自旋电子学（superconducting spintronics）的进一步探索提供了启示。

Abstract: Realizing the spin-resolved electronic properties in superconducting materials stands as a critical frontier, offering both novel fundamental physics and potential for dissipationless spin-based devices. Here, we predict a series of Janus FeX0.5Y0.5 monolayers derived from iron-based superconductors (e.g., FeSe, FeTe, and FeS) by using Kondo-type model and first-principles calculations. These Janus structures exhibit significant spin-splittingelectronic states, large topological band gaps (51.4 meV) and high Néel temperatures (415 K). We further reveal that valley polarization can be effectively tuned via applied in-plane strain and the resulting valley-polarized anomalous Hall conductivity can be manipulated by shifting the Fermi level. Our work suggests a new strategy based on altermagnetism for engineering spin-splitting states in superconducting systems and inspires further exploration of superconducting spintronics.

</details>


### [181] [Self-induced crystalline fluctuation spin-glass state in Mn7C3 binary compounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09065)
*Zekun Yu,Chao Zhou,Kuo Bao,Xiaofeng Wang,Zhaoqing Wang,Jinming Zhu,Enxuan Li,Andong Yao,Yuhan Meng,Yufei Ge,Xingbin Zhao,Shuailing Ma,Pinwen Zhu,Qiang Tao,Tian Cui*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过高温高压方法合成了块体Pnma型Mn7C3自旋玻璃（spin glass），该材料具有三角Ising模型结构、37.4 K的高冻结温度及新颖的竞争机制，并引入了一类具有有序微几何（ordered microgeometries）驱动电子结构无序的新家族自旋玻璃材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶体自旋玻璃因其独特的性质和应用前景而备受关注，但现有材料的性能和应用友好性有待提升，因此需要合成并研究具有新结构和高性能的自旋玻璃材料。

Method: 采用高温高压方法合成块体Pnma型Mn7C3；通过X射线衍射和磁化率测量进行实验表征；结合理论计算与模拟揭示三角Mn单元的自旋阻挫（spin frustration）机制及C原子的极化电荷传递路径。

Result: 该化合物具有基于三角Ising模型（Ising model）的结构，自旋玻璃冻结温度为37.4 K；三角C单元共享单电子形成三叉电子云，该电子是阻挫与竞争的根源；可能的磁构型高度简并，材料具有高鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作引入了一类具有有序微几何驱动电子结构无序的自旋玻璃新家族，并提供了一种可用于人工智能高效硬件和算法设计等领域的应用友好型自旋玻璃材料。

Abstract: Crystalline spin glasses are attractive compounds owing to their unique nature and applications. Here, we synthesised a bulk Pnma-type Mn7C3 spin glass by a high-temperature, high-pressure method. Experimental characterisation including X-ray diffraction and magnetic susceptibility measurements demonstrated that the compound has a triangular Ising-model-based structure, high freezing temperature of 37.4 K, and novel competition mechanism. Theoretical calculations and simulations revealed that the triangular Mn units are spontaneously frustrated and bridge neighbouring Mn units via polarised C atoms and messenger Mn atoms. Triangular C units each share one electron within a three-pronged electron cloud. This electron is the direct cause of frustration and competition in Mn7C3. The competition within the triangular Mn units suggests that the possible magnetic configurations are highly degenerate and that the Mn7C3 spin glass has high robustness. This work introduces a new family of spin glasses with ordered microgeometries that drive electronic structure disorder, and an application-friendly spin-glass material for use in fields like high-efficiency hardware and algorithm design in artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [182] [Multitask Scanning Probe Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09104)
*Aditya Raghavan,Yu Liu,Ian Mercer,JP Maria,Sergei Kalinin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出多任务扫描探针显微镜（multitask scanning probe microscopy）方法，利用多任务高斯过程（multitask Gaussian process）在闭环工作流中自主选择下一测量位置和实验协议，在大面积样品上高效分配多种测量模式，并在AlScN成分梯度晶圆上通过轻敲模式和DART测量的组合实验验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扫描探针显微镜（scanning probe microscopy, SPM）能够提供纳米级的结构、电学、机电、磁学和力学性质表征，但其在晶圆级表征和组合材料探索中需要在大空间范围内高效分配测量资源。不同测量模式在采集时间和探针-样品损伤风险上存在显著差异，使得对空间网格进行穷尽式多模态映射（multimodal mapping）不可行，因此需要一种能自适应分配测量位置和协议的智能工作流。

Method: 作者提出了多任务扫描探针显微镜方法，核心是一个多任务高斯过程模型，它能够同时学习空间关系（spatial relationships）和跨模态关系（cross-modal relationships），并据此在闭环（closed-loop）、实时（live）工作流中自主选择下一测量位置和下一实验协议。该方法实现于自动大样品原子力显微镜（atomic force microscope, AFM）上，采用成对初始测量建立任务间关系，之后利用非重合测量点更新两个响应谱面。

Result: 该方法在成分梯度AlScN晶圆上使用轻敲模式（tapping-mode）和Dual AC Resonance Tracking (DART)测量进行了验证，成功展示了非重合测量点对多个模态响应谱面的同步更新能力。

Conclusion: 该方法将扫描探针显微镜中的主动学习（active learning）从空间采样扩展到了测量模态的自主分配，为将快速、弱扰动的成像模式与较慢的接触、电学、机电、磁学或光谱学测量相结合提供了基础。

Abstract: Scanning probe microscopy provides nanoscale access to structural, electrical, electromechanical, magnetic, and mechanical properties of materials. Its increasing use for wafer-scale characterization and combinatorial materials exploration creates a need to distribute measurements efficiently across large spatial domains. This is particularly important when available modalities differ in acquisition time and potential for tip and sample damage, making exhaustive multimodal mapping over spatial grids impractical. Here, we demonstrate multitask scanning probe microscopy, a live, closed-loop workflow in which a multitask Gaussian process learns spatial and cross-modal relationships and autonomously selects both the next measurement location and the next experimental protocol. The approach is implemented on an automated large-sample atomic force microscope and demonstrated on a composition-spread AlScN wafer using tapping-mode and Dual AC Resonance Tracking (DART) measurements. Paired initial measurements establish the relation between the tasks, after which noncoincident measurements are used to update both response landscapes. The resulting workflow extends active learning in scanning probe microscopy from spatial sampling to autonomous allocation of measurement modalities and provides a basis for combining rapid, weakly perturbative imaging with slower contact, electrical, electromechanical, magnetic, or spectroscopic measurements.

</details>


### [183] [All-Electron Single-Atom Reference Correction for Absolute Transition Energies in Fixed-Reference PAW-XCH Calculations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09216)
*Yinan Wang,Teruyasu Mizoguchi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文针对VASP中密度泛函理论（DFT）计算核心损失谱（core-loss spectrum）过渡能时对绝对能量低估的问题，提出通过自洽单原子计算构造残差原子参考修正（residual atomic reference correction），实现了对K、L1和L2,3核孔（core hole）全电子参考的数值一致性验证，使修正后的光谱接近实验绝对能量尺度，并揭示同元素化学位移由超胞总能的双重差分决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密度泛函理论计算通常会低估核心损失谱的绝对过渡能量，而VASP中同一PAW数据集被同时用于基态和核心激发态占据，当核心占据变化时保留了基态原子参考，遗漏了相关的全电子原子参考变化，导致系统性误差。

Method: 作者利用自洽单原子计算评估全电子原子参考的变化，从价电子参考变化与孤立原子总能响应构造残差原子参考修正；通过与独立的超软赝势（ultrasoft-pseudopotential）实现的对比验证数值一致性，并在Al和Si的L2,3边分析PAW表示对低能3d类未占据态的影响。

Result: 修正后的光谱接近实验绝对能量尺度；K、L1和L2,3核孔的全电子参考与独立超软赝势实现数值一致；对于固定元素、边和核孔方案，原子参考项在能量差中相互抵消，同元素化学位移由超胞总能的双重差分主导；在Al和Si的L2,3边，近边谱形强烈依赖PAW对低能3d类未占据态的表示。

Conclusion: 本文提出的残差原子参考修正方法能够有效弥补VASP中PAW方案在核心激发态下忽略全电子原子参考变化的缺陷，使计算光谱的绝对能量标度与实验对齐，并明确了化学位移的物理来源与PAW表示对近边谱形的影响。

Abstract: The transition energy of a core-loss spectrum comprises a transferable atomic core-reference contribution and a material-dependent response that determines chemical shifts. Density functional theory calculations commonly underestimate absolute transition energies. In VASP, the same PAW dataset is used for the ground and core-excited occupations, retaining the ground-state atomic reference when the core occupation changes and thereby omitting the associated all-electron atomic reference change. We evaluate this change using self-consistent single-atom calculations and construct a residual atomic reference correction from the valence reference change and isolated-atom total-energy response. Comparisons with an independent ultrasoft-pseudopotential implementation demonstrate numerical consistency of the all-electron reference for K, L1, and L2,3 core holes, while representative corrected spectra approach the experimental absolute energy scale. For a fixed element, edge, and core-hole scheme, the atomic reference terms cancel from energy differences, showing that same-element chemical shifts are governed by the double difference of the supercell total energy. At the Al and Si L2,3 edges, the near-edge spectral shape additionally depends strongly on the PAW representation of low-lying 3d-like unoccupied states.

</details>


### [184] [Machine-learning octet $AB$-type binary compounds across chemical space with domain knowledge of the interatomic bond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09257)
*Rohan Kumar,Mariano D. Forti,Aakash A. Naik,Luca M. Ghiringhelli,Thomas Hammerschmidt*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过显式引入原子间成键的领域知识（domain knowledge），利用紧束缚键模型（tight-binding bond model）的递归解生成粗粒化的局部电子结构特征，显著且系统地改进了对八电子AB型二元化合物闪锌矿（zincblende）/岩盐（rocksalt）结构稳定性的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 八电子AB型二元化合物的结构稳定性预测是经典的材料信息学问题，传统结构图和机器学习方法依赖原子级特征（如价电子数、电离势、原子半径），未能充分捕捉电荷转移和原子尺寸差异对4配位与6配位结构相对稳定性的调制作用。

Method: 采用紧束缚键模型的递归解对局部电子结构进行粗粒化表示，编码A-B原子间的成键信息；成对哈密顿量取自双原子分子的密度泛函理论降维本征态。使用核岭回归（Kernel Ridge）或符号回归（symbolic regression）模型集成，结合序贯特征选择进行训练与对比。

Result: 与使用相同从头计算训练集的先前符号回归模型相比，新模型在AB化合物形成能差预测上取得显著改进；并且键信息递归特征数量的增加能够持续提升预测精度。

Conclusion: 显式融入原子间成键的领域知识是一种有效策略，可系统性提升结构稳定性预测模型的准确性，为材料信息学中的特征工程提供了新方向。

Abstract: The prediction of the structural stability of octet $AB$-type binary compounds is a classical materials informatics problem. The challenge is to capture the relative stability of 4-fold coordinated atoms in zincblende ($β$-ZnS) structure and 6-fold coordinated atoms in rocksalt (NaCl) structure, modulated by charge transfer and atomic-size differences. Previous structure maps and machine-learning approaches used atomic features such as valence-electron count, ionization potential and atomic radii, using either physical intuition or symbolic regression. Here, we demonstrate that explicitly incorporating the domain knowledge of the interatomic bonds can significantly and systematically improve the prediction of $β$-ZnS/NaCl stability. We encode this bonding information through a coarse-grained representation of the local electronic structure obtained by a recursive solution of a tight-binding bond model. The underlying pairwise Hamiltonians are taken from downfolded eigenstates of density-functional theory calculations for diatomic molecules and thereby include domain knowledge of the bond between specific $A-B$ pairs. The benefit of this description is demonstrated with an ensemble of independently trained Kernel Ridge or symbolic regression models combined with sequential feature selection. The obtained models are compared to a previous symbolic-regression model using the same set of \emph{ab initio} calculations for octet binaries as training data. We find a significant improvement in the prediction of the formation energy difference of $AB$ compounds as compared to previous works and demonstrate that an increasing amount of bond-informed recursion features improves the predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [185] [Magnetotransport evolution and nonlinear Hall effect in altermagnetic MnTe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09371)
*Wei Zhou,Zhifeng Xue,Yunxing Li,Nannan Tang,Ye Tao,Dingyong Zhong,Jiawei Luo,Donghui Guo,Huichao Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了六方MnTe块体单晶的各向异性磁电阻（anisotropic magnetoresistance, AMR）、平面霍尔效应（planar Hall effect, PHE）和非线性输运行为。在奈尔温度（Néel temperature, TN≈304 K）以下，高温金属态呈现高次谐波，而低温跳跃电导区高阶对称性消失，同时检测到二阶非线性霍尔信号，表明反铁磁MnTe中存在宏观反演不对称响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 六方MnTe是典型的半导体反铁磁体（altermagnet），其性质受本征无序显著影响，但不同输运区间如何塑造其磁输运行为，以及相对论性自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling, SOC）的作用尚未被阐明。

Method: 通过系统性实验测量MnTe块体单晶的各向异性磁电阻（AMR）、平面霍尔效应（PHE）和非线性输运，覆盖高温金属态与低温跳跃电导两个不同的输运区间。

Result: 在奈尔温度（TN≈304 K）以下的高温金属态中，AMR和PHE出现高次谐波，反映磁序、晶体对称性与SOC的相互作用；在较低温度下，高阶对称性消失并与向跳跃电导输运的转变一致，表明载流子局域化削弱了对费米面拓扑的输运敏感性；此外，检测到明显的二阶非线性霍尔信号，证实了反铁磁MnTe中的宏观反演不对称响应。

Conclusion: 这项工作强调了在多种导电机理区间内探索磁输运对于全面理解反铁磁（altermagnetic）性质的必要性，同时揭示无序和SOC在宏观电荷输运中的微妙作用。

Abstract: Hexagonal MnTe is a prototypical semiconducting altermagnet whose properties are heavily influenced by intrinsic disorder, yet how the resulting diverse transport regimes shape its magnetotransport behavior remains to be clarified alongside the role of relativistic spin-orbit coupling (SOC). Here, we present a systematic study of the anisotropic magnetoresistance (AMR), planar Hall effect (PHE), and nonlinear transport in MnTe bulk single crystals. Below the Néel temperature (TN ~ 304 K), the emergence of high-order harmonics in AMR and PHE within the high-temperature metallic regime reveals the interplay of magnetic order, crystalline symmetry, and SOC. At relatively lower temperatures, the disappearance of higher-order symmetries coincides with a transport crossover into the hopping conduction regime, suggesting that carrier localization diminishes the transport sensitivity to the Fermi-surface topology. In addition, we detect distinct second-order nonlinear Hall signals, providing evidence for a macroscopic inversion-asymmetric response in altermagnetic MnTe. Extending the investigations into the localized regime provides key insights into the subtle role of disorder and SOC in macroscopic charge transport. Our work thus underscores the necessity of exploring magnetotransport across diverse conducting regimes to comprehensively understand altermagnetic properties.

</details>


### [186] [Time-resolved study of carbonization and growth of ultrathin 3C-SiC on Si(111) under ultra-high vacuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09431)
*Pranjali Jadhao,Mojdeh Fallahpour,Josef Polčák,Eva Kolíbalová,Michal Horák,Jan Michalička,Petr Bábor,Stanislav Voborný,Tomáš Šikola*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过原位X射线光电子能谱（XPS）与非原位多种表征手段，系统解析了超高真空（UHV）下Si(111)表面经乙烯（C₂H₄）暴露形成碳化硅（SiC）超薄膜的时间演化过程，揭示了从孤岛成核到连续薄膜生长的化学与形态演变规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在解析Si(111)表面SiC超薄膜形成过程中时间依赖的化学与形态演化，以填补对SiC成核与生长早期阶段动态过程的认识空白，为3C-SiC异质外延及后续III族氮化物（III-nitride）等材料在多硅衬底上的生长提供优化依据。

Method: 在800 °C下将清洁的Si(111)衬底暴露于C₂H₄，时间从2分钟至4小时不等，随后依次采用原位XPS以及非原位原子力显微镜（AFM）、扫描电子显微镜（SEM）、俄歇电子能谱（AES）、二次离子质谱（SIMS）和透射电子显微镜（TEM）进行表征。

Result: Si 2p和C 1s谱峰分析显示元素硅逐渐转化为碳化态Si-C相，SiC占比在120至160分钟间超过元素硅，并在180分钟后饱和于约80-81%，残留约19-20%的元素硅；SEM与AFM显示形貌由稀疏孤立岛逐步演化为融合的近连续层；AES深度剖析确认碳掺入近表面区域而非弱吸附污染，岛状结构为早期SiC核；TEM确认闪锌矿晶格及立方碳化硅（3C-SiC）的存在。

Conclusion: 上述结果提供了SiC在Si(111)上成核、聚结与层生长的时分辨图像，对3C-SiC异质外延及SiC/Si(111)作为III族氮化物与碳化物（如Mo₂C）生长模板的优化具有直接参考价值。

Abstract: The early-stage formation of silicon carbide (SiC) on Si(111) by ethylene exposure under ultra-high vacuum (UHV) was investigated to resolve the time-dependent chemical and morphological evolution of an ultrathin layer. Clean Si(111) substrates were exposed to C$_2$H$_4$ at 800 °C for 2 min to 4 h, and the surfaces followed by in-situ X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy (XPS) and ex-situ AFM, SEM, AES, SIMS and TEM. Si 2p and C 1s peak analysis shows the progressive conversion of elemental silicon into a carbidic Si-C phase, the SiC fraction overtaking the elemental component between 120 and 160 min and saturating near 80-81% beyond 180 min, leaving about 19-20% residual elemental silicon. Correlative SEM and AFM reveal a parallel morphological progression, from sparse isolated islands to a coalesced, near-continuous layer. AES depth profiling confirms carbon incorporated into the near-surface region rather than weakly adsorbed as contamination, assigning the islands to early SiC nuclei. TEM confirms the zinc-blende lattice and the presence of cubic silicon carbide (3C-SiC). Together, these results provide a time-resolved picture of SiC nucleation, coalescence and layer growth on Si(111), relevant to 3C-SiC heteroepitaxy, and can be utilized in the optimization of SiC/Si(111) templates for growth of III-nitride and other carbide systems on silicon (e.g. Mo$_2$C).

</details>


### [187] [A spin-bond theory unifying non-relativistic spin splitting and emergent spin-orbit textures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09644)
*S. Allende,R. M. Otxoa*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自旋键理论（spin-bond theory），将偶宇称交变磁体（altermagnet）与奇宇称p波磁体统一到一个代数框架中，揭示两者分别由电子键的厄米自旋振幅与幺正自旋相两分量控制，并发现二者不可交换时出现非共面自旋织构与可动态调谐的综合自旋轨道耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 净磁化消失的磁序可以产生非相对论自旋劈裂能带，但偶宇称交变磁体与奇宇称p波磁体被视为截然不同的现象，缺乏统一的理论框架来阐明其共同的微观起源。

Method: 引入自旋键理论，将电子键拆分为幺正自旋相（unitary spin phases）与厄米自旋振幅（Hermitian spin amplitudes）两个代数分量，分别对应于奇宇称与偶宇称的自旋织构，并通过分析两分量的对易关系识别新的物理区域。

Result: 理论统一了均匀Γ劈裂、键结构交变磁性限制及p波自旋织构等人所熟知的相；进一步发现当幺正与厄米分量不可交换时，产生以动量偶次横向自旋极化为特征的非共面自旋织构，可通过自旋-角分辨光电子能谱直接观测，且该综合自旋轨道耦合可通过几何调控自旋键分量的非对易性来动态调谐。

Conclusion: 该理论为综合自旋轨道耦合的微观调控提供了基础，为实现无外场自旋量子比特等先进应用开辟了途径。

Abstract: Magnetic order with vanishing net magnetization can produce non-relativistic spin-split bands, broadly categorized into even-parity altermagnets and odd-parity \(p\)-wave magnets. Here, we introduce a spin-bond theory that unifies these seemingly distinct phenomena into a single algebraic framework. We demonstrate that non-relativistic spin textures are fundamentally governed by two components of the electronic bond: unitary spin phases and Hermitian spin amplitudes. The unitary sector generates odd-parity p-wave and emergent spin-orbit-like textures, while the Hermitian sector generates even-parity spin fields, including the uniform \(Γ\)-split and bond-structured altermagnetic limits. Beyond unifying known phases, our theory uncovers a mixed non-commuting regime that emerges when the unitary and Hermitian sectors fail to commute, revealing an underlying non-commuting spin-bond structure. This regime generates a non-coplanar spin texture characterized by an even-in-momentum transverse spin polarization, providing a direct spectroscopic fingerprint for spin- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy. Furthermore, we establish that this synthetic spin-orbit coupling can be dynamically tuned by geometrically controlling the non-commutation of the bond sectors. By providing a microscopic foundation for such tuning, our theory paves the way for advanced applications, including field-free spin qubits.

</details>


### [188] [Preserved metallicity and tunable magnetism in Zr-based Janus MXenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09645)
*S. Özcan,B. Biel*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理（first-principles）计算系统研究了Janus ZrMCX2 MXenes（M = Cr, Hf, Nb, Sc, Ti; X = F, Cl, S）的结构、电子、力学和磁性性质。所有组分的负形成能和Born稳定性判据支持其能量与力学稳定性；大多数体系保持金属性，表面官能化可调节费米能级附近的电子态密度并显著影响磁性和力学响应。部分化合物具有铁磁或反铁磁基态，三种呈现半金属性（half-metallicity），且获得可观的磁各向异性能，部分体系Neel温度超过室温。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Janus MXenes凭借化学不对称性为调控二维过渡金属碳化物的性质提供了独特平台，但对其结构稳定性和多功能性质的系统研究尚不充分。作者旨在通过系统的第一性原理计算，评估化学不对称与表面终止对Zr基Janus MXenes电子、磁性和力学性质的调控能力。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算方法，对Janus ZrMCX2体系的结构、电子、力学和磁性性质进行了系统研究；通过形成能和Born稳定性判据验证结构与力学稳定性，并计算了电子态密度、磁性基态、磁各向异性能（magnetic anisotropy energy）及弹性模量等指标。

Result: 绝大多数ZrMCX2体系在强化学不对称条件下仍保持金属性，与常见官能化MXenes的金属-半导体转变形成对比；表面官能化显著影响费米能级附近电子密度及磁性和力学响应。多种化合物具有铁磁或反铁磁（antiferromagnetic）基态，三种显示半金属性，部分体系Neel温度超过室温，且官能化增强弹性刚度。

Conclusion: 化学不对称性和表面终止为实现对磁性及力学性质的有效调控提供了可行途径，同时保持稳健的金属框架；Zr基Janus MXenes被识别为具有前景的金属性和自旋相关二维材料模型体系。

Abstract: Janus MXenes provide a chemically asymmetric platform to tailor the properties of two-dimensional transition metal carbides. Here, we present a systematic first-principles study of the structural, electronic, mechanical, and magnetic properties of Janus ZrMCX2 MXenes (M = Cr, Hf, Nb, Sc, Ti; X = F, Cl, S). All compositions exhibit negative formation energies and satisfy the Born stability criteria, supporting the energetic and mechanical stability of the Janus structures. In contrast to the metal-tosemiconductor transitions frequently reported for functionalized MXenes, we find that the metallic character is preserved in nearly all ZrMCX2 systems, despite the strong chemical asymmetry introduced by the Janus configuration. Surface functionalization nevertheless plays a key role in modulating the electronic density near the Fermi level and strongly influences the magnetic and mechanical responses. Several compounds exhibit ferromagnetic or antiferromagnetic ground states, three display halfmetallicity, and sizable magnetic anisotropy energies are obtained, with Neel temperatures exceeding room temperature in selected cases. Functionalization also enhances the elastic stiffness of the Janus structures. These results demonstrate that chemical asymmetry and surface termination provide effective control over magnetic and mechanical properties while maintaining a robust metallic framework, identifying Zr-based Janus MXenes as promising model systems for metallic and spindependent two-dimensional materials.

</details>


### [189] [Predictive Simulation of Interphases on Li Metal Surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09791)
*Xinyu Li,Jingxuan Ding,Daniel C. Hannah,Yumin Zhang,Qichao Hu,Kang Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种结合通用极化力场（universal polarizable force field）与通用机器学习力场（universal machine learning force field）的模拟方法，能够普遍预测锂金属表面界面相（interphase）的化学组成，从而为AI驱动的电解液发现奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 界面相（interphase）是先进电池中最不为人所理解的组成部分，其性质决定新电池化学能否按设计运行，但由于缺乏原子级层面的界面形成过程知识，一直无法可靠预测新电解液体系会产生何种界面相。

Method: 结合一个可转移的通用极化力场与一个通用机器学习力场，对多种化学组成不同的电解液配方进行界面相化学模拟，直接捕获自发界面反应过程。

Result: 模拟成功重现了实验观察结果：氟化溶剂促进富含LiF的界面相形成，而传统碳酸酯基电解液则产生更具有机来源特征的界面相。模拟建立了电解液化学、盐浓度、分解路径与固体电解质界面（SEI）性质之间的分子级关联。

Conclusion: 该工作开辟了一条通往通用且高通量的界面相预测模拟的途径，使电解液工程不再需要冗长的试错式（Edisonian）方法，为AI驱动的电解液发现奠定基础。

Abstract: Interphases remain the least understood components in advanced batteries. Although their properties dictate whether a new battery chemistry could perform as designed, there has never been a reliable way to predict what an interphase could arise from a new electrolyte system due to the absence of atomistic level knowledge about interphasial formation process. In this work, we attempt to develop a simulation method that can universally predict interphasial chemistries formed on Li metal surface, so that the electrolyte engineering would no longer need lengthy Edisonian approaches. By combining a transferable universal polarizable force field and a universal machine learning force field, we simulate interphasial chemistry across chemically diverse electrolyte formulations, and successfully replicate the experimental observation that fluorinated solvents promote the formation of LiF-rich interphases, whereas interphases of more organic origin arise from conventional carbonate-based electrolytes. By directly capturing these spontaneous interfacial reactions behind these interphasial chemistries, our simulations establish molecular-level relationships between electrolyte chemistry, salt concentration, decomposition pathways, and SEI properties, and opens a route toward universal and high-throughput predictive simulation of interphases that is the foundation for AI-driven electrolyte discoveries.

</details>


### [190] [Polar-vortex-driven interfacial strain coupling in PbTiO3/SrRuO3 Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09815)
*S. A. Raza,V. A. Stoica,H. Zheng,H. G. Lee,A. Ross,U. Saha,L. Q. Chen,L. W. Martin,V. Gopalan,J. W. Freeland*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究在 (PbTiO3)16/(SrRuO3)9/(PbTiO3)16 异质结构中，借助外延应变使铁电层形成约10 nm周期的极性涡旋超结构（polar vortex superstructure），并通过共振X射线反射率（resonant X-ray reflectivity）证实该纳米尺度面内应变调制可传播至 SrRuO3 铁磁层，揭示铁电-铁磁界面间强烈的应变耦合，为纳米磁调制工程提供新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氧化物异质结中的界面耦合是凝聚态物理（condensed-matter physics）的核心问题，它通常在原子尺度通过晶格极化与应变介导的局域相互作用产生；理解并调控这种纳米尺度的界面应变耦合对功能氧化物器件的设计至关重要。

Method: 在 DyScO3(110) 衬底上生长 (PbTiO3)16/(SrRuO3)9/(PbTiO3)16 异质结构，利用适当的外延应变条件使 PbTiO3 层形成周期约10 nm的极性涡旋超结构；采用元素分辨（element-specific）的共振X射线反射率技术，分别探测界面处锶（strontium）与钌（ruthenium）亚晶格的纳米尺度应变调制。

Result: 实验证明 PbTiO3 中极性涡旋超结构引起的面内纳米尺度应变调制可传播进入 SrRuO3 层；对锶与钌亚晶格的元素分辨探测揭示了铁电层与铁磁层之间强烈的界面应变耦合（interfacial strain coupling）。

Conclusion: 本工作为通过界面应变与极化控制来工程化纳米尺度磁性调制（nanoscale magnetic modulation）提供了新的物理见解与实现途径。

Abstract: Interfacial coupling in oxide heterostructures is a central problem in condensed-matter physics, as it typically emerges at the atomic scale through local interactions mediated by lattice polarization and strain. In this work, we investigate nanoscale polar-supertexture-driven interfacial strain coupling in (PbTiO3)16/(SrRuO3)9/(PbTiO3)16 heterostructures grown on DyScO3(110) substrates. Under appropriate epitaxial strain conditions, the PbTiO3 layers form polar vortex superstructures with a periodicity of approximately 10 nm. We demonstrate that the resulting in-plane nanoscale strain modulation propagates into the SrRuO3 layer. Using element-specific resonant X-ray reflectivity, we probe the nanoscale strain modulations of the strontium and ruthenium sublattices at the interface, revealing strong interfacial strain coupling between the ferroelectric and ferromagnetic layers. These findings provide new insights into engineering nanoscale magnetic modulations through interfacial strain and polarization control.

</details>


### [191] [Spatially resolved elastic strain and lattice rotation at threading dislocations in HgCdTe/CdZnTe epilayers by dark-field X-ray microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09841)
*C. Yildirim,A. Benhadjira,P. Ballet,T. N. Tran Caliste,J. Baruchel,C. Detlefs,D. Brellier,M. P. Kabukcuoglu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文采用反射几何自暗场X射线显微术（dark-field X-ray microscopy）对7微米厚(111)取向的Hg1-xCdxTe/Cd1-yZnyTe外延层进行成像，在单帧弱束图像中同时分辨出衬底穿透位错（threading dislocation）及其在层中成核的岛状缺陷，并通过核平均取向差（kernel average misorientation）解析出每个穿透位错对应7微米轴向应变瓣特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从CZT衬底穿透进入MCT外延层的位错决定了中波红外焦平面阵列的少数载流子寿命和暗电流下限，然而在器件级密度下，其局域应变场此前只能通过形貌学方法获取，方法混淆了晶格倾斜与弹性应变的贡献。

Method: 在反射几何下对(111)取向的Hg1-xCdxTe/Cd1-yZnyTe外延层施加暗场X射线显微术；利用浅布拉格角和吸收效应使信号主要来自外延层，同时借助物镜数值孔径使层与衬底的摇摆曲线（rocking curve）卷积；在摇摆曲线两侧采集弱束图像并做差分，在单帧中成像相关缺陷，并结合核平均取向差图测量位错应变特征。

Result: 弱束差分图像在单帧中同时呈现点状衬底穿透位错及其在层内成核的拉长面内岛状特征；核平均取向差图将每个穿透位错分辨为与层厚一致的7微米量级信号，伴随±(4至5)×10^-5的轴向应变瓣。

Conclusion: 反射几何暗场X射线显微术能够在器件级位错密度下直接成像CZT/MCT异质结构中穿透位错及其关联缺陷，并定量提取局域弹性应变场，弥补了传统形貌学方法在区分晶格倾斜与应变方面的不足。

Abstract: Threading dislocations (TDs) propagating from a Cd$_{1-y}$Zn$_{y}$Te (CZT) substrate into a liquid-phase-epitaxy Hg$_{1-x}$Cd$_{x}$Te (MCT) epilayer set the minority-carrier lifetime and dark-current floor of mid-wave infrared focal-plane arrays, yet at device-grade densities their local strain fields have been accessible only through topography, which conflates lattice tilt and elastic strain. We apply dark-field X-ray microscopy in reflection geometry to a \SI{7}{\micro\metre}-thick (111) MCT/CZT epilayer. Shallow Bragg angle and absorption makes the signal layer dominated while the numerical aperture of the objective keeps the layer and substrate rocking curves convolved, so weak-beam images on either side of the rocking curve and their difference image the correlated defects in a single frame: dot-like substrate TDs and the elongated, in-plane island features they nucleate in the layer. Kernel average misorientation resolves each TD as a \SI{7}{\micro\metre} signature, the layer thickness, alongside axial strain lobes of $\pm(4$ to $5)\times10^{-5}$.

</details>


### [192] [Substrate-dependent thermally driven morphological evolution of Pt$_{0.9}$Ni$_{0.1}$ thin films on sapphire and langasite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09909)
*M. Awais Fiaz,M. Greenslit,R. J. Lad,Mauricio Pereira da Cunha,Luke Doucette,S. M. Hollen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过原子力显微镜和图像分割等定量手段，系统追踪了蓝宝石和硅酸镓镧（langasite）衬底上100 nm厚Pt₀.₉Ni₀.₁/Zr合金薄膜在真空退火（室温至800°C）过程中的形貌演化，发现两种衬底上的薄膜均在400–600°C区间经历急剧粗化转变，最终形成小面化晶体，但其尺寸与粗糙度演化路径呈衬底依赖特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解合金薄膜在高温服役条件下的热稳定性及其衬底依赖性形态演化机制，是优化薄膜器件可靠性的关键基础。

Method: 在蓝宝石和硅酸镓镧衬底上沉积100 nm厚Pt₀.₉Ni₀.₁合金薄膜（含10 nm Zr粘附层），进行从室温至800°C的序贯真空退火，并通过原子力显微镜（atomic force microscopy）、图像分割、高度分布分析和有效粗化度量进行量化表征，同时基于Δ⟨r²⟩（其中⟨r²⟩=⟨A⟩/π）进行有效阿伦尼乌斯（Arrhenius）分析。

Result: 两种薄膜在400°C以下保持致密颗粒态，在400–600°C发生急剧粗化转变，至800°C演化为融合的小面化晶体。特征投影面积在硅酸镓镧上从7.84×10²增至1.09×10⁵ nm²，在蓝宝石上从3.32×10²增至1.67×10⁵ nm²；蓝宝石形成更大的小面化晶体，而硅酸镓镧在600°C出现更大粗糙度峰值。面积分布表明该转变以全新大特征集合替代初始颗粒群体而非均匀位移，高度与粗糙度在600°C达到峰值后于800°C部分平滑。

Conclusion: PtNi/Zr薄膜在蓝宝石和硅酸镓镧上的热稳定性由合金迁移率、界面能量学以及衬底依赖的形貌选择三者耦合作用共同决定。

Abstract: We examine the thermal evolution of 100-nm-thick Pt$_{0.9}$Ni$_{0.1}$ alloy films on sapphire and langasite substrates with a 10-nm Zr adhesion layer. Sequential vacuum annealing from room temperature to 800 $^\circ$C produces a substrate-dependent morphological pathway that is quantified by atomic force microscopy, image segmentation, height-distribution analysis, and effective coarsening metrics. Both types of films remain densely granular up to 400 $^\circ$C, undergo a sharp coarsening transition between 400 and 600 $^\circ$C, and evolve into coalesced faceted crystallites by 800 $^\circ$C. The mean projected feature area increases from $7.84 \times 10^2$ to $1.09 \times 10^5$ nm$^2$ on langasite and from $3.32 \times 10^2$ to $1.67 \times 10^5$ nm$^2$ on sapphire. Sapphire therefore develops the larger faceted crystallites, while langasite exhibits the larger roughness maximum at 600 $^\circ$C. Area distributions reveal that the 400-600 $^\circ$C transition does not uniformly shift the initial granular population but instead replaces it with a distinct large-feature ensemble. Height and roughness measurements show a pronounced maximum at 600 $^\circ$C, followed by partial smoothing at 800 $^\circ$C, consistent with rapid coalescence followed by faceting-limited growth. An effective Arrhenius analysis based on $Δ\langle r^2\rangle$, with $\langle r^2\rangle = \langle A\rangle/π$, identifies the 400-600 $^\circ$C interval as the dominant coarsening window. These results show that the thermal stability of PtNi/Zr films on sapphire and langasite is governed by the coupled influence of alloy mobility, interfacial energetics, and substrate-dependent morphological selection.

</details>


### [193] [AES-Debye: an Accurate, Efficient, and Scalable Engine for Debye Scattering Calculations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09916)
*Navid Panchi,Sebastian Kuckuk,Markus Wittmann,Michael Engel,Alberto Leonardi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本论文提出 AES-Debye，一种保持精度的德拜散射方程（Debye scattering equation, DSE）计算框架，通过校正的 bin 中心与数值稳健累加抑制离散化和求和误差，并采用数据局部性感知的并行设计在 CPU/GPU 上高效执行，可在分布式内存 CPU 平台上数分钟内计算包含 9 千万原子的系统的高分辨率全散射（total scattering）谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全散射模型对纳米材料结构与无序性表征至关重要，DSE 提供严格计算路径但直接求代价高昂，而常见的加速策略（如分箱对距离分布或快速傅里叶变换）会引入离散化与混叠伪影，损害漫散射精度。

Method: AES-Debye 框架将成对距离聚合为对分布函数（pair distribution function, PDF），采用校正的 bin 中心和数值稳健累加以抑制离散化与求和误差；同时利用数据局部性感知的并行设计实现 CPU 与 GPU 上的高效执行。

Result: 在分布式内存 CPU 平台上，针对包含 9 千万原子、体积为 (0.1 μm)³ 的系统，在数分钟内完成了高分辨率全散射剖面的计算，展示了强可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该框架将精确弹性全散射计算扩展到大型复杂系统，同时为下游结构分析提供高分辨率 PDF。

Abstract: Total scattering models are essential for characterizing the structure and disorder of nanoscale materials. The Debye scattering equation (DSE) provides a rigorous route to elastic total scattering, but its direct evaluation is computationally demanding because pairwise contributions must be accumulated at every scattering vector, whereas common acceleration strategies based on binned pair-distance distributions or gridded fast Fourier transforms can introduce discretization and aliasing artifacts that compromise diffuse-scattering accuracy. Here, we present AES-Debye, an accuracy-preserving DSE framework that aggregates pair distances into a pair distribution function (PDF) using corrected bin centers and numerically robust accumulation to suppress discretization and summation errors. A data-locality-aware parallel design enables efficient execution on CPUs and GPUs. We demonstrate strong scalability by computing a high-resolution total scattering profile for a system of 90 million atoms, $(0.1,μ\mathrm{m})^{3}$, in minutes on a distributed-memory CPU platform. These capabilities extend accurate elastic total scattering calculations to large, complex systems while simultaneously providing high-resolution PDFs for downstream structural analysis.

</details>


<div id='hep-ex'></div>

### [194] [SuperEM: A Sub-meV Threshold Detector Architecture for Cosmic Neutrino Background and Dark Matter Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08592)
*Zhenjie Li,Xilei Sun,Xiaoshan Jiang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为超导耦合半导体电子倍增（Superconductor-Coupled Semiconductor Electron-Multiplying, SuperEM）探测器的新型结构范式，通过将超导吸收体的超低能量阈值与高偏置高密度半导体P-N结的高增益数字化能力相结合，突破了辐射探测中"不可能三角形"（impossible triangle）的固有局限。信号输运模拟表明，该架构可实现纳秒级瞬态电子漂移，完全在35纳秒内解析，为下一代宇宙中微子背景（CνB）和轻暗物质（Light Dark Matter, LDM）观测站提供了可扩展、高分辨率且快速时间响应的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展辐射探测的运作战域对当代粒子物理、天体物理和宇宙学至关重要。直接探测宇宙中微子背景、确定绝对中微子质量尺度以及搜寻亚GeV轻暗物质需要亚毫电子伏特（sub-meV）能量阈值、优异绝对能量分辨率、快速时间响应以及大规模可扩展性的探测器架构，而现有技术面临"不可能三角形"的内在限制——优化亚毫电子伏特阈值会以牺牲宏观时间响应或空间可扩展性为代价。

Method: 该架构将超导吸收体与强偏置高密度半导体P-N结耦合，入射能量产生非平衡准粒子（quasiparticles），随后通过极薄原子层沉积（Atomic Layer Deposition, ALD）绝缘势垒进行量子隧穿提取。在先前深低温雪崩力学经验验证的基础上，本文建立了完整器件的理论可行性与结构基础，并通过信号输运模拟验证了无掺杂界面配合强漂移场可实现高效纳秒级瞬态电子漂移。

Result: 信号输运模拟证实，无掺杂界面与强漂移场的组合能够实现高效的纳秒级瞬态电子漂移，完全在35纳秒内完成解析。该结果验证了SuperEM架构在超低能量阈值、高时间响应与可大规模扩展性之间同时满足的可行性。

Conclusion: SuperEM架构构成了一种可扩展、高分辨率且快速时间响应的探测框架，适用于下一代宇宙中微子背景和轻暗物质观测站，为突破现有探测器的"不可能三角形"限制提供了基础性解决方案。

Abstract: Expanding the operational boundaries of radiation detection is imperative for contemporary particle physics, astrophysics, and cosmology. At this frontier, the direct detection of the Cosmic Neutrino Background (C$ν$B), the determination of the absolute neutrino mass scale, and the search for sub-GeV Light Dark Matter (LDM) necessitate detector architectures capable of sub-millielectronvolt (sub-meV) energy thresholds, exceptional absolute energy resolution, fast time response, and massive scalability. Current technologies confront an intrinsic limit---the ``impossible triangle''---wherein optimizing for sub-meV thresholds inherently compromises either macroscopic timing response or spatial scalability. Here, we introduce the Superconductor-Coupled Semiconductor Electron-Multiplying (SuperEM) detector, a fundamentally novel structural paradigm designed to bypass this limitation. The architecture couples the ultra-low energy threshold of a superconducting absorber with the intrinsic high-gain digitization of a strongly biased, high-density semiconductor P-N junction. Incident energy yields a proliferation of non-equilibrium quasiparticles, which are subsequently extracted via quantum tunneling across an ultra-thin Atomic Layer Deposition (ALD) insulating barrier. Building upon our prior empirical validation of deep-cryogenic avalanche mechanics, this manuscript establishes the fundamental theoretical feasibility and structural foundation of the complete device. Signal transport simulations confirm that an undoped interface coupled with a strong drift field enables highly efficient, nanosecond-scale transient electron drift, resolving completely within 35 ns. The SuperEM architecture thus constitutes a scalable, high-resolution, and fast time-response framework for next-generation C$ν$B and LDM observatories.

</details>


### [195] [Silent coverage failures in rare-event searches and a degeneracy index that predicts them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09203)
*Davide Pagno*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文研究低背景实验中统计推断程序的覆盖率（coverage）问题，提出泊松-费希尔退化指数（Poisson-Fisher degeneracy index）来量化模型偏离（model departure）对区间估计的影响，并证明标定（calibration）无法弥补模型误设（misspecification）带来的覆盖不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低背景实验中信号强度（signal strength）必须非负且事件数极少，常通过区间估计报告上限。名义频率学派覆盖率（nominal frequentist coverage）既依赖于有效的区间构造，也依赖于充分的数据模型，但模型偏离对覆盖率的系统性影响尚缺乏定量刻画。

Method: 作者在精确泊松计数实验、暗物质反冲谱和双无中微子双贝塔衰变峰搜索三类场景中，对六种区间程序（interval procedures）研究受控模型偏离对上下端点覆盖率的影响。引入泊松-费希尔退化指数 I_PF(δν; ϑ₀) = (β, γ)：将指定期望计数形变投影到完全拟合切空间后，β 为以轮廓标准误（profiled standard-error）为单位的有符号拟合信号偏移，γ 为未吸收残差的泊松-费希尔范数，并利用饱和泊松拟合优度检验（saturated-Poisson goodness-of-fit test）在5%第一类错误率下评估可探测性。

Result: 局部而言，β 的符号标识受威胁的端点；γ 越大则形变越易被拟合优度检验发现。正信号类偏差使发现侧（discovery-side）覆盖退化同时使上限趋于保守；负信号偏差（如高估信号效率）可使上限端点出现覆盖不足。基准模拟器下的标定无法防止其相对数据生成过程的误设；|β|大而γ小的形变可能逃避诊断，需通过受约束的辅助信息或防御性包络（envelope）加以表征。在完全共线约束干扰项基准中，建模形变可恢复所评网格点的覆盖率，但以可量化的区间灵敏度（sensitivity）损失为代价。

Conclusion: 模型误设对低背景实验区间估计的覆盖率有方向性且可量化的影响；名义标定并不稳健，需通过显式建模形变或引入受约束干扰项来保障覆盖性质。

Abstract: Searches for new physics in low-background experiments infer a non-negative signal strength from few events and often report an upper limit. Nominal frequentist coverage requires both a valid interval construction and an adequate data model. We study how controlled model departures affect lower- and upper-endpoint coverage for six interval procedures in an exact Poisson counting experiment, dark-matter recoil spectra, and a neutrinoless-double-beta-decay peak search. We introduce the Poisson--Fisher degeneracy index $\mathcal I_{\mathrm{PF}}(δν;\vartheta_0)=(β,γ)$, which maps a specified expected-count deformation, after projection onto the complete fitted tangent space, to $β$, the signed fitted signal shift in profiled standard-error units, and $γ$, the Poisson--Fisher norm of the unabsorbed residual. Locally, the sign of $β$ identifies the threatened endpoint, while larger $γ$ implies greater detectability by the saturated-Poisson goodness-of-fit test used here at a fixed $5\%$ type-I error rate. Across the studied deformations, positive signal-like bias degrades discovery-side coverage while making upper limits conservative; negative signal bias from overestimated signal efficiency can make the upper endpoint undercover. Calibration under the nominal simulator does not protect against misspecification of that simulator relative to the data-generating process. A plausible deformation with large $|β|$ and small $γ$ may therefore evade diagnosis and should be represented by a nuisance constrained with auxiliary information or included in a defensible envelope. In the exactly collinear constrained-nuisance benchmark, modelling the deformation restores coverage at the evaluated grid points, at a quantifiable cost in interval sensitivity.

</details>


### [196] [Amplitude analysis for charmed meson decays at BESIII](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09338)
*Han Zhang,Zhenxuan Li,Chunyi Guan,Zehui Lu,Hui Li,Liping Yang,Yechun Yu,Nan Zhang,Minggang Zhao,Yu Lu,Bai-Cian Ke,Liaoyuan Dong*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文由BESIII合作组撰写，系统综述了多体粲介子衰变振幅分析（amplitude analysis）的方法论，涵盖概率密度函数与似然函数的构建、归一化技术、探测效率与分辨率修正、多维本底建模（基于XGBoost分类器）、强子与半轻子衰变的振幅形式体系，以及拟合分数、运动学投影和统计不确定度估计等关键分析环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体粲介子衰变的实验测量与中间二体过程的理论预言之间存在鸿沟，振幅分析是弥合这一差距的关键工具。本文旨在为相关研究者提供一套完整、实用且可操作的振幅分析方法论参考。

Method: 文章对BESIII合作组所使用的振幅分析方法进行了系统性梳理，包括构建用于无分箱最大似然拟合的概率密度函数和似然函数；利用蒙特卡洛积分实现归一化处理；将探测效率和分辨率效应纳入分析流程；采用XGBoost分类器进行多维本底建模；同时介绍了强子衰变与半轻子衰变所适用的振幅形式体系及标准共振传播子参数化方案。

Result: 作者完整呈现了振幅分析方法的核心计算细节，覆盖了拟合分数（fit fraction）的计算、运动学投影的产生以及统计不确定度的估计，为实际开展此类分析提供了系统化的方法指南。

Conclusion: 该综述为粲介子多体衰变研究提供了一套完整且经过实践验证的振幅分析框架，有助于缩小实验测量与理论预言之间的差距，并可推广应用于其他涉及多体衰变的物理分析场景。

Abstract: Amplitude analysis bridges the gap between experimental measurements of multibody charmed-meson decays and theoretical predictions of intermediate two-body processes. This work presents a comprehensive overview of the amplitude-analysis methodology employed by the BESIII Collaboration, emphasizing practical implementation. We detail the construction of the probability density function and likelihood function for unbinned maximum-likelihood fits. This encompasses Monte Carlo integration techniques for normalization, the incorporation of detection efficiency and resolution effects, and multidimensional background modeling utilizing XGBoost classifiers. Furthermore, we describe the amplitude formalism for both hadronic and semileptonic decays, incorporating standard resonance-propagator parametrizations. Key analytical aspects, including the evaluation of fit fractions, the generation of kinematic projections, and the estimation of statistical uncertainties, are also discussed.

</details>


### [197] [Automated Signal Integrity Analysis Framework for High-Speed Interconnects in the PPCB-1347-MuPix11 Probe Card](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09462)
*D M S Sultan,R. Plackett,A. E. McDougall,A. J. A. Knight,A. S. Rotelli,J. Vossebeld,D. Bortoletto*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可复用的MATLAB信号完整性（signal integrity，SI）分析框架，将兼容的四端口S参数数据（由VNA测量或电磁仿真获得）转化为可追溯的链路级证据，并在PPCB-1347-MuPix11探针卡的四条1.25 Gbps差分通道上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上仅用单一损耗指标评估高速互连的信号完整性，难以提供可追溯的链路级证据；路径长度等单一变量也被证明不足以作为SI排序依据。需要一个自动化的、可扩展的框架来系统性地比较不同差分路由的信号完整性表现。

Method: 框架采用自动化流水线：预检文件结构、执行功率归一化混合模式变换（power-normalized mixed-mode transformation）、进行路径长度感知的损耗分解（route-length-aware loss decomposition）、构建因果加载体信道模型，并将完整PRBS-31序列传播至眼图（eye-diagram）、条件误码率（conditional-BER）及8b10b编码链路分析中。

Result: 在1.25 Gbps（奈奎斯特频率0.625 GHz）下，DP1–DP4的差分插入损耗（SDD21）范围为-0.350至-0.300 dB，差分到共模转换范围为-31.038至-28.236 dBc，建模的前端输入眼图张开幅度为0.586至0.588 V。路径长度不足以排名：DP3奈奎斯特插入损耗最低，DP1差分共模隔离最强，DP4建模眼图最大；所有解析BER值低于报告下限，无法支持BER排序。

Conclusion: 该框架通过保留路由相关的波形行为、模型预测与有限记录观测之间的区分，提供了从设计审查到校准VNA测量解释之间可扩展的高速互连SI比较分析基础。

Abstract: A reusable MATLAB signal-integrity (SI) framework is presented that converts compatible four-port S-parameter data, measured by VNA or obtained from electromagnetic simulation, into traceable link-level evidence rather than a single loss metric. The framework is demonstrated on the four 1.25 Gbps differential routes (DP1-DP4) of the PPCB-1347-MuPix11 probe card using PTSL CST Microwave 3D-Solver-derived four-port S-parameters and a virtual time-domain solver. The automated pipeline preflights file structures, performs a power-normalized mixed-mode transformation, applies route-length-aware loss decomposition, constructs a causally loaded channel model, and propagates full PRBS-31 sequences into eye-diagram, conditional-BER, and 8b10b-coded-link analyses. At the 1.25 Gbps data rate (Nyquist 0.625 GHz), DP1-DP4 exhibit differential insertion loss (SDD21) from -0.350 to -0.300 dB, differential-to-common conversion from -31.038 to -28.236 dBc, and modeled FEB-input eye openings from 0.586 to 0.588 V. The comparison shows that path length alone is not an adequate SI ranking variable: DP3 has the lowest Nyquist insertion loss, DP1 the strongest differential-to-common isolation, and DP4 the largest modeled eye. All analytical BER values remain below the reporting floor and therefore do not support a BER ranking. By preserving the distinction between route-dependent waveform behavior, model projections, and finite-record observations, the framework provides an extensible basis for comparative high-speed-interconnect SI analysis from design review through calibrated VNA measurement interpretation.

</details>


<div id='math.CA'></div>

# math.CA [[Back]](#toc)

### [198] [A Riemann-Hilbert representation for Sobolev orthogonal polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08397)
*Alex Little*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究相对于 Sobolev 内积（Sobolev inner product）的正交多项式，其中权重函数含有多项式势能 V(x)，V 为偶次（至少四次）且首项系数为正的多项式，并含有一个固定的正参数 λ。作者证明这类 Sobolev 正交多项式可以表示为第一型多重正交多项式（multiple-orthogonal polynomial）的特例，该对应关系借助二阶常微分方程的 WKB 渐近分析建立。基于此，作者进一步给出了 Sobolev 正交多项式所满足的 Riemann–Hilbert 问题，并由此推导出投影核（projection kernel）的 Christoffel–Darboux 型公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sobolev 正交多项式在数理分析中具有重要地位，但相较于经典正交多项式，其结构理论尚不完善。本文旨在通过建立 Sobolev 正交多项式与多重正交多项式之间的联系，为这类多项式提供 Riemann–Hilbert 问题框架和 Christoffel–Darboux 型核公式。

Method: 本文考虑带有多项式势能 V（偶次、次数至少为四、首项系数为正）的 Sobolev 内积，并引入正参数 λ 控制导数项的贡献。作者将对应的 Sobolev 正交多项式表示为第一型多重正交多项式，构建该对应关系的桥梁是一个特定二阶常微分方程的 WKB 渐近分析（WKB asymptotics）。利用该对应关系，作者写出 Sobolev 正交多项式的 Riemann–Hilbert 问题，并从中推导投影核的 Christoffel–Darboux 型公式。

Result: 论文建立了 Sobolev 正交多项式与第一型多重正交多项式之间的精确对应，给出了相应的 Riemann–Hilbert 问题公式，并推导出投影核满足的 Christoffel–Darboux 型公式。

Conclusion: 作者通过将 Sobolev 正交多项式纳入多重正交多项式框架，开辟了利用 Riemann–Hilbert 方法研究这类多项式的路径，并获得了投影核的结构性公式。

Abstract: In this article we consider polynomials orthogonal with respect to the inner product $$\langle P,Q \rangle_S = \int_{\mathbb{R}}P(x)Q(x) \mathrm{e}^{-V(x)} \, \mathrm{d}x + λ\int_{\mathbb{R}}P^\prime(x)Q^\prime(x) \mathrm{e}^{-V(x)} \, \mathrm{d}x$$ where $V$ is a polynomial of even degree (at least four) and positive leading coefficient, and $λ> 0$ a constant. The above inner product is a special case of the so-called Sobolev inner product. We show how one can represent the associated Sobolev orthogonal polynomials as a species of Type I multiple-orthogonal polynomial. This correspondence makes use of the WKB asymptotics of a certain second order ODE. From this we may write a Riemann--Hilbert problem for the Sobolev orthogonal polynomials, from which one can deduce a Christoffel--Darboux-type formula for the projection kernel.

</details>


### [199] [Generalized Freud weight, discrete Painlevé I hierarchy and full asymptotics of Hankel determinants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08455)
*Wen-Gao Long,Lin-Rong Tang,Chao Min*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究广义Freud权（generalized Freud weight）下的首一正交多项式（monic orthogonal polynomials）及Hankel行列式（Hankel determinants），建立了离散Painlevé I层级（discrete Painlevé I hierarchy）的统一结构，并推导了递推系数的二阶微分方程、偏导数、微分恒等式、新的偏微分方程以及完整渐近展开。所有结果在十次Freud权（decic Freud weight）下得到具体验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义Freud权是正交多项式理论中的重要研究对象，其相关的递推系数与Hankel行列式在可积系统（integrable systems）和随机矩阵理论（random matrix theory）中有广泛应用。建立离散Painlevé I层级的统一结构并为这些量提供完整的渐近行为，是推动该领域理论发展的关键。

Method: 利用梯子算子（ladder operators）与相容性条件（compatibility conditions）导出离散Painlevé I层级；结合线性差分方程（linear difference equations）的渐近理论推导完整渐近展开；通过离散Painlevé I层级与微分恒等式获得满足lnβ_n和lnD_n的偏微分方程。

Result: 递推系数β_n满足第m个离散Painlevé I层级成员；推导了β_n满足的二阶微分方程、β_n对参数t_1,...,t_{m-1}的偏导数及D_n相应的微分恒等式；获得lnβ_n和lnD_n满足的新偏微分方程；得到β_n、p(n;T_m;λ)和D_n(T_m;λ)在n→∞时的完整渐近展开，其中对数项ln n仅出现在leading-order贡献中而非余项中。

Conclusion: 本文为广义Freud权下的正交多项式与Hankel行列式建立了完整的理论体系，涵盖离散Painlevé I层级、微分方程、偏微分方程和渐近展开，并以十次Freud权为例验证了所有结果的有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the monic orthogonal polynomials $P_{n}(x;T_{m};λ)$ and the Hankel determinants $D_{n}(T_{m}; λ)$ associated with the generalized Freud weight \[w(x;T_{m};λ) = |x|^{2λ+1}\exp\biggl(-\sum_{k=1}^m t_k x^{2k}\biggr),\quad m \in \mathbb{Z}^+,\; t_{k} \in \mathbb{R} , x\in\mathbb{R}\setminus\{0\},\] where \(T_{m}=\{t_{1},t_{2},\cdots, t_{m}\}\), $t_{m}>0$ and \(λ>-1\).By employing ladder operators and compatibility conditions, we find that all members of the discrete Painlevé I hierarchy have a unified structure and the recurrence coefficient \(β_n\) of $P_{n}(x;T_{m};λ)$ satisfies the $m$-th member of the discrete Painlevé I hierarchy. Besides, we derive the second-order differential equation satisfied by $P_{n}(x;T_{m};λ)$, the partial derivatives of the recurrence coefficients \(β_n\) with respect to parameters \(t_1, t_2, \dots, t_{m-1}\) and the corresponding differential identities for $D_{n}(T_{m}; λ)$. Based on the discrete Painlevé I hierarchy and the above differential identities, we obtain new partial differential equations satisfied by $\lnβ_n$ and $\ln D_{n}(T_{m}; λ)$.Using the discrete Painlevé I hierarchy and the asymptotic theory of linear difference equations, we derive the full asymptotic expansions of the recurrence coefficient $β_n$, the nontrivial leading coefficient $\mathrm{p}(n; T_m; λ)$, and the Hankel determinant $D_n(T_m; λ)$ as $n\to\infty$, for general $T_m$ and $λ>-1$. Notably, while the logarithmic term $\ln n$ appears in the leading-order contributions, it is absent from the remainder terms in these expansions.We illustrate our results under the specific decic Freud weight $w(x;t_1,t_2;λ)=|x|^{2λ+1} \exp\bigl(-x^{10}-t_2x^4-t_1x^2\bigr)$.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [200] [Leveraging generative models to assist Monte Carlo sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07648)
*Marylou Gabrié*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文是一篇教程性综述（tutorial review），探讨利用生成模型（generative models）作为采样工具的新范式。该范式将归一化流（normalizing flows）和扩散模型（diffusion models）等生成模型从传统的数据驱动设定中解放出来，用于采样仅已知到归一化常数的概率分布，旨在解决高维缩放和模态分布采样两大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维概率分布采样是科学计算的核心任务，尽管经典方法（如马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛、退火方法、基于集体变量的增强采样）取得了成功，但它们在处理高维度规模和亚稳态多模态分布时仍面临固有局限。作者旨在引入机器学习与计算统计物理交叉催生的新范式，并为其提供一篇面向物理与机器学习两个领域的可理解教程。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，回顾了生成模型辅助采样这一新兴领域的早期发展。文章不追求文献的穷尽调查，而是选择性地介绍关键思想和方法，包括基于生成模型的精确采样器（exact samplers）以及在没有数据情况下训练这类模型的策略，并讨论其优缺点。

Result: 文章呈现了将归一化流和扩散模型用作灵活概率模型以辅助采样分布（其仅已知到归一化常数）的多种方法论方向，并系统讨论了这些方法的优势与局限，为感兴趣的读者提供了进入该领域的起点。

Conclusion: 作者指出，生成模型作为采样工具是一种快速发展的新范式，有望弥合经典采样的局限，但对物理和机器学习两个领域的读者而言仍处于早期探索阶段，需要进一步研究以完善其理论基础与应用范围。

Abstract: Sampling high-dimensional probability distributions is a central task in scientific computing, with applications ranging from Bayesian inference to statistical physics and molecular simulation. Despite decades of methodological developments, two major challenges remain: scaling to high dimensions and efficiently exploring multimodal distributions characterized by metastable states. Classical approaches such as Markov chain Monte Carlo, tempering methods, or enhanced sampling based on collective variables have achieved major successes, but they also face intrinsic limitations. This tutorial review explores a new paradigm that has recently emerged at the interface of machine learning and computational statistical physics: the use of generative models as tools for sampling. In this context, models such as normalizing flows and diffusion models are not used in their traditional data-driven setting, but rather as flexible probabilistic models that can assist the sampling of distributions known only up to a normalization constant. This manuscript reviews the early development of this rapidly evolving field and discusses several methodological directions, including exact samplers based on generative models and strategies to train such models in the absence of data. While an exhaustive survey of the literature is not attempted, we present a selection of key ideas and methods, along with a discussion of their strengths and limitations. The review is intended to be an accessible tutorial for both physics and machine learning audiences, and it aims to provide a starting point for researchers interested in exploring this exciting area of research.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.HE'></div>

# astro-ph.HE [[Back]](#toc)

### [201] [Constraints on magnetic monopoles from X-ray observations of neutron stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07652)
*Mainak Mukhopadhyay,Daniele Perri,Edward W. Kolb*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用钱德拉（Chandra）、XMM-Newton和Swift-XRT等望远镜对年老孤立毫秒脉冲星（old isolated millisecond pulsars）的存档X射线观测数据，对银河系磁单极子通量（monopole flux, $F_M$）给出了保守的上限约束。在基准催化截面下，得到的通量上限较此前中子星约束有所改进，并在$10^{11}-10^{13}$ GeV/c²质量区间提供了迄今最强的约束，同时从七仙女座（Magnificent Seven）的热辐射中推导出互补约束，表明中子星X射线观测是探测磁单极子的有力工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁单极子（magnetic monopole）若被中子星俘获并催化核子衰变（nucleon decay），衰变产物会热化并产生可观测的X射线表面辐射，因此中子星X射线观测可作为探测磁单极子的天然实验室。已有中子星约束尚不够严格，作者希望利用更为古老的孤立毫秒脉冲星的观测数据来改进对银河系磁单极子通量的上限。

Method: 作者使用存档的钱德拉、XMM-Newton和Swift-XRT观测数据，分析年老孤立毫秒脉冲星的X射线辐射以限制磁单极子诱导的加热效应。在基准催化截面$\sigma_{\Delta B} \sim 10^{-27}$ cm²下，推导了磁单极子通量关于其质量$m_M$的保守上限函数，并进一步利用七仙女座的测量热辐射推导了互补约束。

Result: 得到通量上限$F_M(m_M) \lesssim 6 \times 10^{-19}$ cm⁻²s⁻¹sr⁻¹ 与质量相关因子乘积的解析表达式。这些限制改进了先前的中子星约束，在$10^{11}-10^{13}$ GeV/c²质量范围内提供了迄今为止对磁单极子通量最强的限制，并与现有其他约束相当。

Conclusion: 中子星X射线观测为探测磁单极子提供了强有力的途径，结果支持开展针对年老中子星的专项X射线巡测，以检验磁单极子诱导加热机制（monopole-induced heating）。

Abstract: Magnetic monopoles are captured efficiently by neutron stars, and if they catalyze nucleon decay, the decay products would thermalize and generate observable X-ray surface emission. We use archival Chandra, XMM-Newton, and Swift-XRT data for old isolated millisecond pulsars to place conservative limits on the Galactic monopole flux ($F_M$). For a benchmark cross-section of $σ_{Δ\rm B} \sim 10^{-27}\ {\rm cm^2}$, our constraint as a function of monopole mass $m_M$ is given by $F_{\rm M}(m_M) \lesssim 6 \times 10^{-19}~\mathrm{cm^{-2}s^{-1}sr^{-1}} \times \max \big(4 \times 10^{-6}, \min (2 \times 10^{11}~\mathrm{(GeV/c^2)}/m_{\rm M} , 1 ) \big)$. These limits improve previous neutron-star bounds, provide the strongest constraints to date on $F_M$ for $m_M$ between $10^{11} - 10^{13}\ {\rm GeV/c^2}$, and are competitive to existing constraints for this scenario. We also derive complementary constraints from the measured thermal emission of the Magnificent Seven. Our results demonstrate that neutron star X-ray observations provide a powerful probe of magnetic monopoles and motivate dedicated X-ray searches for old neutron stars as a means to test monopole-induced heating.

</details>


### [202] [Fast and Accurate Prediction of Neutron Star Structure with Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07698)
*Kaushikk V N,Bhaskar Biswas,Stephan Rosswog*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文开发了基于神经网络的替代模型（neural-network surrogate），用于预测中子星的质量、半径和潮汐形变参数，通过残差网络（residual network）和前馈网络（feedforward network）两种架构来加速求解Tolman–Oppenheimer–Volkoff (TOV)方程，在不牺牲精度的前提下将计算速度提升约两个数量级，适用于大规模贝叶斯推断（Bayesian inference）任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在贝叶斯推断致密物质状态方程（equation-of-state, EOS）的过程中，对大量EOS样本求解TOV方程及潮汐扰动方程构成主要计算瓶颈；随着下一代观测设备提供更大规模、更精确的数据集，这一问题将愈发严峻，因此亟需更高效的向前映射求解方法。

Method: 作者开发了从EOS参数和中子星中心密度直接预测星体质量、半径及潮汐形变参数的神经网络替代模型，并对比了传统前馈网络与残差网络两种架构。模型在分段多项式型（piecewise polytropic）EOS参数空间上进行训练，并与直接数值积分结果进行比较验证。

Result: 两种架构在所有三个观测量的预测中决定系数（coefficient of determination）均超过0.999，验证精度极高；相对于直接数值积分，计算速度提升了约两个数量级。残差网络在精度上略有改进，但推理时间略长，总体性能差异较小，表明前馈网络已具备充分的表达能力。

Conclusion: 前馈网络足以胜任该映射任务，而残差连接仅提供增量式改进；尽管如此，残差架构为未来扩展至更丰富的EOS参数化形式或更高维回归任务提供了稳健的基线。所开发的替代模型非常适用于大规模贝叶斯EOS推断和群体统计研究，可有效避免重复TOV求解占据主导计算成本的问题。

Abstract: Solving the Tolman--Oppenheimer--Volkoff (TOV) equations, together with the tidal perturbation equations, for large numbers of equation-of-state (EOS) samples is a major computational bottleneck in Bayesian inference of the dense-matter EOS, and this will become increasingly limiting as next-generation observatories deliver far larger and more precise datasets. We develop neural-network surrogates for the forward TOV mapping that predict neutron star mass, radius, and tidal deformability simultaneously and directly from the EOS parameters and central density. We train and compare two architectures: a conventional feedforward network and a residual network, the latter of which, to our knowledge, has not previously been explored for TOV surrogate modeling. Trained on a piecewise polytropic EOS parameter space, both networks reproduce the numerical solutions to high accuracy, with the coefficient of determination exceeding 0.999 for all three observables, while accelerating the evaluation of stellar observables by roughly two orders of magnitude relative to direct numerical integration. We find that both architectures achieve excellent predictive accuracy at the network sizes considered here, with the residual network providing a modest improvement in accuracy over the feedforward network at the expense of slightly longer inference times. The overall performance differences remain small, indicating that a feedforward network already has sufficient capacity for this mapping while residual connections offer only incremental gains. Nevertheless, the residual architecture provides a robust baseline for future extensions to richer EOS parameterizations or higher-dimensional regression tasks. The resulting surrogates are well-suited to large-scale Bayesian EOS inference and population studies, where repeated TOV evaluations would otherwise dominate the computational cost.

</details>


### [203] [Capture Driven Evolution of Asymmetric Dark Matter in Non-rotating Neutron Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07729)
*Peiran Liu,Wenrong Sun*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一个将暗物质（dark matter）俘获与中子星长期结构演化联系起来的自洽框架，利用三层多方物态方程和双流体Tolman–Oppenheimer–Volkoff方程建模，发现即使在极端条件下暗物质累积对中子星结构和引力波可观测量的影响仍然有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究尚未系统量化暗物质俘获驱动下的不对称暗物质（asymmetric dark matter）在中子星内长期累积对引力波观测量的影响。作者旨在建立一个自洽框架，覆盖天体物理时间尺度，以评估这种效应是否能在当前及近期引力波观测中产生可探测信号。

Method: 采用三种物态方程（polytropic equation of state）建模冷、非旋转中子星的三层结构，并通过双流体Tolman–Oppenheimer–Volkoff方程将中子星物质与暗物质组分耦合；同时引入时间依赖的暗物质俘获形式，重构中子星的长期演化过程。研究覆盖广泛的天体物理条件，包括为最大化俘获效率而设计的极端环境，并在哈勃时间尺度上推导暗物质累积效应的保守上限。

Result: 即使在刻意乐观的假设下，累积的暗物质组分仍处于次主导地位，仅引起有限的结构修饰。具体而言，暗物质累积会增加恒星致密度（compactness），同时抑制Love数和潮汐形变率（tidal deformability）。显著的演化仅出现在类银心（Galactic-center-like）等极端环境中；在现实银河系或星系团暗物质密度下，相应偏差可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 俘获驱动的暗物质累积不太可能在中子星的当前或近期引力波观测中产生可探测的信号特征。

Abstract: We develop a self-consistent framework that connects dark matter capture to the long term accumulation and structural evolution of neutron stars, allowing us to quantify the impact of capture driven asymmetric dark matter on gravitational wave observables over astrophysical timescales. We model cold, non-rotating neutron stars using a three layer polytropic EoS coupled to a dark matter component through the two fluid Tolman Oppenheimer Volkoff equations, and reconstruct the long term evolution using a time dependent dark matter capture formalism. By exploring a wide range of astrophysical conditions, including extreme environments designed to maximize the capture efficiency, we derive conservative upper bounds on the effects of dark matter accumulation over a Hubble time. Even under these deliberately optimistic assumptions, the accumulated dark matter component remains subdominant and induces limited structural modifications. In particular, dark matter accumulation increases the stellar compactness while suppressing the Love number and tidal deformability. Significant evolution occurs only in extreme Galactic center like environments over Hubble time scales, whereas for realistic Galactic or cluster dark matter densities the corresponding deviations remain negligible. We therefore conclude that capture driven dark matter accumulation is unlikely to produce detectable signatures in current or near future gravitational wave observations of neutron stars.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.dis-nn'></div>

# cond-mat.dis-nn [[Back]](#toc)

### [204] [Composite-State Localization Beyond the External Landscape in Non-Hermitian Quasicrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08105)
*Tian Zhou,Xue-Bin Wang,Zhongmin Yang,Tao Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该论文通过研究相互作用非厄米准周期梯子（interacting non-Hermitian quasiperiodic ladder），展示了复合激发（composite excitation）与未束缚激发（unbound excitation）之间的局域化反转（localization inversion）现象，证明内部构型（internal configuration）可作为局域化的可逆控制参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观念认为复合激发的局域化行为通常继承自其组分，论文旨在探索是否存在可逆地控制局域化反转的机制，并寻找超越外部势的新型控制参数。

Method: 论文构建了具有相反复势的两条腿组成的相互作用非厄米准周期梯子模型，利用同梯级对偶（same-rung pair）在一阶抵消外部复势的特性，结合强耦合理论（strong-coupling theory）分析复合态与未束缚态的局域化行为。

Result: 研究发现复合态可保持扩展（extended）状态而未被配对的部分局域化；强耦合理论表明复合态表现为由虚拟未束缚构型产生的弱调制的非厄米准晶。破坏势反对称性可恢复复合能带的直接调制并反转局域化层级；通过工程化构型空间路径，可稳定嵌入局域化连续谱（localized continuum）内的扩展复合能带，即传统束缚态在连续谱中（bound state in the continuum）的反转情形。

Conclusion: 论文确立了内部构型作为局域化的可逆控制参数，为调控复合激发系统提供了新的自由度与物理机制。

Abstract: A composite excitation need not inherit the localization behavior of its constituents. We show that an interacting non-Hermitian quasiperiodic ladder realizes a controllable and reversible localization inversion between composite and unbound excitations, where internal configuration, rather than only the external potential, becomes a control parameter for localization. Opposite complex potentials on the two legs cancel at first order for a same-rung pair but act directly on separated particles, allowing extended composite states to persist while the unpaired sector becomes localized. A strong-coupling theory identifies the composite state as an emergent weakly modulated non-Hermitian quasicrystal generated by virtual unpaired configurations. Breaking the potential antisymmetry restores a direct modulation of the composite band and reverses the localization hierarchy. Engineering configuration-space pathways further stabilizes an extended composite band embedded within a localized continuum, the inverse of the conventional bound-state-in-the-continuum scenario. Our results establish internal configuration as a reversible control parameter for localization.

</details>


### [205] [Clustered Attractor Manifolds and Dynamical Condensation in Self-Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08922)
*Qucheng Gao,Zuyi Yang,Xiao Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文在最小归一化自注意力动力学中研究Transformer层产生的状态依赖交互网络，识别出重叠间隙（overlap gap）为控制其吸引子结构的关键量，并发现注意力凝聚（attention condensation）转变机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer层通过注意力矩阵形成状态依赖的交互网络，token表示决定注意力矩阵，注意力矩阵又更新表示，形成反馈回路。理解这一反馈在热力学极限下如何产生吸引子结构是论文的核心动机。

Method: 作者在最小归一化自注意力（normalized self-attention）动力学框架下进行分析，识别出重叠间隙（overlap gap）作为控制吸引子结构的关键量，并在热力学极限下研究token聚类间的相互作用与稳定性。

Result: 当token形成内部对齐的聚类且类内相似度超过类间相似度一个非消失量时，类间注意力随维度增大呈指数级抑制。这产生了从少数宏观聚类到广泛微观碎片化的高维聚类不动点流形。从非结构化高斯态出发，聚类态仅在注意力锐度超过有限阈值时从弥散背景中成核，产生动态注意力-凝聚转变（attention-condensation transition）。

Conclusion: 研究中发现的聚类不动点流形及其稳定性机制揭示了Transformer自注意力动力学中吸引子结构的普适特征，注意力凝聚转变则为理解注意力锐度阈值行为提供了动力学框架。

Abstract: Transformer layers generate state-dependent interaction networks: token representations determine the attention matrix, which in turn updates the representations. We study this feedback in a minimal normalized self-attention dynamics and identify the overlap gap as the central quantity governing its attractor structure in the thermodynamic limit. When tokens form internally aligned clusters and their similarity to members of the same cluster exceeds that to every other cluster by a nonvanishing amount, inter-cluster attention is exponentially suppressed as the dimension increases. This mechanism produces a high-dimensional manifold of clustered fixed points, ranging from a few macroscopic clusters to extensive microscopic fragmentation, and also controls their stability against perturbations. Starting from an unstructured Gaussian state, we find that clustered states nucleate from the diffuse background only above a finite threshold in attention sharpness, giving rise to a dynamical attention-condensation transition.

</details>

### [206] [Environmental Noise Challenges for the Einstein Telescope - A Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07633)
*Matteo Di Giovanni*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文回顾了爱因斯坦望远镜（Einstein Telescope, ET）——下一代地面干涉式引力波探测器的环境噪声研究现状，总结了地基选址与基础设施设计的主要挑战，并概述了为确保探测器最优性能而正在开发的相关策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ET的灵敏度比现有探测器（如Virgo、LIGO和KAGRA）高约八个量级，覆盖低至2 Hz的低频段，这使其对环境扰动（尤其2-10 Hz区间的噪声）更为敏感。因此，理解和抑制环境噪声是充分发挥ET科学潜力的关键前提。

Method: 本文为一篇综述性章节，系统梳理了ET环境噪声的现有研究成果，涉及低频段内人为噪声（anthropogenic noise）、地震噪声（seismic noise）和基础设施噪声的特征分析，以及选址表征（site characterization）与基础设施设计相关的挑战讨论。

Result: 文章总结了ET面临的主要环境噪声挑战，识别出2-10 Hz频率范围内人为、地震及基础设施噪声为最突出干扰源，并概述了当前正开发的环境噪声缓解策略，以确保ET在低频段获得预期探测灵敏度。

Conclusion: 理解并抑制环境噪声是充分发挥ET科学潜能的前提；综述指出，通过完善的选址表征和基础设施优化设计，有望实现ET高频谱灵敏度的科学目标，并为其后续建设与运行提供指导。

Abstract: Einstein Telescope (ET) will be a next-generation ground based interferometric gravitational-wave detector designed to explore the Universe to an unprecedented depth and detail. With its 10-15 km long arms, underground infrastructure, and advanced cryogenic technologies to suppress thermal noise, ET aims at achieving a sensitivity up to eight orders of magnitude better than current detectors such as Virgo, LIGO, and KAGRA. The coverage of the low-frequency band, starting at 2 Hz, will enable the observation of earlier inspiral phases of compact binary coalescences, enhancing early-warning capabilities and improving multi-messenger follow-up. However, this increased sensitivity also implies a higher susceptibility to environmental disturbances, particularly in the 2-10 Hz range where anthropogenic, seismic and infrastructural noise noise are most relevant. Therefore, understanding and mitigating environmental contributions are essential to fully exploit the scientific potential of ET. This chapter reviews the ongoing studies on environmental noise for ET, summarizes the main challenges related to site characterization and infrastructure design and outlines the strategies currently under development to ensure optimal detector performance.

</details>
