### [207] [Nonresonant optomechanical control of structural phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08899)
*Jiaojian Shi,Yijing Huang,Christian Heide,Elias Hilderbrand,Carl Friedrich Schon,Jan Kottgen,Matthias Wuttig,Burak Guzelturk,Isabel Sedwick,Yukun Li,Haowei Xu,Yuejun Shen,Pooja Donthi Reddy,Viktoryia Shautsova,Mohammad Taghinejad,Duan Luo,Mark L. Brongersma,Kunal Mukherjee,Yuki Kobayashi,Andrew F. May,Eamonn Hughes,Mariano Trigo,David A. Reis,Ju Li,Jian Zhou,Shambhu Ghimire,Aaron M. Lindenberg*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文在硒化锡（SnSe）中展示了非共振光机械相控制（nonresonant optomechanical phase control）的概念，利用介电常数随声子坐标的梯度产生光机械力（optomechanical force），在临界中红外场强下驱动结构相变，形成一种不同于热激发或载流子激发途径的新型结构相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光镊（optical tweezers）展示了光力可实现对微观粒子的操控，近期理论提出此类力可扩展到固体中的结构相变驱动。SnSe因接近多种结构相而具有高热电优值和可切换拓扑晶体绝缘体潜力，是验证该概念的理想材料。

Method: 采用时域拉曼散射（time-domain Raman scattering）测量研究，通过中红外光场对SnSe施加光机械力，该力由介电常数随声子坐标的梯度介导，而非传统光镊的光强梯度；该方法利用介电函数实部的色散（dispersive）机制，绕开共振吸收途径，并可经拉曼过程直接驱动。

Result: 在临界中红外场强之上，SnSe的$A_g$拉曼模式无软化地突然消失，标志新结构相的形成；该相不同于热或载流子激发所诱导的相，并表现出大振幅、长寿命的光学响应调制。原子探针层析（atom probe tomography）还提供了等效直流场驱动下向高对称相转变的证据。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了非共振光机械相控制的概念，开辟了合成具有独特功能性质的隐藏结构相（hidden structural phases）的新途径。

Abstract: Optical tweezers demonstrate how light can exert forces to trap, repel, and manipulate microscopic particles without absorption. Recent theory has suggested that such forces can extend beyond particle manipulation to drive structural phase transitions in solids. Here we apply this optomechanical principle to tin selenide (SnSe), a material where proximity to several different structural phases gives rise to its high thermoelectric figure of merit and makes it a candidate for a switchable topological crystalline insulator. Whereas the force for standard optical tweezers arises from a gradient in the intensity of a light field, the optomechanical force is mediated by a gradient in the dielectric constant as a function of phonon coordinate. Unlike conventional methods that rely on resonant excitation and absorption through the imaginary part of the dielectric function, this approach operates dispersively through the real part and can be directly driven by Raman processes, enabling selective transitions with reduced energy cost and ultrafast response. Using time-domain Raman scattering, we show that above a critical mid-infrared field strength the $A_g$ Raman modes disappear abruptly without softening, signaling the formation of a new structural phase. This phase, distinct from those induced by heating or carrier excitation, exhibits large-amplitude and long-lived modulations in its optical response. Complementing this observation, we show also evidence for an equivalent DC-field-driven structural phase transformation to a higher symmetry phase, as observed by atom probe tomography. Our study demonstrates the concept of nonresonant optomechanical phase control and defines novel opportunities for synthesizing hidden structural phases with unique functional properties.

</details>


### [208] [Wafer-scale monolithic integration of Ce:YIG films and magneto-optical isolators on silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09003)
*Tianchi Zhang,Yucong Yang,Weihao Yang,JieJun Su,Tianyi Ma,Xuan Zhao,Junxian Wang,Di Wu,Zhenyuan Ren,Yi Shuai,Zixuan Wei,Lei Bi*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文报道了通过射频磁控溅射技术在4英寸硅晶圆上成功生长Ce:YIG（掺铈钇铁石榴石）薄膜，实现了2318度/厘米的强法拉第效应、80分贝/厘米的低传播损耗以及3.5%的优异厚度均匀性，并建立了晶圆级非破坏性磁光椭圆偏振测量表征方法和磁光隔离器集成方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅基集成掺铈钇铁石榴石（Ce:YIG）薄膜是非互易光子器件、低温光子调制器和光计算的理想候选材料，但此前在硅上生长的Ce:YIG薄膜仅能达到毫米级尺寸，晶圆级集成及其非破坏性表征一直是未解决的难题。

Method: 采用射频磁控溅射（radio-frequency magnetron sputtering）技术在4英寸硅衬底上生长Ce:YIG薄膜，并自主设计了一套晶圆级非破坏性磁光椭圆偏振测量（magneto-ellipsometry）系统，用于表征薄膜厚度、光学常数和磁光常数在晶圆上的空间分布。

Result: 在4英寸硅晶圆上实现了2318度/厘米的强法拉第效应、80分贝/厘米的低传播损耗和3.5%的优异厚度均匀性，并成功演示了晶圆级环形谐振器型磁光隔离器的集成。

Conclusion: 该工作标志着硅上磁光薄膜的晶圆级异质集成与表征迈出了重要一步，为非互易光子器件阵列、磁光内存计算网络和集成磁光磁力计提供了材料候选方案。

Abstract: Silicon integrated cerium doped yttrium iron garnet (Ce:YIG) thin films are promising candidates for integrated nonreciprocal photonic devices, cryogenic photonic modulators and optical computing applications. However, previously reported Ce:YIG thin film on silicon is limited to milimeter sizes. Wafer-scale integration and non-destructive characterization of high quality Ce:YIG thin films on silicon has been elusive. Here, we report growth of 4-inch wafer-scale Ce:YIG thin films on silicon substrates by radio-frequency magnetron sputtering. Strong Faraday effect of 2318 deg/cm, low propagation loss of 80 dB/cm and excellent thickness uniformity of 3.5% is demonstrated across the 4-inch silicon wafer. Furthermore, a custom designed wafer-scale, non-destructive magneto-ellipsometry was established to characterize the film thickness, optical constants and magneto-optical constants across the wafer. Wafer-scale integration of ring resonator type magneto-optical isolators are also demonstrated. Our work demonstrates a step forward toward wafer-scale heterogeneous integration and characterization of magneto-optical thin films on silicon, providing material candidates for non-reciprocal photonic device arrays, magneto-optical in-memory computing networks and integrated magneto-optic magnetometers.

</details>

### [209] [Beam experiments for reactive ion etching of silicon (Si)-based materials by silicon halide ions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08031)
*Kazuhiro Karahashi,Tomoko Ito,Satoshi Hamaguchi*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用质量选择离子束装置，在超高真空条件下系统测量了Si、SiO₂和Si₃N₄在300至1000 eV能量范围内受硅离子（Si⁺）、卤素离子（F⁺、Cl⁺、Br⁺）及硅卤化物离子（SiF⁺、SiF₃⁺等）照射时的蚀刻产率（etching yield），发现三卤硅离子在1000 eV以上比卤素离子具有更高蚀刻产率，而低能下单卤硅离子则在基底表面沉积硅原子，并为蚀刻工艺与轮廓模拟器提供实验数据库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蚀刻工艺的精确控制和轮廓模拟（profile simulator）需要精确的实验数据支撑，而目前针对不同离子种类照射多种材料表面的蚀刻产率数据尚不完整，尤其是硅卤化物离子的行为尚未被系统表征。

Method: 采用质量选择离子束装置（mass-selected ion beam apparatus），在超高真空（ultra-high vacuum）条件下将单一物种离子照射至Si、SiO₂和Si₃N₄样品表面，测量300至1000 eV能量范围内不同离子的蚀刻产率。

Result: Si⁺在1000 eV以下照射会在三种材料表面沉积硅原子；1000 eV以上，三卤硅离子（SiF₃⁺、SiCl₃⁺、SiBr₃⁺）的蚀刻产率分别高于对应卤素离子，且蚀刻产率依赖于入射离子能量和蚀刻材料（尤其是Si₃N₄）；低入射能下，单卤硅离子（SiF⁺、SiCl⁺、SiBr⁺）在基底上沉积硅，蚀刻阈值能量依赖卤素种类。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了不同离子种类、能量与材料组合下的蚀刻产率系统性实验数据，充实了蚀刻工艺与轮廓模拟器精度提升所需的实验数据库。

Abstract: Etching yields of Si, SiO2, and Si3N4 have been determined for silicon ion (Si+), halogen ions (F+, Cl+, and Br+) and silicon halide ions (SiF+, SiF3+, SiCl+, SiCl3+, SiBr+, and SiBr3+) irradiation in 300 to 1000 eV using a mass-selected ion beam apparatus that can irradiate a single species ion to sample surfaces under an ultra-high vacuum condition. Si+ irradiation below 1000eV deposits silicon atoms on Si, SiO2, and Si3N4 surfaces. The etching yields of silicon tri-halide ions (SiF3+, SiCl3+, and SiBr3+) above 1000 eV are larger than those of halogen ions, respectively, and these etching yields depend on the incident ion energy and the etching material (especially Si3N4). At low incident energy, silicon mono-halide ions (SiF+, SiCl+, and SiBr+) deposit silicon on substrates, and the etching threshold energy depends on the halogen species. This information contributes to a database of experimental values needed to increase the precision of an etching process and a profile simulator.

</details>

### [210] [High Precision Fundamental Physics Experiments at JLab with Spin-transparent Storage Rings of Low-energy Polarized Electron Beams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08551)
*Vladimir Khachatryan,Riad Suleiman,Alberto Accardi,Carlos Ayerbe Gayoso,Marco Battaglieri,Devesh Bhattarai,Silviu Covrig Dusa,Yaroslav Derbenev,Forrest Friesen,Joseph Grames,Paul Guèye,Tyler J. Hague,Ching Him Leung,Wenliang,Li,Magno V. T. Machado,Preet Mann,Vasiliy Morozov,Son Nguyen,Michael Nycz,Howard Oh,Udit Raha,Mario Reig,Marco Schreck,Nathaniel Sherrill,Ye Tian,Zakia Tithi,Alexandre Vieira,Erik Wrightson,Zichen Yin*

Main category: nucl-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新型桌面级储存环（table-sized storage ring）设计，利用低能极化电子束（polarized electron beam）和Mott极化测量（Mott polarimetry），基于自旋透明（spin transparency）原理消除磁偶极矩引起的自旋进动，使电子固有电偶极矩（EDM）和超轻暗物质介导力（如轴子）所诱导的自旋进动得以累积，从而服务于基本物理实验和量子计算应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基本物理实验中测量粒子自旋进动需要自旋透明储存环作为实验工具，而现有方案尚未针对桌面级、低能电子束场景给出专门设计；同时，探索电子EDM和轴子搜索等与CP破坏及宇宙物质-反物质不对称性相关的物理需求日益迫切。

Method: 设计一种高度专门化的桌面级储存环，采用低能极化电子束和Mott极化测量方案。基于自旋透明（spin transparency）假设，使电子在环中沿周期性轨道运行一周后，由磁偶极矩引起的自旋进动在任意束流能量下被抵消；与此同时，由基本物理效应（如EDM或超轻暗物质介导力）引起的自旋进动则持续累积。

Result: 该设计能够在任意束流能量下消除磁偶极矩引起的自旋进动，同时保留EDM和轴子等基本物理效应诱导的自旋进动累积，使测量和搜索成为可能。

Conclusion: 此类储存环不仅为EDM测量和轴子搜索（与CP破坏及宇宙物质-反物质不对称性相关）提供有力工具，还可能在量子计算（quantum computing）领域展现极具前景的应用价值。

Abstract: A breakthrough in fundamental physics experiments measuring particle spin precession may happen if spin-transparent storage rings become adopted tools for such experiments. We present a new design of highly specialized table-sized storage rings, which use low-energy polarized electron beams and Mott polarimetry. Based on the spin transparency ansatz, the spin precession stemming from the magnetic dipole moment is canceled at any beam energy after an electron's turn along the periodic orbit in the ring. Meanwhile, a spin precession induced by the fundamental physics of interest, e.g., the electron's permanent electric dipole moment (EDM) and/or ultralight-dark-matter-mediated forces such as axions, will accumulate. However, capitalizing on such types of rings is not only desirable for measurements of EDMs and axion searches relevant to $CP$ violation and matter-antimatter asymmetry in the Universe, but may also find very promising applications in quantum computing.

</details>


<div id='physics.chem-ph'></div>

# physics.chem-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [211] [Oxygen Reduction Reaction on Platinum Nanocatalysts Produces Long-Lived, Hysteretic Oxygenated Adsorbates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08050)
*Jaehyeon Kim,Lalith Krishna Samanth Bonagiri,Fujia Zhao,Yingjie Zhang*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本研究利用原位拉曼光谱（in situ Raman spectroscopy）跟踪了碱性介质中含活性铂（Pt）表面的纳米颗粒（nanoparticle, NP）催化剂上含氧吸附物种的形成与电位依赖性演化，鉴定出氧还原反应（oxygen reduction reaction, ORR）的三个关键中间体——吸附的OOH、OH和O2，并发现这些含氧吸附物具有高度的长寿命和滞后性，即使在ORR终止后仍持续存在，挑战了中间体仅在催化反应过程中存在的传统观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然单晶催化剂上的中间体物种已被观测，但工业相关纳米颗粒催化剂表面含氧物种的性质与演化过程仍基本未知，而这恰恰是实际电催化应用的核心体系。

Method: 采用原位拉曼光谱技术，在碱性介质中跟踪具有活性铂表面的纳米颗粒催化剂上含氧吸附物的形成与随电位变化的演化；通过对比Ar饱和与O2饱和电解液中的光谱特征，鉴定由氧还原反应产生的中间物种。

Result: 鉴定了三个由ORR产生的关键中间体：吸附的OOH、OH和O2。这些含氧吸附物具有高度长寿命和滞后性，在ORR终止后仍持续存在（即吸附物保留效应），且该效应仅对表面氧化态和电解液阳离子（K+与Li+）表现出适度依赖性，可能由催化剂表面的异质性所促进。

Conclusion: 研究结果凸显了实际催化剂表面吸附结构的复杂性，其往往超出在模型单晶表面上通过测量或模拟所捕获的信息范围，对理解真实电催化体系具有重要意义。

Abstract: Aqueous electrocatalysis generates oxygenated intermediates at catalyst surfaces. While intermediate species on single-crystal catalysts have been observed, the nature and evolution of surface oxygenated species on industrially relevant nanoparticle (NP) catalysts remain largely unknown. Here, using in situ Raman spectroscopy, we tracked the formation and potential-dependent evolution of oxygenated adsorbates in alkaline media on NP catalysts with an active platinum (Pt) surface. By comparing spectroscopic features in Ar- vs O2-saturated electrolytes, we determined three key intermediates produced by the oxygen reduction reaction (ORR): adsorbed OOH, OH, and O2. In contrast to the conventional wisdom that intermediates exist only during catalytic reactions, we found these oxygenated adsorbates to be highly long-lived and hysteretic, and to persist even after the termination of ORR. This adsorbate-retention effect exhibits a modest dependence on the surface oxidation state and the electrolyte cations (K+ vs Li+), and is likely facilitated by the heterogeneous nature of the catalyst surface. The results highlight the complexity of surface adsorption structures on realistic catalysts, which often extends beyond that captured by measurements or simulations on model single-crystal surfaces.

</details>


### [212] [Chemical potentials from structure factors: I. Neutral multi-component mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08357)
*Roya Savoj,Xiaoyu Wang,Musahid Ahmed,Bingqing Cheng*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了S0方法的推广版本，将原本适用于二组分混合物的化学势计算方法扩展至中性多组分混合物。该方法结合高斯过程积分与主动采样策略，可高效计算多组分体系的化学势和混合自由能，并在熔融金属合金及对乙酰氨基酚多晶型在水-乙醇溶剂中的溶解度计算中得到了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多组分混合物的化学势是许多物理化学现象的基础，但精确计算一直具有挑战性。原有的S0方法仅适用于二组分混合物，需要扩展至多组分体系以满足更广泛的实际应用需求。

Method: 首先将统计力学形式论从二组分体系推广到高维组分空间，随后引入结合主动学习（active learning）的高斯过程积分（Gaussian process integration）方案，以高效地积分化学势并采样多样化的组分组合。

Result: 该方法成功计算了熔融金属合金的混合自由能，以及两种对乙酰氨基酚多晶型物在水-乙醇溶剂中的溶解度。

Conclusion: 扩展的S0方法为从中性体相多组分混合物的原子模拟中计算化学势提供了一条实用且可扩展的路径。

Abstract: The chemical potentials of multi-component mixtures underlie many physical and chemical phenomena, but remain challenging to compute. The S0 method enables the computation of chemical potentials from equilibrium molecular dynamics simulations, by leveraging the thermodynamic relationship between particle number fluctuations and derivatives of chemical potentials, followed by numerical integration along different compositions. Here we generalize the S0 method from two-component mixtures to neutral multi-component mixtures. We first extend the statistical mechanical formalism to high-dimensional compositional space, and then introduce a Gaussian process integration scheme combined with active learning to efficiently integrate chemical potentials and sample diverse compositions. We use this method to compute the mixing free energies of a molten metal alloy, and the solubilities of two paracetamol polymorphs in water-ethanol solvents. The extended S0 method provides a practical and scalable route for computing chemical potentials in neutral bulk multi-component mixtures from atomistic simulations.

</details>

### [213] [Double Parton Scattering Effects on the Measurement of the W-Boson Mass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.04082)
*Rui Zhang,Zhen Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究双部分子散射（double parton scattering, DPS）对W玻色子质量测量的影响，发现DPS效应会通过旁观者散射贡献额外横动量，导致CDF探测器测得的W玻色子质量偏大，从而有助于理解CDF-II测量结果与其他测量及标准模型预言值之间的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CDF-II实验测得的W玻色子质量与其他测量值及标准模型预言存在明显偏差，需要寻找可能的物理来源来解释这一偏差。DPS效应来源于单次强子-强子碰撞中的两次部分子-部分子散射，可能通过旁观者散射贡献未充分考虑的系统性效应。

Method: 作者研究了DPS效应对W玻色子质量测量的影响，重点分析DPS通过旁观者散射贡献的额外缺失横动量（missing transverse momentum）以及相关的包含截面（inclusive cross sections），从而考察其对总缺失横动量分布的改变。

Result: 分析表明DPS效应会贡献额外的缺失横动量和包含截面，可能改变总缺失横向动量的分布，从而使得CDF探测器测得W玻色子质量增大，有助于理解CDF-II测量与标准模型预言间的偏差。该结论可在未来的Z玻色子产生中的W类分析中得到进一步验证。

Conclusion: DPS效应可能是导致CDF-II W玻色子质量测量偏高的一个重要因素，同时为在半硬（semi-hard）能区探测量子色动力学（QCD）动力学提供了新的途径。

Abstract: Double parton scattering (DPS) corresponds to events where two parton-parton scatterings occur in a single hadron-hadron collision. The DPS effects may arise from the spectator scatterings that are somewhat related to semi-hard QCD activities. In this work, we investigate the DPS effects on the W-boson mass measurements. Especially, our analysis reveals that the DPS effects contribute additional missing transverse momenta from spectator scatterings as well as relevant inclusive cross sections, potentially altering the distribution of total missing transverse momenta. Consequently, the DPS effects have the potential to cause an increase in the measured W-boson mass by the CDF detector, which helps to understand the deviation of the CDF-II measurements from other measurements and the predicted value in the Standard Model. This could be further validated in upcoming studies, such as the W-like analyses of the Z boson production, which will potentially open new avenues for probing QCD dynamics in the semi-hard regime.

</details>


### [214] [Quest for an Understanding of Pion and Kaon Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07670)
*Zhen-Ni Xu,Daniele Binosi,Craig D. Roberts,José Rodríguez-Quintero*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了强相互作用中π介子和K介子作为（准）Nambu-Goldstone玻色子的内部结构研究现状，指出当前理论与唯象学研究面临困境，并识别了需要更多关注的问题领域，以便充分利用未来十年高能、高亮度实验设施预期产生的大量数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Nambu-Goldstone玻色子（Nambu-Goldstone bosons）与动态整体对称性破缺相联系是物理学中广为人知的现象，但对其束缚态内部结构中Nambu-Goldstone特性的表现几乎一无所知。在强相互作用中，π介子和K介子即（准）Nambu-Goldstone玻色子，而正在或即将运行的高能、高亮度实验设施将产生大量数据，有望绘制其内部结构图，但当前理论与唯象学处于困境之中，亟需梳理现状并明确研究重点。

Method: 本文采用综述（snapshot）方式，对当前Nambu-Goldstone玻色子内部结构研究的状态进行系统梳理，突出当前争论中涉及的问题，并识别需要更多关注的研究领域。

Result: 文章指出了理论与唯象学研究中存在的争议点（issues under debate）和值得更大关注的研究方向，以为未来十年内可能获得的大量实验数据的最佳利用提供指导。

Conclusion: 为使未来十年高能、高亮度设施产生的海量数据得到最充分的利用，理论界需正视Nambu-Goldstone玻色子内部结构研究中被忽视的方面，并优先处理当前争论中的核心问题。

Abstract: The emergence of massless (Nambu-Goldstone) bosons in association with a dynamically global broken symmetry is a long known and widespread phenomenon in physics. However, practically nothing is known about the expressions of Nambu--Goldstone boson character on the internal structure of these bound states. Indeed, their structure is often ignored. In strong interactions, pions and kaons are the (would-be) Nambu-Goldstone bosons and experiments underway or planned at existing or anticipated high-energy, high-luminosity facilities will gather data that it is hoped will enable maps to be drawn of their internal structure. Meanwhile, theory and phenomenology find themselves in something of a quagmire. Herein, we provide a snapshot of the current status, highlighting issues under debate and identifying areas that deserve greater attention so that best use can be made of what is likely to be a huge volume of data delivered in the next decade or so.

</details>


### [215] [Topological analysis of scale-invariant spatial fluctuations in ultrarelativistic heavy-ion collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08259)
*Salman Khurshid Malik,Ramni Gupta,Fakhar Ul Haider,Balwan Singh*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种两阶段拓扑机器学习框架，利用拓扑数据分析（TDA）和深度学习在二维角空间（η, φ）中提取QGP到强子物质相变的临界涨落信号，成功恢复了归一化阶乘矩的幂律标度，实现了间歇性指数的精确重建，为探测QCD临界点和强相互作用物质的相结构提供了鲁棒的数据驱动工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重离子碰撞中QGP到强子物质的相变和QCD临界点可通过间歇性分析研究末态粒子间的空间涨落来识别。然而临界涨落信号极其微弱，仅占总事件样本的百分之几，被压倒性的非临界背景严重稀释，使得传统间歇性分析不足以保证信号提取的可靠性。

Method: 采用两阶段拓扑机器学习框架。第一阶段将粒子事件表示为二维点云，构建基于Delaunay的子层次过滤（sub-level set filtration）以提取Betti曲线作为多尺度拓扑不变量，通过方位角随机化修正多重性偏差，并由TopoPointNet（TPN）和提升决策树（BDT）两种互补架构进行分类。第二阶段应用粒子级密度滤波，剥离弥散的热背景并隔离密集的临界团簇。整个流程以EPOS作为背景模型，在二维角（η, φ）相空间中开展基于CMC的间歇性分析。

Result: 两阶段流水线成功恢复了归一化阶乘矩的幂律标度，能够准确重建（η, φ）空间中的间歇性指数，表明该框架可有效提取被严重稀释的临界信号。

Conclusion: 拓扑机器学习被确立为一种鲁棒的数据驱动工具，可用于探测LHC能量下重离子碰撞中的QCD临界点及强相互作用物质的相结构。

Abstract: The QGP-to-hadronic matter phase transition and QCD critical point in heavy-ion collisions can be identified by studying spatial fluctuations among final-state particles using intermittency analysis. First CMC-based intermittency analysis in the two-dimensional angular ($η$, $\varphi$) phase space, using EPOS as the background model is presented. Critical fluctuation signals are extremely weak, constituting only a few percent of the total event sample and are severely diluted by the overwhelming non-critical background, rendering traditional intermittency analyses insufficient for reliable signal extraction. To extract the weak critical signal, we employ a two-stage topological machine learning framework combining Topological Data Analysis (TDA) with deep learning. In the first stage, particle events are represented as two-dimensional point clouds and a Delaunay-based sub-level set filtration is constructed to extract Betti curves as multiscale topological invariants, corrected for multiplicity bias via azimuthal randomisation and classified by two complementary architectures, a TopoPointNet (TPN) and Boosted Decision Trees (BDT). Since event-level classification alone is insufficient to restore the critical scaling, a second stage applies a particle-level density filter, explicitly stripping away the diffuse thermal background and isolating the densely packed critical clusters. The two stage pipeline successfully restores the power-law scaling of the normalized factorial moments, enabling accurate recovery of the intermittency index in ($η$, $\varphi$) space and establishing topological machine learning as a robust data driven tool for probing the QCD critical point and the phase structure of strongly interacting matter in heavy-ion collisions at LHC energies.

</details>


### [216] [QCD Sum Rule Analysis of Triply Heavy $(Q\bar{Q})(Q\bar{q})$ Tetraquark States with $J^P=0^{\pm}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08385)
*Wen-Shuai Zhang,Chun-Gui Duan,Zhi-Hui Guo,Liang Tang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在量子色动力学求和规则（QCD sum rules）框架下，系统研究了量子数为J^P=0^±的(c(\bar{c})(c\bar{q})和(b\bar{b})(b\bar{q})四夸克态（tetraquark states）的质量谱与衰变模式。基于两种颜色构型构建了18个插值流（interpolating currents），提取出c系统四个态（T_{3c,0}(4760)、T_{3c,0}(5000)、T_{3c,0}(5040)、T_{3c,0}(5370)）和b系统的质量区间，并分析了其可能的衰变路径与实验探测前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索强相互作用框架下多夸克态（exotic hadron）的结构与性质，特别是包含双重味夸克（double-heavy）的四夸克态，这对检验量子色动力学（QCD）的低能行为及寻找奇异强子态具有重要意义。

Method: 在QCD求和规则框架下，基于两种颜色构型[8_c]_{Q\bar{Q}}⊗[8_c]_{Q\bar{q}}和[1_c]_{Q\bar{Q}}⊗[1_c]_{Q\bar{q}}构造18个插值流，通过计算两点关联函数（two-point correlation functions）得到稳定求和规则（stable sum rules）并从中提取四夸克态的质量谱。

Result: 识别出四个(c\bar{c})(c\bar{q})四夸克态：两个J^P=0^+态T_{3c,0}(4760)、T_{3c,0}(5000)及两个J^P=0^-态T_{3c,0}(5040)、T_{3c,0}(5370)。(b\bar{b})(b\bar{q})体系的质量分别落在13.72–14.02 GeV（0^+）和13.90–14.22 GeV（0^-）区间。T_{3c,0}(5000)、T_{3c,0}(5040)和T_{3c,0}(5370)可衰变到粲偶素（charmonium）加粲介子（charmed meson），预期衰变宽度显著；而T_{3c,0}(4760)及所有(b\bar{b})(b\bar{q})四夸克态因通过解离机制（fall-apart mechanism）的两体强衰变在动力学上被禁止，预期较窄。

Conclusion: T_{3c,0}(4760)及所有预测的(b\bar{b})(b\bar{q})四夸克态是实验中通过含有D或\bar{B}介子伴随轻强子或光子末态进行搜索的理想候选体。

Abstract: Within the framework of QCD sum rules, we systematically investigate the mass spectra and possible decay patterns of the $(c\bar{c})(c\bar{q})$ and $(b\bar{b})(b\bar{q})$ tetraquark states with quantum numbers $J^{P}=0^{\pm}$. Based on two distinct color configurations, $[8_c]_{Q\bar{Q}} \otimes [8_c]_{Q\bar{q}}$ and $[1_c]_{Q\bar{Q}} \otimes [1_c]_{Q\bar{q}}$, we construct 18 interpolating currents for these states, and obtain stable sum rules for a subset of them. By calculating the corresponding two-point correlation functions, we extract their mass spectra. For the $(c\bar{c})(c\bar{q})$ system, we identify four possible tetraquark states: two with $J^{P}=0^+$, namely $T_{3c,0}(4760)$ and $T_{3c,0}(5000)$, and two with $J^{P}=0^-$, denoted as $T_{3c,0}(5040)$ and $T_{3c,0}(5370)$. For the $(b\bar{b})(b\bar{q})$ system, the extracted masses are found to lie in the ranges $13.72$--$14.02$ GeV for the $J^{P}=0^+$ states and $13.90$--$14.22$ GeV for the $J^{P}=0^-$ states. We further analyze their possible decay modes. Our results indicate that $T_{3c,0}(5000)$, $T_{3c,0}(5040)$, and $T_{3c,0}(5370)$ can decay into a charmonium state and a charmed meson, and are therefore expected to have appreciable decay widths. By contrast, $T_{3c,0}(4760)$ and all predicted $(b\bar{b})(b\bar{q})$ tetraquark states are expected to be relatively narrow, since the corresponding two-body strong decays via the fall-apart mechanism are kinematically forbidden. Therefore, $T_{3c,0}(4760)$ and all predicted $(b\bar{b})(b\bar{q})$ tetraquark states are promising candidates for experimental searches in final states containing a $D$ or a $\bar{B}$ meson, accompanied by light hadrons or a photon.

</details>


### [217] [Near-threshold resonances in $e^+e^-$ annihilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08769)
*S. G. Salnikov,A. E. Bondar,A. I. Milstein*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了强子对产生过程中近阈共振现象，指出终态相互作用（final-state interactions）是导致低相对速度下反应截面显著增大的自然原因，并展示在几乎所有已知案例中终态相互作用都能成功解释大量实验所观测到的近阈截面能量依赖行为。对各类过程中近阈共振的系统研究可为大距离强子相互作用提供新信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近阈共振在许多强子对产生过程中被发现，导致产生粒子在低相对速度下反应截面显著增大，但其物理成因及系统规律尚需系统梳理与阐明。

Method: 采用综述性研究方法，系统考察多类强子对产生过程中的近阈共振现象，通过比较实验观测与基于终态相互作用的理论预言来确定其成因。

Result: 综述表明，在几乎所有已知案例中，终态相互作用都能成功解释大量实验所展现的近阈截面非平凡能量依赖；这表明近阈共振的自然成因是慢强子间的终态相互作用。

Conclusion: 对各类过程中近阈共振的综合研究可以提供关于大距离下强子间相互作用的新信息。

Abstract: Near-threshold resonances have been discovered in many hadron pair production processes, resulting in a significant increase in reaction cross sections at low relative velocities of the produced particles. The natural cause of such resonances is the interaction between slow hadrons in the final state. Our review demonstrates that, in virtually all known cases, final-state interactions successfully explain the results of numerous experiments demonstrating a nontrivial energy dependence of cross sections for processes near reaction thresholds. A comprehensive study of near-threshold resonances in various processes can provide new information about the interaction between hadrons at large distances.

</details>


### [218] [The Living Guide of Machine Learning for Particle Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09531)
*Claudius Krause,Ramon Winterhalder,Matthew Feickert,Benjamin Nachman*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 作者将2020年启动的HEP-ML Living Review（粒子物理机器学习活体综述）转换为截至2026年6月1日的档案式参考资源，并以新的HEP-ML Living Guide（活体指南）取而代之，后者通过精选、注释和指引读者到基础性与代表性工作来应对该领域文献数量急剧增长和快速多样化的现状。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该领域文献自2020年以来数量增长超过一个数量级，方法远超早期的分类（classification）与生成（generation）任务范畴，原始的全量文献收录模式已无法维持，也不适合当前成熟且快速多样化的领域需求。

Method: 将原Living Review冻结为覆盖截至2026年6月1日文献的档案性参考资源，确保第一阶段HEP-ML工作的稳定记录；同时构建新的HEP-ML Living Guide，采用策展（curate）、注释（annotate）和指引（point to）的策略，不再穷举全部文献。

Result: 原Living Review作为档案资源继续存在，稳定记录HEP-ML领域第一阶段成果；新Guide面向用户提供精选与注释性的导航入口，帮助研究者进入该领域。文章同时说明变更原因及新资源的运作机制。

Conclusion: 面对领域体量膨胀和生态成熟的现实，从"全量清单"转向"策展导航"是合理的资源模式转型，体现了社区维护型资源随领域发展阶段动态调整的必然性。

Abstract: We started the Living Review of Machine Learning for Particle Physics (HEP-ML Living Review) in 2020 as a community-maintained, near-comprehensive bibliography of machine learning in particle physics. The field was then growing faster than any single researcher could follow, finding the relevant papers was hard, and a structured, continuously updated reference paid off immediately. Since then the literature has grown by more than an order of magnitude, the methods reach far beyond the classification and generation tasks of the early years, and the community has built its own ecosystem of topic-specific reviews, benchmark papers, and software frameworks. The original model no longer serves this field well, and we can no longer sustain it. We therefore change direction. We freeze the Living Review as an archival reference covering the literature up to 1 June 2026, where it remains a stable record of the first phase of HEP-ML. A new resource, the HEP-ML Living Guide, replaces it. It does not list everything. It curates, it annotates, and it points readers to foundational and representative work, so that researchers can find their way into a mature and rapidly diversifying field. In this article we explain why we make this change and how the new resource works.

</details>


### [219] [Probing Dark Matter and Phantom Field Effects on Neutrino Oscillations around Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07615)
*Ikrom Ergashov,Bakhtiyor Narzilloev,Ibrar Hussain,Bobomurat Ahmedov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本研究采用两味中微子模型（two-flavor model），探究黑洞附近幻影场暗物质（phantom field dark matter）背景下暗物质对中微子味振荡的影响。研究发现径向运动的中微子引力效应被抵消，振荡相位随距离与质量平方差增加；而偏转中微子则因暗物质产生额外相移，轻微改变质量差项与总相位。数值分析揭示了透镜质量与暗物质参数之间的简并性，且暗物质对引力场中高斯波包（Gaussian wave packet）描述的退相干影响甚微。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究极端引力环境中暗物质对中微子味振荡的影响，旨在借助中微子天文学（neutrino astronomy）在极强场环境中为暗物质探测提供新的潜在途径。

Method: 采用两味中微子振荡模型，在黑洞背景中存在幻影场暗物质（DMPF）条件下，分别考察径向运动与偏转两类中微子路径的振荡相位；结合数值分析检验解析结果，并将中微子建模为高斯波包以研究引力场中的退相干行为。

Result: 径向运动的中微子引力效应被抵消，振荡相位随距离和质量平方差（mass-splitting）增大，行为类似平直时空（flat spacetime）；偏转中微子则因暗物质产生额外相移，略微改变质量差项与总相位，并出现透镜质量与暗物质参数间的简并性（degeneracy），影响随源角（source angle）变化的跃迁概率曲线；暗物质对引力场中高斯波包的退相干影响极小，振荡持续时间由绝对中微子质量主导。

Conclusion: 弯曲时空与暗物质显著影响中微子振荡，径向与偏转路径表现出不同的相位行为；研究结果为在极端环境中通过中微子天文学探测暗物质提供了潜在的理论线索。

Abstract: This study investigates how dark matter in the background of the phantom field (DMPF) near a black hole influences neutrino flavor oscillations using a two-flavor model. It is found that gravitational effects are cancelled for radially moving neutrinos, causing the oscillation phase to increase with distance and mass-splitting, similar to flat spacetime. However, deflected neutrinos experience additional phase shifts due to dark matter, which slightly alters the mass-difference term and the total phase. Numerical analysis supports these findings and reveals a degeneracy between lens mass and dark matter parameters, affecting the transition probability curves based on source angle changes. Neutrinos modeled as Gaussian wave packets show that decoherence in a gravitational field is minimally influenced by dark matter, with the absolute neutrino mass determining the oscillation duration. Our findings imply that curved spacetime and dark matter significantly impact neutrino oscillations, offering potential insights into dark matter through neutrino astronomy in extreme environments.

</details>


### [220] [Perturbative Dissipation in Minimal Warm Inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08345)
*Daniel J. H. Chung,Nidhi Sudhir,Lian-Tao Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析了最小暖暴胀（warm inflation）模型中微扰耗散模，发现摩擦系数主要由Landau阻尼和等离激元衰变主导，其线性正比于α，远弱于Chern-Simons扩散（∝α⁵）的α幂次抑制，但该贡献因慢滚（slow roll）而受到抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解暖暴胀模型中微扰（perturbative）耗散过程的具体机制，特别是摩擦系数（friction coefficient）的普适行为及其红外（IR）物理依赖性，以便更好地刻画暖暴胀动力学的非微扰标度敏感性。

Method: 在最小暖暴胀模型中考虑轴子-暴胀子（axion-inflaton）通过轴子-规范耦合（axion-gauge coupling）耦合到胶子热浴（gluon thermal bath），对所考虑的耗散过程进行微扰分析，识别主导机制为软类空间胶子的Landau阻尼（Landau damping）与硬壳胶子的等离激元衰变（plasmon decay），并考察摩擦系数对非微扰磁质量标度的敏感性。

Result: 摩擦系数的整体线性正比性于α，该α幂次抑制远弱于Chern-Simons（CS）扩散已知贡献中的α⁵ 抑制；同时该贡献因慢滚受到抑制。研究表明这可以理解为导数耦合（derivative coupling）下局部自发CPT破坏（local spontaneous CPT violation）的抑制，而时间分离下不衰减的非局部变量（nonlocal variables）时间关联使得不受抑制的摩擦系数成为可能。

Conclusion: 暖暴胀模型中摩擦系数的耗散过程在红外区域主导，线性依赖于α，但由于慢滚而受抑制；未衰减的非局部变量时间关联为不受抑制的耗散提供了条件。

Abstract: We present an analysis of the perturbative modes of dissipation in the minimal warm inflation model where an axion-inflaton is coupled to a gluon thermal bath through the axion-gauge coupling. The corresponding friction coefficient, which generically is governed by IR physics, for the processes we consider is dominated by Landau damping of soft space-like gluons and the plasmon decay of hard on shell gluons. Since both of these processes are IR dominated, the friction coefficient becomes sensitive to the non-perturbative magnetic mass scale $m_{g}^{-1} \sim (αT)^{-1}$ which in turn leads to the friction coefficient's overall linear proportionality to $α$, which is much less suppressed in $α$ powers compared to the well understood term from Chern-Simons (CS) diffusion $(\propto α^5)$. On the other hand, this contribution to the friction coefficient is suppressed due to slow roll. We show that this can be viewed as a suppression of local spontaneous CPT violation for derivative couplings in general and that time correlations of nonlocal variables which do not fall off at large time separations are what allow an unsuppressed friction coefficient.

</details>


### [221] [Small-instanton effects in an atlas of KSVZ axion models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08602)
*Ning Chen,Saurabh K. Shukla*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用朴素量纲分析（naive dimensional analysis）研究KSVZ模型中矢量样夸克（vector-like quarks）带来的小瞬子（small-instanton）对轴子势能的贡献，发现当增加矢量样夸克拷贝数时，瞬子尺寸积分由最小尺寸（接近紫外截断尺度）主导，诱导的势能可与普通QCD贡献竞争甚至超越之，从而修正轴子质量与轴子-光子耦合之间的标准QCD关系，为轴子搜索开辟新的参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究小瞬子效应对轴子势能的影响，探索其在超出标准模型（beyond Standard Model）扩展中如何改变轴子质量与耦合关系的预测。

Method: 采用朴素量纲分析方法，考察包含单种矢量样夸克、多份拷贝及多种不同矢量样夸克三类KSVZ模型场景，并施加双圈规范跑动下规范耦合至普朗克尺度保持微扰（perturbative）的条件；分析各场景下费米子零模（zero-modes）含量差异，以及不同的质量插入和标量-汤川（scalar–Yukawa）收缩组合对零模饱和（saturation）的要求。

Result: 增加矢量样夸克拷贝数可使瞬子尺寸积分由接近紫外截断尺度的最小尺寸瞬子主导，其贡献对紫外完备化敏感，诱导势能可与QCD贡献竞争或超越。当小瞬子诱导的磁化率（susceptibility）与QCD磁化率相当或更大时，在固定衰变常数f_a下物理轴子质量m_a被增强，而轴子-光子耦合g_{aγγ}仍由f_a和反常比E/N控制。

Conclusion: 标准QCD中m_a、f_a与g_{aγγ}之间的关系被修改，开放了(m_a, g_{aγγ})平面中此前未被探测的轴子搜索新区域。

Abstract: We investigate small-instanton contributions to the axion potential across a range of KSVZ models containing vector-like quarks~(VQs), using naive dimensional analysis. We consider scenarios containing a single VQ, multiple identical copies, and sets of distinct VQs, requiring in each case that the gauge couplings remain perturbative up to the Planck scale under the two-loop gauge running. The associated fermion zero-mode content varies between these cases, requiring different combinations of mass insertions and scalar--Yukawa contractions for its saturation. Increasing the copies of VQs can render the instanton-size integral dominated by instantons of the smallest size, corresponding to the scale near the ultraviolet~(UV) cut-off. The resulting contribution then becomes sensitive to the UV completion and the induced potential can compete with, or dominate over, the ordinary QCD contribution. Assuming that the QCD and small-instanton potentials are aligned, we determine the resulting axion-mass shift and its consequences for the axion--photon coupling. When the small-instanton induced susceptibility becomes comparable to or larger than the QCD susceptibility, the physical axion mass of $m_a$ is enhanced at fixed decay constant~$f_a$, while the axion-photon of $g_{aγγ}$ coupling remains controlled by $f_a$ and the anomaly ratio of $E/N$. The standard QCD relation among $m_a$, $f_a$ and $g_{aγγ}$ is consequently modified, opening new regions of the $(m_a,g_{aγγ})$ plane for axion searches.

</details>


### [222] [The Challenge of Detecting Quantum Nature of Gravitational Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08803)
*Yu Miyauchi,Hidetoshi Omiya,Atsuhisa Ota,Hiroki Takeda,Takahiro Tanaka*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨量子引力波（quantum gravitational waves）是否可通过压缩（squeezing）产生可观测信号。研究表明，暴胀产生的双模压缩在全球源模式下不可观测，但若探测器初始态被制备为压缩态，量子化引力波可产生正的压缩见证（squeezing witness），而经典引力场只能产生位移。然而，该信号受限于极小的引力子-探测器耦合强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在回答一个核心问题：压缩效应能否作为量子引力波的可观测特征。由于实际探测器只耦合特定的波包模式，全球源模式中的压缩是否仍可观测是一个关键的理论悬而未决问题。

Method: 作者通过分析不同场景下的压缩可观测性来展开论证：（1）暴胀双模压缩在可及的单模扇区中退化为非压缩热态；（2）随机背景中相位非相干性会洗去压缩效应；（3）探测器立体角覆盖有限会强烈压制孤立源的压缩信号。进一步，作者对比量子化引力波与经典外引力场在探测器处于压缩初态时产生的不同响应。

Result: 结果表明，源端压缩在多种实际场景下均被强烈压制或完全不可观测；但若探测器初始制备在压缩态，量子化引力波可产生正压缩见证，而经典引力场仅产生位移（displacement）。然而，该信号受限于极小的引力子-探测器耦合，信号强度极其微弱。

Conclusion: 探测器端压缩可以消除对入射波压缩的需求，但无法克服弱引力相互作用本身带来的信号压制。

Abstract: We investigate whether squeezing can provide an observable signature of quantum gravitational waves. Because a realistic detector couples only to a particular wave-packet mode, squeezing in global source modes need not remain observable. We show that inflationary two-mode squeezing reduces to an unsqueezed thermal state in the accessible one-mode sector, phase incoherence washes out squeezing in stochastic backgrounds, and the limited coverage of the solid angle of detectors strongly suppresses squeezing from isolated sources. We then show that source squeezing is not essential, {\it i.e.}, a quantized gravitational wave can generate a positive squeezing witness if the detector state is initially prepared in a squeezed state, whereas a classical external gravitational field cannot, producing only a displacement. However, the resulting signal is bounded by the extremely small graviton--detector coupling. Thus, detector squeezing can remove the need for squeezed incident waves, but not the suppression caused by weak gravitational interaction.

</details>


### [223] [Effects of Born-Infeld Electrodynamics on Chiral Symmetry Restoration and Meson Susceptibilities in Holographic QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09489)
*Hiwa A. Ahmed,Peshwaz A. Abdoul*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在全息QCD框架下，基于非线性带电Born-Infeld黑洞背景，数值研究了有限温度和化学势下的手征对称性破缺及相变。研究发现物理夸克质量下为手征交叉转变（临界温度0.1477 GeV），手征极限下为一级相变（临界温度0.1337 GeV），并系统讨论了临界奇异夸克质量、介子磁化率行为以及Born-Infeld参数对手征相图的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全息QCD（holographic QCD）方法为研究强相互作用物质的相结构提供了重要视角，而手征对称性破缺（chiral symmetry breaking）及其相变是QCD相图的关键组成部分。在拓扑QCD框架下，仍需进一步理解有限化学势和有限温度下的手征相变行为，特别是非线性体电动力学（nonlinear bulk electrodynamics）对手征相图的影响尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 基于非线性带电Born-Infeld黑洞背景构建（holographic）模型，从体标量场的渐近行为提取手征凝聚（chiral condensate）作为序参量。通过数值方法研究不同夸克质量、化学势及Born-Infeld参数β下的手征相变类型与临界温度，并利用介子磁化率（meson susceptibilities，χ_π−χ_σ）辅助验证相边界行为。

Result: 零化学势下，物理夸克质量对应半交叉相变（伪临界温度T_pc=0.1477 GeV），手征极限下变为一级相变（T_c=0.1337 GeV）。零轻夸克质量下，临界奇异夸克质量m_s=37 MeV将一阶与二阶相变区域分开。在有限化学势和物理奇异质量（m_s=95 MeV）下，相变为二级，且T_c随μ增大而降低。Born-Infeld参数β越小，二阶相边界向更高温度移动，但不改变过渡阶次或在研究范围内引入临界端点。

Conclusion: 研究结果与先前软壁（soft-wall）模型研究一致，表明Born-Infeld参数β通过改变相边界位置来修饰手征相图，但在所研究的参数范围内不改变相变阶次。这一工作强调了非线性体电动力学在修改手征相图（chiral phase diagram）中的重要作用。

Abstract: Within a holographic QCD framework, we numerically investigate chiral symmetry breaking and the associated phase transition at finite temperature and chemical potential. The model is constructed on a nonlinear charged Born-Infeld black hole background. The chiral condensate, extracted from the asymptotic behavior of the bulk scalar field, serves as the primary order parameter. At zero chemical potential, we find a chiral crossover transition for physical quark masses with a pseudocritical temperature of $T_{pc}=0.1477$ GeV. In the chiral limit, the transition becomes first-order with a critical temperature of $T_{c}=0.1337$ GeV. A critical strange quark mass of $m_s=37$ MeV, at zero light quark mass, separates first- and second-order transition regions. For finite chemical potential ($μ$) and a physical strange mass ($m_s=95$ MeV) with massless light quarks, the transition remains second-order, with $T_c$ decreasing as $μ$ increases. These results are further supported by the behavior of meson susceptibilities $(χ_π-χ_σ)$, which exhibit a rapid thermal decay and convergence across the phase boundary. Introducing the Born-Infeld parameter $β$ shifts the second-order phase boundary to higher temperatures for smaller $β$ (stabilizing the chirally broken phase) but does not alter the transition order or introduce a critical endpoint within the studied range. Our findings are consistent with previous soft-wall model studies and highlight the significant role of nonlinear bulk electrodynamics in modifying the chiral phase diagram.

</details>


### [224] [Prospects for early heavy-quark measurements at the EIC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09608)
*Allencris John Rubesh Rajan,Laboni Manna*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文评估了电子-离子对撞机（EIC）早期运行阶段的重味夸克对电产生（heavy-quark-pair electroproduction）前景，包括ep和eA碰撞中的包含式测量以及ep碰撞中的双粲介子电产生。基于预期束流能量和亮度，估算总截面和微分分布，并给出事件产额估计，结果表明在中等到大Bjorken-x区域统计精度较HERA显著提升，同时识别了eA碰撞中核修饰效应（核遮蔽、反遮蔽和EMC效应）可探测的运动学区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为EIC早期物理项目中的重味夸克电产生研究提供理论基础，评估其可观测性和统计可行性，以此作为粒子物理层面（parton-level）的基线，支持后续实验可行性研究及高阶理论计算。

Method: 基于EIC预期的束流能量和亮度，估算ep与eA碰撞中重味夸克对及双粲产生的总截面和微分分布（包括光子虚度Q²、重夸克横向动量与快度）；在共线因子化（collinear factorisation）框架下，使用核部分子分布函数（nuclear PDFs）计算eA碰撞的核修饰比ReA，并对不同Bjorken-x区间和Q²范围进行系统分析。

Result: 给出早期科学计划的积分亮度下预期事件产额，展示中等到大x_Bj区域统计精度较HERA测量有显著提升；在eA碰撞中确定了核遮蔽（shadowing）、反遮蔽（anti-shadowing）和类EMC抑制（EMC-like suppression）可由早期EIC数据触及的运动学区域。

Conclusion: 这些结果作为parton-level基线，可为EIC的可行性研究提供支撑，并为未来更高阶的理论和实验探究提供参考基准。

Abstract: We assess the prospects for inclusive heavy-quark-pair electroproduction in electron-proton ($ep$) and electron-nucleus ($e$A) collisions, alongside double-charm-pair ($D^0 \bar{D}^0$ and $D^0D^0$) electroproduction in $ep$ collisions, during the early operational phase of the Electron-Ion Collider (EIC). Based on the anticipated beam energies and luminosities of the EIC, we estimate the total cross sections and differential distributions for photon virtuality $Q^2$, heavy-quark transverse momentum, and rapidity. This analysis evaluates different Bjorken-$x$ ($x_{\rm Bj}$) intervals across a range of $Q^2$ values. Expected event yields are estimated from the projected integrated luminosities of the early science programme, demonstrating the substantial improvement in statistical precision over previous HERA measurements at moderate and large $x_{\rm Bj}$. For electron--nucleus collisions, we compute the nuclear modification ratio $R^{e\text{A}}$ within collinear factorisation using nuclear parton distribution functions, and identify the kinematic regimes in which shadowing, anti-shadowing, and EMC-like suppression are accessible with early EIC data. These results are intended to serve as a parton-level baseline to support feasibility studies and to provide a reference for future higher-order theoretical and experimental investigations at the EIC.

</details>


<div id='math.FA'></div>

# math.FA [[Back]](#toc)

### [225] [A counterexample to the Kato conjecture for positive commutators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07805)
*Rupert L. Frank,Paata Ivanisvili*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文通过证明算子 i[arctan(P), arctan(Q)] 为非负且非零，否证了 Kato 正性判据（positivity criterion）的猜想性逆命题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kato 的正性判据给出了位置算子 Q 和动量算子 P 的函数的对易子（commutator）非负的充分条件，但其逆命题是否成立一直是一个猜想。本文旨在检验这一猜想。

Method: 作者构造了位置与动量算子的反正切（arctangent）函数组合，并针对该具体的对易子算子 i[arctan(P), arctan(Q)] 进行了正性分析，证明其非负且非零。

Result: 算子 i[arctan(P), arctan(Q)] 被证明是非负（nonnegative）且非零（nonzero）的，这构造了一个满足正性条件但不符合 Kato 判据形式的反例，从而否证了其逆命题。

Conclusion: Kato 正性判据的猜想性逆命题不成立，即在位置—动量对易子正性方面，Kato 判据并非必要条件。

Abstract: We disprove the conjectural converse to Kato's positivity criterion for commutators of functions of the canonical position and momentum operators $Q$ and $P$ by showing that the operator \[ i\,[\,\arctan(P),\,\arctan(Q)\,] \] is nonnegative and nonzero.

</details>

### [226] [Engineering correlated phases through manipulation of Van Hove singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07714)
*Thomas P. Sheerin,Maria Ramirez,Chris A. Hooley,Luke C. Rhodes*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究通过热点抛物重整化群（hot-spot parquet renormalization group）与截断单位泛函重整化群（truncated-unity functional renormalization group）两种方法，系统地研究了二维正方晶格Hubbard模型中范霍夫奇点（VHSs）的精确函数形式如何影响关联电子系统的有序相，并通过调节单一跳迁参数t₃实现了Van Hove奇点发散强度从对数型到幂律型的转变，揭示了这一转变对预测有序态的决定性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明，态密度中Van Hove奇点的精确函数形式深刻影响关联电子系统中实现的有序相，但关于VHS发散形式如何控制有序态的完整图景仍不清晰。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统研究VHS特性对有序相选择的调控作用。

Method: 作者采用热点抛物重整化群和截断单位泛函重整化群两种互补方法，对费米能级附近具有Van Hove奇点的二维正方晶格Hubbard模型进行理论研究。通过调节单一跳迁参数t₃（跨越临界值t₃c），可连续改变态密度中VHS的发散强度——从对数型（t₃<t₃c）到幂律型（t₃=t₃c），再到每个Van Hove点分裂为两个对数型点（t₃>t₃c）。同时研究了掺杂对有序相的影响。

Result: 研究发现，系统所处的VHS发散类型区域强烈影响预测的有序态。在Van Hove填充下出现的铁磁态（ferromagnetic state）对费米能级的微小偏移极不稳定，极易让位于依赖电子型或空穴型掺杂特征的不同有序态。

Conclusion: 这些结果强调了调控Van Hove奇点特性以控制关联材料中有序态的重要性，并为在新系统中设计这些相提供了设计规则。

Abstract: Controlling the ordered phases of correlated electron systems remains a central challenge in quantum materials design. Divergences in the electronic density of states, known as Van Hove singularities (VHSs), are one obvious route to such control. It is clear from recent work that the exact functional form of these divergences can profoundly affect which phases are realized; a full picture, however, remains elusive. In this work, we use both the hot-spot parquet renormalization group and the truncated-unity functional renormalization group to theoretically study the emergent correlated states of a two-dimensional square-lattice Hubbard model with VHSs at or near the Fermi level. By varying a single hopping parameter, $t_3$, we are able to change the strength of the VHS divergence in the density of states from logarithmic (for $t_3 < t_{3c}$) to power-law (for $t_3 = t_{3c}$). Further increase of $t_3$ ($t_3 > t_{3c}$) causes each original Van Hove point to split into two, both of the conventional logarithmic type. We show that which of these regimes we are in strongly influences the predicted ordered states. We also study the dependence on doping, and find that the ferromagnetic state that occurs at Van Hove filling in these models is unstable to very small shifts in the Fermi level, often giving way to distinct ordered states depending on whether the model is electron- or hole-doped. These results highlight the importance of tuning VHS properties to control ordered states in correlated materials, and offer design rules to engineer these phases in novel systems.

</details>


### [227] [Metallic Gross-Neveu criticality and superconductivity on the $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ SLAC fermion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07990)
*Feng-Yu Zhang,Yin-Kai Yu,Zi-Xiang Li,Shuai Yin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一个具有SO(3)对称性的双层SLAC费米子模型，通过调节层间相互作用，系统展现出两个不同的量子相变：主相变是狄拉克半金属（DSM）到SO(3)破缺有序相之间的连续相变，属于Gross-Neveu-SO(3)普适类（N=6），该相变规避了标准GNY普适类；第二个相变在更强耦合下驱动系统进入层间SO(3)对称的超导态，属于量子涨落驱动的一级相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超越传统Gross-Neveu-Yukawa（GNY）范式之外的狄拉克临界性已成为凝聚态物理的重要前沿。作者希望通过引入新的模型平台，研究非标准GNY普适类中的临界行为，并探索具有SO(3)对称性的超导态的实现途径。

Method: 构建了一个具有SO(3)对称性的双层SLAC费米子模型，设计了可调节的层间相互作用。采用大规模量子蒙特卡洛（QMC）模拟方法研究系统的量子相图，并通过精确提取临界指数来确定相变的普适类。

Result: 系统随相互作用增加经历两个不同相变。第一个是连续相变，从狄拉克半金属到SO(3)破缺有序相，属于N=6的Gross-Neveu-SO(3)普适类，由于涌现序只开闭了部分巡游费米子的能隙而规避了标准GNY普适类。第二个相变在更强耦合下指向层间SO(3)对称超导态，数值证据表明其为量子涨落驱动的一级相变。

Conclusion: 该研究为探索超越标准GNY普适类的狄拉克临界性提供了新见解，同时也为在凝聚态体系中实现具有SO(3)对称性的超导态提供了一个新颖的平台。

Abstract: The realization of Dirac criticality beyond the conventional Gross-Neveu-Yukawa (GNY) paradigm has become a major frontier in condensed matter physics. In this work, we introduce an $\mathrm{SO}(3)$-symmetric bilayer SLAC fermion model with tunable inter-layer interactions that exhibits a rich quantum phase diagram. As the interaction strength increases, the system undergoes two distinct phase transitions. The primary transition is a continuous boundary separating a Dirac semimetal (DSM) from an $\mathrm{SO}(3)$-broken ordered phase. Crucially, this transition evades the standard GNY universality class because the emergent order only gaps out a subset of the itinerant fermions. Using large-scale quantum Monte Carlo (QMC) simulations, we establish that this transition belongs to the Gross-Neveu-$\mathrm{SO}(3)$ universality class with $N=6$ irreducible Dirac cones and precisely extract the corresponding critical exponents. At stronger couplings, a second transition drives the system into an inter-layer $\mathrm{SO}(3)$-symmetric superconducting (SC) state. We provide strong numerical evidence that this transition is a quantum-fluctuation-driven first-order transition. Our study provides new insights into the exploration of Dirac criticality beyond the standard GNY universality class, and also offers a novel platform to realize superconductivity featuring $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ symmetry.

</details>


### [228] [Quantum geometric localization length and localization criticality in an ideally flat Chern band](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08042)
*Xu-Cheng Wang,Yang Qi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究提出，在孤立且理想的平坦Chern带（Chern band）中，局域化长度（localization length）由量子几何（quantum geometry）决定。通过在最大化局域的混合Wannier基（hybrid Wannier basis）中执行转移矩阵（transfer matrix）计算，作者观察到随量子度量（quantum metric）调谐而发生的从狄拉克不动点（Dirac fixed point）控制普适标度区到非普适连续可变临界指数区的交错，并在普适区中验证了局域化长度对量子几何长度的线性依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在揭示平坦Chern带中局域化行为背后的量子几何起源，并试图为整数量子霍尔转变（integer quantum Hall transition）临界性长期存在的争议提供新的视角。

Method: 作者在最大化局域的混合Wannier基中执行转移矩阵计算，该基的空间延展精确由量子几何长度刻画，通过调谐Chern带的量子度量来研究对应的局域化转变及其临界标度行为。

Result: 观察到从由狄拉克不动点控制的普适区到具有连续变化临界指数的非普适区的交错；在普适区内，局域化长度对量子几何长度表现出显著的线性依赖，支持其量子几何本质。

Conclusion: 这些发现为扭曲莫尔超晶格（twisted moiré superlattices）等量子霍尔系统中的局域化提供了全新的量子几何视角，并为整数量子霍尔转变临界性的长期争论提供了新的启示。

Abstract: We propose that the localization length in an isolated, ideally flat Chern band is set by quantum geometry. We explore the corresponding localization transition and its critical scaling by applying transfer matrix calculations in the maximally localized hybrid Wannier basis, whose spatial spread is exactly characterized by a quantum geometric length. Remarkably, upon tuning the quantum metric of the Chern band, we observe a crossover from a universal regime controlled by the Dirac fixed point to a non-universal regime with continuously varying critical exponents. Within the universal regime, the localization length exhibits a pronounced linear dependence on the quantum geometric length, supporting its quantum geometric nature. These findings provide a novel quantum geometric perspective on the localization in quantum Hall systems such as twisted moiré superlattices, and shed new light on the long-standing controversy over the criticality of the integer quantum Hall transition.

</details>


### [229] [Geometrical approach and topological electron density in the $p_x + ip_y$ superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09415)
*Karyn Le Hur,Kabir Narayanan,Óscar Mañas Chambert*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文对方格上px+ipy超导体在几何与物理层面进行了分析，重点研究半填充下的拓扑相变。通过布洛赫球上两个一维Z(Z2)不变量构造了由布里渊区特定狄拉克点导出的局域拓扑标记，并将其与可由角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）测量的动量分辨电子密度关联，证明其具有温度鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解px+ipy超导体在半填充附近的拓扑相变本质，并建立可从实验测量（如ARPES）获取的局域拓扑标记，以跨越理论与实验之间的鸿沟。

Method: 从布里渊区中的特定狄拉克点出发，通过在布洛赫球（Bloch sphere）上定义两个一维Z（Z2）不变量的叠加，构造局域拓扑标记；将该标记与频率积分的电子谱函数（即动量分辨电子密度）相关联，并分析其在温度扰动下的保持性；进一步通过实空间关联函数分析该标记的实空间表示，并考察拓扑超流密度等物理响应。

Result: 局域拓扑标记在引入温度效应后仍保持良好保护；在动量空间中围绕特定点的小面积积分可揭示态密度的Van Hove对数轮廓（存在于局域标记导数中），同时保留拓扑信息；拓扑相变对应受保护的半金属态。

Conclusion: 所提出的局域拓扑标记既能被ARPES等实验手段直接探测，又蕴含完整的拓扑信息及Van Hove奇异性的物理特征，为px+ipy超导体的拓扑相变提供了可观测的理论描述框架。

Abstract: We present an analysis on the geometrical and physical nature of the $p_x + ip_y$ superconductor on the square lattice, with an emphasis on the topological phase transition at half-filling. We develop a local topological marker from specific Dirac points within the Brillouin zone, which is introduced via the addition of two one-dimensional (1D) $\mathbb{Z}$ $(\mathbb{Z}_2$) invariants defined on the Bloch sphere. We relate this topological marker to the electron spectral function integrated on frequency through the local momentum-resolved electron density, which may be measured via Angle Resolved Photoemission Spectroscopy (ARPES), and show that it remains well-protected including temperature effects. Integrating on a small area around a specific point in momentum space associated to the measure uncertainty, this also reveals the Van Hove logarithmic profile of the density of states in the derivative of the local marker while preserving the topological information. Topological transitions correspond to a protected semi-metal. We analyse the real space representation of this topological marker from correlation functions. We present physical responses such as the topological superfluid density.

</details>


### [230] [Momentum-Selective Electron and Spin Dynamics under Ultrafast Photoexcitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08104)
*Gusein Bedirkhanov,Nagamalleswararao Dasari,Alexander I. Lichtenstein,Evgeny A. Stepanov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用近期发展的实时多体（real-time many-body）框架，解析了光激发关联电子系统中动量选择性的超快动力学。研究预测了电子加热的瞬态节点-反节点各向异性，揭示了反铁磁涨落的非热、类淬火激发行为，并识别了局域磁矩形成及其光致熔化的动力学实验可探测特征，为动量分辨泵浦-探测实验提供了统一的微观图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间分辨光谱学的发展使研究者能够直接探测关联量子材料中动量选择的非平衡动力学，但解释这些观测需要实时理论框架来同时捕捉局域电子关联与非局域集体涨落之间的相互作用，而现有非平衡方法尚不具备此能力。

Method: 采用近期发展的实时多体理论框架，对光激发的关联电子系统进行动量分辨的时间演化模拟，追踪电子加热、光谱权重转移、磁涨落动力学以及实时局域自旋磁化率。

Result: 预测了电子加热中的瞬态节点-反节点（nodal-antinodal）各向异性，为时间分辨光电子发射和拉曼（Raman）实验中争论的动量依赖响应提供了微观解释；识别了导致瞬态反节点带隙内（in-gap）态的非热光谱权重转移；发现反铁磁涨落经历远超电子温度的非热、类淬火激发，同时保持其关联长度不变，随后通过动量空间磁振子级联（magnon cascade）向低动量模弛豫；并通过实时局域自旋磁化率识别了局域磁矩形成及其光致熔化的动力学特征。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了超快电子与磁性动力学的统一微观图像，为解释动量分辨的泵浦-探测实验提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Recent advances in time-resolved spectroscopies have enabled direct access to the momentum-selective nonequilibrium dynamics of correlated quantum materials, revealing a strongly momentum-dependent response of electrons and collective excitations. Interpreting these observations requires a real-time theoretical framework that consistently captures the interplay between strong local electronic correlations and nonlocal collective fluctuations, a capability that remains beyond state-of-the-art nonequilibrium approaches. Using a recently developed real-time many-body framework, we resolve the momentum-selective ultrafast dynamics of a photoexcited correlated electron system. We predict a transient nodal-antinodal anisotropy in electronic heating, providing a microscopic explanation for the momentum-dependent response debated in time-resolved photoemission and Raman experiments, and identify the nonthermal spectral-weight transfer responsible for the transient antinodal in-gap states observed in ultrafast photoemission. We further uncover a momentum-selective magnetic response, in which antiferromagnetic fluctuations undergo a strongly nonthermal, quench-like excitation far above the electronic temperature while preserving their correlation length, before relaxing through a momentum-space magnon cascade toward lower-momentum modes. Finally, by tracking the real-time local spin susceptibility, we identify a dynamical, experimentally accessible signature of local-moment formation and its photoinduced melting. Our results establish a unified microscopic picture of ultrafast electronic and magnetic dynamics, providing a framework for interpreting momentum-resolved pump-probe experiments.

</details>


### [231] [Fermionic Lattice Supersolidity in the Attractive Three-Color Fermi-Hubbard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08178)
*Xiang Li,Yu Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用行列式量子蒙特卡洛（determinant quantum Monte Carlo）模拟研究了方形光晶格上具有色依赖吸引相互作用的三种颜色费米-哈伯德模型，发现量子简并温度下可出现晶格费米超固态（supersolid），表现为电荷密度波（charge-density-wave）与色超流（color-superfluid）序共存，且该系统对现有实验技术高度可达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期实验中首次实现了半填充三色费米-哈伯德模型，为探索超越传统SU(2)体系的奇异物态提供了新颖平台。此前超固态在超冷费米子体系中的实现仍缺乏简单且实验可及的方案，作者旨在提出一个只需简单晶格几何与可调原位相互作用的系统来研究费米超固态。

Method: 作者构建了具有色依赖吸引相互作用的三色费米-哈伯德模型，并采用行列式量子蒙特卡洛模拟方法进行计算研究。模型包含一个中等至强相互作用的两色子系统与一个弱耦合的第三色环境。

Result: 在量子简并温度下，该系统出现晶格费米超固态，其特征为电荷密度波序与色超流序共存。这种超固态来源于中等至强相互作用的两色子系统与弱耦合第三色环境之间的相互作用。

Conclusion: 方形光晶格上的吸引三色费米-哈伯德模型为探索超冷晶格费米子的超固态提供了一个实验上易于达成的系统，仅需简单晶格几何和易调的原位相互作用，且该超固态有望被现有实验技术直接探测。

Abstract: The recent experimental realization of the half-filled three-color Fermi-Hubbard model on a square optical lattice provides a novel platform for exploring exotic states of matter beyond conventional SU(2) systems. In this Letter, we investigate the three-color Fermi-Hubbard model with color-dependent attractive interactions using determinant quantum Monte Carlo simulations. We find that, at quantum degenerate temperatures, a lattice Fermi supersolid state emerges from the interplay between a moderately-to-strongly interacting two-color subsystem and a weakly coupled third-color environment. This supersolid state, characterized by the coexistence of charge-density-wave and color-superfluid orders, is highly promising for experimental detection with current techniques. Our results demonstrate that the attractive three-color Fermi-Hubbard model on a square optical lattice offers an experimentally accessible system for exploring the supersolidity of ultracold lattice fermions, requiring only a simple lattice geometry and easily tunable onsite interactions.

</details>


### [232] [Plasmon modes in quadratic and cubic nodal line semimetals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08185)
*Wei Li,Jing-Rong Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维二次与三次色散节线半金属（nodal line semimetals, NLSMs）中的等离激元（plasmon）模式，在随机相位近似（random phase approximation, RPA）框架下推导了一环极化率并数值求解三维Lindhard积分，发现等离子体频率随载流子密度呈现不同的标度律（二次色散为$ω_p\sim n^{1/2}$，三次色散在大掺杂下为$ω_p\sim n^{2/3}$、小掺杂下为$ω_p\sim n^{3/4}$），并揭示了薄环极限下普适的$\sqrt{2}$各向异性双峰结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性色散NLSMs中的等离激元已被充分研究，但高阶色散（二次与三次）NLSMs的等离激元行为仍缺乏理解。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统刻画高阶色散NLSMs的集体激发模式及其密度标度规律。

Method: 在随机相位近似框架下推导一环极化率（one-loop polarizability），并通过对完整三维Lindhard积分的数值计算评估其系数。基于态密度（density of states）的幂律行为与带内（Drude）响应分析等离激元频率的标度律，并在薄环极限下考察各向异性。

Result: 二次色散NLSMs呈现$ω_p\sim n^{1/2}$的密度标度，三次色散NLSMs在大掺杂区呈现$ω_p\sim n^{2/3}$、小掺杂区交叉为$ω_p\sim n^{3/4}$。$n^{3/4}$定律因态密度发散导致$r_s>1$而在低掺杂区不可靠，可观测窗口被限制在$10^{18}\lesssim n\lesssim10^{19}$ cm$^{-3}$。长波带内极化主导等离子体频率，带间贡献在$Ω\llμ$时小两到三个数量级。两种系统在薄环极限下共享等离子体各向异性$Ω_p^z/Ω_p^\perp\to\sqrt{2}$。

Conclusion: 这些标度律源自态密度的幂律行为和带内响应，分别与双层和三层石墨烯在相空间功率计数上具有相似性。$\sqrt{2}$双峰虽可通过HREELS观测，但无法区分二次与三次色散；密度标度指数才是真正区分的标志性特征。该工作为高阶色散NLSMs的集体动力学提供了理论框架，并给出了可通过HREELS检验的实验可测信号。

Abstract: Nodal line semimetals (NLSMs) host distinctive topological properties and unconventional collective excitations. While plasmons in linear NLSMs are well studied, those of higher-order dispersive NLSMs remain poorly understood. We investigate plasmon modes in 3D quadratic and cubic NLSMs within the random phase approximation (RPA), valid for $r_s\ll1$ but breaking down in the small-doping cubic regime where the density of states diverges. Deriving the one-loop polarizability and evaluating its coefficients numerically from the full 3D Lindhard integral, we find distinct carrier-density scaling laws: quadratic NLSMs exhibit $ω_p\sim n^{1/2}$, while cubic NLSMs show $ω_p\sim n^{2/3}$ at large doping crossing over to $ω_p\sim n^{3/4}$ at small doping. The $n^{3/4}$ law is not quantitatively reliable: the diverging density of states drives $r_s>1$ already at $n\sim10^{18}$ cm$^{-3}$, limiting the observable window to at most $10^{18}\lesssim n\lesssim10^{19}$ cm$^{-3}$; below this, beyond-RPA correlations are essential. These scalings originate from the power-law density of states and intraband (Drude) response, and are analogous in phase-space power counting to bilayer and trilayer graphene, respectively. The long-wavelength intraband polarization dominates the plasmon frequency, while interband contributions are subleading (two to three orders of magnitude smaller for $Ω\llμ$). Both systems share a plasmon anisotropy $Ω_p^z/Ω_p^\perp\to\sqrt{2}$ in the thin-ring limit, a generic consequence of the torus Fermi-surface geometry. This $\sqrt{2}$ doublet, though observable by HREELS, cannot distinguish quadratic from cubic dispersion; the density scaling exponent is the true distinguishing signature. Our results provide a framework for the collective dynamics of higher-order dispersive NLSMs and suggest experimentally testable signatures accessible by HREELS.

</details>


### [233] [Quantum Formulation of Chiral Vortical Effect in Weyl Semi-metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08269)
*B. Q. Song*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文发展了一种手性涡旋效应（chiral vortical effect, CVE）的全量子化表述，通过求解微观自旋波函数的精确演化，对半经典理论和假定的分布函数进行了自下而上的定量检验。研究表明CVE分布函数基于无基态的Floquet谱，本质上是一种非平衡现象，仅在慢旋转、高化学势和各向同性三个条件同时满足时才近似恢复热平衡费米分布，从而回归半经典结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CVE是旋转外尔费米子（Weyl fermion）中产生轴向电流（axial current）的效应，现有描述依赖于半经典（semiclassical）框架。作者旨在构建一个完全量子化的CVE理论框架，以精确求解微观自旋波函数演化，从而对半经典理论进行自下而上的定量检验，并澄清不同参考系中假定的分布函数f_CVE的物理本质。

Method: 作者发展了一种完全量子化的CVE公式化方法，求解带自旋微观波函数（microscopic spinful wavefunctions）的精确演化。该理论基于半金属（semimetals）系统而非基本外尔粒子，以提供更易实验探测的实现平台，并通过限定旋转速度、化学势、系统尺寸和费米速度等参数区间，系统比较量子解与半经典理论的预测。

Result: 结果表明，f_CVE定义在无基态的Floquet谱（ground-state-free Floquet spectrum）上，与热平衡费米分布（fermi form, f_F）在定性上截然不同，说明CVE是非平衡现象，与其他手性输运效应不同。f_F仅在三个条件同时满足时近似成立：(1)慢旋转ωR/v_F≪1，(2)高化学势μ/(ℏv_F R)≫1，(3)各向同性（isotropic symmetry），其中R为尺寸，v_F为费米速度。在这些条件下理论恢复包括电流响应系数和磁化贡献在内的已确立半经典结果；否则将揭示量子现象，如"空洞态"（void states）、偏离半经典公式j_CVE∼μ² 以及与v_F无关的电荷泵浦（charge pumping）。

Conclusion: CVE本质上是非平衡（non-equilibrium）现象，与热平衡分布定性不同；仅在慢旋转、高化学势及各向同性三个极限条件下，半经典描述才近似成立。该量子理论基于半金属系统，相比基本外尔粒子提供了更易实验检测的途径，并揭示出半经典框架之外的新量子效应。

Abstract: The chiral vortical effect (CVE) is the generation of an axial current in a rotating Weyl fermion; its description is presently based on semiclassical frameworks. In this work, we develop a fully quantum formulation for CVE, solving the exact evolution of microscopic spinful wavefunctions, which enables a bottom-up quantitative test of semi-classical theories and postulated distributions $f_{\text{CVE}}$ in different reference frames. Notably, it shows that $f_{\text{CVE}}$ is over a ground-state-free Floquet spectrum, qualitatively distinct from a thermal equilibrium distribution (i.e., fermi form $f_F$), underscoring CVE as a non-equilibrium phenomenon, distinguished from other chiral transports. The $f_F$ only approximately holds when three conditions are simultaneously fulfilled: (1) slow rotation $ωR/v_F\ll 1$, (2) high chemical potential $μ/(\hbar v_F R)\gg 1$, (3) isotropic symmetry, where $R$ is the size, $v_F$ is fermi velocity. In these conditions, the theory recovers established semiclassical results, including the current-response coefficients and the magnetization contribution; otherwise, it uncovers quantum phenomena such as ``void states", deviation from the semiclassical formula $j_{\text{CVE}} \sim μ^2$, a $v_F$-independent charge pumping. The theory is based on semimetals, providing more experimentally accessible detection than fundamental Weyl particles.

</details>


### [234] [Elementary magnons and interacting multi-magnon quasiparticles in the effective spin-$\frac{1}{2}$ kagome-staircase magnet Co$_{3}$V$_{2}$O$_{8}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08587)
*Yuan Xiao,Jiajun Mo,Zhenmeng Jiang,Haoyang Leng,Otkur Omar,Yanjun Li,Fengyi Song,Jianjun Ying,Shang Gao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究利用高分辨时域太赫兹光谱技术，系统测绘了三维笼目阶梯化合物Co₃V₂O₈在不同温度和磁场下的磁激发谱，确定了其有效自旋1/2哈密顿量，并首次揭示了由强交换各向异性稳定的相互作用多磁子准粒子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 各向异性磁体的激发谱为连接微观哈密顿量与相互作用驱动的准粒子提供了直接桥梁，但高能区多磁子激发的性质及其与交换各向异性的关系尚未被充分理解，亟需系统实验表征。

Method: 采用高分辨率时域太赫兹（terahertz）光谱技术，对三维笼目阶梯（kagome-staircase）化合物Co₃V₂O₈进行随温度和磁场变化的偏振分辨测量；结合此前报道的非弹性中子散射（inelastic neutron scattering）色散关系，拟合确定有效自旋1/2哈密顿量。

Result: 低能区识别出磁偶极活性的单磁子模，并追踪其在铁磁相和自旋密度波（spin-density-wave）相中的演化；确定了一个具有强各向异性交换的有效自旋1/2哈密顿量，可定量复现单磁子谱。高能区观测到场斜率是单磁子模2至4倍的尖锐分支，以及不同磁子数分支间的反交叉（anticrossing），其锐利度和偏振依赖与计算的多磁子连续谱显著偏离。

Conclusion: 高能尖锐分支被识别为可由强交换各向异性稳定的相互作用多磁子（interacting multi-magnon）准粒子，该非相互作用模型为理解高能响应提供了基准。

Abstract: The excitation spectrum of an anisotropic magnet provides a direct link between its microscopic Hamiltonian and interaction-driven quasiparticles. Here we use high-resolution time-domain terahertz spectroscopy to map the magnetic excitations of the three-dimensional kagome-staircase compound Co$_{3}$V$_{2}$O$_{8}$ as functions of temperature and magnetic field. At low energies, polarization-resolved spectra identify magnetic-dipole-active one-magnon modes and track their evolution across the ferromagnetic and spin-density-wave phases. Combining their field dependence with previously reported inelastic-neutron-scattering dispersions, we determine an effective spin-$\frac{1}{2}$ Hamiltonian with strongly anisotropic exchange that quantitatively reproduces the one-magnon spectrum. This model provides a noninteracting benchmark for the high-energy response, where we observe sharp branches with field slopes that are two to four times those of the one-magnon modes, together with anticrossings between branches of different magnon numbers. Their sharpness, polarization dependence, and departure from the calculated multi-magnon continua identify them as interacting multi-magnon quasiparticles that can be stabilized by strong exchange anisotropy.

</details>


### [235] [Nonequilibrium dynamics of doped Chern ferromagnets: a case study for false vacuum decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08937)
*Kilian Kuhlbrodt,Olivier Huber,Oliver Breach,Weijie Li,Xiaodong Xu,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Martin Kroner,Siddharth A. Parameswaran,Atac Imamoglu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究利用moire量子材料中的耗散态制备，通过圆偏振拉盖尔-高斯光束光泵浦实现亚稳态中真真空泡的可控制备，观测并研究了Chern铁磁体的非平衡动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚稳态假真空衰变（metastable false vacuum decay）在物理中普遍存在但其动力学机制尚未被充分理解。moire量子材料中的耗散态制备为探索这类物理提供了独特平台，因为它允许生成非哈密顿量基态的奇异量子态。

Method: 利用圆偏振拉盖尔-高斯光束对孔的自旋-能谷自由度进行光泵浦，以制备嵌入亚稳态中的真真空泡。实验中精确调控泵浦功率控制气泡的初始尺寸，并通过施加与铁磁性矫顽场相当的外磁场来观测气泡的收缩或扩张行为。研究覆盖整数和分数量子异常霍尔绝缘体态对应的相称填充因子。

Result: 观测到真真空泡的塌缩或扩张取决于由泵浦功率控制的初始尺寸，源于畴壁与体动力学之间的相互作用。在外磁场与铁磁性矫顽场相当的量级时，在整数和分数量子异常霍尔绝缘体态的相称填充下观察到自旋极化衰减时间延长达两个数量级。

Conclusion: 实验表明，铁磁畴的非平衡动力学对Chern绝缘体态附近的精确填充因子比传统输运或光学测量更为敏感，揭示了此类材料中亚稳态动力学与拓扑态填充之间的深层联系。

Abstract: Even though metastable false vacuum decay is ubiquitous in physics, its underlying dynamics are still not well understood. Dissipative state preparation in moiré quantum materials provides an exceptional setting for exploring this physics since it allows the possibility of generating exotic quantum states that are not the ground state of the system Hamiltonian. Motivated by recent experiments demonstrating steady-state optical orientation of the spin-valley degree of freedom of holes, here we investigate dynamics of itinerant and Chern ferromagnets in the presence of an opposing magnetic field. Optical pumping using a circularly polarized Laguerre-Gauss beam allows us to deterministically prepare a true vacuum bubble embedded inside a metastable state. Depending on its initial size controlled by the pump power, we observe that the bubble collapses or expands due to an interplay between domain wall and bulk dynamics. For external magnetic fields comparable to the coercive field of ferromagnetism, we observe up to two-orders-of-magnitude prolongation of the spin polarization decay time at commensurate fillings corresponding to integer and fractional Chern insulator states. Our experiments reveal that the nonequilibrium dynamics of the ferromagnetic domains is substantially more sensitive to the precise filling factor around Chern insulator states than standard transport or optical measurements.

</details>


### [236] [Spin Polarization of a Two-Dimensional Electron Liquid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09533)
*G. A. Nikolaev,M. E. Sergeev,I. V. Kukushkin,A. V. Shchepetilnikov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过实验证明，强关联二维电子系统（two-dimensional electron system）的纵向电阻可以直接定量测量其自旋极化（spin polarization），并建立了无需拟合参数的磁输运与自旋极化之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强关联二维电子系统中，自旋极化是一个关键物理量，但以往缺乏直接从输运测量中定量提取自旋极化的方法。本文旨在建立一种无需额外标定的、基于输运测量的自旋极化定量方案。

Method: 采用电探测电子自旋共振（electrically detected electron spin resonance）作为独立标定自旋态的方法，将其与纵向电阻测量相结合，建立磁输运与自旋极化之间的无参数（parameter-free）关系。

Result: 提取的自旋极化能够定量解释电探测自旋共振信号随磁场和温度的变化，并可在广泛的载流子密度与磁场范围内映射电子液体的磁学状态。

Conclusion: 纵向电阻可作为强关联二维电子系统自旋极化的直接定量测量手段，仅凭输运测量即可重构自旋极化，为研究电子液体的磁学态提供了一条新途径。

Abstract: We show experimentally that the longitudinal resistance of a strongly correlated two-dimensional electron system provides a direct quantitative measure of its spin polarization. Using electrically detected electron spin resonance as an independent calibration of the spin state, we establish a parameter-free relation between magnetotransport and spin polarization, enabling spin polarization to be reconstructed from transport measurements alone. The extracted spin polarization quantitatively explains the magnetic field and temperature dependence of the electrically detected spin-resonance signal and allows the magnetic state of the electron liquid to be mapped over a broad range of carrier densities and magnetic fields.

</details>


### [237] [Uniaxial stress effects on magnetic and electric properties of the ground state in a centrosymmetric magnetic skyrmion host Gd$_2$PdSi$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09680)
*Hiraku Saito,Naoki Kobayashi,Takuro Kawasaki,Tatsuya Nakamura,Akiko Kikkawa,Yasujiro Taguchi,Yoshinori Tokura,Taro Nakajima*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究通过施加垂直于c轴的压缩单轴应力，对中心对称磁性斯格明子化合物Gd₂PdSi₃的磁有序和电阻率进行了磁化、电阻率和中子衍射测量，实验数据表明该系统未表现出单q磁有序所预期的各向异性磁电性质，从而判定其磁性基态为多q磁有序结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gd₂PdSi₃已被证实在外场诱导的第一相中展现出三重q磁斯格明子晶格相并伴随巨大的拓扑霍尔效应，但其零场基态的磁结构究竟是单q还是多q仍未明确，需要实验加以判定。

Method: 对样品施加垂直于c轴的压缩单轴应力，并同时开展磁化率（magnetization）、电阻率（resistivity）和中子衍射（neutron diffraction）三类实验测量。

Result: 实验数据未显示出预期由单q磁有序导致的磁性和电学各向异性，表明该体系基态并非单q结构。

Conclusion: Gd₂PdSi₃的磁性基态为多q（multi-q）磁有序结构。

Abstract: We investigate effects of uniaxial stress to magnetic orders and electrical resistivity of the centrosymmetric magnetic skyrmion compound Gd$_2$PdSi$_3$, which has a hexagonal crystal structure composed of triangular lattice layers of magnetic Gd$^{3+}$ ions. This compound is known to exhibit the triple-$q$ magnetic skyrmion lattice phase with a giant topological Hall effect in the first field induced phase [T. Kurumaji $\textit{et al}$. Science $\textbf{365}$, 914-918 (2019)]. In contrast to the established picture of the field-induced phase, the ground state of this system still remains to be studied. Although previous studies reported the existence of the incommensurate magnetic modulations described by a magnetic modulation wave vector ${\bf q}=(q,0,0)$ where $q\sim 0.14$ and its equivalents, it is still unclear whether the magnetic structure is a single-$q$ structure or a multiple-$q$ structure. In the present study, we performed magnetization, resistivity and neutron diffraction measurements with a compressive uniaxial stress applied perpendicular to the $c$ axis. The observed data revealed that the system did not exhibit anisotropic magnetic and electric properties expected from a single-$q$ magnetic order, suggesting that the magnetic ground state of this system is a multi-$q$ magnetic order.

</details>


### [238] [Robust spin pseudogap and spin-charge separation in the $σt$-$J$ model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09689)
*Zheng-Yuan Yue,Jia-Xin Zhang,Zheng-Cheng Gu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用无限投影纠缠对态（iPEPS）算法研究 σt-J 模型的有限温性质，发现其与 t-J 模型及易平面变体共享相似的赝能隙自旋热力学（自旋磁化率在 T*∼J 处出现峰值），并通过张量网络重整化（TNR）证实该模型中存在更低温度下的 Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless（BKT）转变。关键在于，与 t-J 模型不同，σt-J 模型中这些特征在掺杂下保持稳健，且掺杂空穴会增强 xy 自旋平面内的反铁磁关联。研究者进一步构建了基于投影对称群（PSG）的奴隶费米子平均场理论，成功解释了掺杂下的稳健自旋赝能隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: t-J 模型中的相弦效应（phase string effect）导致掺杂空穴与自旋背景之间存在强烈干涉，阻碍了对高温超导等强关联物理的清晰理解。σt-J 模型通过翻转自旋向下跳跃项的符号来消除这一效应，因此有必要系统研究该模型的有限温性质，以厘清掺杂与自旋关联之间的相互作用。

Method: 采用无限投影纠缠对态（iPEPS）算法计算有限温性质，并与 t-J 模型及其易平面变体进行对比；运用张量网络重整化（TNR）方法探测自旋扇区的 BKT 转变；最后发展基于数值确定跳跃与配对关联的投影对称群（PSG）约束的奴隶费米子平均场理论。

Result: 三类模型均表现出相似的自旋赝能隙热力学特征（自旋磁化率在 T*∼J 处有峰值，比热具有宽峰）；σt-J 模型因降低的 U(1) 自旋旋转对称性，在更低的 T_BKT<T* 处经历 BKT 转变；与 t-J 模型不同，σt-J 模型在掺杂下这些特征保持稳健，掺杂空穴甚至增强 xy 自旋面内的反铁磁关联，使 T* 和 T_BKT 对掺杂仅有微弱的依赖性；构建的奴隶费米子平均场理论成功解释了掺杂下稳健的自旋赝能隙。

Conclusion: σt-J 模型为理解掺杂体系中自旋赝能隙与 BKT 转变的稳健性提供了重要平台。相弦函数的消除使得掺杂空穴不再破坏而是增强自旋关联，这为强关联电子体系中赝能隙现象的物理机制提供了新的视角，且验证了基于 PSG 选择的奴隶费米子平均场理论的合理性。

Abstract: The $σt$-$J$ model, obtained from the usual $t$-$J$ model by flipping the sign of the spin-down hopping term, has been proposed to eliminate the strong interference between doped holes and the spin background, known as the phase string effect. In this work, we investigate the finite-temperature properties of the $σt$-$J$ model using infinite projected entangled-pair state (iPEPS) algorithms, in comparison with the $t$-$J$ model and its easy-plane variants. We show that these models share similar spin-pseudogap thermodynamics, with a maximum in the spin susceptibility at $T^* \sim J$ and a broad specific-heat peak. Tensor network renormalization (TNR) further provides strong evidences for a Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless (BKT) transition in the spin sector of the $σt$-$J$ model at a lower temperature $T_\text{BKT} < T^*$ due to its reduced $\mathrm{U}(1)$ spin-rotation symmetry. Crucially, in contrast to the $t$-$J$ model and to its easy-plane variants that also exhibit BKT transitions, these signatures remain robust upon doping: the doped holes preserve and even enhance the antiferromagnetic correlations in the $xy$ spin plane, leading to a weak doping dependence of both $T^*$ and $T_\text{BKT}$. Finally, we develop a slave-fermion mean-field theory for the $σt$-$J$ model, whose projective symmetry group (PSG) is selected based on numerically determined hopping and pairing correlations, and show that it explains the robust spin pseudogap upon doping.

</details>


### [239] [Anomalous temperature dependence of polaron mobility in a nonlinear double-well potential: unbiased X-propagator approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09883)
*Stefano Ragni,Osor S. Barišić,Naoto Nagaosa,Andrey S. Mishchenko*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无偏X-传播子方法（unbiased X-propagator method），用于计算任意非线性电子-声子相互作用下的有限温度光学电导率σ(ω)和直流迁移率μ，并将其应用于耦合双阱晶格势的极化子模型。在中等耦合下迁移率呈现三段温度行为，产生传统线性耦合极化子模型不具有的凹形温度依赖；在强耦合下迁移率变为非单调，并伴随光谱权重重分布。对于SrTiO₃相关参数，结果定性地重现了异常凹形迁移率和Mott-Ioffe-Regel极限破坏，支持非线性耦合到软非谐晶格模式作为稀薄极性金属中异常输运的微观机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统极化子模型通常假设线性电子-声子耦合，难以解释强非谐极性材料（如SrTiO₃）中观察到的异常输运行为，因此需要发展能处理任意非线性电子-声子相互作用的计算方法。

Method: 作者开发了一种无偏X-传播子方法（unbiased X-propagator method）来计算有限温度下非线性电子-声子相互作用系统的光学电导率和直流迁移率，并将其应用于耦合双阱晶格势的极化子模型——这是强非谐极性材料的最小模型。

Result: 中等耦合下迁移率展现三个温度区间：阱内局域、与势垒的热竞争、势垒无关的高温动力学，产生传统模型没有的凹形温度依赖；强耦合下迁移率变为非单调，其温度演化反映在光学光谱权重的特征性重分布中；对SrTiO₃参数可定性重现异常凹形迁移率和Mott-Ioffe-Regel极限破坏。

Conclusion: 非线性耦合到软非谐晶格模式（soft anharmonic lattice mode）是稀薄极性金属中异常输运的微观机制。

Abstract: We develop an unbiased X-propagator method for calculating finite-temperature optical conductivity $σ(ω)$ and dc mobility $μ$ for arbitrary nonlinear electron-phonon interaction. We apply it to a polaron coupled to a double-well lattice potential, a minimal model for strongly anharmonic polar materials. At moderate coupling, the mobility exhibits three temperature regimes associated with confinement within one well, thermal competition with the barrier, and barrier-insensitive high-temperature dynamics. This sequence produces a concave temperature dependence of the mobility that is absent in conventional linear-coupling polaron models. At strong coupling, the mobility becomes nonmonotonic, and its temperature evolution is reflected in a characteristic redistribution of optical spectral weight. For parameters relevant to SrTiO$_3$, our results qualitatively reproduce both the anomalous concave mobility and the onset of violation of the Mott-Ioffe-Regel limit, thereby supporting nonlinear coupling to a soft anharmonic lattice mode as a microscopic mechanism for anomalous transport in dilute polar metals.

</details>


### [240] [Tunable Multiband Geometry and Fractional Phases in Higher Vortexable Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09911)
*Xiaohan Wan,Siddhartha Sarkar,Ting Cao,Mark Rudner,Di Xiao,Kai Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文以更高涡旋性（higher vortexability）的莫尔体系为平台，在完整平带希尔伯特空间中开展精确对角化研究，揭示了零磁场下从阿贝尔到非阿贝尔拓扑相的多带相变级联，并证明波函数量子几何本身可作为调控竞争拓扑态之间的直接参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究聚焦于更高涡旋性作为通向具有更高朗道能级类量子几何（higher-Landau-level-like quantum geometry）拓扑平带的途径；本文提出补充视角，即涡旋性可提供能带色散、简并度和拓扑保持不变但波函数几何连续可调的多带结构，从而为研究几何驱动的多带拓扑相提供新平台。

Method: 对分数填充的更高涡旋性莫尔体系进行系统的精确对角化（exact diagonalization）研究，保留整个平带希尔伯特空间而非投影到单一能带；在固定填充下，通过改变波函数几何而非色散或拓扑来驱动相变或交叉，并在屏蔽库仑相互作用下考察不同几何区域的非阿贝尔拓扑序行为。

Result: 在零磁场下发现包括整数和分数激子绝缘体、阿贝尔分数陈绝缘体、Moore-Read态和Read-Rezayi态在内的阿贝尔与非阿贝尔相级联；在固定填充下，不同相之间的转变仅由波函数几何变化驱动；与Moore-Read和Read-Rezayi态相关的填充下，带间混合会移动最优量子几何区域，但不会抑制非阿贝尔拓扑序。

Conclusion: 更高涡旋性的莫尔能带构成了零磁场下探索几何驱动多带拓扑相的可调平台，其量子几何可作为竞争拓扑态之间的直接调控参数。

Abstract: Higher vortexability is often viewed as a route to topological flat bands with higher-Landau-level-like quantum geometry. Here we emphasize a complementary perspective: it provides a tunable multiband structure in which wave function geometry can be varied continuously while the band dispersion, degeneracy, and topology remain fixed. We perform systematic exact-diagonalization studies of many-body phases in fractionally filled higher vortexable moiré systems, retaining the full flat-band Hilbert space rather than projecting onto a single band. The multiband treatment reveals a cascade of Abelian and non-Abelian phases at zero magnetic field, including integer and fractional exciton insulators, Abelian fractional Chern insulators, Moore-Read and Read-Rezayi states. At fixed filling, different phases are connected through transitions or crossovers driven solely by changes in wave function geometry, highlighting quantum geometry itself as a direct tuning parameter between competing topological states. At fillings associated with Moore-Read and Read-Rezayi states, our calculations show that interband mixing shifts the optimal quantum geometry regime without suppressing non-Abelian topological order under screened Coulomb interaction. Our results establish higher vortexable moiré bands as a tunable platform for exploring geometry-driven multiband topological phases at zero magnetic field.

</details>


### [241] [Cell Natural Orbitals in Interacting Topological Bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09932)
*Nishchhal Verma,Harshitra Mahalingam,Daniel Muñoz-Segovia,Raquel Queiroz*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于奇异值分解的“元胞自然轨道”（Cell Natural Orbitals, CNOs）方法，以系统化处理拓扑能带中投影相互作用所面临的空间局域性问题。该方法通过分解能带投影态密度形状因子，识别出相互作用强度的内在层级结构，并以手性极限下的魔角双层石墨烯为例验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑能带（topological bands）对指数局域且对称的Wannier函数存在阻碍，这使得标准范式——用有限范围的局域轨道表示投影相互作用——难以适用。因此需要一种新的框架来忠实捕捉拓扑能带的形状因子和量子几何（quantum geometry），以便在实空间中分析关联效应。

Method: 作者对能带投影态密度形状因子进行奇异值分解（singular-value decomposition），提出基于几何的Hilbert空间截断方案。分解的本征态被定义为元胞自然轨道（CNOs），即元胞约化单粒子密度矩阵的本征态，其占据数衡量了忠实表示能带波函数重叠所需的最小轨道复杂度。

Result: 在手性极限的魔角扭曲双层石墨烯（magic-angle twisted bilayer graphene）中，主导CNO位于AA位点，类似于重费米子模型中的f-费米子；次级CNO通道的相互作用矩阵元逐渐减弱，可在静态平均场水平处理。该方法还揭示了元胞内电荷密度变化如何产生单粒子谱函数的动量依赖。

Conclusion: CNOs为拓扑能带与实空间关联之间提供了几何信息引导的桥梁，建立了分析量子材料中相互作用与涌现相（emergent phases）的系统性框架。

Abstract: Topological bands exhibit obstruction to exponentially localized and symmetric Wannier functions, challenging the standard paradigm of representing projected interactions in terms of local orbitals with finite range. To faithfully capture the form factors and quantum geometry of topological bands we introduce a singular-value decomposition of the band-projected density form factors, enabling a geometry-based truncation scheme of the Hilbert space, exposing an intrinsic hierarchy on band-projected interactions that is determined by the underlying wavefunctions. This decomposition is most naturally described in terms of Cell Natural Orbitals (CNOs), as the eigenstates of the unit-cell reduced one-particle density matrix, whose occupation provide a measure of the minimal orbital complexity required to faithfully represent the band wavefunctions overlaps. The CNO decomposition identifies systematically the minimal number of local orbitals needed to reproduce short-ranged interactions while resolving the hierarchy of interaction strengths across CNO channels. Applied to magic-angle twisted bilayer graphene in the chiral limit, we find that the dominant CNO is centered at the AA site, resembling the $f$-fermion of the heavy-fermion model. The subdominant CNO channels carry progressively weaker interaction matrix elements, allowing them to be treated at the static mean-field level, while the dominant channel requires a dynamical self-energy. The formalism illustrates how variations of charge density within the unit cell generate momentum dependence in the CNO envelope function and, consequently, dispersion in the single-particle spectral function. More broadly, our results establish CNOs as a geometry-informed bridge between band topology and real-space correlations, providing a systematic framework for analyzing interactions and emergent phases in quantum materials.

</details>

### [242] [Determination of acoustic nonlinearity parameters using thermal modulation of ultrasonic waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07685)
*Hongbin Sun,Jinying Zhu*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种利用热调制超声波的测试方法及其理论框架，通过温度变化引起的热应变激发材料非线性响应，基于二次多项式模型拟合实验曲线，从而测定材料的声学非线性参数（α, β, δ）。该方法在铝、钢、完整及损伤混凝土样品上得到验证，与传统振动或声激励方法相比，热调制方法产生更均匀、缓慢变化且更大的应变场，能够使用简单的超声测试装置精确测量α, β, δ的绝对值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非线性声学方法多依赖振动或声激励来驱动非线性，这些方法产生的应变场不均匀、变化快，难以精确测量材料的绝对非线性参数。本研究旨在开发一种以热效应作为非线性驱动力的新方法，以更准确地测定材料声学非线性参数。

Method: 提出热调制超声波的测试方法及理论框架：通过温度变化诱导热应变，激发材料非线性响应并调制沿材料传播的超声波；利用二次多项式模型对相对波速变化与温度变化的实验曲线进行曲线拟合，从多项式系数中提取声学非线性参数α, β, δ。
