Result: 实验结果表明相对波速变化与温度变化之间存在强相关性；参数α、β、δ分别控制相关曲线的滞回间隙（hysteretic gap）、平均斜率（average slope）和曲率（curvature）；该方法在铝、钢、完整及损伤混凝土样品上均得到验证，获得的非线性参数与文献报道值具有合理一致性。

Conclusion: 热调制方法相比振动或声激励方法能产生更均匀、缓慢变化且更大的应变场；将热效应作为非线性的驱动力而非不利影响因素，该方法能够使用简单的超声测试装置以良好精度测量α、β、δ的绝对值。

Abstract: This study presents a test method and its theoretical framework to determine the acoustic nonlinearity parameters ($α,β,δ$) of material using thermal modulation of ultrasonic waves. Temperature change induced thermal strain excites the nonlinear response of the material and modulates the ultrasonic wave propagating in it. Experimental results showed a strong correlation between the relative wave velocity change and the temperature change. With a quadratic polynomial model, the acoustic nonlinearity parameters were obtained from the polynomial coefficients by curve fitting the experimental curves. Their effects on thermal-induced velocity change were discussed. The parameters $α,β,δ$ govern the hysteretic gap, average slope, and curvature of the correlation curve, respectively. The proposed theory was validated on aluminum, steel, intact and damaged concrete samples. The obtained nonlinear parameters show reasonable agreements with values reported in the literature. Compared to other nonlinear acoustic methods using vibration or acoustic excitation, the thermal modulation method generates more uniform, slow changing, and larger strain field in the test sample. Employing thermal effect as the driving force for nonlinearity instead of an undesired influencing factor, this method can measure the absolute values of $α,β,δ$ with good accuracy using a simple ultrasonic test setup.

</details>


### [243] [200 mm Wafer-Scale Monolithic 3D Integration of Atomic Layer-Deposited Oxide Semiconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09508)
*Chang Niu,Linjia Long,Luqi Zheng,Shuting Du,Jian-Yu Lin,Kisoo Nam,Zehao Lin,Chang Liu,Juanjuan Lu,Haiyan Wang,Haitong Li,Peide D. Ye*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本论文在200毫米硅晶圆上实现了单片三维集成（monolithic 3D integration，M3D），将基于原子层沉积（atomic layer deposition，ALD）氧化铟（InOx）器件的三层结构（包含铁电、增强型和耗尽型场效应晶体管）集成在一起，制造器件数超过10万个，并进一步开发了面向大规模语言模型工作负载的四层三维存内计算加速器，相比二维基线实现了1.4倍至2.9倍的速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统硅基互补金属氧化物半导体（CMOS）技术面临缩放极限，单片三维集成通过向第三维扩展功能器件堆叠密度来突破这一瓶颈，为下一代人工智能硬件提供新的集成平台。

Method: 采用原子层沉积的氧化铟（InOx）作为沟道材料，在200毫米硅晶圆上制造含铁电、增强型和耗尽型场效应晶体管的三层M3D集成器件，并通过定制InOx工艺设计套件（process design kit，PDK）开发四层三维存内计算（computing-in-memory，CIM）加速器，用于大规模语言模型推理。

Result: 制造器件超过10万个，阈值电压标准偏差低至0.04伏，平均电子迁移率高达91.6立方厘米每伏特每秒，且跨层电路完全功能正常。四层CIM加速器相比二维基线实现1.4倍至2.9倍加速，并具有可比的能量延迟积改善。

Conclusion: 验证了ALD InOx M3D集成作为一种可扩展且与CMOS兼容的平台，适用于下一代人工智能硬件和先进电子器件。

Abstract: Monolithic 3D (M3D) integration offers a pathway to overcome the scaling limits of conventional silicon complementary metal-oxide-semiconductor (CMOS) technology by extending dense vertical stacking of multifunctional logic and memory devices. Here, we demonstrate wafer-scale M3D integration of three tiers of atomic-layer-deposited (ALD) indium oxide (InOx)-based devices (>100,000 fabricated), including ferroelectric, enhancement-mode, and depletion-mode field-effect transistors, on 200 mm silicon wafers. We achieve threshold voltage standard deviation as low as 0.04 V, average electron mobility up to 91.6 cm2V-1s-1, and fully functional cross-tier circuits. A four-tier 3D computing-in-memory (CIM) accelerator targeting large language model workloads is developed using a custom InOx process design kit, delivering 1.4x to 2.9x speedup and comparable energy-delay product improvements over 2D baselines. These results establish ALD InOx M3D integration as a scalable and CMOS-compatible platform for next-generation artificial intelligence hardware and advanced electronics.

</details>


<div id='cs.ET'></div>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [244] [Reservoir Computing with Heterogeneous Magnetic Metamaterials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08879)
*R. Yagan,C. Swindells,I. T. Vidamour,G. Venkat,J. C. Gartside,E. Vasilaki,M. O. A. Ellis,T. J. Hayward*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出并实验验证了一种基于磁性纳米环异质阵列的纳米磁储层计算（reservoir computing）器件，利用多通道平面霍尔效应（planar Hall effect）读出，通过几何异质性（track width从500 nm到300 nm）引入额外的计算自由度，在非线性信号变换和Mackey-Glass时间序列预测任务中显著降低了归一化均方根误差，为可扩展的磁性计算架构提供了新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理储层计算（physical reservoir computing）利用物理系统的固有非线性和历史依赖动力学来完成机器学习任务，具有极低训练开销的优势；然而如何在单一器件中获取多样化的动力学特征、增强储层表达能力（expressivity）仍是关键挑战。本文旨在通过设计几何异质的磁性纳米环阵列，探索几何异质性作为额外计算自由度的可行性。

Method: 构建一个由相互连接的磁性纳米环组成的异质阵列储层计算机，包含轨道宽度（track width）从500 nm系统递减至300 nm的子阵列，以在单一储层内引入几何多样化的磁动力学；采用多通道平面霍尔效应进行读出，并通过旋转驱动磁场的时间调制方式施加输入信号。实验中评估了该器件在非线性信号变换和Mackey-Glass时间序列预测任务上的性能，并利用主成分分析（principal component analysis, PCA）分析特征相关性。

Result: 多通道组合读出相比单通道读出显著降低了归一化均方根误差（normalized root-mean-square error），且最优通道组合取决于具体任务需求；主成分分析表明，一小部分相关的降维特征即可捕获大部分计算相关信息并抑制噪声贡献。

Conclusion: 受控的几何异质性（geometric heterogeneity）能够增强储层的表达能力和计算性能，并提供了一种实验上易于获取的额外自由度；多输出磁性超材料（magnetic metamaterials）可以作为可配置的动力学模块，为构建可扩展的磁性计算器件网络提供可行路线。

Abstract: Physical reservoir computing utilizes the intrinsic nonlinear and history-dependent dynamics of physical systems to perform machine-learning tasks with minimal training overhead. Here, we introduce a nanomagnetic reservoir computer based on a heterogeneous array of interconnected magnetic nanorings, combined with multi-channel planar Hall effect readout. The device comprises subarrays of rings with systematically varied track widths ranging from 500 nm to 300 nm, enabling access to the heterogeneous dynamics of geometrically diverse magnetic systems within a single reservoir. By applying time-varying input signals as modulations of a driving rotating magnetic field, we evaluate the nanoring reservoir's performance on nonlinear signal transformation and Mackey-Glass time-series prediction tasks. We find that combining outputs from multiple width-dependent channels significantly reduces the normalized root-mean-square error compared to single-channel readout, with the optimal channel combinations depending on task requirements. These results demonstrate that geometric heterogeneity provides an additional, experimentally accessible degree of freedom and complementary computational features. Principal component analysis further reveals that a reduced subset of correlated features captures most of the computationally relevant information while suppressing noise contributions. These results demonstrate that controlled geometric heterogeneity enhances reservoir expressivity and suggest a route toward scalable magnetic computing architectures in which multi-output magnetic metamaterials serve as configurable dynamical building blocks for device networks.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [245] [Optimally embedded tight binding for reproducing geometry dependent observables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07684)
*Jonas J Telle,Gunnar F Lange*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出“最优嵌入紧束缚”（optimally embedded tight binding, OETB）框架，将紧束缚模型中的轨道嵌入位置视为几何调优参数，通过参考响应（如从头算或实验）固定这些参数，从而在不损失能带结构精度的前提下定量复现非线性光学响应等几何依赖物理量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧束缚模型中位置算符被简化为晶胞内轨道位置（即嵌入），虽然能够准确复现能带结构，但在依赖几何的响应（如与位置算符相关的输运或光学性质）上常常失效。作者希望解决这一矛盾，建立既能保持能带精度又能正确描述几何响应的小型模型。

Method: 作者将轨道嵌入视为待定几何调优参数，并通过将紧束缚可观测量分解为几何无关与几何相关两部分，利用位置导数高效地求取最优嵌入。文中给出了关键量（如量子几何张量，quantum geometric tensor）的显式导数表达式，并针对GaAs和CdS进行数值拟合。

Result: 所构建的GaAs和CdS紧束缚模型在保持能带结构精度的同时，定量复现了非线性光学响应；在玩具模型和陈绝缘体（Chern insulator）V2O3中，几何效应可显著改变局域度量迹（local metric trace）。

Conclusion: 轨道嵌入应被视为真正的模型参数，并须通过物理数据显式标定，从而获得准确的最小模型（minimal models）。

Abstract: In tight-binding models, the position operator is reduced to intra-cell orbital positions (embeddings). While accurately reproducing band structures, such models often fail for geometry dependent responses depending on the position operator. To address this, we investigate the role of these embeddings and introduce the general framework of optimally embedded tight binding. Treating the embeddings as geometric tuning parameters to be fixed against a reference response (obtained from ab-initio computation or experiment), we obtain tight-binding models of GaAs and CdS which quantitatively reproduce non-linear optical responses at no cost to band structure accuracy. The optimal embeddings are determined efficiently using position derivatives obtained from decomposing tight-binding observables into a geometry independent and dependent part, and explicit derivatives are provided for key quantities such as the quantum geometric tensor. The decomposition reveals where geometric effects dominate, and we show in both toy models and in the Chern insulator V2O3 how geometry can dramatically alter the local metric trace. Our results highlight that orbital embeddings should be treated as a genuine model parameter which should be explicitly fixed against physical data to get accurate minimal models.

</details>


### [246] [Ferroelectric-controllable spin-orbit torque in two-dimensional multiferroic heterostructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07731)
*Weiyi Pan,Gusthavo M. S. Brizolla,Jaroslav Fabian*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文基于第一性原理计算（first-principles calculation），以多铁异质结 Fe₃GeTe₂/In₂Se₃ 为代表体系，证明了通过切换 In₂Se₃ 层的铁电极化方向可以显著调控自旋轨道矩（spin-orbit torque, SOT）的矩臂分布（torkance）。当面内磁化时反转极化方向可使总矩臂提升至原值的150%以上，其机制源于极化诱导的 Fermi 面附近 Γ 点电子态重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋轨道矩（SOT）可实现磁化的电学调控，是下一代自旋电子学器件（spintronic devices）的关键。作者希望在二维范德华（van der Waals）体系中实现 SOT，并通过铁电性实现高效非易失操控，从而为可调逻辑器件和高存储密度应用提供新方案。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算方法，构建多铁 Fe₃GeTe₂/In₂Se₃ 异质结作为代表体系，系统计算不同铁电极化方向下体系内磁化依赖的矩臂（torkance）分布，并对矩臂进行原子分辨和对称性（时间反演奇偶）分解分析。

Result: 当磁化处于面内（转矩最大方向）时，将 In₂Se₃ 极化从向上翻转为向下可使总矩臂增强至原值的150%以上；该变化主要源于时间反演奇矩臂（time-reversal-odd torkance）的 z 分量调制，其中 Fe₃GeTe₂ 中间 Fe 层的原子分辨矩臂变化约达233%。Fermi 面附近 Γ 点的电子态在极化切换后发生显著重构，是矩臂变化的微观起源。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了范德华多铁异质结中 SOT 的可电学调控潜力，提供了一种通过铁电极化实现电可调 SOT 的可行策略，为未来可编程自旋电子学器件（programmable spintronic devices）铺平道路。

Abstract: Spin-orbit torque (SOT), which enables electrical control of magnetization, plays a crucial role in the development of next-generation spintronic devices. Realizing SOT in two-dimensional van der Waals systems, together with achieving efficient nonvolatile manipulation via ferroelectricity, would be highly beneficial for the implementation of tunable logic devices with enhanced storage density. In this work, based on first-principles calculation and using a multiferroic Fe$_{3}$GeTe$_{2}$/In$_{2}$Se$_{3}$ heterostructure as a representative example, we demonstrate that switching the ferroelectric polarization of the In$_{2}$Se$_{3}$ layer induces a pronounced modification in the magnetization-dependent distribution of torkance within the heterostructure. Specifically, when the magnetization is in the plane, where the torque is maximal, reversing the polarization of In$_{2}$Se$_{3}$ from upward to downward enhances the total torkance to more than 150% of its original value. This substantial variation primarily originates from the polarization-induced modulation of the $z$ component of the time-reversal-odd torkance, which is mainly associated with an approximately 233% change in the atomic-resolved torque contributed from the middle Fe layer in Fe$_{3}$GeTe$_{2}$ layer. Further analysis reveals that the electronic states near $Γ$ on the Fermi surface undergo significant reconstruction upon polarization switching, which is responsible for the observed variation in the time-reversal-odd torque. Our results not only provide new insights into the functional potential of van der Waals multiferroic heterostructures, but also offer a viable strategy for achieving electrically tunable SOT, paving the way for future programmable spintronic devices.

</details>


### [247] [Dynamical quantum phase transitions in a hybrid quantum dot system with superconducting and ferromagnetic leads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07744)
*Antoni Jankiewicz,Kacper Wrześniewski,Ireneusz Weymann*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文利用含时数值重整化群（time-dependent numerical renormalization group）技术，理论研究了同时耦合铁磁电极与超导电极的单个量子点系统的非平衡动力学，分析了耦合参数突变和轨道能级移动引发的时序演化行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米尺度混合系统（nanoscale hybrid systems）中，铁磁与超导电极共存会引入超导配对关联与交换场的竞争，理解其非平衡动力学行为对揭示这类系统的基本物理至关重要。

Method: 采用含时数值重整化群技术，以完全非微扰（fully non-perturbative）的方式捕捉系统对突变参数变化的响应；通过对引线耦合参数的突然修改或轨道能级移动来驱动系统，并计算局部超导配对关联（superconducting pairing correlations）与磁化强度（magnetization）的时间演化，同时考察Loschmidt回波（Loschmidt echo）与回返函数（return function）以探测动力学量子相变（dynamical quantum phase transitions）的特征。

Result: 计算得到了局部超导配对关联与磁化的时间演化，分析了相关能量谱，并确定了Loschmidt回波与回返函数的行为，揭示了超导配对与铁磁接触诱导交换场（induced exchange field）之间非平凡的竞争关系。

Conclusion: 本研究深化了对纳米尺度混合系统动力学行为的理解，阐明了超导配对关联与铁磁交换场竞争在非平衡过程中的具体表现。

Abstract: We theoretically explore the non-equilibrium dynamics of a single quantum dot system coupled to both ferromagnetic and superconducting electrodes. To investigate its time evolution, we utilize the time-dependent numerical renormalization group technique, which captures the system's response to abrupt parameter changes in a fully non-perturbative manner. Our analysis focuses on dynamics following a sudden modification in the couplings to the leads or a shift of the orbital level. In particular, we calculate the time evolution of the induced local superconducting pairing correlations and magnetization. In this context, the relevant energy spectra are examined. Moreover, we study the behavior of the Loschmidt echo and the return function to shed light on the signatures of dynamical quantum phase transitions. The determined dependencies reveal non-trivial competition between relevant correlations, involving superconducting pairing and ferromagnetic-contacted induced exchange field, and deepen our understanding of nanoscale hybrid systems' dynamical behavior.

</details>


### [248] [Probing intermittent polariton vortex dynamics with two-point correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07748)
*R. Cherbunin,A. Liubomirov,P. Kozhevin,M. Chukeev,M. Petrov,S. Kavokina,A. Nalitov,A. Kavokin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用时间分辨率为80皮秒的超导单光子探测器，研究了受限激子极化激元玻色凝聚体的时空动力学，发现一阶与二阶关联函数均呈现由凝聚体在阱中动力学主导的时间振荡，并识别出安德罗诺夫-霍普夫分岔附近稳态与极限环之间的间歇性随机转变，理论上解释为自排斥与库池介导吸引相互补偿的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激子极化激元（exciton-polaritons）凝聚体的时空动力学是量子流体与玻色凝聚研究的前沿问题，但此前缺乏高时间分辨率的实验手段来精细观测其关联函数的时间演化特性。本文旨在通过超导单光子探测器的高时间分辨率，揭示凝聚体在受限势阱中的动力学细节。

Method: 采用时间分辨率为80皮秒的超导单光子探测器（superconducting single-photon detector），对捕获在势阱中的激子极化激元玻色凝聚体进行测量，计算其二阶关联函数（second-order correlation function）和一阶关联函数（first-order correlation function）的时间依赖行为。

Result: 一阶与二阶关联函数均呈现出显著的随时间振荡，其演化由阱中极化激元凝聚体的动力学所支配。在安德罗诺夫-霍普夫分岔（Andronov-Hopf bifurcation）附近，观测到稳态与极限环（limit-cycle）之间的间歇性随机转变，表现为关联函数的不对称扭曲。

Conclusion: 确定性动力学与随机（stochastic）动力学的丰富交织可通过自排斥（self-repulsion）与库池介导吸引（reservoir-mediated attraction）之间的相互补偿机制在理论上予以解释，这项工作为理解极化激元凝聚体中非线性动力学与相变的相互关系提供了新的实验证据。

Abstract: Superconducting single-photon detectors with time resolution of 80 ps have been used to study the spatiotemporal dynamics of a trapped bosonic condensate of exciton-polaritons. Both second- and first-order correlation functions are found to exhibit pronounced oscillations in time governed by the dynamics of the polariton condensate in the trap. We have identified the intermittent regime of stochastic transitions between stationary and limit-cycle regimes near the Andronov-Hopf bifurcation manifested in asymmetric distortions of the correlation function. This rich interplay of deterministic and stochastic condensate dynamics is explained theoretically as a manifestation of mutually compensating self-repulsion and reservoir-mediated attraction.

</details>


### [249] [All-electrical Coherent Control of a Single Rare-earth Spin Qubit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07929)
*Yaowu Liu,Dasom Choi,Stefano Reale,Jeongmin Oh,Seorhin Choi,Lei Fang,We-hyo Soe,Arzhang Ardavan,Andreas J. Heinrich,Soo-hyon Phark,Fabio Donati*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究在扫描隧道显微镜（STM）中利用三维磁场控制，实现了对与Ti原子交换耦合的单个Er电子自旋的全电学相干控制，通过各向异性交换耦合实现了近GHz的拉比频率，比现有稀土自旋量子比特记录提高十倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单自旋量子比特（single spin qubit）的电学控制是纳米级、高速可扩展量子器件的重大前沿方向，但将这一技术扩展到高屏蔽的稀土4f电子在固态平台上仍是实验挑战。

Method: 使用扫描隧道显微镜电子自旋共振（STM-based electron spin resonance）并结合三维磁场控制，系统测量Er自旋的共振频率和拉比（Rabi）频率，以全面映射Er g张量（g-tensor）和Er-Ti交换相互作用的各向异性；通过电学调制各向异性的Er-Ti耦合来实现对Er自旋的有效驱动。

Result: 研究揭示了Er的g张量和Er-Ti交换相互作用均具有显著的各向异性（anisotropy），利用该各向异性交换耦合实现了高效的Er自旋电学驱动，获得接近GHz的拉比频率，相比现有稀土自旋量子比特记录提升了十倍。

Conclusion: 各向异性交换耦合（anisotropic exchange）可作为电学访问高屏蔽稀土自旋的一般性资源（general resource），为固态量子器件中稀土自旋的超快和局域控制开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Electrical control of single spin qubits is a major frontier for nanoscale, high-speed, and scalable quantum devices. Yet, extending it to highly shielded rare-earth 4f electrons remains an experimental challenge across solid-state platforms. Here we demonstrate all-electrical coherent control of a single Er electron spin, which is exchange-coupled to a nearby Ti atom. Scanning tunneling microscopy-based electron spin resonance with three-dimensional magnetic-field control enables comprehensive mapping of the resonance and Rabi frequencies, revealing pronounced anisotropies in both the Er g-tensor and the Er-Ti exchange interaction. The electrical modulation of the anisotropic Er-Ti coupling results in an efficient drive of the Er spin, allowing us to achieve near-gigahertz Rabi frequencies - a ten-fold improvement over the present record for rare-earth spin qubits. By establishing anisotropic exchange as a general resource for electrically accessing shielded rare-earth spins, our results open a new route to ultrafast and local control of rare-earth spins in solid-state quantum devices.

</details>


### [250] [Multiphysics Modeling of Thermo-Viscoelastic Damage in Functionally Graded Abradable Coatings with Probabilistic Geometric Tolerance Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08116)
*Amjad El-Mellouhi,Yassine Adjal,Khaled Dhibi,Fedwa El-Mellouhi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一个集成黏弹性软化、渐进损伤、沉积导致的微观组织调制和几何公差的多物理场-概率统计算框架（multiphysics–probabilistic framework），用于分析航空发动机中功能梯度可磨耗涂层（functionally graded abradable coating）的耐久性。结果表明周期性性能调制相比单调梯度会增大周期间损伤并集中于局部波峰处，且通过概率分析得到的右偏损伤分布与超阈值概率无法由标称几何下的确定性分析复现。框架具备空间/时间收敛验证、闭式单元测试、嵌套蒙特卡洛收敛及响应面校验，并强调数值验证与实验验证的明确区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空发动机中用于控制叶尖间隙的功能梯度可磨耗涂层，其耐久性受温度依赖的黏弹性软化、渐进损伤、沉积诱导的微观结构调制及几何公差等多个效应共同支配，而现有模型通常将这些效应分开处理，缺乏统一的综合框架来评估涂层在多种耦合因素下的可靠性与失效风险。

Method: 作者将这些效应集成到一个统一的多物理场-概率统计算框架中，求解域为局部穿厚度涂层柱，由预设应变和温度履历驱动，并包含热本征应变（thermal eigenstrain）和涂层-基底膨胀失配。损伤分布通过概率传播（propagating geometric tolerances through the coupled solver）获得，并使用嵌套蒙特卡洛（nested Monte Carlo）收敛与Wilson置信区间，以及保留验证的多项式响应面（hold-out-validated polynomial response surface）作为辅助验证手段。

Result: 研究发现，周期性性能调制相对于单调梯度会增加周期间（end-of-cycle）损伤，并将最大应力集中在局部调制波峰处。在耦合求解器中传播对称几何公差后，损伤分布呈强烈右偏（right-skewed），调制梯度的分类级超阈值概率（exceedance probability）高于单调梯度，且该超阈值行为无法由标称几何下的确定性分析获得。实现通过空间与时间细化、闭式单元测试、嵌套蒙特卡洛收敛和保留验证响应面得到了验证。

Conclusion: 该框架将沉积参数与公差带关联至损伤超阈值风险，支持分级及多层涂层系统的可靠性导向筛选（reliability-oriented screening）。作者同时强调数值验证（numerical verification）与实验验证（experimental validation）的明确区分，并将所模拟案例定位为通用基准（generic benchmark）而非某特定材料模型。

Abstract: In aircraft engines, functionally graded abradable coatings are used to control blade-tip clearance, but their durability is governed by effects that are often treated separately in existing models, including temperature-dependent viscoelastic softening, progressive damage, deposition-induced microstructural modulation, and geometric tolerances. This study integrates these effects within a unified multiphysics--probabilistic framework. The solved domain is a local through-thickness coating column driven by prescribed strain and temperature histories that include thermal eigenstrain and coating--substrate expansion mismatch. The results show that periodic property modulation increases end-of-cycle damage relative to the monotonic gradient and concentrates the maximum stress within a localized modulation crest. Propagating symmetric geometric tolerances through the coupled solver produces a strongly right-skewed damage distribution and a higher classification-level exceedance probability for the modulated gradient than for the monotonic gradient. This exceedance behavior cannot be obtained from a deterministic analysis performed at nominal geometry. The implementation is verified through spatial and temporal refinement, closed-form unit tests, nested Monte Carlo convergence with Wilson confidence intervals, and a hold-out-validated polynomial response surface. Numerical verification is clearly distinguished from experimental validation, and the simulated case is presented as a generic benchmark rather than an identified material model. The framework links deposition parameters and tolerance bands to damage-exceedance risk, supporting reliability-oriented screening of graded and multilayer coating systems.

</details>


### [251] [Magnetoelastic coupling in stripe-domain states of yttrium iron garnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08353)
*Nimisha Arora,Daniel Prestwood,Takashi Kikkawa,Eiji Saitoh,Jack Gartside,Will Branford,Hidekazu Kurebayashi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究在3微米厚YIG薄膜的条状磁畴态中研究了磁弹性耦合，发现低频频段的条状磁畴磁振子分支被以3.5 MHz间隔的场无关声子梳调制，耦合率介于0.33–0.54 MHz，合作度约10⁻¹，系统处于弱耦合区域。通过有限元模拟揭示，由于相位和磁畴符号抵消，超过99%的相干重叠被消除，从而产生弱耦合；全耦合模拟进一步表明，高效的传播声子产生需要空间非对称的磁振子模式，确立磁织构可作为磁振子—声子耦合的控制参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究磁织构（magnetic texture）如何影响磁振子—声子耦合（magnon–phonon coupling），特别是在条状磁畴态中的耦合行为，以探索磁织构作为耦合控制参数的可能性。

Method: 采用宽带铁磁共振（broadband ferromagnetic resonance）实验测量YIG薄膜的条状磁畴磁振子分支；通过解析拟合提取耦合率与合作度；利用有限元模拟（finite-element simulations）进行磁振子—声子模式重叠计算以及全耦合模拟验证。

Result: 观测到场无关声子梳频率间隔3.5 MHz，与GGG衬底受限厚度剪切模式预测值吻合；耦合率为0.33–0.54 MHz，合作度约10⁻¹，系统处于弱耦合区域且无可分辨的回避交叉（avoided-crossing）间隙；超过99%的相干重叠因相位和磁畴符号抵消而消失；全耦合模拟证实声子可介导远程YIG层激发，且高效传播声子产生需空间非对称磁振子模式。

Conclusion: 尽管局部磁弹性耦合可观，但由于条状磁畴结构中相干重叠的显著抵消，系统整体上呈现弱耦合；研究确立磁织构作为磁振子—声子耦合的有效控制参数，为基于磁织构调控的混合量子系统设计提供新思路。

Abstract: We study magnetoelastic coupling in stripe-domain magnetic states of $3\,\mathrm{μm}$-thick YIG thin films grown on a GGG substrate. Broadband ferromagnetic resonance reveals low-frequency stripe-domain magnon branches modulated by a field-independent phonon comb with a frequency spacing of $3.5\,\mathrm{MHz}$, matching the value predicted for confined thickness-shear modes of the GGG substrate. Analytical fitting yields coupling rates that vary between $0.33$--$0.54\,\mathrm{MHz}$ and cooperativities of order $10^{-1}$, indicating that the system is in the weak-coupling regime without resolvable avoided-crossing gaps. Magnon-phonon mode-overlap calculations using finite-element simulations show that the weak coupling arises from phase and domain-sign cancellation: the local magnetoelastic coupling is sizable, but more than $99\%$ of the coherent overlap cancels across the stripe texture. Fully coupled simulations further demonstrate phonon-mediated excitation of a remote YIG layer and show that efficient propagating-phonon generation requires spatially asymmetric magnon modes, establishing magnetic texture as a control parameter for magnon--phonon coupling.

</details>


### [252] [Quantum-Device Simulation of Optical Decoherence of Hole-Spin Qubits in Self-Assembled Quantum Dots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08475)
*Jyun-Jie Jiang,Pericles Philippopoulos,Félix Beaudoin,Hong Guo*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种设备级建模流程，将真实的半导体自组装量子点（SAQD）几何结构、多能带电子结构分析与电驱动自旋控制、带间光学跃迁和开放系统动力学模型相结合，用于估计由重复带间吸收-发射循环引起的Rabi振荡阻尼。以门控GaAs SAQD为例，研究预测了均匀磁场下空穴自旋Rabi频率及其在外部照明下的阻尼行为，并发现量子点高度、自旋控制条件和照明光谱需要共同优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋-光子界面（spin-photon interface）是量子技术中远距离自旋量子比特通信的关键，但带间光学激发会阻尼电驱动的空穴自旋Rabi振荡（Rabi oscillation），这一问题在半导体自组装量子点中尤为突出，目前缺乏设备级层面的定量评估方法。

Method: 开发了一种集成多物理场的设备级建模工作流程，涵盖真实SAQD几何结构、多能带电子结构分析（multiband electronic-structure analysis）、电驱动自旋控制、带间光学跃迁（interband optical transition）以及开放系统动力学（open-system dynamics）模型，用于估算由重复吸收-发射循环引起的Rabi振荡阻尼。

Result: 在沿生长方向施加均匀磁场B₀=2 T的门控GaAs SAQD中，计算得到空穴自旋Rabi频率为37.3 MHz。当以中心波长790 nm的宽带LED照明时，将光功率从0.3 mW增加到1.5 mW会使Rabi振荡衰减时间从90.3 ns缩短至17.5 ns。增加量子点高度虽降低了电子-空穴重叠和发射率，但由此产生的红移使带间跃迁与LED光谱重叠增强，反而增大了光子诱导的Rabi振荡阻尼。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在半导体自旋-光子器件中，几何结构、自旋控制条件和照明光谱应进行协同优化（co-optimization），以实现自旋-光子接口功能与空穴自旋相干保持之间的平衡。

Abstract: Spin-photon interfaces are essential for communications between distant spin qubits in quantum technologies, but the interband optical excitation can also damp electrically driven hole-spin Rabi oscillations in semiconductor self-assembled quantum dots (SAQDs). We report a device-level modeling workflow that integrates realistic SAQD geometry and multiband electronic-structure analysis with models of electrically driven spin control, interband optical transitions, and open-system dynamics. This workflow enables device-level estimation of Rabi-oscillation damping arising from repeated interband absorption-emission cycles. As an example, for a gated GaAs SAQD subjected to a uniform magnetic field $B_0$ along the growth direction of the SAQD, we predict the Rabi frequency of the hole spin qubit and its damping under external illumination. At $B_0=2$ T, the calculations yield a hole-spin Rabi frequency of 37.3 MHz. When the electrically driven SAQD is illuminated by a broadband LED centered at a wavelength of 790 nm, increasing the optical power from 0.3 to 1.5 mW shortens the Rabi-oscillation decay time from 90.3 to 17.5 ns. Increasing the SAQD height reduces the electron-hole overlap and thus the emission rate, but the resulting redshift moves the interband transitions into stronger spectral overlap with the LED spectrum, thereby increasing the rate of repeated absorption-emission cycles and enhancing photon-induced Rabi-oscillation damping. The results show that geometry, spin-control conditions, and illumination spectrum should be co-optimized in semiconductor spin-photon devices.

</details>


### [253] [Crystalline Group-IV Josephson Junction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08500)
*Frederik H. Knudsen,Axel Leblanc,Patrick J. Strohbeen,Jechiel van Dijk,Yiliu Li,Logan Kusher,Arunav Bordoloi,Alisa Danilenko,Xiangchao Ma,Salva Salmani-Rezaie,Javad Shabani*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于掺镓锗（gallium-doped germanium）超导性的全外延（fully epitaxial）方法，通过分子束外延（molecular beam epitaxy）原位生长约瑟夫森结，实现原子级锐利界面和晶体弱连接，并观察到磁场下开关电流的非常规增强现象，为CMOS兼容的超导量子比特提供了新平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统超导量子电子学依赖Al/AlOₓ约瑟夫森结，其中非晶AlOₓ弱连接被认为承载二能级系统（two-level systems），限制了相干时间；而晶体约瑟夫森结虽具有原子有序界面，却受限于复杂制备工艺和外延生长的固有不对称性，亟需一种兼具结构相干性、制备简便性和可扩展性的替代方案。

Method: 采用基于掺镓锗超导性的全外延路线，通过分子束外延完全原位生长约瑟夫森结，形成原子级锐利的界面和晶体弱连接，实现超短间距（ultra-short regime）下的强约瑟夫森耦合。

Result: 器件展现出强约瑟夫森耦合特性；在施加磁场下观察到开关电流（switching current）的非常规增强现象，该现象被归因于Al接触中的准粒子辅助热化过程（quasiparticle-assisted thermalization processes）。

Conclusion: 该平台兼具结构相干性、制备简便性和可扩展性，为在融合元素transmon（merged element transmon）架构中实现低无序、CMOS兼容的超导量子比特提供了有前景的路径。

Abstract: Conventional superconducting quantum electronics rely on well-established Josephson junctions made of Al/AlO$_{x}$ where the weak link AlO$_{x}$ is amorphous and is believed to host two-level systems that limit coherence. Crystalline Josephson junctions exhibit atomically ordered interface quality but remain constrained by complex fabrication and intrinsic asymmetry of epitaxial growth. Here, we demonstrate a fully epitaxial approach based on superconductivity in gallium-doped germanium, enabling the realization of Josephson junctions entirely grown in situ by molecular beam epitaxy. These devices feature atomically sharp interfaces and crystalline weak links, resulting in strong Josephson coupling in the ultra-short regime. We observe an unconventional enhancement of the switching current under applied magnetic field, which we attribute to quasiparticle-assisted thermalization processes from the Al contacts. This platform combines structural coherence, fabrication simplicity, and scalability, offering a promising route toward low-disorder, CMOS-compatible superconducting qubits in a merged element transmon architecture.

</details>


### [254] [Spin-Orbital Hall Nano-Oscillators using PtCr/NiFe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08504)
*Utkarsh Shashank,Akash Kumar,Daegeun Jo,Thi Ngoc Anh Nguyen,Jong-Guk Choi,Sambit Ghosh,Michal Strach,Lunjie Zeng,Andrew B. Yankovich,Roman Khymyn,Ahmad A. Awad,Eva Olsson,Peter M. Oppeneer,Johan Åkerman*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过将Cr的轨道霍尔效应（orbital Hall effect）与Pt的自旋转换能力结合于均匀的PtCr轻/重金属合金中，实现了巨大的自旋轨道矩（spin-orbit torques），制备出自旋-轨道霍尔纳米振荡器，将相干自动振荡的阈值电流密度从~1.07×10^12降至~4.4×10^11 A m^-2，为低功耗非线性自旋电子学（spintronics）和轨道电子学（orbitronics）器件提供了可扩展的材料平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PtCr合金展现出异常大的电流诱导力矩，但轨道输运（orbital transport）的贡献以及这些力矩能否维持相干非线性磁化动力学仍未解决。本研究旨在阐明轨道输运机制，并利用该机制实现低功耗的相干纳米振荡器。

Method: 作者利用均匀的PtCr/Cr合金异质结构（PtCr/NiFe heterostructures），使Cr产生的轨道霍尔电流被Pt转换为自旋电流，从而产生巨大的自旋轨道矩。通过实验测量PtCr/NiFe异质结构的有效力矩效率与阈值电流密度，并结合第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）分析Cr合金化对自旋与轨道霍尔电导率的影响。

Result: PtCr/NiFe异质结构的有效力矩效率从Pt/NiFe的~0.14提升至Pt0.38Cr0.62/NiFe的~0.40，尽管Pt被大量稀释；相干自动振荡的阈值电流密度从~1.07×10^12降至~4.4×10^11 A m^-2。第一性原理计算表明，Cr合金化抑制本征自旋霍尔电导率（intrinsic spin Hall conductivity），同时增强轨道霍尔电导率（orbital Hall conductivity），只有纳入轨道输运才能复现实验观察到的力矩增强。

Conclusion: 合金工程（alloy engineering）能够通过本征的轨道中介贡献（intrinsic orbital-mediated contribution）实现巨大的自旋轨道矩，使相干自动振荡无需工程化多层结构即可实现，从而确立了一种用于低功耗非线性自旋电子学与轨道电子学器件的可扩展材料平台。

Abstract: The orbital Hall effect provides a promising route for generating angular-momentum currents beyond conventional spin Hall physics. PtCr alloys exhibit unusually large current-induced torques, but the contribution of orbital transport and the ability of these torques to sustain coherent nonlinear magnetization dynamics remain unresolved. Here we demonstrate spin-orbital Hall nano-oscillators by exploiting a homogeneous heavy-metal/light-metal alloy in which orbital Hall currents generated by Cr are converted by Pt into spin currents, producing giant spin-orbit torques. Using PtCr/NiFe heterostructures, the effective torque efficiency increases from ~0.14 in Pt/NiFe to ~0.40 in Pt0.38Cr0.62/NiFe despite substantial Pt dilution, enabling coherent auto-oscillations with the threshold current density reduced from ~ 1.07 x 10^12 to ~ 4.4 x 10^11 A m^-2. First-principles calculations show that Cr alloying suppresses the intrinsic spin Hall conductivity while enhancing the orbital Hall conductivity, and reproduce the observed torque enhancement only when orbital transport is included. Our combined experimental and first-principles results show that alloy engineering enables giant spin-orbit torques through an intrinsic orbital-mediated contribution, enabling coherent auto-oscillations without engineered multilayers and establishing a scalable materials platform for low-power nonlinear spintronic and orbitronic devices.

</details>


### [255] [High second Chern number induced by long-range hopping in a four-dimensional Dirac model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08670)
*Zheng-Rong Liu,Xiang Liu,Rui Chen,Bin Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究表明，在四维（4D）狄拉克模型中引入长程跳跃（long-range hopping）可诱导出高二阶陈数（second Chern number）的拓扑相，并能将平庸绝缘体转变为非零二阶陈数的拓扑绝缘体，为四维拓扑态工程提供了新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四维拓扑体系为探索超越三维的拓扑现象提供了有前景的平台，但以往对四维拓扑绝缘体的研究主要集中在四维狄拉克模型上，其第二陈数取值受限，多样性不足。

Method: 在四维狄拉克模型中引入长程跳跃项，系统研究其对体系拓扑性质的影响，考察不同长程跳跃参数下的拓扑相变及其对应的第二陈数取值。

Result: 长程跳跃的引入能够在四维狄拉克模型中诱导出具有高第二陈数的拓扑相；同时，长程跳跃还能将平庸绝缘体转变为具有非零第二陈数的拓扑绝缘体。

Conclusion: 长程跳跃是工程化四维拓扑态的有力手段，为超越最小模型（minimal models）实现非常规拓扑相提供了新的可能性。

Abstract: Four-dimensional (4D) topological systems provide a promising platform for exploring topological phenomena beyond three dimensions. So far, extensive recent studies on 4D topological insulators have focused on the 4D Dirac model, while its second Chern number is restricted to a limited set of values. In this work, we demonstrate that introducing long-range hopping into the 4D Dirac model induces topological phases with high second Chern numbers. Furthermore, we show that the long-range hopping can transform a trivial insulator into a topological insulator with a nonzero second Chern number. Our work establishes long-range hopping as a powerful route for engineering 4D topological states and reveals new possibilities for realizing unconventional topological phases beyond minimal models.

</details>


### [256] [Thermodynamic evidence for interaction-driven first-order topological quantum phase transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08697)
*Surajit Dutta,Nadav Auerbach,Chiho Yoon,Tonghang Han,Matan Uzan,Zhengguang Lu,Niladri-Sekhar Kander,Yaozhang Zhou,Yuri Myasoedov,Martin E. Huber,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Long Ju,Fan Zhang,Eli Zeldov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究利用纳米SQUID探针磁学测量技术，首次实现对自旋轨道近邻化菱面体石墨烯量子反常霍尔（QAH）态局部轨道磁化的实时空间可视化，结合自洽Hartree-Fock计算，直接证明了层反铁磁、QAH和层极化绝缘态之间的连续转变为一阶相变，并观察到磁畴涨落，提供了一阶拓扑量子相变的直接热力学证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非相互作用系统中，拓扑量子相变（topological quantum phase transition）通过连续能隙闭合与重开发生；但在强相互作用系统中，理论长期预测竞争有序态会驱动一阶相变，这一可能性此前在实验上尚未得到解决。近期对电荷中性菱面体石墨烯关联相的输运研究被解释为连续拓扑转变的证据，需要直接的实验验证。

Method: 采用基于纳米SQUID探针的磁学测量（nanoSQUID on tip magnetometry）技术，直接成像自旋轨道近邻化（spin orbit proximitized）菱面体石墨烯量子反常霍尔态的局部轨道磁化；结合自洽Hartree-Fock计算，重构局部热力学能隙并追踪磁化在竞争关联态间的演化。

Result: 首次实现对具有创纪录陈数（Chern number）的QAH相进行实空间可视化；测量与计算共同表明，层反铁磁（layer antiferromagnetic）、QAH和层极化绝缘态之间的顺序转变为一阶相变，伴随轨道磁化的不连续跳变。在相边界附近观察到波动的磁畴，为近简并竞争有序态之间的相共存提供了直接的微观证据。

Conclusion: 这些观测提供了关于一阶拓扑量子相变的首次直接热力学证据，为通过相竞争与相共存机制理解相互作用驱动的拓扑量子相变建立了微观框架。

Abstract: Topological quantum phase transitions in non interacting systems occur through continuous gap closing and reopening. In strongly interacting systems, however, competing ordered states have long been predicted to drive first order transitions, although this possibility has remained experimentally unresolved. Recent transport studies of correlated phases in charge neutral rhombohedral graphene were interpreted as evidence for continuous topological transitions. Here, using nanoSQUID on tip magnetometry, we directly image the local orbital magnetization of a spin orbit proximitized rhombohedral graphene quantum anomalous Hall (QAH) state. We provide the first real space visualization of a QAH phase with a record Chern number, reconstruct its local thermodynamic gap, and track the evolution of its magnetization across competing correlated states. Combined with self consistent Hartree Fock calculations, these measurements show that the sequential transitions between the layer antiferromagnetic, QAH, and layer polarized insulating states are first order, accompanied by discontinuous changes in orbital magnetization. Near the phase boundaries, we observe fluctuating magnetic domains, providing direct microscopic evidence of phase coexistence between nearly degenerate competing ordered states. Together, these observations provide the first direct thermodynamic evidence for first order topological quantum phase transitions and establish a microscopic framework for understanding interaction driven topological quantum phase transitions through phase competition and coexistence.

</details>


### [257] [Interaction between Rydberg Excitons in Cuprous Oxide Revealed through Resonant Second Harmonic Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08705)
*Andreas Farenbruch,Henje Stolz,Peter Grünwald,Dirk Semkat,Nikita Siverin,Dmitri R. Yakovlev,Dietmar Fröhlich,Manfred Bayer*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过二次谐波产生（second harmonic generation, SHG）实验和半经典理论，研究了氧化亚铜（Cu₂O）黄系激子（exciton）在不同泵浦强度下的光谱行为，发现激子间相互作用导致能级红移、谱线展宽和强度饱和效应，并提出里德伯阻塞（Rydberg blockade）机制可定量解释实验结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索高主量子数（principal quantum number）激子间的相互作用机制，因为激子作为玻色准粒子，其相互作用行为与原子间的范德华（van der Waals）相互作用是否一致尚不清晰，需要通过实验定量表征激子-激子相互作用的强度与标度律。

Method: 实验上采用皮秒脉冲激光（峰值强度最高10 GW/cm²）激发Cu₂O黄系激子并测量SHG光谱，观察不同泵浦功率下谱线的能量位移、线宽和强度标度；理论上构建了全相干的SHG半经典理论，将激子视为由距离依赖势相互作用的玻色子，通过里德伯阻塞机制解释谱形变化与饱和现象；同时结合双光子吸收测量导出激子-激子相互作用的定量数值，并排除三光子吸收产生的电子-空穴等离子体屏蔽效应。

Result: 实验观察到随泵浦功率增加，SHG谱线向低能方向移动且谱线展宽；低功率下谱线强度与泵浦功率平方成正比，高功率下饱和并进一步降低。理论模型与实验定量吻合，得到的激子-激子相互作用量级与原子范德华理论预测一致，但激子间相互作用随主量子数的标度行为与原子情形显著不同。

Conclusion: 由于可排除电子-空穴等离子体屏蔽效应的可能贡献，实验结果表明激子与原子之间的基本物理存在本质差异，激子-激子相互作用不能简单地类比于原子间范德华相互作用来描述。

Abstract: We report experimental and theoretical investigations of interacting excitons of the yellow series in cuprous oxide (Cu$_2$O) with principal quantum numbers up to by means of second harmonic generation (SHG). Using picosecond pulsed laser excitation up to 10 GW/cm$^2$ peak intensity we observe a pronounced change of the spectra with increasing pump laser intensity: an energetic shift to lower absolute energies and a spectral broadening. The absolute intensities of the spectral lines scale for low powers with the square of the pump power, but saturates at higher powers. At still higher powers the SHG intensity is actually reduced. To explain these results quantitively, we developed a semi-classical theory of resonant SHG where the process of SHG is fully coherent. The excitons are assumed to be bosons interacting by a distance dependent potential giving rise to both the changes in spectral line shape and the saturation by a $\it{Rydberg}$ blockade. The concomitant measurement of two-photon absorption allows to derive quantitative values for the exciton-exciton interaction. While the results agree in order of magnitude with those calculated by state-of-the art atomic-like van der Waals interaction theory, the scaling with principle quantum number is quite different. As a possible screening by an electron-hole plasma created by three-photon absorption into blue and violet band states could be ruled out, our results point toward fundamental differences between excitons and atoms.

</details>


### [258] [Time-Reversal-Invariant Altermagnetic Acoustic Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08827)
*Tianzhi Xia,Han-Rong Xia,Jinglin Liu,Xiying Fan,Zebin Zhu,Zhen Gao*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究首次在经典声波系统中实现了时间反演对称（TRS）保持的交变磁（altermagnetic）声子晶体，通过引入两个赝自旋自由度和构造保留物理时间反演作用效果的赝时间反演算子，实验上直接观测到赝自旋依赖的能带劈裂，并展示了亚晶格-赝自旋锁定的调控能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁体（altermagnets）作为兼具自旋劈裂电子能带与零净磁化强度的新型磁性材料备受关注，但将其范式推广到经典波系统面临根本困难——传统实现方案要求打破时间反演对称性（time-reversal symmetry, TRS）。本文旨在突破这一局限，在保持真实TRS的条件下实现交变磁经典波系统。

Method: 作者引入两个赝自旋（pseudospin）自由度，构造一个赝时间反演算子（pseudo-time-reversal operator），该算子忠实复现物理时间反演的作用效果而不破坏实际TRS。基于这一理论框架，作者设计并实验制备了首个时间反演不变的交变磁声子晶体（acoustic crystal），并通过声学测量验证其性质。

Result: 声学测量在严格保持TRS的条件下直接观测到赝自旋依赖的能带劈裂（pseudospin-dependent band splitting）——这是交变磁性的标志性特征。此外，该交变磁声子晶体展现出亚晶格-赝自旋锁定（sublattice-pseudospin locking）行为，可实现对声学赝自旋劈裂与滤波的灵活调控。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了声子晶体作为探索交变磁物理的通用平台，并为非磁性器件中受自旋启发的波动操控开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Altermagnets have emerged as a new class of magnetic materials that combine spin-split electronic bands with zero net magnetization. Extending this paradigm to classical-wave systems has, however, been fundamentally challenging because conventional realizations require broken time-reversal symmetry (TRS). Here, we overcome this limitation by introducing two pseudospin degrees of freedom and constructing a pseudo-time-reversal operator that faithfully reproduces the action of its physical counterpart while preserving actual TRS. Building on this framework, we theoretically propose and experimentally realize the first time-reversal-invariant altermagnetic acoustic crystal. Acoustic measurements directly reveal pseudospin-dependent band splitting--a defining hallmark of altermagnetism--under strictly TRS-preserving conditions. Moreover, the altermagnetic acoustic crystal exhibits sublattice-pseudospin locking, enabling flexible control over acoustic pseudospin splitting and filtering. Our work establishes acoustic crystals as a versatile platform for exploring altermagnetic physics and opens new avenues for spin-inspired wave manipulation in nonmagnetic devices.

</details>


### [259] [Electronic bistability, discontinuous switching and stochasticity in a two-dimensional semiconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08828)
*S. Jana,M. Kravtsov,A. Ermakov,X. Zhou,A. Kudriashov,L. Elesin,X. Zhou,A. L. Shilov,D. A. Svintsov,T. Taniguchi,K. Watanabe,K. S. Novoselov,A. Avsar,A. Principi,D. A. Bandurin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在单一化学均匀的黑磷（black phosphorus）晶体中，利用双栅极结构演示了一种具有本征电子双稳态（intrinsic electronic bistability）的晶体管。该器件通过巨斯塔克效应（giant Stark effect）调控能带和形成带间隧穿结，实现了竞争性导电机制（扩散导电、双隧穿通道、齐纳击穿），产生负微分电导、不连续开关、磁滞和统计稳定但本质上随机的切换行为，为下一代计算架构提供了单一静电可编程元件中整合非线性、开关、记忆与随机性的新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统晶体管本质上缺乏双稳态，当前实现开关和记忆功能需要借助外加材料手段（如掺杂隧穿结、忆阻器中的灯丝或相变），增加了器件复杂性和工艺难度。本文旨在探索在单一化学均匀晶体中实现无需外部材料工程的"本征电子双稳态"，以简化器件结构并为下一代计算架构提供更基础的功能集成方案。

Method: 采用双栅极黑磷晶体管，其中黑磷的带隙在垂直电场下因巨斯塔克效应而变窄，导致栅极不仅能调制载流子密度（carrier density），还能重塑能带分布，在沟道中形成带间隧穿结（interband tunnel junctions）。系统扫过双栅参数空间，以表征不同导电机制（扩散导电、两种隧穿通道和齐纳击穿）之间的竞争及其对输运性质的影响。

Result: 实验观察到多种导电机制的竞争性相互作用，产生负微分电导（negative differential conductance）和跨导、不连续开关（discontinuous switching）以及由栅极历史设定状态的磁滞现象。切换行为本质上是随机的，但在窄栅压范围内在统计上是稳定的，从而提供了一种电可编程的随机源（electrically programmable source of randomness）。

Conclusion: 基于这一原理的器件有望在其他二维半导体中实现，为下一代计算架构开辟道路——将非线性、开关、记忆和随机性整合在单一静电可编程元件中，避免了传统材料工程手段的复杂性，并可能支持新型计算范式如随机计算（stochastic computing）或忆阻计算架构。

Abstract: Bistability - two stable electronic states under the same bias - underlies switching and memory, but is usually absent in transistors and must be engineered through material means: doped tunnel junctions, filaments in memristors, or phase transitions. Here we demonstrate a transistor with intrinsic electronic bistability in a single chemically homogeneous crystal. In dual-gated black phosphorus, whose band gap narrows under a perpendicular electric field due to a giant Stark effect, the gates not only modulate carrier density but also reshape the band profile, forming interband tunnel junctions in the channel. Transport across the two-gate parameter space reveals competing conduction regimes - diffusive, two tunnelling channels and Zener breakdown - whose interplay produces negative differential conductance and transconductance, discontinuous switching, and hysteresis with the state set by gate history. Moreover, the switching remains intrinsically stochastic, yet statistically stable within a narrow range of gate voltages, providing an electrically programmable source of randomness. Devices based on this principle should be realisable in other two-dimensional semiconductors, opening a route to next-generation computing architectures in which nonlinearity, switching, memory and stochasticity are integrated within a single electrostatically programmable element.

</details>


### [260] [Layer-Locked Chiral Topological Superconductivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08843)
*Yijie Mo,Zhongbo Yan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一种实现层锁定拓扑相（layer-locked topological phases）的普适机制，并基于该机制在具有s波配对（s-wave pairing）的非辛双层反铁磁体系（nonsymmorphic bilayer antiferromagnetic system）中研究了层锁定手性拓扑超导电性（layer-locked chiral topological superconductivity）的实现。研究识别出三种不同的栅极可调拓扑相，并建立了近量子化的层分辨Chern数与手性Majorana边界态、涡旋核Majorana零模及近量子化热霍尔响应的层锁定行为之间的直接对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在发现实现层锁定拓扑相的普适机制，并将其应用于探索手性拓扑超导电性的新实现平台，填补该方向的理论空白。

Method: 通过提出层锁定拓扑相的普适机制作为理论指导，在非辛双层反铁磁体系中结合s波配对构建模型，利用层分辨Chern数（layer-resolved Chern numbers）表征各拓扑相，并分析手性Majorana边界态、涡旋核Majorana零模及热霍尔响应等特征物理量。

Result: 识别出三种可通过栅极调控区分的拓扑相，并证实层分辨Chern数的近量子化行为与Majorana边界态、涡旋核零模及热霍尔输运的层锁定特性存在直接对应关系。

Conclusion: 非辛双层反铁磁体系结合s波配对为实现层锁定手性拓扑超导电性提供了可行平台，该工作为层锁定拓扑相的实验探测和器件应用奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We uncover a universal mechanism for realizing layer-locked topological phases. Guided by it, we investigate the realization of layer-locked chiral topological superconductivity-the superconducting analogue of the quantum anomalous layer Hall effect-in a nonsymmorphic bilayer antiferromagnetic system with s-wave pairing. We identify three distinct gate-tunable topological phases and establish a direct correspondence between the nearly quantized layer-resolved Chern numbers and the layer-locking behavior of chiral Majorana edge states, vortex-core Majorana zero modes, and nearly quantized thermal Hall responses.

</details>


### [261] [Relaxation-driven flat bands and topology in moiré transition metal dichalcogenide heterobilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08917)
*Mitchell Luskin,Max Geier,Liang Fu,Ziyan Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文指出莫尔过渡金属二硫族化物（moiré transition metal dichalcogenide, TMD）异质双层通常被连续介质理论建模为拓扑平凡能带，但这忽略了晶格弛豫产生的赝磁场效应。作者开发了将弛豫分解为三个通道的连续介质模型，以WSe₂/WS₂为原型，证明赝磁场本身即可使第三、第四价带因陈数（Chern number）±1而打开能隙，且赝电场和莫尔势修正可进一步窄化带宽、增强能隙。多体相互作用下这些拓扑性质依然存活，说明异质双层是一类完全由晶格弛豫驱动的新型拓扑材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续介质理论建模莫尔TMD异质双层时得到拓扑平凡的能带，与同质双层具备拓扑能带和分数量子霍尔态（fractional Chern insulator, FCI）形成鲜明对比。作者认为这一结论源于忽略了晶格弛豫产生的赝磁场，该效应本质上存在于所有莫尔材料中，因此需要建立更完整的理论框架。

Method: 开发了一种新的连续介质模型，将晶格弛豫分解为三个通道：含更高傅里叶谐波的修正莫尔势、赝电场（标量形变）势以及赝磁场（矢量）势。以WSe₂/WS₂异质双层为原型进行数值研究，并结合神经网络变分蒙特卡洛（neural-network variational Monte Carlo）方法检验多体相互作用的稳定性。

Result: 赝磁场单独即可在广泛的扭转角和晶格失配范围内使第三、第四价带打开具有陈数±1的能隙；莫尔势修正和赝电场势进一步窄化带宽并增强能隙。弛豫使贝里曲率（Berry curvature）和量子度规（quantum metric）相对于刚性模型更加平滑，使能带更接近理想陈极限，有利于量子反常霍尔效应（quantum anomalous Hall effect）、FCI态和高能带填充下的平带超导。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了从第一性原理计算到多体可观测量之间的新框架，证明莫尔异质双层是一类完全由内在晶格弛豫驱动拓扑性的新型拓扑材料。

Abstract: Moiré transition metal dichalcogenide (TMD) heterobilayers are commonly modeled by a continuum theory that yields topologically trivial bands, in contrast to their homobilayer counterparts which host topological bands and fractional Chern insulators (FCI). We show this conclusion is an artifact of neglecting the pseudomagnetic field generated by lattice relaxation, an effect intrinsic to every moiré material. We develop a continuum model that resolves relaxation into three channels: a modified moiré potential with higher Fourier harmonics, a pseudoelectric (scalar deformation) potential, and a pseudomagnetic (vector) potential. Using WSe$_2$/WS$_2$ as a prototype, we find that the pseudomagnetic field alone gaps the third and fourth valence bands with Chern numbers $\pm 1$ over a broad range of twist angle and lattice mismatch, while the moiré potential correction and pseudoelectric potential narrow the bandwidth and enhance the bandgaps, which survive many-body interactions using neural-network variational Monte Carlo calculations. Relaxation also smoothens the Berry curvature and quantum metric relative to the rigid model, moving the band closer to the ideal Chern limit, beneficial for the quantum anomalous Hall effect, FCI states, and flat-band superconductivity when filled to higher bands. Our work establishes a new framework that connects first-principles calculations, through the continuum model, to many-body observables. Using this framework, we show moiré heterobilayers as a new class of topological materials whose topology is driven entirely by intrinsic lattice relaxation.

</details>


### [262] [Hyperbolic exciton-plasmon polaritons in MoS$_2$/MoOCl$_2$ van der Waals heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08923)
*Rajveer Fandan,Jorge Pedrós*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文预测单层MoS₂覆盖在面内双曲导体（in-plane hyperbolic conductor）MoOCl₂上可支持双曲激子-等离激元极化激元（hyperbolic exciton-plasmon polaritons），其行为受晶体学方向、薄层厚度和双曲模式阶数调控；通过各向异性转移矩阵模型结合耗散三能级哈密顿量，得到耦合能g_A=64.7 meV和g_B=61.2 meV的上中下三个极化激元分支，强耦合在激子线宽达50 meV时仍可持续。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于各向同性金属或光学微腔的极化激元平台对传播方向的控制能力有限，亟需开发能够实现方向性调控的纳米光子平台。

Method: 采用各向异性转移矩阵模型（anisotropic transfer-matrix model）耦合耗散三能级哈密顿量（dissipative three-level Hamiltonian），系统研究MoS₂/MoOCl₂异质结构中金层等离激元与自旋轨道劈裂的A、B激子之间的波矢依赖反交叉（anticrossing）行为。

Result: 获得了下、中、上三个极化激元分支，耦合能分别为g_A=64.7 meV和g_B=61.2 meV；旋转面内波矢可调谐等离激元-激子失谐，增加MoOCl₂厚度可激活高阶双曲法布里-珀罗模式（Fabry-Pérot modes），其耦合遵循近似有效模式体积（effective-mode-volume）标度；强耦合在线宽达50 meV时仍保持，归一化光谱分辨率比大于1。

Conclusion: MoS₂/MoOCl₂提供了一种无需光刻（lithography-free）的方向性、多模式激子-等离激元极化激元工程平台，适用于可见光谱范围。

Abstract: Polaritons formed by strong coupling between excitons and confined electromagnetic modes underpin emerging nanophotonic technologies, yet platforms based on isotropic metals or optical microcavities provide limited control over propagation direction. Here we predict that monolayer MoS$_2$ on the in-plane hyperbolic conductor MoOCl$_2$ supports hyperbolic exciton-plasmon polaritons governed by crystallographic direction, slab thickness, and hyperbolic mode order. Using an anisotropic transfer-matrix model coupled to a dissipative three-level Hamiltonian, we obtain wavevector-dependent anticrossings between the MoOCl$_2$ plasmons and the spin-orbit-split A and B excitons of MoS$_2$, yielding lower, middle, and upper polariton branches with coupling energies $g_A$ = 64.7 meV and $g_B$ = 61.2 meV. Rotating the in-plane wavevector tunes the plasmon-exciton detuning, while increasing the MoOCl$_2$ thickness activates higher-order hyperbolic Fabry-Pérot modes. Their coupling follows an approximate effective-mode-volume scaling. Strong coupling persists for excitonic linewidths up to 50 meV, with the normalised spectral-resolution ratio remaining above unity. These results establish MoS$_2$/MoOCl$_2$ as a lithography-free platform for directional and multimode exciton-plasmon polariton engineering in the visible spectral range.

</details>


### [263] [Phonon-assisted transport and hole-phonon coupling in GaAs double quantum dots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09116)
*Jin Leng,Wei-Zhu Liao,Hao-Tian Jiang,Di Liu,Bao-Chuan Wang,Gang Cao,Hai-Ou Li,Guo-Ping Guo*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了门控砷化镓（GaAs）双量子点中空穴-声子耦合效应，在有限源漏偏压下观察到电荷稳定图中特定的振荡条纹图案，并将其归因于非弹性隧穿过程中的声子发射，理论模型与实验观测吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空穴-声子相互作用在半导体量子点的输运和退相干（decoherence）过程中起重要作用，需要深入理解其具体机制和效应。

Method: 在集成量子点接触（QPC）电荷传感器的门控GaAs双量子点体系中，于有限源-漏偏压条件下测量电荷稳定图；构建包含压电空穴-声子耦合的理论模型，并与实验图案进行对比。

Result: 在特定电荷转变区域附近出现明显的振荡条纹图案，理论模型成功复现这些图案；分析表明振荡仅出现在特定电荷构型中。

Conclusion: 实验结果直接揭示了半导体量子点中声子辅助输运与相干空穴-声子相互作用的机制，为理解量子点中的退相干过程提供了新的实验依据。

Abstract: Hole-phonon interactions play an important role in transport and decoherence processes in semiconductor quantum dots. Here we investigate hole-phonon coupling in a gate-defined GaAs double quantum dot integrated with a quantum point contact charge sensor. Under finite source-drain bias, pronounced oscillatory stripe patterns appear near specific charge transition regions in the charge stability diagram. We attribute these oscillations to phonon emission during inelastic interdot tunneling. A theoretical model including piezoelectric hole-phonon coupling reproduces the observed patterns. Furthermore, our analysis shows that the oscillations emerge only in particular charge configurations. Our results provide direct insight into phonon-assisted transport and coherent hole-phonon interactions in semiconductor quantum dots.

</details>


### [264] [Gradient-based optimization of non-Abelian fractional quantum states in patterned superlattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09245)
*Yifei Guan,Lichen Yu,Zizhuang Liu,Xin Lu,Jianpeng Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于梯度下降算法的逆向设计框架，用于在图案化介电超晶格与双层石墨烯耦合的器件中实现非阿贝尔分数量子霍尔（non-Abelian fractional quantum Hall）态。通过对超晶格结构参数进行优化，在1/2填充下利用精确对角化计算，在三角、六角以及kagome图案的超晶格中均发现了鲁棒的非阿贝尔分数量子霍尔绝缘体（FCI）态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前实验上已实现的分数量子霍尔绝缘体（FCI）态大多可视为承载阿贝尔（Abelian）任意子激发的分数量子霍尔（FQH）态的晶格类似物，而非阿贝尔FCI态的实现仍是该领域的重要挑战。图案化介电超晶格具有高度结构灵活性和可调性，为构筑拓扑平带提供了多功能的平台，因此有望在该类系统中实现非阿贝尔FCI态。

Method: 作者提出一个基于梯度下降（gradient-descent）算法的实际工作流程，对图案化介电超晶格的结构参数（如超晶格常数和垂直电势降）进行优化，以获得具有与第一激发朗道能级相近量子几何性质的平带切尔诺数（flat Chern band）。随后在优化后的平带1/2填充下执行精确对角化（exact diagonalization）计算，以验证非阿贝尔FCI态的实现。该工作流程分别应用于三角、六角和kagome三种图案的超晶格。

Result: 梯度优化后的平带Chern带在1/2填充下自然产生了非阿贝尔FCI态。在三角、六角和kagome图案的超晶格中，均在与超晶格常数和垂直电势降张成的广阔参数空间区域内发现了鲁棒的非阿贝尔FCI态。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个实验上可行的框架，用于在真实的图案化超晶格器件中探索非阿贝尔FCI态，同时展示了器件层面的逆向设计在工程操控超越阿贝尔范式的关联拓扑物质方面的潜力。

Abstract: The realization of fractional Chern insulator (FCI) states in moiré heterostructures has attracted intense interest in the study of correlated states emerging from topological flat bands. So far, most experimentally realized FCI states may be interpreted as lattice analogues of fractional quantum Hall (FQH) states hosting Abelian anyonic excitations. Realizing non-Abelian FCI states is an important challenge in the field. Patterned dielectric superlattices provide a versatile platform for engineering topological flat bands. Such systems offer substantial structural flexibility and tunability, because their lattice patterns, periods, and other structural parameters can all be designed and fabricated. Here we propose to realize non-Abelian FCI states in patterned dielectric superlattices coupled to bilayer graphene. Specifically, we provide a realistic workflow based on a gradient-descent algorithm to design non-Abelian fractional states in bilayer graphene superlattices. The experimentally relevant structural parameters of the superlattices are gradient-optimized to favor a flat Chern band with quantum-geometric properties reminiscent of those of the first excited Landau level. Exact diagonalization calculations at 1/2 filling of the optimized flat Chern band naturally yield non-Abelian FCI states. We apply this workflow to triangular, honeycomb, and kagome patterned superlattices and find robust non-Abelian FCI states over a large region of the parameter space spanned by the superlattice constant and vertical potential drop. Our work thus establishes an experimentally feasible framework for exploring non-Abelian FCIs in realistic patterned-superlattice devices. It also demonstrates the potential of device-level inverse design to engineer correlated topological matter beyond the Abelian paradigm.

</details>


### [265] [Size-Dependent Band-Tail Localization in Oxide Semiconductors Revealed by Direct Density-of-States Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09283)
*Chang Niu,Kisoo Nam,Aravindh Shankar,Jian-Yu Lin,Sanjeev Khare,Sumi Lee,Pramey Upadhyaya,Peide D. Ye*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究利用基于锁相放大器的电场穿透技术，直接映射In基氧化物半导体薄膜晶体管（TFT）中的有效态密度（DOS），揭示了无序主导的输运机制中带尾态的局域化行为，并展示了通道长度、温度和材料工程对有效DOS局域化的系统调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着非晶氧化物半导体晶体管向低维通道尺度缩放，无序诱导的局域化（disorder-induced localization）预计将变得更加重要，但该输运机制所涉及的电电子态在实验上难以直接分辨，需要一种器件级探针来获得有效态密度的直接图谱。

Method: 采用基于锁相放大器（lock-in）的电场穿透技术，直接测量In基氧化物半导体薄膜晶体管中的量子电容（quantum capacitance）、载流子密度（carrier density）和化学势（chemical potential），从而映射有效态密度；并结合通道长度、温度、薄膜厚度、O2退火及In/Ga/Zn组分等变量进行系统表征。

Result: 提取的量子电容、载流子密度和化学势揭示了无序主导的输运区域，其中带尾态（band-tail states）不仅是被动陷阱，还具备屏蔽能力和部分输运活性；有效DOS随通道长度呈指数抑制，表现出尺寸依赖的带尾局域化，并解释了局域化诱导的阈值电压偏移机制；低温测量显示无序主导的DOS被强烈抑制而扩展扩散态几乎不变；通过调节薄膜厚度、O2退火和In/Ga/Zn组分可系统抑制无序和有效DOS局域化。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了直接有效DOS映射作为局域化物理的器件级探针，为低维氧化物半导体电子学中无序工程的工程化提供了路径。

Abstract: Disorder-induced localization is expected to become increasingly important as amorphous oxide semiconductor transistors are scaled toward low-dimensional channels, yet the electronic states responsible for this transport regime remain difficult to resolve experimentally. Here, we use a lock-in-based electric-field penetration technique to directly map the effective density of states (DOS) in In-based oxide semiconductor thin-film transistors (TFTs). The extracted quantum capacitance, carrier density, and chemical potential reveal a disorder-dominated transport regime in which band-tail states are not merely passive traps, but become screening-active and partially transport-active. Geometry-dependent DOS mapping shows an exponential suppression of the effective DOS with channel length, demonstrating size-dependent band-tail localization and providing a microscopic origin for a distinct localization-induced threshold-voltage roll-off mechanism. Temperature-dependent measurements show that the disorder-dominated DOS is strongly suppressed at low temperatures, while extended diffusive states remain nearly unchanged, confirming the localization origin. By tuning film thickness, O2 annealing, and In/Ga/Zn composition, we further demonstrate systematic suppression of disorder and effective-DOS localization. This work establishes direct DOS mapping as a device-level probe of localization physics and provides a pathway for engineering disorder in low-dimensional oxide semiconductor electronics.

</details>


### [266] [Polarization engineered all 2D Graphene/Ferroelectric hybrid for persistence-free photoresponse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09300)
*Navkiranjot Kaur Gill,Shaili Sett,Saloni Kakkar,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Arindam Ghosh*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作将原子级薄的滑动铁电体3R-MoS2与双层石墨烯集成，构建了兼具高灵敏度和快速响应（约数十毫秒级）的范德华光电探测器，实现了无持续光电导效应（persistence-free photoresponse）的可调响应，单次测量中内量子效率为10%、可探测最小光子数为31。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于陷阱介导光门控机制的石墨烯范德华杂化光电探测器虽然灵敏度高，但在快速探测重复光信号方面表现不佳，难以同时实现快速和高灵敏度探测，这是该领域长期存在的难题。

Method: 将菱面体堆叠（rhombohedrally stacked, 3R）构型的原子级薄MoS2与双层石墨烯集成，构建边缘接触的双栅极场效应晶体管（dual-gated field-effect transistor）架构的范德华光电探测器。利用3R-MoS2的自发面外极化对外界光场的响应，产生光学可控的门控效应（gating effect），在面外位移场下选择性约束双层石墨烯中的电荷载流子，从而实现无持续效应的可调光响应。

Result: 实验实现了一种无温度依赖的光电响应，响应时间约为数十毫秒量级（受测量仪器限制）。在超高灵敏度模式下验证了可重复的光学信号检测和光子计数分辨率，测得内量子效率（internal quantum efficiency）为10%，单次测量最小可探测光子数为31。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了3R-MoS2在调控界面电荷动力学中的功能价值，确立了石墨烯与铁电3R-MoS2杂化体系作为超高灵敏度光电子器件有前景的平台。

Abstract: Graphene-based van der Waals hybrid photodetectors typically work on trap-mediated photogating mechanism, exhibiting high sensitivity, but under-perform in the fast detection of repetitive optical signals. Designing photodetectors that are simultaneously fast and highly sensitive has therefore remained difficult. In this work, we realize both attributes by integrating atomically thin sliding ferroelectrics in the design architecture, thereby uniting semiconducting properties with intrinsic polarization fields capable of efficiently governing interfacial photocarrier dynamics. We report a bilayer graphene-bilayer MoS2 (with MoS2 in a rhombohedrally stacked (3R) configuration) van der Waals photodetector with edge-contacted dual-gated field-effect transistor architecture. The photo-induced modulation in spontaneous out-of-plane polarization of 3R-MoS2 and selective confinement of charge carriers in bilayer graphene under an out-of-plane displacement field results in a tunable persistence-free photoresponse. Here, the photoinduced polarization change in 3R-MoS2 produces an optically controlled gating effect that alters the electrostatic environment of bilayer graphene, resulting in a temperature-independent photoresponse with rapid response times of the order of 10's of milliseconds (limited by the measurement instrument). We demonstrate reproducible detection of optical signals and examine the photon-counting resolution of this structure in high-sensitivity regimes, where we determine its internal quantum efficiency to be 10 percent with minimum detectable photon number of 31 in single shot measurements. This work highlights the functionality of 3R-MoS2 in manipulating the interfacial charge dynamics and establishes the hybrid of graphene and ferroelectric 3R-MoS2 as a promising platform for ultra-sensitive optoelectronic devices.

</details>


### [267] [Magnetically induced Circular Photogalvanic Effect in Symmetric Two-dimensional Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09309)
*Peng Liu,Fanhao Jia,Ruixue Li,Yuan Li,Igor Zutic,Gaofeng Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一种磁光电流效应（magneto-circular photogalvanic effect, MCPGE），通过引入磁性作用使具有中心对称性的材料也能产生依赖光场手征性的光电流，突破了传统圆光电流效应（CPGE）对反转对称破缺材料的限制。作者以单层SbH吸附于可调磁化强度的磁性衬底为模型，通过有效哈密顿量阐明其机制，并利用Bethe-Salpeter方程分析了单个粒子与激子（exciton）层面的光吸收响应，拓宽了CPGE的物理机制及应用前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统圆光电流效应（circular photogalvanic effect, CPGE）仅在缺乏反转对称性（inversion symmetry）的体材料或低维材料中被观察到，这极大地限制了其应用范围。作者希望寻找一种途径，使具有中心对称性的材料也能产生依赖光手征性的光电流，从而拓展光电器件的材料选择空间。

Method: 作者提出利用磁效应（magnetic effect）克服反转对称性限制，设计磁性衬底上的单层SbH模型，通过构建有效哈密顿量（effective Hamiltonian）来阐明磁致圆光电流效应（MCPGE）的物理机制。同时，采用Bethe-Salpeter方程描述激子中的库仑相互作用，分别分析单粒子（single-particle）与激子（excitonic）两个层面上的光学吸收响应。

Result: 通过单层SbH/磁性衬底体系的有效哈密顿量证明，在可调磁化强度（magnetization）的磁性衬底上，具有中心对称性的材料可以产生依赖光场螺旋度（helicity）的光电流，即MCPGE的可行性得以验证。此外，Bethe-Salpeter方程分析揭示了单粒子及激子层面的光学吸收响应特征。

Conclusion: 该研究拓宽了圆光电流效应（CPGE）的物理机制，使得对称性材料也能用于手征依赖的光电探测，为新型光电器件（optoelectronic devices）的设计提供了新的可能性。

Abstract: Photocurrents that depend on the helicity of the incident light can be generated in both bulk and low-dimensional materials lacking inversion symmetry, known as the circular photogalvanic effect (CPGE). We propose that by employing a magnetic effect, the limitation on the inversion symmetry broken materials can be overcome, such that helicity-dependent photocurrent can be generated in a symmetric material, i.e., a magneto-circular photogalvanic effect (MCPGE). As a proof of principle, we elucidate the mechanism of such an MCPGE through an effective Hamiltonian of a monolayer SbH on a magnetic substrate with an adjustable magnetization. Moreover, the associated response in optical absorption is analyzed, both single-particle and excitonic, through a Bethe-Salpeter equation to describe the Coulomb interaction in excitons. Our result broadens the mechanism of CPGE and opens new opportunities for optoelectronic devices.

</details>


### [268] [Parity-Resolved Quantum Capacitance and Quantum Inductance in Topological, Trivial, and Normal Nanowire Interferometers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09334)
*Viktoriia Pinchenkova,Valerii K. Kozin,Maximilian Hünenberger,Daniel Loss,Jelena Klinovaja*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过精确对角化方法计算四种纳米线实现（拓扑纳米线、含一个或两个安德烈耶夫束缚态的非拓扑超导纳米线、以及纯正常纳米线）的宇称分辨量子电容和逆量子电感随磁通的变化，证明零能马约拉纳束缚态的特征信号（如h/e周期性和h/2e宇称偏移）并非拓扑超导性的充分判据，而近零能双安德烈耶夫束缚态产生的h/2e周期分量可作为明确的非马约拉纳标志。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电容测量虽然有望成为马约拉纳器件（Majorana devices）的读出工具，但拓扑平凡的安德烈耶夫束缚态（Andreev bound states）也能产生类似马约拉纳的量子电容响应，仅凭电容测量不足以识别拓扑相。为此需要引入量子电感（quantum inductance）作为额外的相位敏感探针，以区分拓扑与非拓扑信号。

Method: 考察与四个纳米线实现两端耦合的量子点，通过精确对角化（exact diagonalization）计算宇称分辨量子电容C_Q和逆量子电感L_Q^{-1}作为磁通（magnetic flux）的函数，对比马约拉纳束缚态、单个/两个安德烈耶夫束缚态及纯正常纳米线四种情形的响应特征。

Result: 零能马约拉纳束缚态的典型特征——C_Q和L_Q^{-1}中的h/e周期性和h/(2e)宇称间磁通偏移——在特定真实参数区间内可由拓扑平凡的安德烈耶夫束缚态甚至纯正常纳米线重现，不能作为拓扑超导性的充分指示。而两个近零能安德烈耶夫束缚态可产生与电荷2e转移相关的显著h/(2e)周期分量，构成明确的非马约拉纳特征。

Conclusion: 低能投影显示马约拉纳、单安德烈耶夫态与正常纳米线的情形可映射到同一最小低能模型，解释了它们尽管物理起源不同却具有相似磁通响应的现象；马约拉纳束缚态相关量子电容/量子电感信号需结合其他判据谨慎解释。

Abstract: Quantum-capacitance measurements convert the curvature of a quantum-dot energy in a flux-threaded nanowire loop into fast parity-sensitive signals and, therefore, have become a promising readout tool for Majorana devices. However, Majorana-like quantum-capacitance responses can also arise from topologically trivial Andreev bound states, making capacitance alone insufficient to identify a topological phase. To analyze this problem, we consider a quantum dot coupled to both ends of four nanowire realizations: a topological nanowire hosting Majorana bound states, non-topological superconducting nanowires hosting one or two Andreev bound states, and a fully normal nanowire. Motivated by proposals to use quantum inductance as an additional phase-sensitive probe, we compute both the parity-resolved quantum capacitance $C_\mathrm{Q}$ and inverse quantum inductance $L_\mathrm{Q}^{-1}$ as functions of the magnetic flux by exact diagonalization. We show that signatures associated with zero-energy Majorana bound states, such as $h/e$ periodicity and an $h/(2e)$ flux shift between even and odd parity sectors in $C_\mathrm{Q}$ and $L_\mathrm{Q}^{-1}$, are not sufficient indicators of topological superconductivity. In certain realistic parameter regimes, similar Majorana-like behavior can arise from a trivial Andreev bound state and even from a purely normal nanowire. By contrast, two nearly zero-energy Andreev bound states can generate a pronounced $h/(2 e)$-periodic component associated with charge-$2e$ transfer, providing a clear non-Majorana signature. A low-energy projection shows that the Majorana, single-Andreev-state, and normal cases can be mapped onto the same minimal low-energy model explaining their similar flux-dependent responses despite their different physical origins.

</details>


### [269] [Noisy Braiding of Majorana Modes: A Comparison of Nanowire Trijunction and Quantum-Dot-Assisted Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09416)
*Dibyajyoti Sahu,Suhas Gangadharaiah*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过微观比较纳米线三结（nanowire trijunction）与量子点辅助（quantum-dot-assisted）两种马约拉纳（Majorana）编织架构，在完整含时的Bogoliubov–de Gennes动力学模拟下，发现点辅助架构在更短时间尺度上实现更低错误率，且该优势在噪声条件下仍然保持，并将不同错误贡献与器件几何直接关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 马约拉纳零模（Majorana zero modes）因其非阿贝尔编织统计（non-Abelian braiding statistics）被认为是拓扑量子计算的有前景平台，但编织操作的鲁棒性依赖于物理实现——有限时间操作、残余耦合和环境噪声都可能将局域激发转化为逻辑错误。作者旨在通过两种代表性编织架构的微观比较，量化不同实现方案的错误表现并揭示其几何根源。

Method: 对纳米线三结和量子点辅助两种编织架构进行微观比较，模拟完整含时的Bogoliubov–de Gennes（BdG）动力学，在无噪声和有噪声两种条件下，并对噪声进行空间分辨分析（spatially resolved analysis）以区分不同位置噪声的影响。

Result: 点辅助架构在更短时间尺度内实现比三结架构更低的错误率，源于其更局域化的交换机制（localized exchange mechanism），且此优势在噪声条件下仍然存在。空间分辨分析显示，点辅助几何中作用于点的快速噪声产生的错误小于等价的线上噪声，而慢的准静态噪声（quasi-static noise）作用于点时成为主要限制因素。

Conclusion: 研究将不同错误贡献直接关联到器件几何，为抗噪马约拉纳量子门的设计提供了具体原则，表明器件结构选择对最终量子门保真度具有决定性影响。

Abstract: Majorana zero modes have emerged as one of the most promising platforms for topological quantum computation, since their non-Abelian braiding statistics allow quantum information to be encoded nonlocally and manipulated through braiding operations that are, in principle, protected against local perturbations. In practice, however, a braid is only as robust as its physical implementation: finite-time operation, residual couplings, and environmental noise can all convert local excitations into logical errors during the exchange process. Here, we address this question through a microscopic comparison of two representative braiding architectures, a nanowire trijunction and a quantum-dot-assisted setup, simulating the full time-dependent Bogoliubov--de Gennes dynamics under both noiseless and noisy conditions. We show that the dot-assisted architecture consistently achieves a lower error over a shorter timescale than the trijunction, owing to its more localized exchange mechanism. This advantage persists in the presence of noise, and a spatially resolved analysis further reveals that, in the dot-assisted geometry, fast noise localized on the dot produces a smaller error than equivalent noise on the wires, whereas slow, quasi-static noise on the dot becomes the dominant limitation. Taken together, these findings link the different error contributions directly to device geometry, pointing to concrete design principles for noise-resilient Majorana-based quantum gates.

</details>


### [270] [Quantum sensing composite excitations in an anisotropic ferromagnet via a qubit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09463)
*Amey S. Rodge,Tarek Moussa,Akashdeep Kamra,Bashab Dey*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过理论分析，研究了利用与磁振子模式（magnon mode）通过直接色散相互作用（dispersive interaction）耦合的量子比特（qubit）进行谱学探测的方法，旨在探测有序磁体中量子压缩磁振子数态（squeezed-magnon number states）的量子叠加性质，并阐明了该方法的适用范围、局限性与设计方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有序磁体（ordered magnets）的本征基态及磁振子激发具有纠缠与非经典数态特征，需要一种有效手段来探测这些非经典磁基态所隐含的磁振子数态叠加（superposition）。近期已有研究展示了利用量子比特与磁振子模式色散耦合来进行此类探测的途径，本文旨在进一步刻画该量子比特谱学方法的探测能力与局限性。

Method: 本文采用理论推导的方式，系统分析量子比特谱学（qubit spectroscopy）对各类量子压缩磁振子激发态的探测效果。具体而言，研究者考察了不同量子压缩磁振子数态叠加结构在谱学信号中的可分辨性，并针对低频振幅的压缩相干磁振子态，分析了多跃迁导致的频率拥塞与谱峰线宽之间的权衡关系。

Result: 研究表明，该谱学方法天然适用于揭示各类量子化压缩磁振子数态（squeezed-magnon number states）中的量子叠加结构；然而，当激发态涉及多个压缩福克态（squeezed Fock states）的叠加时，可能的跃迁数量及相应谱峰急剧增多，探测难度显著上升，需要线宽更窄的量子比特。此外，对于低振幅压缩相干磁振子态，该方法需要在多跃迁引起的频率拥挤和谱峰线宽之间做出权衡。

Conclusion: 该工作为部署高品质量子比特以探测磁系统中自旋激发的复合性（composite nature）奠定了理论基础，并提供了相应的设计方程，为未来实验实施提供了指导。

Abstract: Ordered magnets harbor intrinsically squeezed ground states and magnonic excitations characterized by entanglement between spins and nonclassical magnon number composition. A pathway to detecting the superpositions of noneigenmode magnon number states underlying these nonclassical magnetic ground states has recently been demonstrated by utilizing a qubit coupled to the magnon mode via a direct dispersive interaction. Here, we theoretically develop this qubit spectroscopy further delineating the capabilities and limitations of this qubit spectroscopy for sensing the quantum superpositions that underlie the excited states. We demonstrate that the spectroscopy lends itself naturally to unraveling the superpositions that underlie the various quantized squeezed-magnon number states. However, excited states comprising superpositions of multiple squeezed Fock states become increasingly hard due to the large number of possible transitions, and resulting peaks, in the qubit spectroscopy thereby requiring qubits with narrower linewidths. Along the same lines, we theoretically demonstrate the qubit spectroscopy of a low amplitude coherent squeezed-magnon state analyzing the tradeoff between frequency crowding due to multiple transitions and peak linewidths. Our work lays the groundwork and design equations for deploying high-quality qubits towards sensing the composite nature of spin excitations in magnetic systems.

</details>


### [271] [Long-range exchange interaction controls the fine structure of excited trion states in semiconductor quantum dots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09554)
*M. M. Glazov,E. L. Ivchenko*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文发展了半导体量子点中带电激子（trion）长程电子-空穴交换相互作用的微观理论，推导了有效长程交换哈密顿量，解释了激发态三激子精细结构的来源，并建立了统一描述带电激子复合物精细结构的理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基态单重态三激子（singlet trion）通过时间反演对称性在未配对载流子的自旋分量上保持简并，而激发态三激子由于电子-电子和电子-空穴交换相互作用的竞争表现出丰富的精细结构，需要对长程交换相互作用进行微观理论描述。

Method: 针对自旋3/2重空穴（heavy-hole）和简单自旋1/2价带模型分别推导了有效长程交换哈密顿量；分析了长程交换相互作用对单重态和三重态三激子组态的混合效应，并获得了长程交换参数的解析表达式。

Result: 长程交换相互作用混合了单重态和三重态三激子组态，产生了由量子点形状决定的双各向异性（anisotropic）精细结构劈裂以及相关的偏振依赖光学光谱；长程交换参数获得了解析表达式。

Conclusion: 该理论建立了半导体量子点中激发态三激子精细结构的统一微观描述，为解释带电激子复合物的偏振分辨光学光谱提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We develop a microscopic theory of the long-range electron-hole exchange interaction in charged excitons (trions) confined in semiconductor quantum dots. While the ground-state singlet trion remains degenerate in the spin component of unpaired charge carrier by time-reversal symmetry, excited trion states exhibit a rich fine structure resulting from the interplay of electron-electron and electron-hole exchange interactions. We derive the effective long-range exchange Hamiltonian for both the spin-$3/2$ heavy-hole and a simple spin-$1/2$ valence band models. The long-range exchange interaction mixes singlet and triplet trion configurations, giving rise to anisotropic fine-structure splittings and related polarization-dependent optical spectra determined by the quantum-dot shape. Analytical expressions are obtained for the long-range exchange parameters. The developed theory establishes a unified microscopic description of the fine structure of excited trions in semiconductor quantum dots and provides a framework for interpreting polarization-resolved optical spectroscopy of charged excitonic complexes.

</details>


### [272] [Buried germanium quantum well proximitised by magnetic field-resilient superconducting platinum iridium germanosilicide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09611)
*Praveen Viswanathan,Erik Lemmens Sjöstrand,Davide Costa,Marinus Fischer,Athique Ahmed,Sara Martí-Sánchez,Lucas E. A. Stehouwer,Jordi Arbiol,Anasua Chatterjee,Giordano Scappucci,Karina L. Hudson*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在锗（Ge）异质结构上集成了一种新型超导材料铂铱锗硅化物（PtIrSiGe），其临界磁场高达1.9特斯拉、临界温度约1.85开尔文，并通过顶向下光刻（top-down lithography）制备工艺，在栅极定义的约瑟夫森结（Josephson junction）/超导量子干涉器件（SQUID）中实现了对埋层锗量子阱的近邻化（proximitisation）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合超导体-半导体系统是量子技术（从超导-自旋界面到拓扑量子器件）的多功能平台，但可扩展实现需要兼具高临界磁场（>1T）且在低温可用条件下与低无序半导体异质结构集成。

Method: 通过顶向下光刻工艺，将PtIrSiGe超导体与具有空穴迁移率μ=1.3×10^6 cm²/Vs的平面锗集成，并在500°C退火条件下保持锗量子阱完整性与二维空穴气（2D hole gas）的迁移率与密度不受破坏。

Result: PtIrSiGe的垂直临界磁场达B⊥=1.9T，临界温度Tc约1.85K；集成后锗量子阱及二维空穴气的迁移率与密度在500°C退火下保持不变，并在Ge/SiGe异质结构上成功演示了栅极定义的约瑟夫森结/SQUID中埋层锗量子阱的近邻化。

Conclusion: 该结果展示了高临界场超导体与锗半导体异质结构的兼容集成能力，为可扩展的混合量子器件提供了可行路径，同时验证了工艺温度与锗外延工艺的兼容性。

Abstract: Hybrid superconductor-semiconductor systems provide a versatile platform for quantum technologies, ranging from superconducting-spin interfaces to topological quantum devices. Progress toward scalable implementations requires superconductors that exhibit high critical fields ($>1$T) at accessible temperatures integrated with low-disorder semiconductor heterostructures. Here we demonstrate a superconducting platinum iridium germanosilicide (PtIrSiGe), with critical out-of-plane magnetic field up to $B_{\perp} = 1.9$T and critical temperature of $T_c\sim 1.85$K, integrated with planar germanium with mobility $μ= 1.3\times 10^6$cm$^{2}$/Vs via top-down lithography fabrication. We show that the integrity of the germanium quantum well and mobility and density of the 2D hole gas are preserved despite annealing at $500°$C, a temperature comparable to that used for strained germanium epitaxy. We further demonstrate proximitisation of a buried germanium quantum well in a gate-defined Josephson junction/SQUID on a Ge/SiGe heterostructure.

</details>


### [273] [Symmetry Breaking by Interfacial Dead Layers: Observation of Forbidden Self-Induced Spin-Orbit Torque in Symmetric Ferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09614)
*Athira Ravindran K,Raghvendra Posti,Manish Kumar Mohanta,Abhishek Kumar,Damanpreet Kaur,Alberto Anadón,Sebastien Petit-Watelot,Juan-Carlos Rojas-Sánchez,Michel Hehn,Puru Jena,Debangsu Roy*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究在名义上对称、无重金属层的MgO/NiFe/MgO三层结构中观察到稳健的自生自旋轨道转矩（spin-orbit torque, SOT），并确定其对称性破缺来源为底界面处1.8 nm的磁性死层（magnetic dead layer）。通过与Kim-Lee漂移扩散理论、密度泛函计算及结构表征的定量对比，作者将寄生死层重新定义为功能性的自旋电子学组件，为在标准磁性异质结构中实现全铁磁自旋轨道转矩提供了普适路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为铁磁体中产生自旋轨道转矩需要重金属层或工程化结构不对称来打破反演对称性，而在对称结构中此类转矩在理论上是被禁止的。作者旨在探索在缺少这些传统条件的标准磁性异质结构中是否仍能产生自旋轨道转矩，并理解其对称性破缺的物理起源。

Method: 作者结合了多种实验与理论手段，包括谐波霍尔测量（harmonic Hall measurements）、SQUID磁力计和X射线光电子能谱来表征磁性行为与界面结构；同时将实验数据与Kim和Lee提出的漂移扩散（drift-diffusion）理论进行定量对照，并通过密度泛函计算（density-functional calculations）验证NiFe的内禀自旋霍尔电导率（spin Hall conductivity）足以支持观测到的自旋电流。

Result: 实验鉴定出对称性破缺源于底界面处1.8 nm的磁性死层；实验数据与漂移扩散理论定量吻合，理论提取的死层厚度为1.2 nm，与结构表征结果一致。此外，密度泛函计算确认NiFe具备足够的内禀自旋霍尔电导率以支持观测到的自旋电流。

Conclusion: 这项工作将原本视为寄生效应（parasitic）的死层重新定位为功能性自旋电子学组件，从而建立了一条普适的、全铁磁的路线，可在标准磁性异质结构中产生自旋轨道转矩，拓展了自旋轨道转矩器件的材料设计空间。

Abstract: Conventionally, spin-orbit torques (SOTs) in ferromagnets require heavy-metal layers or engineered structural asymmetry to break inversion symmetry. In this work, we report the observation of robust, self-generated SOTs in a nominally symmetric, heavy-metal-free MgO/NiFe/MgO trilayer - a geometry where such torques are theoretically forbidden. By combining harmonic Hall measurements with SQUID magnetometry and X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy, we identify the symmetry-breaking origin: a 1.8 nm magnetic dead layer at the bottom interface. Crucially, we demonstrate a quantitative agreement between our data and the drift-diffusion theory predicted by Kim and Lee, yielding a theoretically extracted dead-layer thickness (1.2 nm) which matches structural characterization. Furthermore, density-functional calculations confirm that NiFe possesses sufficient intrinsic spin Hall conductivity to support the observed spin currents. These results reframe the parasitic dead layer as a functional spintronic component, establishing a universal, all-ferromagnetic route to SOTs in standard magnetic heterostructures.

</details>


### [274] [Probing crystal-field modulations with magnetic adatoms on the incipient charge-density-wave superconductor $2H$-NbS$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09753)
*Werner M. J. van Weerdenburg,Margarete Huisinga,Constantin Flommersfeld,Lisa M. Rütten,Katharina J. Franke*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究利用扫描隧道显微镜（scanning tunneling microscope）的针尖操控单个磁性原子在2H-NbS₂表面移动，通过测量Yu-Shiba-Rusinov（YSR）激发谱的变化，揭示了该材料中隐藏的晶场（crystal-field）调制，建立了一种在原子尺度探测晶场调制的新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2H-NbS₂是一种具有初现电荷密度波（incipient charge-density-wave）行为和超导态同时存在的过渡金属二硫化物，局域晶格不稳定性在其中扮演关键角色。作者旨在开发一种在原子尺度探测这些隐藏的晶场调制的手段，以理解缺陷和晶格不稳定性如何影响此类材料的原子环境。

Method: 将单个磁性原子沉积在2H-NbS₂表面，利用扫描隧道显微镜的针尖沿表面操控（manipulate）该原子，通过测量不同位置处Yu-Shiba-Rusinov（YSR）激发谱的变化来映射原子周围的局域环境，并同时考察本征点缺陷附近的局域结构。

Result: 发现尽管该材料的超导态在空间上是均匀的，YSR激发能量却强烈依赖于磁性原子的位置；进一步分析确定，这一效应的主要来源是局域晶场环境的变化。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了一种在原子尺度研究晶场调制的新方法，揭示了缺陷和晶格不稳定性如何塑造初现电荷密度波材料的原子尺度景观。

Abstract: The interplay between multiple quantum phases in layered materials may lead to incipient quantum behavior, where the material's ground state is close to a phase transition and sensitive to local disorder. The transition metal dichalcogenide material $2H$-NbS$_2$ exhibits incipient charge-density-wave behavior along with a well-developed superconducting state, creating a scenario where the local lattice instabilities play a crucial role. Here we present how an individual magnetic atom on $2H$-NbS$_2$ can be applied as a local sensor to reveal hidden crystal-field modulations. By manipulating the adatom across the surface with the tip of a scanning tunneling microscope, we measure variations in the Yu-Shiba-Rusinov (YSR) excitation spectra and map the local environment around an intrinsic point defect. We find that while the superconducting state is spatially uniform, the YSR excitation energy strongly depends on the position of the atom. We determine that the main contribution to this effect originates from variations in the local crystal-field environment. These results establish a new approach to investigate crystal-field modulations at the atomic scale and reveal how defects and lattice instabilities shape the atomic landscape of an incipient charge-density-wave material.

</details>


### [275] [Disorder-robust trivial Majorana-like states from smooth confinement in chiral superconducting nanowires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09758)
*Eslam Ahmed,Jorge Cayao,Yukio Tanaka*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Majorana纳米线中近零能态在对称性保持无序下的鲁棒性背后的实空间机制。通过将手性对称Bogoliubov-de Gennes哈密顿量中的低能态分解为两个相反手性的归一化分量，作者证明无序诱导的能级劈裂受限于这两个分量的空间重叠度。数值演示表明，在体拓扑相变之下，平滑约束可产生部分分离的手性分量，其重叠呈指数级小，从而即使存在强标量无序，也会形成保持近零能量的全局平庸Majorana-like安德烈耶夫束缚态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Majorana纳米线中的近零能量态可能源于拓扑平庸机制（如平滑空间不均匀性和无序），因此零能钉扎不足以作为体拓扑性的证据，需要更可靠的诊断工具来判断低能态是否真正受到拓扑保护。

Method: 对于手性对称的Bogoliubov-de Gennes哈密顿量（BdG Hamiltonian），将低能态分解为两个相反手性的归一化分量，数学上证明无序诱导的能级劈裂受限于两分量的空间重叠；随后在有限Rashba纳米线中，采用平滑化学势和配对势剖面进行数值验证。

Result: 在体拓扑相变之下，平滑约束产生部分分离的手性分量，其重叠为指数级小，导致即使存在强标量非磁性无序，全局平庸的Majorana-like安德烈耶夫束缚态（Andreev bound states）仍保持近零能量。手性重叠度提供了一种不依赖于体拓扑不变量的直接诊断低能态抗局域扰动的保护能力的工具。

Conclusion: 手性分量的空间重叠度可作为低能态局域保护能力的直接诊断指标，与体拓扑不变量无关，从而为区分真正拓扑Majorana模式和拓扑平庸的Majorana-like态提供了新的判据。

Abstract: Near-zero-energy states in Majorana nanowires can arise from topologically trivial mechanisms such as smooth spatial inhomogeneity and disorder, making zero-energy pinning alone insufficient evidence of bulk topology. Here we identify a real-space mechanism governing their robustness to symmetry-preserving disorder. For a chiral-symmetric Bogoliubov-de Gennes Hamiltonian, we decompose a low-energy state into two normalized components of opposite chirality and show that disorder-induced splitting is bounded by their spatial overlap. We demonstrate this result in a finite Rashba nanowire with smooth chemical potential and pairing profiles.
