Below the bulk topological transition, smooth confinement produces partially separated chiral components with exponentially small overlap, yielding globally trivial Majorana-like Andreev bound states that remain near zero energy even under strong scalar, nonmagnetic disorder. The chiral overlap therefore provides a direct diagnostic of the protection of low-energy states against local perturbations, independent of the bulk topological invariant.

</details>


### [276] [Topological phase rectification via Aharonov-Bohm interference in a Majorana--quantum-dot interferometer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09845)
*Jia Liu,Hao-Yuan Yang,Yuan Hong,Li Ma,Feng Chi,Zi-Chuan Yi,Li-Ming Liu,Zhen-Guo Fu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出并理论研究了基于量子点-马约拉纳干涉仪（quantum-dot–Majorana interferometer）的超导整流器。通过磁通调控Aharonov-Bohm相位，干涉在非整数磁通下产生持久电流背景，使电流-相位关系进入单向导通的单极性（unipolar）区间。作者引入单极性因子η_u和拓扑二极管品质因子Z_TD，建立了单向性对量子点能级、自旋极化等参数的鲁棒性，并指出该方案可通过标准直流输运测量提供清晰的实验信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前拓扑超导二极管的研究仍缺乏兼具高整流性能与明确可区分拓扑机制（4π周期马约拉纳通道）的实验方案。作者旨在通过量子干涉机制将拓扑整流与常规超导整流从原理上区分开来，并建立可直接测量的实验可观测判据。

Method: 理论构建量子点-马约拉纳干涉仪模型，利用磁通控制的Aharonov-Bohm相位调控平凡2π周期量子点通道与拓扑4π周期马约拉纳通道之间的量子干涉。定义带符号的单极性因子η_u（|η_u|>0.5标记单极性区间），并建立其与常规二极管效率η的定量关系；进一步从η_u的傅里叶谱定义拓扑二极管品质因子Z_TD。

Result: 非整数磁通下量子干涉产生持久电流背景I_off，使电流-相位关系转为单极性区间。单极性对量子点能级变动、自旋极化和马约拉纳杂化具有鲁棒性，随马约拉纳耦合和Rashba自旋轨道相互作用增强而提升，并在现实温度与准粒子毒化（quasiparticle poisoning）条件下保持稳定。Z_TD的非零值提供了不依赖模型的4π周期马约拉纳通道标志。

Conclusion: 量子点-马约拉纳干涉仪是实现高性能拓扑超导二极管的有前景途径，其清晰实验特征可通过标准直流输运测量观测，为区分拓扑与平凡整流机制提供了可操作的判据。

Abstract: We propose and theoretically investigate a topological superconducting rectifier based on a quantum-dot--Majorana interferometer. The Aharonov-Bohm phase, controlled by a magnetic flux threading the interferometer loop, tunes the quantum interference between a trivial $2π$-periodic quantum-dot channel and a topological $4π$-periodic Majorana channel. At non-integer flux, this interference generates a persistent current background $I_{\rm off}$ that shifts the current-phase relation into a unipolar regime, in which the supercurrent flows strictly in one direction. We introduce a signed unipolarity factor $η_u$, with $|η_u|>0.5$ defining the unipolar regime, and establish its quantitative relationship to the conventional diode efficiency $η$. The unipolarity proves robust against variations of the quantum-dot level, spin polarization, and Majorana hybridization, is enhanced by stronger Majorana coupling and Rashba spin-orbit interaction, and persists at realistic temperatures and under quasiparticle poisoning. We further propose a topological diode figure of merit $\mathcal{Z}_{\rm TD}$, defined from the Fourier spectrum of $η_u$, whose nonzero value provides a model-independent signature of the $4π$-periodic Majorana channel and distinguishes topological from trivial rectification mechanisms. Our findings establish the quantum-dot--Majorana interferometer as a promising route toward high-performance topological superconducting diodes with clear experimental signatures accessible via standard dc transport measurements.

</details>

### [277] [Critical damping in linear system with two degrees of freedom: surprises and pitfalls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08656)
*Maxim D. Arnold,Oleg V. Gendelman,Vadim Zharnitsky*

Main category: physics.class-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了通用二自由度（two-degree-of-freedom）系统的临界阻尼（critical damping）概念，指出其对应于具有最大多重性的实特征值（等于特征频率的负几何平均），并分析了该情况下的阻尼矩阵性质及其物理可实现性。 


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典临界阻尼概念通常局限于单自由度系统，本文旨在将这一概念推广至更具一般性的二自由度系统，并刻画其若干关键性质与物理实现条件。

Method: 通过解析分析二自由度系统的特征值结构，将临界阻尼定义为实特征值取得最大多重性的情形，并系统考察阻尼矩阵的唯一性（在模态坐标反射意义下）、非对角性与正定性等代数性质。

Result: 临界阻尼对应特征频率的负几何平均，为任意初始条件下最快的渐近衰减率；临界阻尼矩阵在模态坐标反射意义下唯一且一般情况下是非对角的；当特征频率差异较大时，该矩阵并非正定，需引入提供负有效阻尼（negative effective damping）的有源元件（active elements）方能实现。

Conclusion: 二自由度系统的临界阻尼在工程上可能无法通过纯无源元件实现，尤其在特征频率差异显著时，需要依赖有源系统提供负阻尼，这为系统设计与物理实现提出了新的挑战。

Abstract: In this Brief Communication, we establish the notion of critical damping for generic two-degree-of-freedom system. For given set of masses and stiffnesses, the critical damping corresponds to the real eigenvalue with maximal multiplicity, equal to minus geometrical mean of the eigenfrequencies. This case corresponds to the fastest possible asymptotic decay rate for generic initial conditions. The damping matrix for the critical case is unique up to reflection of one modal coordinate and, generically, non-diagonal. Quite surprisingly, for large difference of the eigenfrequencies, it is also not positive definite. Therefore, physical realization of the critical case will require active elements that provide negative effective damping.

</details>

### [278] [Elliptic solutions to matrix CKP equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08774)
*A. Zabrodin*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 本文研究矩阵CKP方程的椭圆型解，推导了极点及其处矩阵系数的运动方程，发现与标量情形一致，运动方程为一阶，区别于KP和BKP层级中的二阶运动方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CKP方程是卡拉泰奥多里-卡多马-佩特维亚什维利（CKP）层级中的核心对象，其矩阵化推广（matrix CKP equation）的椭圆型解结构是理解该层级可积性质的关键。此前标量CB情况已明确，矩阵情形下的极点动力学尚待系统研究。

Method: 本文针对矩阵CKP方程研究一类椭圆型解，通过分析解的极点（poles）展开结构，推导出极点自身及极点处矩阵系数的运动方程（equations of motion），并将其与KP和BKP层级中的对应结果进行对比。

Result: 研究获得了极点运动方程及其处矩阵系数的运动方程，两者均为一阶方程，与标量情形一致；这与KP和BKP层级中运动方程为二阶的情况形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 矩阵CKP方程的椭圆型解具有一阶极点动力学结构，这一特性是该方程（乃至CKP层级）区别于KP和BKP层级的一个本质特征，在矩阵推广情形下依然成立。

Abstract: A class of elliptic solutions to the matrix CKP equation is studied. Equations of motion for their poles and matrix coefficients at the poles are obtained. As in the scalar case, they are of the first order, in contrast to what takes place in the KP and BKP hierarchies, where the equations of motion are of the second order.

</details>


### [279] [Isochronous and underdamped waveforms of modified Emden oscillators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09008)
*J. de la Cruz,H. C. Rosu*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 本文通过广义交换因子化方法（generalized commutative factorization approach）系统地求解了任意自然幂次 q 的修正 Emden 非线性振子（modified Emden nonlinear oscillators）的 Bernoulli 型波形，揭示了奇偶 q 之间的动力学二分法（odd-even dynamical dichotomy）：奇数 q 对应等时振荡（isochronous oscillation），偶数 q 对应欠阻尼行为（underdamped behavior）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Emden 型非线性振子在非线性动力学与力学系统中具有广泛代表性，但其任意自然幂次下的精确波形与周期性质此前缺乏统一的理论框架，需要一种系统化方法进行分类求解与动态行为刻画。

Method: 采用广义交换因子化方法推导 Bernoulli 型波形解析解；利用 Lurie 耗散描述建立拉格朗日（Lagrangian）表述；对奇数 q 情形引入 Sabatini 意义的广义极坐标（generalized polar-coordinate）分析验证等时性；对偶数 q 情形运用 Bendixson-Dulac 判据结合径向速度函数证明周期轨道不存在。

Result: 奇数 q 情形得到周期 T = 2π/ω 的等时振子，周期与振幅及初始条件无关；偶数 q 情形呈现欠阻尼衰减且无周期轨道。文中给出了 q = 1, 2, 3, 4 的显式波形与相图，并提供了阻尼情形的非指数包络公式及等时情形的奇异区域界。

Conclusion: 该框架统一解释了修正 Emden 振子的奇偶动态二分法，为等时性与耗散行为的解析表征提供了完整工具，并隐含若干潜在应用前景。

Abstract: Bernoulli-type waveforms for modified Emden nonlinear oscillators of arbitrary natural power $q$ are obtained through a generalized commutative factorization approach. These oscillators display a well-defined odd-even dynamical dichotomy, which is discussed in detail: the odd-$q$ cases entail isochronous oscillators whose period $T = 2π/ω$ is independent of amplitude and initial conditions, while the even-$q$ cases display underdamped behavior. The Lagrangian formulation is presented in the Lurie's dissipative description. The isochronous regime and the period of the solutions in the odd case are also confirmed through a generalized polar-coordinate analysis in the spirit of Sabatini's work. The absence of periodic orbits for even $q$ is shown to be a consequence of the
  Bendixson-Dulac criterion applied to the radial velocity function. Explicit waveforms and their phase portraits are
  presented for $q = 1, 2, 3, 4$, along with the non exponential envelope formulas for the damped cases and
  singular-region bounds for the isochronous ones. A few possible applications are also mentioned.

</details>


<div id='physics.ins-det'></div>

# physics.ins-det [[Back]](#toc)

### [280] [CMOS Image Sensors for CEvNS Detection at Nuclear Reactors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07676)
*Santiago Ezequiel Perez,Dario Rodrigues,Miguel Sofo-Haro*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本文提出将互补金属氧化物半导体图像传感器（CMOS Image Sensor，CIS）用于反应堆中微子实验，针对相干弹性中微子-核散射（coherent elastic neutrino-nucleus scattering，CEvNS）探测。通过主动屏蔽策略和解析背景模型，在保守参数下可在不到50天内探测CEvNS，改进性能后可将探测时间缩短至约7天，确立CIS作为下一代CEvNS实验有前景的平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反应堆中微子实验需要高灵敏度、低成本的探测器来探测CEvNS信号，而传统探测器在物理背景抑制和噪声控制方面面临挑战。本文旨在探索CIS技术作为新型探测器在该领域的可行性。

Method: 提出一种主动屏蔽（active-shielding）策略以减少物理背景；开发了解析模型来描述暗电流（dark current）和读出噪声（readout noise）在给定帧率（frame rate, fps）下对探测器固有背景的影响；基于此模型对探测时间进行参数扫描评估。

Result: 在保守条件下（100 DRU背景、约100克灵敏硅质量、1个电子读出噪声、200fps），距3.6 GW热功率反应堆16米处不到50天即可探测CEVNS；将读出噪声降至0.2个电子并将帧率提升至1000fps后，探测时间显著缩短至约7天。

Conclusion: CIS技术被确立为反应堆监测和精密中微子测量的有前景平台；厚CMOS传感器（thick CMOS sensors）凭借其并行和非破坏性读出（parallel and non-destructive readout）特性，成为下一代CEVNS实验的特别有吸引力的候选方案。

Abstract: In this work we present Complementary Metal-Oxide-Semiconductor (CMOS) Image Sensors (CIS) for reactor neutrino experiments targeting coherent elastic neutrino-nucleus scattering (CEvNS). We have developed an active-shielding strategy for physical background reduction along with an analytical model describing the impact of intrinsic detector backgrounds from dark current and readout noise at a given frame rate (fps). Assuming a conservative background of 100 DRU, and a feasible CIS-based detector with $\sim$100g of sensitive silicon mass, 1$e^-$ of readout noise, and 200fps, it can detect CEvNS in less than 50 days at 16 meters from a 3.6 GW thermal reactor core. Improving the detector performance to 0.2$e^-$ readout noise and 1000fps substantially reduces the detection time to about 7 days. These results establish CIS technology as a promising platform for reactor monitoring and precision neutrino measurements, with thick CMOS sensors featuring parallel and non-destructive readout emerging as particularly attractive candidates for next-generation CEvNS experiments.

</details>


### [281] [Openable Force-Balanced Halbach Magnets: From Fibonacci Sphere Simulations to Icosahedral Realizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09323)
*Ingo Rehberg,Helmut Soltner,Peter Blümler*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本文通过理论和实验研究了可机械开启的Halbach球形磁体结构，推导出沿特定切割平面实现近乎零开启力的几何条件，并在二十面体近似的Halbach球上实验验证了开启力可降低几个数量级，同时保持优秀的磁场均匀性。该理论框架可推广至高阶多极矩及球柱形构型，为磁共振成像等大体积高均匀度永磁系统提供了新方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁体设计领域长期以来的目标是利用永磁体完全包围一个高度均匀的磁场区域，同时保持对该区域的实用可及性（practical access），这一矛盾长期困扰着该领域。现有方案往往在磁场均匀性与机械可开启性之间难以兼顾。

Method: 以离散磁子单元构成的偶极（dipolar）Halbach球为研究对象，理论推导了沿特定切割平面实现无力开启（force-free opening）的条件，这些条件定义了连续的一组几何构型，使张性磁力相互抵消、只保留剪切分量。实验上采用二十面体近似（icosahedral approximation）构建Halbach球，同时测量开启力和磁场特性进行验证。

Result: 实验结果证实，在保持优异磁场均匀性的前提下，开启力可降低数个数量级。理论框架不仅适用于偶极情形，还可推广到高阶多极矩Halbach系统，并扩展至球柱形（spherocylindrical）Halbach构型。

Conclusion: 该研究为设计大体积、高均匀度且机械可及的永磁系统提供了通用理论指导，对磁共振（magnetic resonance）等应用场景具有显著潜力，开辟了新一代可开启永磁体的工程实现路径。

Abstract: A long-standing goal in magnet design is to completely surround a volume of highly homogeneous magnetic field with permanent magnets while maintaining practical access to that volume. In this work, we present a theoretical and experimental investigation of mechanically accessible spherical magnets in Halbach configuration that can be opened with minimal or vanishing force. Focusing on dipolar Halbach spheres composed of discrete magnetic subunits, we derive conditions for force-free opening along specific cutting planes. These conditions define a continuous set of geometries for which tensile magnetic forces cancel, leaving only shear components, enabling mechanically effortless opening. The theoretical predictions are validated experimentally using icosahedral approximations of the Halbach sphere, for which both opening forces and magnetic field properties are measured. The results demonstrate that excellent field homogeneity can be preserved while reducing opening forces by orders of magnitude. Although discussed in detail for the dipolar case, the theoretical framework is general and applicable to higher-order multipole Halbach systems. Finally, the concepts are extended to spherocylindrical Halbach configurations, highlighting their potential for large-volume, highly homogeneous, and mechanically accessible permanent-magnet systems for magnetic resonance

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.soft'></div>

# cond-mat.soft [[Back]](#toc)

### [282] [A transient nonlinear finite element framework and implementation of coupled electro-chemo-mechanics of polyelectrolyte hydrogels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07638)
*Bibekananda Datta,Brandon K. Zimmerman,Thao D. Nguyen*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个基于有限元分析（finite element analysis）的数值框架，用于模拟聚电解质（polyelectrolyte, PE）水凝胶的电-化学-力学（electro-chemo-mechanical）耦合行为，并通过用户单元（UEL）子程序在Abaqus/Standard中实现，验证其准确性后应用于受限压缩与双层弯曲等场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PE水凝胶表现出复杂的力学变形、非线性应力响应、溶剂输运和离子扩散耦合行为，可能导致意想不到的溶胀动力学和变形模式，因此需要先进的计算工具来高效设计基于PE水凝胶的装置（如软体机器人执行器、微流控阀门和药物递送系统）。

Method: 采用有限元分析构建数值框架，模拟PE水凝胶在稀离子溶液中的电-化学-力学耦合行为，并以用户元素（UEL）子程序形式在Abaqus/Standard中实现该模型。

Result: 通过与文献中DMAEA凝胶在不同离子强度溶液中的瞬态自由溶胀实验对比验证了模型与UEL实现的正确性；应用该框架研究了受限压缩下的固结行为和水凝胶双层的瞬态弯曲行为。模拟结果表明，外部溶液离子强度、固定电荷密度和Flory-Huggins参数对瞬态溶胀和固结行为的幅度具有显著影响。

Conclusion: 所建立的数值框架能够有效模拟PE水凝胶的耦合电-化学-力学行为，为PE水凝胶基器件的设计与优化提供了可靠的计算工具，并揭示了关键参数对溶胀与固结行为的重要调控作用。

Abstract: Polyelectrolyte (PE) hydrogels exhibit complex behavior characterized by large mechanical deformations, nonlinear stress response, solvent transport, and ion diffusion. The interplay between these mechanisms can lead to unexpected swelling dynamics, deformation patterns, and stress response. As such, advanced computational tools are needed for the efficient design of PE hydrogel-based devices, such as actuators and sensors for soft robotics, microfluidic valves, and drug delivery systems. In this work, we develop a numerical framework to simulate the coupled electro-chemo-mechanical behavior of PE hydrogels using finite element analysis. Applying this framework, an electro-chemo-mechanical model for PE hydrogels in a dilute ionic solution is implemented as a user element (UEL) subroutine in Abaqus/Standard. The model and UEL implementation are validated by comparing to experiments in the literature for transient free-swelling of a DMAEA gel in a solution of varying ionic strengths, then applied to study the consolidation behavior under confined compression and the transient bending behavior of a hydrogel bilayer. The simulations show that the ionic strength of the external solution, fixed charge density, and Flory-Huggins parameter play significant roles in the magnitude of the transient swelling and consolidation behavior.

</details>


### [283] [Reply to Smallenburg: Near-melting nucleation and the exponential growth of hard-sphere nucleation times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07644)
*Roseanna N. Zia*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文是对一篇评论（Comment）的回应，该评论声称平衡共存态（equilibrium coexistence）"易于实现"。作者通过计算反驳这一观点，强调自发成核（spontaneous nucleation）时间随体积分数偏离53%而急剧增长，并重申Frenkel熵交换机制（entropy exchange mechanism）在该现象中的核心作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对Smallenburg在评论中对作者先前前瞻性论文（Perspective）的批评，指出其基于单一状态点做出的"平衡共存易实现"结论是未经检验的过度推广，且忽略了Frenkel机制的关键作用。作者旨在纠正这一可能引发的误解。

Method: 作者提供了新计算（calculations）来直接回应评论中的观点，并结合Frenkel提出的熵交换机制，对近熔点（near-melting point）附近的成核时间尺度进行定量分析，说明自发相分离在动力学上的可达性范围。

Result: 计算表明，自发成核时间随体积分数偏离53%仅增加十分之几百分点即呈天文数量级增长。Frenkel机制预测，自发相分离在动力学上仅在下至约53%体积分数的范围内可达，其余大部分相包络（phase envelope）区域需要极长的等待时间。

Conclusion: 评论中所宣称的"平衡共存易于实现"的结论是错误的，因为它忽略了成核时间随参数微小变化而急剧增长的事实。Frenkel的熵交换机制提供了理解这一现象的正确框架，也是回应评论误导性观点的基础。

Abstract: Smallenburg reports near-melting point simulations and observes a well-predicted spontaneous nucleation with mixed, finite-time morphology, in a Comment on our recent Perspective. The Comment's incorrect broader takeaway --- that equilibrium coexistence is "readily achievable" --- rests on an untested generalization from a single state point near the phase envelope, and misses entirely the intriguing role played by Frenkel's underlying mechanism. We reiterate the salient point missed by the Comment: the nucleation time grows astronomically with just tenths of a percent of volume fraction away from 53%. This phenomenology emerges from the entropy exchange mechanism Frenkel described, which predicts that spontaneous phase separation is dynamically accessible only down to about 53% volume fraction from the melting point, and astronomically long waiting times through most of the remaining phase envelope. We provide here calculations to address the potential misconception created by the Comment.

</details>


### [284] [Transient fluid removal at soft interfaces: Contact-time-controlled squeeze-out in a cylinder-on-flat contact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08378)
*R. Xu,B. N. J. Persson*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文研究了刚性PMMA圆柱与软PDMS基底之间的平面对圆柱（cylinder-on-flat）接触中瞬态流体的排除过程。实验在低滑动速度下进行，发现滑动过程中的摩擦力主要取决于法向载荷施加后的总接触时间而非滑动距离，且与静态挤压排除计算结果吻合，表明低速滑动下流体排除过程与静态接触本质上相同，滑动引起的弹性流体动力效应（elastohydrodynamic effects）影响很小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究中可变形橡胶块的流体排除常受边缘刮擦机制干扰，难以单独考察滑动对流体挤压排除的影响。圆柱几何构型可消除这一机制，从而更直接地探究滑动对流体排除过程的作用。

Method: 实验采用刚性PMMA圆柱与软PDMS基底间的平面对圆柱接触构型，在甘油（glycerol）中以低滑动速度v=3 μm/s进行，使用不同表面粗糙度的圆柱试样，并将测量到的摩擦力与静态挤压排除计算预测的平均表面间距和真实接触面积进行对比分析。

Result: 实验发现，经过初始弹性加载阶段后，滑动过程中的摩擦力主要取决于法向载荷施加以来的总接触时间，而非此前的滑动距离；随后的摩擦演化对总接触时间呈近似相同的依赖性。静态挤压排除计算对平均表面间距和真实接触面积的预测值与由摩擦力推断的结果合理一致。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在静态接触与低速滑动条件下，流体排除过程基本由相同的挤压排除机制主导，滑动诱导的弹性流体动力效应在这些条件下影响甚微。

Abstract: We study transient fluid removal in cylinder-on-flat contacts between stiff PMMA cylinders and a soft PDMS substrate. The cylinder geometry eliminates the edge-scraping mechanism that can occur for deformable rubber blocks, allowing the influence of sliding on fluid squeeze-out to be examined more directly. Experiments were performed mainly in glycerol at the low sliding speed $v=3 \ {\rm μm/s}$ using cylinders with different surface roughness. After the initial elastic-loading stage, we find that the friction during sliding depends primarily on the total time elapsed since application of the normal load rather than on the preceding sliding distance. The subsequent friction evolution follows approximately the same dependence on total contact time. Stationary squeeze-out calculations predict the evolution of the mean surface separation and real contact area, in reasonable agreement with that inferred from the measured friction. These results show that essentially the same squeeze-out process governs fluid removal during stationary contact and low-speed sliding, and that sliding-induced elastohydrodynamic effects have only a minor influence under these conditions.

</details>


### [285] [Heat and mass transport in a three-dimensional mesoscale odd fluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08568)
*Yuxing Jiao,Mingcheng Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文针对三维各向异性奇流体（odd fluids，输运系数张量具有非零反对称分量的流体）的介观模拟模型，基于动理学理论推导了热传导方程与自扩散方程及其对应的热导率张量和自扩散系数张量，通过模拟验证了理论结果，并进一步研究了三维奇流体在受限几何中的热与质量输运行为，发现与正常各向同性流体形成鲜明对比，稳态温度与密度分布显著受奇输运系数影响而发生形变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期工作已针对各向同性二维奇流体提出介观模拟模型，并扩展到具有柱对称性的三维各向异性情形，同时通过动理学理论推导了该模型的纳维-斯托克斯方程（Navier-Stokes equation）与黏度张量，但热传导和自扩散等输运性质尚未被系统推导和研究。

Method: 基于动理学理论（kinetic theory）推导三维介观奇流体的热传导方程与自扩散方程，获得相应的热导率张量（thermal conductivity tensor）和自扩散系数张量（self-diffusivity tensor）；并通过介观模拟在受限几何中研究热与质量输运行为，以模拟结果验证理论推导。

Result: 理论推导的热导率与自扩散系数张量结果获得了模拟验证；在受限几何中，三维奇流体的稳态温度与密度分布受到奇输运系数（odd transport coefficients）的显著影响而变形，与正常各向同性流体的行为形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 三维奇流体在受限几何中的热输运与质量输运行为本质上受奇输运系数的调控，输运系数张量的反对称分量会对稳态温度与密度分布产生显著形变效应，这体现了奇流体区别于普通流体的独特输运特性。

Abstract: Fluids with nonvanishing antisymmetric components of the transport coefficient tensor are named odd fluids. In our previous works, we proposed a mesoscale simulation model for isotropic two-dimensional odd fluids and then extended it to the three-dimensional case to model an anisotropic odd fluid with cylindrical symmetry. The Navier-Stokes equation and the viscosity tensor of this three-dimensional mesoscale odd fluid were derived previously via a kinetic theory. Herein, we derive the heat conduction and self-diffusion equations, along with the corresponding thermal conductivity and self-diffusivity tensors. These theoretical results are validated by simulations. We further investigate heat and mass transport behaviors of three-dimensional odd fluids in a confined geometry through our mesoscale model. In striking contrast to normal isotropic fluids, the steady-state temperature and density distributions of confined odd fluids are significantly deformed by the odd transport coefficients.

</details>


### [286] [Interface dynamics in tissue invasion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08735)
*Nino Despeignes,Lila Sarfati,Marc Durand,Frédéric van Wijland*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究利用水动力学（hydrodynamics）描述来刻画两种具有不同本构性质（constitutive properties）活体组织之间的界面，发现界面会涌现出依赖于水动力学尺度的毛细表面张力（capillary surface tension），该张力可稳定长波长扰动；同时，活性力差异会使前沿传播从推动波（pushed wave）转变为伯格斯波（Burgers wave），且涌现表面张力在强驱动和低黏度条件下不足以稳定界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活体组织界面的稳定性是组织形态发生和疾病进程中的关键问题，但目前缺乏基于水动力学描述的系统性理论理解，特别是界面涌现表面张力及其与前沿传播机制的关联尚不清楚。

Method: 采用活体组织的水动力学描述框架，以两类组织稳态压力（homeostatic pressure）之差和控制活性力（active force）之差作为两个独立控制参数，分别研究界面涌现的表面张力行为及传播波模式转变。

Result: 发现界面会生成依赖于水动力学尺度的涌现毛细表面张力，该张力使界面在大波长扰动下非常稳定；活性力差异导致前沿传播从推动波转变为伯格斯波，且在大驱动强度和低黏度条件下，涌现表面张力不足以稳定界面。

Conclusion: 组织界面的稳定性由水动力学尺度下涌现的毛细表面张力主导，但其稳定能力受活性力驱动和黏度的约束，这为理解活体组织界面的动力学行为提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We rely on a hydrodynamic description of living tissues to describe the interface separating two of them with distinct constitutive properties. Using the difference in their homeostatic pressures as a control parameter, we show that the interface generates an emergent capillary surface tension that depends on the hydrodynamic scale, and that makes it very stable to large wavelengths perturbations. Using the difference of active forces the two tissues experience as a control parameter, we not only find that the front propagation mechanism shifts from the pushed wave to the Burgers wave, but we also find that the emergent surface tension is not sufficient to stabilize the interface at large enough drive and low enough viscosity.

</details>


### [287] [Depletant-DNA Induces Chirality in the Condensed Domains of Lyotropic Liquid Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08970)
*Zeba Afia Hasan,Elizabeth Adeogun,Harold Hatch,Jacob Monroe,Karthik Nayani*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文报告了色团液晶（chromonic liquid crystal）——色甘酸钠（disodium cromoglycate, DSCG）溶液中，由DNA作为耗尽剂（depletant）引起的相行为变化。研究发现DNA（无论是单链还是双链）能够在比常用耗尽剂聚乙二醇（polyethylene glycol）低约千倍体积分数下诱导DSCG的相凝聚，并通过引入宏观手性（chirality）增加DSCG聚集体长度，且可用于PCR后DNA的快速光学检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索DNA作为新型高效耗尽剂在液晶相行为调控中的潜力，并开发一种能够快速光学检测扩增DNA的简单平台。

Method: 通过偏振光学显微镜（polarized optical microscopy）表征扭转角以揭示手性；利用荧光标记DNA成像确认DNA仅存在于连续相；采用紫外-可见吸收光谱（UV-vis absorption spectroscopy）测量引入手性后DSCG聚集体的吸收系数变化。

Result: DNA可在比聚乙二醇低约千倍的体积分数下诱导DSCG相凝聚；DNA引入的手性使吸收系数显著降低，表明DSCG平均聚集体长度大幅增加；纳摩尔浓度的DNA（典型于PCR扩增后水平）可诱导DSCG发生各向同性-双相（isotropic-biphasic）相变。

Conclusion: DNA是一类极其高效的耗尽剂，能够同时调控向列型液晶的相凝聚和手性传递，为检测扩增DNA提供了一种简单、快速的光学平台。

Abstract: We report on the phase behavior of solutions of a chromonic liquid crystal, disodium cromoglycate (DSCG) resulting from the presence of DNA that acts as a depletant. We show that DNA (both single and double stranded) can induce phase condensation of DSCG at volume fractions a thousand-fold lower than a commonly studied depletant of similar size, namely polyethylene glycol. Twist angle characterization via polarized optical microscopy reveals DNA introduces macroscopic chirality within the condensed phase, despite being present only in the continuous phase- as confirmed by imaging of fluorescently tagged DNA. UV-vis absorption spectroscopy reveals that the introduction of chirality reduces the absorption coefficient strongly, indicating that induced chirality leads to considerable increase in the average lengths of DSCG aggregates. We also show that DNA at concentrations (~nmol/L) typical for a post amplification event (such as PCR) can induce an isotropic-biphasic phase transition of DSCG enabling a fast optical method to report the presence of DNA. Overall, these findings establish DNA as exceptionally efficient depletants that regulate both phase condensation and chirality transfer in lyotropic liquid crystals and thereby providing a simple optical platform for detection of amplified DNA.

</details>


### [288] [An Active Matter Pathway for Non-Equilibrium Liquid-Liquid Extractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08978)
*Jude C. Obijiaku,Oladipupo P. Ogolo,Damilola E. Fadipe,Angela Deneke,Karthik Nayani*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该论文报告了一种由马兰戈尼流（Marangoni flow）驱动的金属离子跨油水界面（oil-water interface）非平衡输运路径，发现界面张力梯度引发的马兰戈尼流可将萃取速率提高至少三倍，并提出了一种液-液萃取的新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统液-液萃取通常基于扩散主导的传质过程，作者试图探索是否存在一种由界面流动效应驱动的非平衡输运机制，以突破传统萃取动力学的限制。

Method: 在特定条件下，使金属物种吸附到装饰有萃取剂（extractant）的油水界面上，借此产生界面张力梯度（interfacial tension gradient），从而诱导马兰戈尼流的产生；随后观测该流动条件下的金属去除行为及萃取动力学特征。

Result: 马兰戈尼流的产生伴随着金属从水相中的去除，且萃取动力学呈现非扩散行为（non-diffusive behavior）；在存在马兰戈尼流时，萃取速率显著提高，至少提高三倍；界面失稳强度与界面张力的降低相耦合，而这种降低具有金属依赖性，可实现速率介导的选择性萃取。

Conclusion: 该研究表明马兰戈尼流驱动的界面输运为液-液萃取提供了一种根本不同的范式（paradigm），为未来研究方向和技术开发开辟了新的可能性。

Abstract: We report on a Marangoni flow-driven pathway for non-equilibrium transport of metal ions across an oil-water interface. Under specific conditions, we show that the adsorption of a metallic species onto the extractant-decorated oil-water interface creates interfacial tension gradients that give rise to Marangoni flows. Strikingly, we observe that these flows are accompanied by removal of metal from the aqueous phase and that the extraction kinetics follow a non-diffusive behavior. The extraction rates are significantly higher - at least a threefold increase - when Marangoni flows are present. We show that the strength of the interfacial instability is coupled to the lowering of the interfacial tension- which is metal-dependent and therefore can lead to rate-mediated selective extractions. Overall, our study shows a fundamentally different paradigm for liquid-liquid extractions opening up a range of new directions of inquiry and future technologies.

</details>


### [289] [Effective one-body interactions due to the presence of a liquid-vapor interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09375)
*Melih Gül,Roland Roth*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文在经典密度泛函理论（classical density functional theory）框架下，利用方阱相互作用的几何结构，提出了基于加权密度（weighted densities）的微扰理论贡献形式，并将其用于研究溶剂液-气界面对溶解纳米粒子产生的有效单体相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目的是构建一种新的密度泛函微扰理论形式，以描述二元方阱混合物中纳米粒子在液-气界面附近受到的有效相互作用，并验证该相互作用的计算结果在不同途径下的一致性。

Method: 方法基于经典密度泛函理论，利用方阱相互作用的几何结构，借鉴基础测度理论（fundamental measure theory）的思想，以加权密度形式提出微扰理论对密度泛函的贡献；随后计算溶剂液-气界面对稀释纳米组分产生的有效单体相互作用。

Result: 有效单体相互作用会强烈地将纳米粒子吸引至界面。该有效相互作用势既能通过全混合体系在低但非零纳米粒子浓度下的密度分布计算，也可在纳米粒子密度趋于零的稀极限下利用Widom插入定理（Widom insertion theorem）获得，两条途径的结果表现出极好的一致性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，所提出的加权密度微扰泛函能够准确捕捉液-气界面对纳米粒子的强吸引效应，并且两种独立计算路径的高度一致性验证了该方法的可靠性与适用性。

Abstract: In this study we investigate the behavior of additive binary square-well mixtures within the framework of classical density functional theory. By leveraging on the geometrical structure of the square-well interaction, we propose a novel form of the perturbation theory contribution to the density functional in terms of weighted densities, inspired by fundamental measure theory. We apply this functional in order to study the effective one-body interaction due to the liquid-vapor interface of the solvent acting on a dilute component of dissolved nano particles. The effective one-body interaction attracts the nano particles strongly to the interface. We show that this effective interaction potential can be calculated either from the density profiles of the full mixture, at low but non-vanishing concentrations of the nano particles, or by employing the Widom insertion theorem in the dilute limit of vanishing density of nano particles. Both routes display excellent agreement.

</details>


### [290] [When Spectroscopies Speak the Same Language: Unifying Rheology, Electrochemical Impedance, and Dielectrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09423)
*Shivangi Mittal,Sachin Shanbhag,Yogesh M. Joshi*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本工作通过线性响应理论（linear response theory）的统一框架，对三类谱学技术——流变/力学谱学（rheological/mechanical spectroscopy）、电化学阻抗谱（electrochemical impedance spectroscopy）和宽带介电谱（broadband dielectric spectroscopy）——进行系统比较，揭示其共同基本结构与细微差异，并讨论数据验证与分析方法的跨领域通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谱学技术虽广泛用于测量物理系统在振荡扰动下的动力学响应，但各领域间常因术语与习惯差异而缺乏沟通。本文旨在通过线性响应理论这一共同数学框架，建立不同谱学方法间的统一视角，促进思想与方法在不同领域间的无缝交流。

Method: 从线性响应理论的视角切入，对三类谱学技术进行统一分析。作者通过比较各技术向线性响应理论的映射方式、基本构建模块（building blocks）、基础模型，以及时域和频域响应函数，来揭示它们之间的概念相似性与细微差别；同时讨论克拉默斯–克勒尼希关系（Kramers-Kronig relations）、等效电路（equivalent circuits）和多模模型（multimode models）等数据验证与分析手段。

Result: 研究指出三类谱学技术在数学框架和基本物理原则上高度相通，但各自在映射细节、模型构件和数据分析惯例上存在细微差异。共享基础使得跨领域的方法移植与思想交换具有可行性。

Conclusion: 不同谱学领域虽有应用场景与术语上的差异，但其共同的理论根基为跨领域协作与知识迁移提供了基础，有助于推进各谱学方法在数据分析和模型构建方面的协同发展。

Abstract: Spectroscopic techniques measure the dynamical response of physical systems subjected to oscillatory perturbations. For small perturbations around the equilibrium state, these spectroscopic methods are unified by the common mathematical framework of linear response theory. This work presents a unified perspective on rheological or mechanical spectroscopy, electrochemical impedance spectroscopy, and broadband dielectric spectroscopy through the lens of linear response theory. Subtle conceptual similarities and differences among these techniques are highlighted by analyzing their mapping to the linear response theory, basic building blocks, elementary models, and time and frequency domain response functions. Data validation and analysis approaches, including Kramers-Kronig relations, equivalent circuits, and multimode models are discussed. The shared fundamentals of different spectroscopies enable seamless exchange of ideas across domains.

</details>

### [291] [Fluid-Structure Interaction and Underwater Hydrostatic Implosion of Thin-Walled Metallic Cylinders in Semi-Confined Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07647)
*Bolaji Oladipo,Helio Matos,Arun Shukla,Sumanta Das*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本研究利用LS-DYNA中结构化的任意拉格朗日-欧拉（ALE）公式，对半受限流体环境中金属圆柱体的静水压溃行为及其流固耦合（FSI）效应进行了全面的数值研究。模型成功复现了实验测得的3.69 MPa压溃压力，并以1.01%的误差预测了首次水锤压力峰值。研究发现材料类型、长径比和束缚直径显著影响压溃行为、压力演化和流体运动，为非潜水下压力壳体和水下结构的设计与安全评估提供了定量指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下结构在极端静水压载荷下可能发生压溃失效，其过程中结构与周围流体的强耦合效应（包括水锤压力、射流和空化）难以通过实验完整捕捉。采用实验测量的压力-时间历史难以揭示全场的流固耦合机制，因此需要高保真的数值模型来系统研究材料属性、几何参数和束缚条件对压溃行为的影响。

Method: 采用LS-DYNA中结构化的任意拉格朗日-欧拉（Arbitrary Lagrangian-Eulerian, ALE）公式，对半受限流体环境中的金属圆柱体在静水压载荷下的动态压溃与流固耦合行为进行数值模拟。首先通过对比实验测得压溃压力（3.69 MPa）和水锤压力峰值进行模型验证，随后系统变化材料类型（铝和钛）、圆柱体长径比（L/D）以及束缚直径，考察其对压溃行为、压力演化和流体运动的影响。

Result: 数值模型以1.01%的误差准确预测了首次水锤压力峰值。钛圆柱体由于更高的刚度和屈服强度，表现出更剧烈的压溃、超过70 MPa的水锤压力以及更大的动能和应变能累积；较低长径比产生更突然的压溃，而较高长径比促进渐进式轴对称变形；更大的束缚直径增强了射流形成并提高了流体速度。全场流固耦合分析捕捉到径向射流、峰值流体速度和内部空化等仅靠压力-时间历史无法观察到的关键现象。

Conclusion: 结构几何、材料刚度和束缚条件共同决定了压溃诱导的能量传递机制，全场流固耦合分析对于理解和预测水下结构在极端静水压载荷下的失效行为至关重要。研究结果为潜水下压力壳体、海底管道及其他水下结构的设计与安全评估提供了定量参考依据。

Abstract: This study presents a comprehensive numerical investigation of the dynamic behavior and fluid-structure interactions (FSI) of metallic cylinders undergoing hydrostatic collapse in semi-confined fluid environments using a structured Arbitrary Eulerian-Lagrangian (ALE) formulation in LS-DYNA. The numerical model reproduces the experimentally measured collapse pressure of 3.69 MPa and predicts the first water hammer peak with a 1.01% error, demonstrating high predictive fidelity. Following validation, the effects of material type (aluminum and titanium), cylinder slenderness ratio (L/D), and confinement diameter on collapse behavior, pressure evolution, and fluid motion are examined. Titanium cylinders exhibited sharper collapses, higher water hammer pressures exceeding 70 MPa, and greater kinetic and strain energy accumulation than aluminum due to their higher stiffness and yield strength. Lower L/D ratios produced more abrupt collapses, whereas higher L/D ratios promoted more gradual, axisymmetric deformation. Larger confinement diameters intensified jet formation and increased fluid velocities. The simulations provide mechanistic insight into the coupling between structural deformation and surrounding fluid, showing that geometry, material stiffness, and confinement govern collapse-induced energy transfer. Full-field FSI analysis captures key phenomena, including radial jetting, peak fluid velocities, and internal cavitation, that are not evident from pressure-time histories alone. These findings provide quantitative guidance for the design and safety assessment of subsea pressure housings, marine pipelines, and other underwater structures subjected to extreme hydrostatic loading.

</details>


### [292] [Exact solution for the motion of a rigid particle with $\boldsymbol{S_4}$ and $\boldsymbol{C_{2v}}$ symmetry settling under gravity in a viscous fluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09585)
*Piotr Zdybel,Maria L. Ekiel-Jeżewska*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本研究给出了具有C2v和S4对称性、均匀密度的刚性颗粒在粘性流体（雷诺数远小于1）中重力沉降动力学的精确完整解。S4对称性使问题完全可积，所有轨道由单一守恒量C标记，颗粒运动呈现准周期性，水平轨迹呈花瓣状开放曲线，并被两个同心包络圆约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低雷诺数粘性流体中沉降的刚性颗粒是一个经典流体力学问题，但此前对具有特定对称性颗粒的完整精确动力学解尚属空白。该研究旨在利用S4对称性实现理论上的完全可积性，从而给出颗粒沉降运动的闭合形式解。

Method: 该研究利用S4对称性将问题约化为完全可积系统，并引入单个守恒量0≤C≤1来标记所有轨道。通过将倾斜角θ和自旋角ψ表达为时间的周期雅可比椭圆函数（Jacobi elliptic functions），方位角φ通过带复特征值的第三类不完全椭圆积分来表示，由此将φ分解为均匀漂移与严格周期调制两部分。

Result: 研究发现0<C<1时存在两个不同时间尺度，导致颗粒运动呈准周期性演化并伴随显著的水平位移。垂直质心位移以周期性的不完全椭圆积分表达，轨道平均沉降速度简化为两个完全椭圆积分的比值。水平质心轨迹呈花瓣状开放曲线，由两个同心包络圆约束，包络半径具有极简精确表达式；文章还阐明了轨迹尖点的来源，并给出严格周期花瓣的共振条件。

Conclusion: 该工作为具有S4对称性的刚性颗粒在低雷诺数沉降问题提供了精确且完整的动力学解，揭示了准周期运动、包络轨迹和尖点等丰富的几何与动力学结构，并且证明当旋转-平移耦合趋于零时包络半径趋于无穷大。

Abstract: We provide an exact and complete solution for the dynamics of a rigid particle of uniform density with $C_{2v}$ and $S_4$ symmetry, settling under gravity in a viscous fluid at a Reynolds number much smaller than unity. The $S_4$ symmetry renders the problem exactly integrable, with all orbits labelled by a single conserved quantity $0\le C\le 1$. We show that, for $0<C<1$, there are two different time scales, which lead to quasi-periodic evolution, with a significant time-dependent horizontal displacement. We obtain the tilt $θ$ and spin $ψ$ Euler angles as periodic Jacobi elliptic functions of time, and the azimuthal angle $φ$ through an incomplete elliptic integral of the third kind with a complex characteristic, which splits $φ$ into a uniform drift and a strictly periodic modulation. The orientation period and the drift rate (corresponding to a constant angular velocity around the gravity direction) follow in a closed form. The vertical centre-of-mass displacement is obtained in terms of periodic in time incomplete elliptic integrals, and the periodic orbit-averaged settling velocity reduces to a single ratio of complete elliptic integrals. The horizontal component of the motion in the laboratory frame of reference is obtained exactly and algebraically in terms of all three Euler angles, so it is quasi-periodic. The horizontal component of the centre-of-mass position traces rosette-like, in general open curves confined by two concentric `envelope' circles. We determine very simple exact expressions for radii of both envelopes and demonstrate that they tend to infinity for a family of shapes with the rotation-translation coupling decreasing to zero. We also explain the origin of cusps at the rosette-like trajectory and provide a commensurability condition selecting strictly periodic rosettes.

</details>
